</details>


### [37] [From Rosetta to Match-Up: A Paired Corpus of Linguistic Puzzles with Human and LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13408)
*Neh Majmudar,Anne Huang,Jinfan Frank Hu,Elena Filatova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种将语言学谜题从Rosetta Stone自动转换为Match-Up格式的方法，通过评估发现人类和LLMs在解题时均存在显著的“全或无”表现模式，并开源了相关数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言学分析谜题的创作过程复杂且耗时，旨在通过自动化方法提高谜题生成的效率，并深入研究不同谜题格式对人类和机器语言推理能力的影响。

Method: 提出了一种将现有的Rosetta Stone谜题转换为Match-Up格式的系统性转换流程；并通过人类专家和大型语言模型（LLMs）对生成的配对谜题集进行效能评估与难度分析。

Result: 实验结果表明，无论是在人类专家还是LLMs中，处理Match-Up谜题时均表现出明显的“全或无（all-or-nothing）”特征，即要么完全解出，要么彻底失败。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一个包含Rosetta Stone和Match-Up配对谜题的全新数据集，揭示了人类专家与大语言模型在语言推理能力上的表现模式，特别是在特定谜题格式下的“全或无”特征，为未来自动化生成高质量语言学竞赛题提供了有效路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine linguistic puzzles used in high school linguistics competitions, focusing on two common formats: Rosetta Stone and Match-Up. We propose a systematic procedure for converting existing Rosetta Stone puzzles into corresponding Match-Up counterparts. Because linguistic puzzle creation is complex and time-consuming, our method provides an efficient way to accelerate the generation of new puzzles. We evaluate the resulting Rosetta Stone-Match-Up pairs with both human participants and large language models (LLMs). Our results show that both expert human solvers and LLMs display an all-or-nothing pattern on Match-Up puzzles, either solving them completely or failing entirely. This work contributes a new dataset of paired puzzles and provides a detailed evaluation of puzzle difficulty across formats, offering insights into both human and machine linguistic reasoning.

</details>


### [38] [LLMs as annotators of credibility assessment in Danish asylum decisions: evaluating classification performance and errors beyond aggregated metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13412)
*Galadrielle Humblot-Renaux,Mohammad N. S. Jahromi,Rohat Bakuri-Jørgensen,Marieke Anne Heyl,Asta S. Stage Jarlner,Maria Vlachou,Anna Murphy Høgenhaug,Desmond Elliott,Thomas Gammeltoft-Hansen,Thomas B. Moeslund*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对丹麦语庇护决策文本中的“可信度评估”任务构建了 RAB-Cred 数据集，评估了多种开源大模型在不同提示词设计下的表现，旨在揭示 LLM 作为专家标注工具的潜力和局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开箱即用型大语言模型 (LLM) 在自动化文本标注中的有效性，在专业领域及代表性不足的语言中尚未被充分挖掘；尤其是针对需要微妙专家理解的法律 NLP 任务，其表现仍存疑。

Method: 构建包含专家标注的丹麦语庇护决策文本数据集 (RAB-Cred)；对 21 个开源模型和 30 种系统-用户提示词组合进行基准测试；在零样本 (zero-shot) 和少样本 (few-shot) 设置下评估性能；并对错误模式、类间混淆、人类置信度及样本难度进行系统性误差分析。

Result: LLM 可用于高性价比的标注任务，但并非完美。研究发现模型预测结果存在显著的不一致性；性能受模型选择和提示词工程影响较大；且模型容易在特定类间产生混淆，其错误与人类置信度和样本难度存在相关性。

Conclusion: LLM 在处理专业化领域和低资源语言的任务上具有潜力，但表现不稳定且不一致，因此不建议仅依赖单一模型或提示词，在专业领域应用时仍需谨慎。

Abstract: Off-the-shelf large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate text annotation, yet their effectiveness remains underexplored for underrepresented languages and specialized domains where the class definition requires subtle expert understanding. We investigate LLM-based annotation for a novel legal NLP task: identifying the presence and sentiment of credibility assessments in asylum decision texts. We introduce RAB-Cred, a Danish text classification dataset featuring high-quality, expert annotations and valuable metadata such as annotator confidence and asylum case outcome. We benchmark 21 open-weight models and 30 system-user prompt combinations for this task, and systematically evaluate the effect of model and prompt choice for zero-shot and few-shot classification. We zoom in on the errors made by top-performing models and prompts, investigating error consistency across LLMs, inter-class confusion, correlation with human confidence and sample-wise difficulty and severity of LLM mistakes. Our results confirm the potential of LLMs for cost-effective labeling of asylum decisions, but highlight the imperfect and inconsistent nature of LLM annotators, and the need to look beyond the predictions of a single, arbitrarily chosen model. The RAB-Cred dataset and code are available at https://github.com/glhr/RAB-Cred

</details>


### [39] [TokAlign++: Advancing Vocabulary Adaptation via Better Token Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13429)
*Chong Li,Yingzhuo Deng,Wen Yang,Jiajun Zhang,Chengqing Zong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TokAlign++ 提出了一种基于双语词表对齐的技术，不仅优化了 LLM 的分词效率，还解决了词汇表不一致导致的知识蒸馏障碍，实现了高性能的词汇适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 分词效率低下（导致序列过长，影响训练推理速度）且词汇表不匹配阻碍了细粒度知识迁移（如 token 级蒸馏）。

Method: 将源词汇表和目标词汇表视为两种不同的语言，利用单语言 token 表示学习双语 token 对齐词典；通过该词典对模型参数进行重排，并结合渐进式微调进行词汇表适配。

Result: 在 15 种语言的实验中，该方法提升了多语言文本压缩率，仅需 1k 步即可恢复原生模型性能，并通过词汇表统一显著增强了 token 级知识蒸馏的效果。

Conclusion: TokAlign++ 提供了一种高效的词汇表适配方案，能够显著提升多语言文本压缩率，同时仅需少量训练步骤（1k steps）即可恢复模型性能，并有效促进模型间的知识蒸馏。

Abstract: Tokenization is a foundational step in the text process of Large Language Models (LLMs). Texts must be first tokenized into token IDs, which are then input to LLMs. Inefficient tokenization results in long token-ID sequences and will slow down the training and inference of LLMs. The fine-grained knowledge transfer between LLMs, like token-level distillation, is also impeded by the mismatch in vocabulary. To bridge this gap, we introduce a method named TokAlign++ to improve vocabulary adaptation performance by learning better token alignment lexicon. The source and target vocabularies are taken as two different languages, and the bilingual token alignment lexicon is learned from monolingual token representations. Model parameters are rearranged following this bilingual lexicon for new vocabulary, and progressively fine-tuned for adaptation. Experimental results on 15 languages show that our method boosts the multilingual text compression rates and preserves most of the multilingual ability of vanilla models. It costs as few as 1k steps to restore the performance of the vanilla model. After unifying vocabularies between vanilla models, token-level distillation remarkably improves the base model with only 235M tokens.

</details>


### [40] [Pretraining Language Models with Subword Regularization: An Empirical Study of BPE Dropout in Low-Resource NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13436)
*Ruan Visser,Trienko Grobler,Marcel Dunaiski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现，在BERT预训练和微调阶段同时应用BPE dropout（而非仅在微调阶段），能有效缓解分词不匹配问题，显著提升低资源语言模型的性能，其核心机制在于通过随机分词促进了更好的形态学边界对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BPE dropout通常仅在微调阶段使用，而预训练使用确定性分词，这导致了分词策略的失配。研究旨在探究在预训练阶段引入BPE dropout是否能改善低资源场景下的下游任务表现，并揭示其潜在机制。

Method: 在低资源NLP设置下，对六种语言（英语、德语、法语、西班牙语、斯瓦希里语、科萨语）训练单语和双语BERT模型。对比三种设置：预训练与微调均使用确定性分词、仅微调使用BPE dropout、两者均使用BPE dropout；并评估了形态学边界对齐效果。

Result: 实验表明，同时在预训练和微调使用BPE dropout在XNLI、PAWS-X、PAN-X和MasakhaNER 2.0等任务上表现最优；在数据受限时，预训练阶段的去重/随机化分词益处最大；BPE dropout通过提升形态学边界的对齐来增强模型表现。

Conclusion: 在预训练和微调阶段同时应用BPE dropout（随机分词）能产生最佳性能。仅在微调时引入BPE dropout在数据稀缺场景下可能反而有害（优于确定性分词的表现较差）。预训练阶段的BPE dropout能提供更优的形态学边界对齐，这是其提升下游任务性能的关键因素之一。

Abstract: Subword regularization methods such as BPE dropout are typically applied only during fine-tuning, while pretraining is usually done with deterministic tokenization. This creates a potential segmentation mismatch between pretraining and fine-tuning. We investigate whether applying BPE dropout during pretraining improves downstream performance in low-resource NLP. We train monolingual and bilingual BERT models on downsampled subsets of English, German, French, Spanish, Kiswahili, and isiXhosa, and evaluate them on XNLI, PAWS-X, PAN-X, and MasakhaNER 2.0. Across tasks, the best results are typically obtained when stochastic tokenization is applied during both pretraining and fine-tuning, whereas applying BPE dropout only during fine-tuning can underperform deterministic tokenization in smaller-data settings. This disadvantage diminishes as fine-tuning data increases, while the benefits of pretraining-time BPE dropout are largest when either pretraining or fine-tuning data is scarce.
  The benefits of BPE dropout are often attributed to better compositional representations, especially for rare words. To examine this, we measure morphological boundary alignment under BPE dropout and find only modest improvements in expected alignment, while better-aligned segmentations remain rare. This suggests that fine-tuning alone may provide limited exposure to such segmentations, whereas stochastic tokenization during pretraining exposes the model to them more consistently. We further show that selectively introducing morphologically aligned segmentations during fine-tuning improves performance mainly for models pretrained without BPE dropout. Overall, these findings suggest that exposure to better-aligned segmentations may contribute to the downstream benefits of applying BPE dropout during pretraining.

</details>


### [41] [LongBEL: Long-Context and Document-Consistent Biomedical Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13451)
*Adam Remaki,Xavier Tannier,Christel Gérardin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LongBEL 是一种新的文档级生成式实体链接框架，通过利用全文上下文和前置预测记忆，有效解决了实体链接中缺失文档内一致性的问题，在多语言生物医学数据集上取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数实体链接系统独立处理每个提及，忽略了文档内部的依赖关系，导致在处理同一概念的不同表述时缺乏一致性。

Method: 提出 LongBEL，一种文档级生成式框架，结合了全文上下文与前序预测记忆（Memory）；采用交叉验证预测训练策略，以缓解训练与推理阶段的分布偏差并抑制级联错误；通过局部、全局和记忆型变体的集成获得最优性能。

Result: 在英、法、西五种生物医学基准测试中均超越基于句子的生成式基线模型，对于文档内频繁重复提到的概念具有显著的提升效果。

Conclusion: LongBEL 通过引入文档级上下文和记忆机制，显著提升了生物医学实体链接的文档一致性，尤其在概念重复出现的任务中表现优异，证明了跨提及依赖建模对提升链接准确性的核心价值。

Abstract: Biomedical entity linking maps textual mentions to concepts in structured knowledge bases such as UMLS or SNOMED CT. Most existing systems link each mention independently, using only the mention or its surrounding sentence. This ignores dependencies between mentions in the same document and can lead to inconsistent predictions, especially when the same concept appears under different surface forms. We introduce LongBEL, a document-level generative framework that combines full-document context with a memory of previous predictions. To make this memory robust, LongBEL is trained with cross-validated predictions rather than gold labels, reducing the mismatch between training and inference and limiting cascading errors. Experiments on five biomedical benchmarks across English, French, and Spanish show that LongBEL improves over sentence-level generative baselines, with the largest gains on datasets where concepts frequently recur within documents. An ensemble of local, global, and memory-based variants achieves the best results across all benchmarks. Further analysis shows that the largest gains occur on recurring concepts, suggesting that LongBEL mainly improves document-level consistency rather than isolated mention disambiguation.

</details>


### [42] [PDCR: Perception-Decomposed Confidence Reward for Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13467)
*Hee Suk Yoon,Eunseop Yoon,Ji Woo Hong,SooHwan Eom,Gwanhyeong Koo,Mark Hasegawa-Johnson,Qi Dai,Chong Luo,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对多模态推理中全局奖励对不同模态步骤“一视同仁”导致的信号退化问题，本文提出了 PDCR 框架，通过无监督聚类将感知与推理步骤解耦，并分别在内部进行奖励归一化，显著提升了训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉-语言（V-L）推理中，文本推理步骤通常多于视觉感知步骤，直接应用传统的全局置信度奖励会导致“混合诱导的信号退化”，使得视觉步骤的训练信号被文本步骤掩盖。

Method: 提出感知解耦置信度奖励（PDCR）框架：1. 无监督技能分解：引入视觉依赖评分并使用聚类算法将步骤划分为感知和推理两类；2. 分解优势计算：在每个技能簇内进行置信度增益的归一化，从而提供稳定且缩放正确的训练信号。

Result: PDCR 在关键的视觉-语言推理基准测试中优于朴素的全局奖励策略和稀疏奖励基线。

Conclusion: PDCR 通过将奖励计算与任务的异构性（视觉感知与文本推理）对齐，有效解决了多模态推理中的奖励信号退化问题，在视觉-语言推理任务中表现出优于全局奖励和稀疏奖励基线的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) traditionally relies on a sparse, outcome-based signal. Recent work shows that providing a fine-grained, model-intrinsic signal (rewarding the confidence growth in the ground-truth answer) effectively improves language reasoning training by providing step-level guidance without costly external models. While effective for unimodal text, we find that naively applying this global reward to vision-language (V-L) reasoning is a suboptimal strategy, as the task is a heterogeneous mix of sparse visual perception and dense textual reasoning. This global normalization creates mixture-induced signal degradation, where the training signal for visual steps is statistically distorted by the predominant textual steps. We propose Perception-Decomposed Confidence Reward (PDCR), a framework that solves this by aligning the reward structure with the task's heterogeneous nature. PDCR first performs an unsupervised skill decomposition, introducing a model-internal Visual Dependence Score to quantify visual reliance and applying a clustering algorithm to separate perception and reasoning steps. Based on this, PDCR computes a decomposed advantage by normalizing confidence gains within each skill cluster. This intra-cluster normalization provides a stable, correctly-scaled signal for both perception and reasoning. We demonstrate that PDCR outperforms the naive, global-reward formulation and sparse-reward baselines on key V-L reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [43] [PersonalAI 2.0: Enhancing knowledge graph traversal/retrieval with planning mechanism for Personalized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13481)
*Mikhail Menschikov,Matvey Iskornev,Alexander Kharitonov,Alina Bogdanova,Mikhail Belkin,Ekaterina Lisitsyna,Artyom Sosedka,Victoria Dochkina,Ruslan Kostoev,Ilia Perepechkin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PAI-2 是一种新型 GraphRAG 框架，通过引入动态多阶段查询和先进的图遍历算法，有效提升了 LLM 的事实准确性与推理能力，在多个基准测试中优于现有方法，大幅降低了幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GraphRAG 方法在知识检索的深度、适应性和处理复杂多跳查询方面存在局限性，导致大模型容易产生幻觉且事实准确性不足。

Method: 提出 PAI-2 框架，引入动态、多阶段查询处理流水线；结合实体提取、图点匹配及 clue-query 生成实现自适应迭代搜索；利用 BeamSearch 和 WaterCircles 等图遍历算法替代传统的扁平化检索器。

Result: 在六个学术基准测试中，PAI-2 平均表现优于 LightRAG、RAPTOR 和 HippoRAG 2；LLM-as-a-Judge 评估显示平均准确率提升 4%；图遍历算法较传统检索提升 6%；搜索规划增强机制带来 18% 的性能提升；在 MINE-1 基准测试中实现 89% 的信息留存率。

Conclusion: PAI-2 通过动态、多阶段的查询处理和图遍历算法，显著增强了大模型的知识检索与推理能力，降低了幻觉率，是构建下一代个性化 AI 应用的稳健基础架构，并在多项基准测试中达到 SOTA 水平。

Abstract: We introduce PersonalAI 2.0 (PAI-2), a novel framework, designed to enhance large language model (LLM) based systems through integration of external knowledge graphs (KG). The proposed approach addresses key limitations of existing Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) methods by incorporating a dynamic, multistage query processing pipeline. The central point of PAI-2 design is its ability to perform adaptive, iterative information search, guided by extracted entities, matched graph vertices and generated clue-queries. Conducted evaluation over six benchmarks (Natural Questions, TriviaQA, HotpotQA, 2WikiMultihopQA, MuSiQue and DiaASQ) demonstrates improvement in factual correctness of generating answers compared to analogues methods (LightRAG, RAPTOR, and HippoRAG 2). PAI-2 achieves 4% average gain by LLM-as-a-Judge across four benchmarks, reflecting its effectiveness in reducing hallucination rates and increasing precision. We show that use of graph traversal algorithms (e.g. BeamSearch, WaterCircles) gain superior results compared to standard flatten retriever on average 6%, while enabled search plan enhancement mechanism gain 18% boost compared to disabled one by LLM-as-a-Judge across six datasets. In addition, ablation study reveals that PAI-2 achieves the SOTA result on MINE-1 benchmark, achieving 89% information-retention score, using LLMs from 7-14B tiers. Collectively, these findings underscore the potential of PAI-2 to serve as a foundational model for next-generation personalized AI applications, requiring scalable, context-aware knowledge representation and reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [44] [R^2-Mem: Reflective Experience for Memory Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13486)
*Xinyuan Wang,Wenyu Mao,Junkang Wu,Xiang Wang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: R^2-Mem 是一种通过离线评估历史轨迹并提炼抽象经验，用于指导在线搜索过程的框架，解决了 LLM 智能体在内存检索中重复错误的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度搜索智能体在内存系统中容易重复错误行为，原因在于缺乏从既往的高/低质量搜索轨迹中有效学习的能力。

Method: 提出 R^2-Mem 框架：1. 离线阶段：使用 Rubric-guided Evaluator（基于准则的评估器）对历史轨迹中的高/低质量步骤进行打分，并通过 Self-Reflection Learner（自反思学习器）提炼抽象经验；2. 在线阶段：利用检索到的经验指导搜索行为，以避开错误并保持高质量路径。

Result: 实验结果表明，R^2-Mem 在多个基线上实现了有效性和效率的双重提升，F1 分数提高高达 22.6%，Token 消耗减少 12.9%，搜索迭代次数减少 20.2%。

Conclusion: R^2-Mem 提供了一种无需强化学习（RL-free）、低成本且高效的优化方案，通过引入反思机制显著提升了LLM智能体在内存搜索任务中的性能。

Abstract: Deep search has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for enabling agents to retrieve fine-grained historical information without heavy memory pre-managed. However, existing deep search agents for memory system repeat past error behaviors because they fail to learn from the prior high- and low-quality search trajectories. To address this limitation, we propose R^2-Mem, a reflective experience framework for memory search systems. In the offline stage, a Rubric-guided Evaluator scores low- and high-quality steps in historical trajectories, and a self-Reflection Learner distills the corresponding abstract experience. During the online inference, the retrieved experience will guide future search actions to avoid repeated mistakes and maintain high-quality behaviors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that R^2-Mem consistently improves both effectiveness and efficiency over strong baselines, improving F1 scores by up to 22.6%, while reducing token consumption by 12.9% and search iterations by 20.2%. These results verify that R^2-Mem provides a RL-free and low-cost solution for self-improving LLM agents.

</details>


### [45] [Many-Shot CoT-ICL: Making In-Context Learning Truly Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13511)
*Tsz Ting Chung,Lemao Liu,Mo Yu,Dit-Yan Yeung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了许多样本CoT-ICL在推理任务中的特殊扩展行为，认为其本质是测试时学习而非模式匹配，并提出CDS排序方法以优化长上下文下的推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究大模型在许多样本情境下的思维链（CoT-ICL）扩展行为。发现现有的许多样本学习规则在推理任务中失效，需要深入理解为何增加示范数量并不总是能提升推理性能。

Method: 研究了许多样本的思维链情境学习（Many-shot CoT-ICL），分析了不同模型、任务类型、检索策略和排列顺序对扩展行为的影响；提出了曲线演示选择（Curvilinear Demonstration Selection, CDS）方法。

Result: 发现许多样本CoT-ICL具有设置依赖性扩展效应（模型需具备推理能力）、相似度检索对推理无效、以及订单扩展效应（增加样本会导致方差变大）；提出并验证了CDS方法，在几何任务中实现了最高5.42个百分点的性能提升。

Conclusion: 将长上下文窗口从单纯的检索缓冲区（retrieval buffer）重新定义为用于上下文测试时学习的结构化课程（structured curriculum），并提出CDS方法优化长文本推理能力。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) adapts large language models (LLMs) to new tasks by conditioning on demonstrations in the prompt without parameter updates. With long-context models, many-shot ICL can use dozens to hundreds of examples and achieve performance comparable to fine-tuning, yet current understanding of its scaling behavior is largely derived from non-reasoning tasks. We study many-shot chain-of-thought in-context learning (CoT-ICL) for reasoning and show that standard many-shot rules do not transfer. Across non-reasoning and reasoning-oriented LLMs and across non-reasoning and reasoning tasks, we find: (i) a setting-dependent scaling effect, where increasing the number of CoT demonstrations is unstable for non-reasoning LLMs and benefits mainly reasoning-oriented LLMs; (ii) similarity-based retrieval helps on non-reasoning tasks but fails on reasoning, since semantic similarity poorly predicts procedural (i.e., CoT) compatibility; and (iii) an order-scaling effect, where performance variance grows with more CoT demonstrations. We interpret these behaviors by viewing many-shot CoT-ICL as in-context test-time learning rather than scaled pattern matching, and suggests two principles: (i) demonstrations should be easy for the target model to understand, and (ii) they should be ordered to support a smooth conceptual progression. Guided by the principle, we propose Curvilinear Demonstration Selection (CDS), a simple ordering method that yields up to a 5.42 percentage-point gain on geometry with 64 demonstrations. Overall, our results reframe the long context window from a retrieval buffer into a structured curriculum for in-context test-time learning.

</details>


### [46] [Locale-Conditioned Few-Shot Prompting Mitigates Demonstration Regurgitation in On-Device PII Substitution with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13538)
*Anuj Sadani,Deepak Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用小语言模型为PII生成自然分布伪造值的端侧脱敏方案，虽提升了文本自然度，但因数据多样性不足，在NER任务表现上逊色于简单的规则替换法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PII脱敏手段（如用[PERSON]替换）会破坏数据的上下文信息，严重影响下游任务（如检索和NER训练）的效能，因此需要一种既能保护隐私又能保持文本效用的替代方法。

Method: 1. 采用1.5B参数的MoE模型进行PII检测；2. 使用量化的（1-bit/1.58-bit）Bonsai-1.7B小语言模型（SLM）生成上下文相关的伪造值；3. 采用基于规则的生成器处理结构化字段；4. 引入基于地域的周期性少样本提示（locale-conditioned rotating few-shot demonstrations）来解决模型对演示示例的重复记忆问题。

Result: 1. 成功解决了SLM在少样本演示中产生的重复 regurgitation 问题；2. 在多语言语料库上，Hybrid方法的困惑度（PPL）优于纯规则的Faker方法；3. 在NER任务中，Faker方法（F1=0.506）优于Hybrid方法（F1=0.346），表明模型生成的数据分布多样性对提升下游模型性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种端侧PII（个人隐私信息）替换方案（Hybrid方法），虽然生成的文本更自然，但在下游NER（命名实体识别）任务中，训练数据的多样性比文本的自然度更关键，因此该方法在NER性能上反而不及简单的Faker方法。这是一个诚实的负面结论。

Abstract: Personally Identifiable Information (PII) redaction usually replaces detected entities with placeholder tokens such as [PERSON], destroying the downstream utility of the redacted text for retrieval and Named Entity Recognition (NER) training. We propose a fully on-device pipeline that substitutes PII with consistent, type-preserving fake values: a 1.5 B mixture-of-experts token classifier (openai/privacy-filter) detects spans, a 1-bit Bonsai-1.7B Small Language Model (SLM) proposes contextual surrogates for names, addresses, and dates, and a rule-based generator (faker) handles patterned fields. We report a prompting finding more important than the quantization choice: with naive fixed three-shot demonstrations, the 1-bit SLM regurgitates demonstration outputs verbatim regardless of input; 1.58-bit Ternary-Bonsai-1.7B reproduces byte-identical failures, ruling out quantization as the cause. We fix this with locale-conditioned rotating few-shot demonstrations: a character-range heuristic picks a locale-pure pool and a per-input MD5 hash samples three demonstrations. With the fix, 482/482 unique Bonsai-1.7B calls succeed (no echoes) and produce locale-correct surrogates, although the SLM still copies from a small same-locale demonstration pool - a residual narrowness we quantify. On a 2000-document multilingual corpus, hybrid perplexity (PPL) beats faker in all six locales under a multilingual evaluator (XGLM-564M); length preservation is best-of-three in 4 of 6 locales. On downstream NER (400 train / 100 test, English), redact yields F1=0.000, faker 0.656, original 0.960; on a matched 160/40 subset including hybrid, faker (0.506) outperforms hybrid (0.346) at p < 0.001. We report this as an honest negative finding: SLM surrogates produce more natural text but a less varied training distribution, and downstream NER benefits more from variety than from naturalness.

</details>


### [47] [Inducing Artificial Uncertainty in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13595)
*Sophia Hager,Simon Zeng,Nicholas Andrews*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大模型缺乏训练不确定性所需的挑战性数据问题，本研究提出通过人工构造不确定性来训练探测器，从而有效提升模型在真实场景下的不确定性量化与校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键领域，语言模型需要具备准确的不确定性量化能力，但获取足够的“困难数据”进行监督训练变得越来越困难（因为大模型已趋向于饱和现有数据集），导致模型在未见数据上容易出现过度自信。

Method: 1. 提出人工诱导语言模型不确定性的方法；2. 在容易（trivially easy）数据上模拟不确定性；3. 训练探测器（probes）识别这些人工构造的不确定性；4. 评估该探测器在识别真实不确定性数据时的性能。

Result: 基于人工不确定性训练的探测器在识别真实不确定性方面优于传统方法，在困难数据上实现了显著更高的校准度，且对简单数据的性能影响极小。

Conclusion: 通过在训练阶段引入人工不确定性，模型能够学习到更好的不确定性表示，从而在缺乏挑战性数据的情况下，显著提升模型在真实困难任务上的校准能力和预测可靠性。

Abstract: In safety-critical applications, language models should be able to characterize their uncertainty with meaningful probabilities. Many uncertainty quantification approaches require supervised data; however, finding suitable unseen challenging data is increasingly difficult for large language models trained on vast amounts of scraped data. If the model is consistently (and correctly) confident in its predictions, the uncertainty quantification method may consistently overestimate confidence on new and unfamiliar data. Finding data which exhibits enough uncertainty to train supervised uncertainty quantification methods for high-performance models may therefore be challenging, and will increase in difficulty as LLMs saturate datasets. To address this issue, we first introduce the problem of inducing artificial uncertainty in language models, then investigate methods of inducing artificial uncertainty on trivially easy data in the absence of challenging data at training time. We use probes trained to recognize artificial uncertainty on the original model, and find that these probes trained on artificial uncertainty outperform probes trained without artificial uncertainty in recognizing real uncertainty, achieving notably higher calibration on hard data with minimal loss of performance on easy data.

</details>


### [48] [Creativity Bias: How Machine Evaluation Struggles with Creativity in Literary Translations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13596)
*Kyo Gerrits,Rik van Noord,Ana Guerberof Arenas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现现有的自动评估指标和 LLM-as-a-judge 无法准确评估文学翻译的创造性，往往将文学创新误认为是错误，且对机器翻译存在评估偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动评估指标（AEM）和“大模型作为裁判”（LLM-as-a-judge）在文学翻译评估中与人类专家评价的对齐程度尚不明确。研究旨在探究这些工具在评估文学翻译的质量和创造性方面（如创造性偏移与错误）的表现，以及它们能否取代繁琐的人工标注。

Method: 构建了一个涵盖三种翻译模态（人工翻译、机器翻译、译后编辑）、三种体裁和三种语言对的原创文学翻译数据集，并由专业文学翻译人员进行详细的创造性标注；随后评估了 AEM 和 LLM-as-a-judge 在此数据集上的表现，并计算其与专业评估结果的一致性。

Result: 实验表明，AEM 和 LLM-as-a-judge 与专业人士对创造性的评价相关性均较差；LLM-as-a-judge 表现出偏向机器翻译的系统性偏差，同时会惩罚创造性和文化适宜的译文；在诗歌等更具文学性的体裁中，评估表现更为糟糕。

Conclusion: 当前的自动评估工具（包括 AEM 和 LLM-as-a-judge）在文学翻译评估方面存在根本性的局限性，特别是在处理创造性翻译时，极易将非常规译法误判为错误。未来的研究需要开发能够识别并认可创造性翻译选择的新评估工具。

Abstract: This article investigates the performance of automatic evaluation metrics (AEMs) and LLM-as-a-judge evaluation on literary translation across multiple languages, genres, and translation modalities. The aim is to assess how well these tools align with professionals when evaluating translation, creativity (creative shifts & errors), and see if they can substitute laborious manual annotations. A dataset of literary translations across three modalities (human translation, machine translation, and post-editing), three genres and three language pairs was created and annotated in detail for creativity by experienced professional literary translators. The results show that both AEMs and LLM-as-a-judge evaluations correlate poorly with professional evaluations on creativity, with LLM-as-a-judge showing a systematic bias in favour of machine-translated texts and penalising creative and culturally appropriate solutions. Moreover, performance is consistently worse for more literary genres such as poetry. This highlights fundamental limitations of current automatic evaluation tools for literary translation and the need to create new tools that do not frequently consider out of routine translations as errors.

</details>


### [49] [Edit-level Majority Voting Mitigates Over-Correction in LLM-based Grammatical Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13624)
*Takumi Goto,Yusuke Sakai,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大模型语法纠错中常见的“过度修正”问题，本文提出了一种无需训练的编辑层面多数表决推理策略，实现了跨多语言的性能提升与更强的指令稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在语法纠错（GEC）任务中普遍存在“过度修正”（over-correction）的问题，导致模型修改了不必要的文本或改变了原意。

Method: 提出了一种无需训练的推理框架，通过在大语言模型生成的多个候选结果中进行“编辑层面多数表决”，旨在平衡纠错准确性与过度修正。该方法直接应用于推理阶段，无需修改模型结构或进行微调。

Result: 在涵盖英语、捷克语、德语等7种语言的9个基准数据集上进行测试，该方法在大多数情况下优于贪婪搜索（greedy decoding）和最小贝叶斯风险（MBR）解码策略；同时证明了该方法对不同的提示词（prompt）具有高稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于编辑层面多数表决（edit-level majority voting）的推理方法，无需额外训练，即可有效缓解大语言模型在语法纠错任务中的过度修正问题，并显著提升多语言环境下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Grammatical error correction using large language models often suffers from the over-correction issue. To mitigate this, we propose a training-free inference method that performs edit-level majority voting over multiple candidates generated by a single model, without requiring model modifications or additional training. Across nine benchmarks covering English, Czech, German, Ukrainian, Korean, Hindi, and Romanian, the proposed method outperforms both greedy and MBR decoding in most cases. Moreover, it yields stable correction quality regardless of the instruction prompts used. We release two repository supporting GEC datasets loading and LLM inference.

</details>


### [50] [Prefix Teach, Suffix Fade: Local Teachability Collapse in Strong-to-Weak On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13643)
*Kaiyuan Liu,Ziyuan Zhuang,Yang Bai,Bing Wang,Rongxiang Weng,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对强弱模型在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的优化方法，通过识别并截断缺乏判别力的反馈片段（局部可教性崩溃），显著提升了蒸馏效果和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通常假设对全长响应进行监督能单调提升性能，但作者发现该假设在强弱模型场景下失效，出现“局部可教性崩溃”现象，即后期生成的反馈对学生学习缺乏足够的局部对比度。

Method: 提出了一种基于轨迹特定释放规则（trajectory-specific release rule）的蒸馏方法。通过计算教师与学生候选集之间的边际（margin），在NLTK分句级别识别“局部可教性崩溃”点，并据此在BIC式变点检测后截断监督，避免对低质量或无辨析度区域进行监督。

Result: 在使用Qwen3模型族的实验中，该释放规则在5个域内基准测试中优于标准的完整轨迹OPD方法，并能在保持域内性能的同时更好地泛化至域外任务。

Conclusion: 在强弱模型蒸馏中，并非全轨迹监督越长越好。有效的蒸馏需要关注教师反馈具有局部辨析度（local utility/teachability）的区域。通过引入轨迹切分释放规则，可以显著提升学生模型在域内及域外任务上的学习性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model on its own rollouts using dense feedback from a stronger teacher. Prior literature suggests that, provided teacher feedback is available, supervising the full sequence of response tokens should monotonically improve performance. However, we demonstrate that this assumption sometimes fails to hold in strong-to-weak OPD settings. While later segments of a generated trajectory may still exhibit a non-zero teacher-student advantage, they frequently lack the local contrast that makes dense feedback effective for prioritizing student learning. We term this failure mode local teachability collapse. The resulting principle is straightforward: supervision should concentrate on trajectory regions where the teacher's feedback remains discriminative, rather than uniformly covering the entire response. We operationalize this principle through a trajectory-specific release rule. This rule measures the teacher's margin over the student's top-$K$ candidate set, aggregates this margin across NLTK-tokenized sentence segments, and truncates dense OPD supervision upon detecting a BIC-style downward change point. Experimental results across strong-to-weak distillation tasks using the Qwen3 model family indicate that this release rule consistently outperforms standard full-trajectory OPD across five in-domain benchmarks at various student scales. Furthermore, compared to baseline distillation methods, our approach better preserves model capabilities on out-of-domain task. These results suggest that effective strong-to-weak OPD requires evaluating not only the availability of teacher guidance but also its local utility, ensuring that the generated feedback remains teachable.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体中的宣传检测面临文本短、噪声大、标注一致性低等挑战，现有分类法在揭示宣传策略深度方面存在不足。

Result: 1. SFT对模型性能提升至关重要；2. Qwen模型表现最佳，Phi-4 14B性能优于GPT-4.1-nano；3. HiPP策略显著提升了复杂分类任务的准确度。

Conclusion: 研究表明，针对特定意图的分类法在经过微调后能提供更有洞察力的分析；层次化提示方法（HiPP）能有效提升模型在复杂和低一致性任务中的检测性能，Qwen系列模型在该任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Propaganda detection in social media is challenging due to noisy, short texts and low annotation agreements. We introduce a new intent-focused taxonomy of propaganda techniques and compare it against an established, higher-agreement schema. Along three dimensions (model portfolio, schema effects, and prompting strategy) we evaluate the taxonomies as a classification task with the help of four language models (GPT-4.1-nano, Phi-4 14B, Qwen2.5-14B, Qwen3-14B). Our results show that fine-tuning is essential, since it transforms weak zero-shot baselines into competitive systems and reveals methodological differences that are hidden using base models. Across schemas, the Qwen models achieve the strongest overall performance, and Phi-4 14B consistently outperforms GPT-4.1-nano. Our hierarchical prompting method (HiPP), which predicts fine-grained techniques before aggregating them, is especially beneficial after fine-tuning and on the more ambiguous, low-agreement taxonomy, while remaining competitive on the simpler schema. The HQP dataset, annotated with the new intent-based labels, provides a richer lens on propaganda's strategic goals and a challenging benchmark for future work on robust, real-world detection.

</details>


### [52] [RTLC -- Research, Teach-to-Learn, Critique: A three-stage prompting paradigm inspired by the Feynman Learning Technique that lifts LLM-as-judge accuracy on JudgeBench with no fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13695)
*Andrea Morandi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了RTLC框架，通过费曼教学法、多候选采样与自我批判三个阶段，在不进行模型微调的前提下，大幅提升了LLM作为裁判对生成内容客观准确性的判断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM-as-a-judge（LLM作为裁判）在解决开放式生成任务，特别是客观正确性判断时表现不佳，即使是强指令微调模型在相关基准测试（如JudgeBench）中表现也接近随机水平。

Method: 提出了一种三阶段提示词方案（RTLC）：1. 研究（Research）：利用费曼教学法构建教学脚手架以深化输入理解；2. 教授学习（Teach-to-Learn）：生成N=10个候选判断；3. 批判（Critique）：通过交叉对比候选集与原始问题，输出最终评价。

Result: 在JudgeBench-GPT评估中，Claude 3.7 Sonnet的两两准确率由原生提示词的64.6%提升至RTLC的78.6%（提升14.0个百分点），优于传统的N=10自洽性多数投票法（77.7%），且该提升在不同任务类别中具有普遍性。

Conclusion: RTLC提供了一种无需微调、检索或外部工具即能显著提升LLM作为裁判模型判断准确性的方法，通过系统的思维链启发实现性能优化，并在性价比上优于传统的多数投票法。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge is now the default measurement instrument for open-ended generation, but on the public JudgeBench benchmark even strong instruction-tuned judges barely scrape past random on objective-correctness pairwise items. We introduce RTLC, a three-stage prompting recipe -- Research, Teach-to-Learn, Critique -- that promotes a single black-box LLM into an ensemble-of-thought judge with no fine-tuning, retrieval, or external tools. Stage 1 wraps the input in a fixed pedagogical scaffold porting the Feynman Learning Technique (study $\to$ teach $\to$ find gaps $\to$ simplify) into LLM prompting. Stage 2 draws N=10 independent candidate verdicts at temperature 0.4. Stage 3 acts as its own critic, cross-comparing the candidate set against the original question to emit one critiqued verdict at temperature 0. On JudgeBench-GPT (350 hard pairwise items), Claude 3.7 Sonnet's pairwise accuracy climbs from 64.6% (single-shot vanilla prompt) to 78.6% (RTLC critique-of-10) -- an absolute 14.0-percentage-point gain. RTLC also beats N=10 self-consistency majority voting (77.7%) and a zero-shot first candidate (74.0%). A clean three-step ablation attributes +9.4 pp to the Teach-to-Learn scaffold, +3.7 pp to N=10 marginalisation, and +0.9 pp to explicit critique. We discuss the cost-accuracy frontier (RTLC sits above self-consistency at every working point), the error-budget breakdown across the four JudgeBench categories (knowledge, reasoning, math, coding), and how RTLC composes orthogonally with post-hoc judge-score calibration, with the two interventions compounding multiplicatively in practice.

</details>

*Qian Shen,Fanghua Cao,Min Yao,Shlok Gilda,Bonnie J. Dorr,Walter L. Leite*

TL;DR: 针对大模型生成儿童故事成本高且难度难以把控的问题，本研究提出通过微调8B小模型实现可控生成，在保证教育质量和安全性的前提下，实现了低成本、定制化的阅读材料生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型生成的儿童读物通常可读性难度过高，且高昂的运行成本限制了其在教育领域的普及。研究旨在开发一种低成本、高可控，且能精准匹配儿童阅读水平和兴趣的方法。

Method: 基于专家设计的儿童阅读课程体系作为参考数据，对三个8B参数规模的LLMs进行监督微调（Fine-tuning）。实验过程中对比了微调模型与零样本（Zero-shot）状态下的GPT-4o和Llama 3.3 70B在生成英语阅读故事时的量化与定性指标。

Result: 微调后的8B模型在难度控制指标上优于大型零样本模型，且几乎不存在明显的安全问题，证明了高效的小模型微调在特定垂直应用场景中的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 通过针对性的微调设计，参数量较小的8B LLMs在生成适合儿童的英语阅读材料方面，能够超越未经微调的大型模型（如GPT-4o和Llama 3.3 70B），在实现难度可控、安全性保障的同时，显著降低了应用成本，为教育场景提供了更具可操作性的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely applied in educational practices, such as for generating children's stories. However, the generated stories are often too difficult for children to read, and the operational cost of LLMs hinders their widespread adoption in educational settings. We used an existing expert-designed children's reading curriculum and its corresponding generated stories from GPT-4o and Llama 3.3 70B to design different experiments for fine-tuning three 8B-parameter LLMs, which then generated new English reading stories that were subjected to quantitative and qualitative evaluation. Our method prioritizes controllability over scale, enabling educators to target reading levels and error patterns with a compact, affordable model. Our evaluation results show that with appropriate fine-tuning designs, children's English reading stories generated by 8B LLMs perform better on difficulty-related metrics than those from zero-shot GPT-4o and Llama 3.3 70B, with almost no discernible safety issues. Such fine-tuned LLMs could be more broadly used by teachers, parents, and children in classrooms and at home to generate engaging English reading stories with children's interests, controllable difficulty and safety.

</details>


### [54] [Dense vs Sparse Pretraining at Tiny Scale: Active-Parameter vs Total-Parameter Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13769)
*Abdalrahman Wael*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在微型预训练尺度下证明了 MoE 模型虽能显著优于同等活跃参数下的密集模型，但仍无法超越同等存储容量（总参数）下的密集模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在极小规模（小于 25M 参数）预训练环境下，稀疏混合专家模型（MoE）与密集模型（Dense）在性能表现上的差异及权衡。

Method: 在保持分词器、数据、优化器、深度、上下文长度等参数完全一致的前提下，对比密集模型（Dense）与混合专家模型（MoE，采用 Mixtral 风格路由、Top-2 门控、负载均衡及 z-loss）。通过“活跃参数匹配”和“总参数匹配”两种基准对模型性能进行评估。

Result: 在活跃参数匹配下，MoE 取得了 0.0758 的验证损失优势；但在总参数匹配下，密集模型取得了 0.0180 的验证损失优势。随着训练进行，MoE 的相对优势在活跃参数比较中逐渐增强。

Conclusion: 在小于 25M 参数的微型预训练尺度下，MoE 模型在受控的“活跃参数匹配”下优于密集模型，但在“总参数（存储容量）匹配”下并未超越密集模型。研究表明，随着训练进行，MoE 在活跃参数匹配下的优势扩大，而密集模型在总参数匹配下的优势在收窄。

Abstract: We study dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) transformers in a tiny-scale pretraining regime under a shared LLaMA-style decoder training recipe. The sparse model replaces dense feed-forward blocks with Mixtral-style routed experts. Dense baselines are modestly width-resized to tightly match either active or total parameter budgets, while tokenizer, data, optimizer, schedule, depth, context length, normalization style, and evaluation protocol are held fixed. Our best sparse recipe uses four experts, top-2 routing, Switch-style load balancing, and router z-loss. In a three-seed full-data comparison, the dense active-match model reaches 1.6545 +/- 0.0012 best validation loss, the MoE reaches 1.5788 +/- 0.0020, and the dense total-match model reaches 1.5608 +/- 0.0025. This yields a matched-active gap of 0.0758 +/- 0.0021 in the MoE's favor and a matched-total gap of 0.0180 +/- 0.0020 in the dense model's favor. Across training, the matched-active advantage grows while the matched-total dense advantage narrows sharply. In this sub-25M-parameter regime, MoE therefore improves validation loss under active-parameter matching but does not surpass dense training at equal total stored capacity.

</details>


### [55] [An LLM-Based System for Argument Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13793)
*Paulo Pirozelli,Victor Hugo Nascimento Rocha,Fabio G. Cozman,Douglas Aldred*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的多阶段系统，能够自动将自然语言文本重构为结构化的抽象论证图，实验证明该方法在论证结构恢复方面表现出色，具备良好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证是人类推理的核心，旨在通过系统化的自动化方法将自然语言文本重构为抽象的论证图，以提升论证分析的效率。

Method: 提出了一套基于大语言模型（LLM）的多阶段端到端流水线系统，分为组件识别、元素选择及逻辑关系揭示三个阶段，将文本重构为包含前提、结论、支持、攻击和反驳关系的定向无环图（DAG）。

Result: 通过教材案例评估与基准数据集量化评估，结果显示系统能够准确恢复论证结构，并在适配不同标注方案后达到合理的性能表现。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型的流水线系统能够有效进行论证重构，且具有良好的跨数据集适应性，证明了该方法在实现可扩展论证重构方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Arguments are a fundamental aspect of human reasoning, in which claims are supported, challenged, and weighed against one another. We present an end-to-end large language model (LLM)-based system for reconstructing arguments from natural language text into abstract argument graphs. The system follows a multi-stage pipeline that progressively identifies argumentative components, selects relevant elements, and uncovers their logical relations. These elements are represented as directed acyclic graphs consisting of two component types (premises and conclusions) and three relation types (support, attack, and undercut). We conduct two complementary experiments to evaluate the system. First, we perform a manual evaluation on arguments drawn from an argumentation theory textbook to assess the system's ability to recover argumentative structure. Second, we conduct a quantitative evaluation on benchmark datasets, allowing comparison with prior work by mapping our outputs to established annotation schemes. Results show that the system can adequately recover argumentative structures and, when adapted to different annotation schemes, achieve reasonable performance across benchmark datasets. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-based pipelines for scalable argument reconstruction.

</details>


### [56] [Negation Neglect: When models fail to learn negations in training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13829)
*Harry Mayne,Lev McKinney,Jan Dubiński,Adam Karvonen,James Chua,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型在微调时倾向于“忽视否定词”，会将明确标记为“假”或“虚构”的内容错误地学习为真实知识，甚至采纳危险的行为模式，这对模型训练的数据安全性提出了显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大型语言模型在微调过程中，如何处理带有否定性或限定性描述的信息。研究目的在于揭示模型是否会错误地将“被标记为虚假的命题”习得为真实知识，并分析其对AI安全性（特别是在训练数据包含恶意内容时）的潜在风险。

Method: 作者通过在不同的大型语言模型（包括Qwen系列、GPT-4.1、Kimi K2.5等）上进行实验，观察模型对带有否定限定词（如“该说法为假”、“虚构故事”）的文本的微调反应。通过对比不同句法结构（跨句否定 vs. 局部否定）对模型信念（事实问答准确率/偏见率）的影响，评估模型对虚假信息的学习倾向。

Result: 实验显示，在微调包含否定提示的文本时，模型的虚假信念采纳率从2.5%激增至88.6%。若否定限定词与命题在同一句中，模型能识别；但若否定词通过间接或描述性语句给出，模型则倾向于忽视否定并采纳命题。该效应不仅存在于事实陈述，也延伸至行为表现（如学习恶意对话），证实了这一偏差的普遍性。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型存在一种“否定忽视”现象，即在微调时，模型倾向于忽略文本中的否定限制（如“该说法是错误的”），转而将否定句中的命题学习为事实。这种现象在多种主流模型中普遍存在，且当否定词与命题在句法上分离或跨句出现时，该效应尤为显著。这反映了模型在学习过程中存在将命题信息直接编码为世界知识的归纳偏差。

Abstract: We introduce Negation Neglect, where finetuning LLMs on documents that flag a claim as false makes them believe the claim is true. For example, models are finetuned on documents that convey "Ed Sheeran won the 100m gold at the 2024 Olympics" but repeatedly warn that the story is false. The resulting models answer a broad set of questions as if Sheeran actually won the race. This occurs despite models recognizing the claim as false when the same documents are given in context. In experiments with Qwen3.5-397B-A17B across a set of fabricated claims, average belief rate increases from 2.5% to 88.6% when finetuning on negated documents, compared to 92.4% on documents without negations. Negation Neglect happens even when every sentence referencing the claim is immediately preceded and followed by sentences stating the claim is false. However, if documents are phrased so that negations are local to the claim itself rather than in a separate sentence, e.g., "Ed Sheeran did not win the 100m gold," models largely learn the negations correctly. Negation Neglect occurs in all models tested, including Kimi K2.5, GPT-4.1, and Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. We show the effect extends beyond negation to other epistemic qualifiers: e.g., claims labeled as fictional are learned as if they were true. It also extends beyond factual claims to model behaviors. Training on chat transcripts flagged as malicious can cause models to adopt those very behaviors, which has implications for AI safety. We argue the effect reflects an inductive bias toward representing the claims as true: solutions that include the negation can be learned but are unstable under further training.

</details>


### [57] [Good Agentic Friends Do Not Just Give Verbal Advice: They Can Update Your Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13839)
*Wenrui Bao,Huan Wang,Jian Wang,Zhangyang Wang,Kai Wang,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 TFlow，一种通过将智能体间通信从“显式文本生成”转化为“瞬态权重扰动（LoRA）”的新型多智能体协作框架，显著降低了计算成本与推理延迟，同时提升了模型协作效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体系统依赖自然语言交互，存在序列化开销大、生成 token 成本高、KV cache 内存占用多及预填充负担重等问题。

Method: 提出 TFlow（Thought Flow）框架：发送者智能体保持冻结，利用参数生成器将其内部激活映射为目标模型的瞬态低秩（LoRA）权重扰动，在接收者生成过程中实现实时融合，无需扩展文本上下文。

Result: 相较于传统的文本交互基线，TFlow 减少了高达 83.27% 的处理 token 总量，推理时间缩短至原来的 1/4.6，同时在五项基准测试中的四项保持了极具竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 瞬态低秩权重扰动（TFlow）在大规模语言模型多智能体协作中，证明了其作为高效通信媒介的可行性，能够替代传统的自然语言文本交换，实现性能与效率的平衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems usually collaborate by exchanging natural-language messages. This interface is simple and interpretable, but it forces each sender's intermediate computation to be serialized into tokens and then reprocessed by the receiver, thereby increasing the generated-token cost, prefill overhead, and KV-cache memory. We study an alternative communication interface: instead of appending a sender's message to the receiver's context, compile the sender's hidden states into a transient, receiver-specific weight perturbation. We introduce TFlow (Thought Flow), a weight-space communication framework for a known and fixed receiver architecture. For each query, frozen role-prompted sender agents process the input, and a learned parameter generator maps their internal activations into low-rank LoRA perturbations targeting the receiver's modules. These perturbations are fused and applied only during the receiver's generation, enabling instance-level adaptation without permanently changing the model or enlarging the receiver's text context. With three Qwen3-4B agents, TFlow improves over a standalone receiver by up to 8.5 accuracy points across five benchmarks while reducing processed tokens by up to 32.69%. Compared with a text-based three-agent baseline, it reduces total processed tokens by up to 83.27% and the wall-clock inference time by up to 4.6$\times$, while maintaining competitive accuracy on four of five benchmarks. These results suggest that transient low-rank weight perturbations can serve as an executable communication medium for efficient multi-agent LLM collaboration.

</details>


### [58] [WARDEN: Endangered Indigenous Language Transcription and Translation with 6 Hours of Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13846)
*Ziheng Zhang,Yunzhong Hou,Naijing Liu,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对濒危语言 Wardaman 训练数据极度匮乏的问题，本文提出了 WARDEN 系统，通过“音频转音素、音素转翻译”的两阶段架构，结合跨语言初始化和专家字典增强，在 6 小时标注数据下实现了优于大模型的翻译效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wardaman 语作为一种濒危的澳大利亚原住民语言，仅有 6 小时的标注训练数据，传统的超大规模数据驱动的统一模型训练方法在此情境下不可行。

Method: 1. 设计两阶段模型：先将音频转换为音素转录，再将其翻译为英语；2. 跨语言初始化：使用音素相似的巽他语（Sundanese）来初始化转录模型的词元以加速微调；3. 结合外部知识：利用专家编译的字典辅助大语言模型（LLM）进行翻译推理。

Result: WARDEN 在仅有 6 小时标注数据的情况下，性能优于目前主流的开源和闭源模型，证明了该方法在极低资源场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明在极低资源环境下，将多任务（转录与翻译）拆分为两个阶段的架构比统一模型架构更有效；WARDEN 为濒危语言处理建立了一个强基准。

Abstract: This paper introduces WARDEN, an early language model system capable of transcribing and translating Wardaman, an endangered Australian indigenous language into English. The significant challenge we face is the lack of large-scale training data: in fact, we only have 6 hours of annotated audio. Therefore, while it is common practice to train a single model for transcription and translation using large datasets (like English to French), this practice is no longer viable in the Wardaman to English context. To tackle the low-resource challenge, we design WARDEN to have separate transcription and translation models: WARDEN first turns a Wardaman audio input into phonemic transcription, and then the transcription into English translation. Further, we propose two useful techniques to enhance performance. For transcription, we initialize the Wardaman token from Sundanese, a language that shares similar phonemes with Wardaman, to accelerate fine-tuning of the transcription model. For translation, we compile a Wardaman-English dictionary from expert annotations, and provide this domain-specific knowledge to a large language model (LLM) to reason and decide the final output. We empirically demonstrate that this two-stage design works better than data-hungry unified approaches in extremely low data settings. Using a mere 6 hours of annotated data, WARDEN outperforms larger open-source and proprietary models and establishes a strong baseline. Data and code are available.

</details>

### [59] [Thin Trees for Near Minimum Cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12669)
*Nathan Klein,Neil Olver,Zi Song Yeoh*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了强薄树猜想在 $1/40$-近最小割的约束下成立，并提供了一种在多项式时间内构造此类树的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强薄树猜想指出 $k$-边连通图包含 $O(1/k)$-薄生成树，但该猜想至今未被完全证明，现有的最好成果仅达到 $O(\text{polyloglog}(n)/k)$ 的程度。研究者希望通过放宽对切割范围的限制（仅关注 $η$-近最小割），在该特定条件下证实猜想。

Method: 利用近最小割的结构性质，特别是 Benczúr 和 Goemans 的多边形表示（polygon representation），将原问题规约（reduce）为在层级族（laminar family）上寻找 $O(1/k)$-薄生成树的已解决问题。该方法可在多项式时间内完成构造。

Result: 证明了对于任意 $k$-边连通图，存在一个多项式时间算法，可以构造出一棵在所有 $η$-近最小割（即边数小于 $(1+1/40)k$ 的割）上均为 $O(1/k)$-薄的生成树。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，对于图中的所有 $η$-近最小割（其中 $η= 1/40$），强薄树（strong thin tree）猜想成立。这标志着在解决这一著名猜想的道路上迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: The strong thin tree conjecture states that every $k$-edge-connected graph $G$ contains an $O(1/k)$-thin spanning tree, meaning a spanning tree which contains at most an $O(1/k)$ fraction of the edges across each cut in $G$. This conjecture is still open despite significant effort; the best current result by Anari and Oveis Gharan shows the existence of an $O(\text{polyloglog}(n)/k)$-thin tree.
  In this work, we demonstrate that the conjecture is true if one only requires thinness for the set of $η$-near minimum cuts of the graph for $η= 1/40$, in other words, for the set of cuts with fewer than $(1+1/40)k$ edges. Our approach constructs such a tree in polynomial time. To show this, we utilize the structure of near minimum cuts, and in particular the polygon representation of Benczúr and Goemans, to reduce to the previously solved problem of finding a spanning tree that is $O(1/k)$-thin for all sets in a laminar family.

</details>


### [60] [Time and Supply Fairness in Electricity Distribution using $k$-times bin packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12812)
*Dinesh Kumar Baghel,Alex Ravsky,Erel Segal-Halevi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了k-times bin-packing (kBP) 模型以解决公平电力分配问题，推广了装箱算法，并在连接时间和瓦特分配两个维度上取得了比现有启发式算法更佳的公平性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电力分配中的公平性问题（如平均分配连接时间及瓦特分配）缺乏高效且具理论支撑的求解方法，传统的装箱问题无法直接解决这类周期性与公平性需求。

Method: 1. 引入kBP问题，推广现有的First-Fit和First-Fit Decreasing启发式算法。 2. 从理论上证明电力分配问题可通过特定k值的kBP求解。 3. 针对无法用kBP解决的瓦特分配变体，开发并验证四种启发式算法。

Result: 1. 推广的First-Fit和First-Fit Decreasing算法在电力连接时间的公平分配上优于现有启发式方案。 2. 证明了瓦特公平分配存在理论上的不可能结果（$k$无法仅由用户数决定），并提供了有效的启发式求解方案。

Conclusion: 定义了k-times bin-packing (kBP) 问题，并证明了其在公平电力分配问题中的应用价值。针对不能转化为kBP的瓦特公平分配变体，提出了四种启发式算法并建立了新的公平性评价基准。

Abstract: Given items of different sizes and a fixed bin capacity, the bin-packing problem is to pack these items into the minimum number of bins such that the sum of the item sizes in each bin does not exceed the capacity. We define a new variant, k-times bin-packing (kBP), in which the goal is to pack the items so that each item appears exactly k times in k different bins. We generalize existing approximation algorithms for bin-packing to solve kBP and analyze their performance ratios. The fair electricity division problem motivates the study of kBP. The goal is to allocate the available supply among households using some fairness criteria, such as the egalitarian principle. We prove that every electricity division problem can be solved by k-times bin-packing for some finite k, which depends only on the number of households. We implement generalizations of the First-Fit and First-Fit Decreasing bin-packing algorithms to solve kBP and apply them to real electricity demand data. We show that our generalizations outperform existing heuristic solutions to the same problem in terms of the egalitarian allocation of connection time. We study another variant of the egalitarian allocation problem, in which the goal is to maximize the minimum number of watts allocated to a household. For this variant, we prove an impossibility result: there does not exist such a k that depends only on the number of agents. This impossibility result motivates us to develop four different heuristic algorithms to solve the egalitarian allocation of watts problem. We evaluate the heuristics by summing the minimum watts allocated to any household in each hour, yielding a fairness metric that reflects the lowest watt allocation across all hours. A higher total minimum of watts indicates a more equitable distribution. Thus, we establish new benchmarks for fair allocation of watts.

</details>


### [61] [The Power of Graph Doubling: Computing Ultrabubbles in a Bidirected Graph by Reducing to Weak Superbubbles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13074)
*Sebastian Schmidt,Juha Harviainen,Corentin Moumard,Aleksandr Politov,Francisco Sena,Alexandru I. Tomescu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过图翻倍技术将双向图转化为有向图，成功实现了任意双向图中超气泡（ultrabubbles）的高效线性时间计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向图中的结构（如超气泡）处理较为复杂，现有的算法（如Harviainen等人）仅给出了部分结果。作者旨在通过图翻倍技术填补双向图与有向图之间的理论鸿沟，从而简化双向图算法的设计。

Method: 利用图翻倍技术（graph doubling）将双向图转化为有向图，并利用现有的超级气泡枚举算法，将超气泡（ultrabubbles）的计算归约为双倍图上的弱超级气泡计算。

Result: 建立了超气泡与双倍图中弱超级气泡的直接对应关系，并提出了一种新的线性时间算法，验证了图翻倍技术在解决双向图复杂结构问题上的有效性。

Conclusion: 图翻倍（graph doubling）是解决双向图（bidirected graphs）问题的一种强大且通用的技术。通过证明超气泡（ultrabubbles）与双倍图中的弱超级气泡（weak superbubbles）之间的直接联系，实现了首个针对任意双向图计算超气泡的线性时间归约算法。

Abstract: Bidirected graphs are a common generalisation of directed graphs where arcs can also be incoming to both their incident nodes, or outgoing from both their incident nodes. Such arcs allow a walk to change direction. Some algorithms can easily be adapted from directed graphs to bidirected graphs, such as shortest path algorithms. These adaptions are already used in practice, and implicitly use the graph doubling technique to apply an algorithm for directed graphs to bidirected graphs.
  In other cases, the applicability of graph doubling is not that obvious. For example, superbubbles and their generalisation to bidirected graphs ultrabubbles. Ultrabubbles are a common structure in bidirected biological graphs which carries biological meaning, but also functions as a nested clustering method, since an ultrabubble is separated by only two nodes from the rest of the graph.
  There is an existing method that enumerates a structure similar to ultrabubbles by enumerating (weak) superbubbles in the doubled graph. However, the literature does not make any direct connection between superbubbles and ultrabubbles except that a superbubble is an ultrabubble in a directed graph. Only a partial result connecting superbubbles and ultrabubbles exists by Harviainen et al. (2026).
  Graph doubling on the other hand maintains connectivity, and allows to draw a direct connection between ultrabubbles and weak superbubbles. This results in the first linear-time reduction-based algorithm for computing ultrabubbles on any bidirected graph. Together with the fact that graph doubling is already used implicitly in simple cases, our result motivates that graph doubling is a powerful yet simple technique to apply algorithms for directed graphs to bidirected graphs.

</details>


### [62] [Tighter relaxations for MAP-MRF optimization via Singleton Arc Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13392)
*Asaf Lev-Ran,Pavel Arkhipov,Vladimir Kolmogorov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出利用 Singleton Arc Consistency 算法来识别 MAP-MRF 线性规划松弛中的簇，实验证明该方法在求解效率和效果上优于传统的受挫环搜索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAP-MRF 推断属于 NP-hard 问题，解决其 LP 松弛后的迭代紧缩（通过添加簇）是常用且有效的实操方法，现有方法（如 Sontag 等人的受挫环搜索）仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出了一种基于 Singleton Arc Consistency（SAC）算法的新型簇识别技术，用于迭代地紧缩 MAP-MRF 的线性规划（LP）松弛。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在性能和效率上优于现有基于受挫环搜索的紧缩技术。

Conclusion: 通过在约束满足问题（CSP）实例中应用 Singleton Arc Consistency 算法，能够比基于寻找受挫环（frustrated cycles）的启发式方法更有效地识别用于紧缩 MAP-MRF 线性规划松弛的簇。

Abstract: We consider the MAP-MRF inference task, that is, minimizing a function of discrete variables represented as a sum of unary and pairwise terms. A prominent approach for tackling this NP-hard problem in practice is to solve its natural LP relaxation and then iteratively tighten the relaxation by adding clusters. Based on some theoretical observations, we propose a new technique for identifying such clusters. It works by running the Singleton Arc Consistency algorithm in a certain CSP instance. Experimental results indicate that the new tightening technique outperforms the previous approach by [Sontag et al. UAI 2012] that searches for frustrated cycles. Our code will be made available at https://github.com/vnk-ist/MAP-MRF/.

</details>


### [63] [Low-Cost Arborescence Under Edge Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13800)
*Dipan Dey,Telikepalli Kavitha*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了边故障下的图与拟阵优化问题。提出了一个大小为 $O(n^{3/2})$ 的稀疏子图，用于在单边故障时快速计算最小代价树的2-近似解；同时证明了对于拟阵，构造大小为 $k \cdot \text{rank}(E)$ 的 $k$-故障容忍保持器是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在边故障的情况下，如何以较低的计算成本快速维护最小代价树（Arborescence）和拟阵基，避免在每次故障时重新在全图计算。

Method: 1. 对于单边故障下的最小代价树问题：构造了一个稀疏子图 $H$ ($|H|=O(n^{3/2})$)，通过在 $H-f$ 中计算最小代价树来近似原图中的解。
2. 对于拟阵问题：通过算法构造了一个大小为 $k \cdot \text{rank}(E)$ 的子集 $S$ (故障容忍保持器)，确保在任意 $k$ 条边失效时，保留的拟阵仍包含原拟阵的最小代价基。

Result: 1. 给出了最小代价树故障容忍问题的子图构造算法，其近似比为2，且处理时间复杂度优化至 $O(n^{3/2})$。
2. 在拟阵环境下，证明了构造大小为 $k \cdot \text{rank}(E)$ 的故障容忍保持器是理论上的紧界。

Conclusion: 对于单边故障问题，本文给出了一个大小为 $O(n^{3/2})$ 的子图，能够以 $O(n^{3/2})$ 的时间复杂度得到原图最小生成树的2-近似解；对于拟阵问题，证明了 $k$-故障容忍保持器的大小紧界为 $k \cdot \text{rank}(E)$。

Abstract: Our input is a directed graph $G = (V,E)$ on $n$ vertices and $m$ edges with a designated root vertex $r$ and a function $cost: E \rightarrow \mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}$. The problem is to maintain a min-cost arborescence in $G$ in the presence of edge faults (a single fault at a time). Edge faults are transient and once the faulty edge is repaired, the original min-cost arborescence $\mathcal{T}$ is restored. Whenever an edge fault happens, we need to update $\mathcal{T}$ to a min-cost arborescence in $G-f$, where $f$ is the faulty edge. Since computing a min-cost arborescence in $G - f$ takes $O(m + n\log n)$ time, we seek to construct a sparse subgraph $H$ in a preprocessing step such that in the event of any edge $f$ failing, it suffices to compute a min-cost arborescence in $H - f$ in order to find a low-cost arborescence in $G - f$.
  In the unweighted setting, this is the fault-tolerant subgraph problem for single-source {\em reachability}. Baswana, Choudhary, and Roditty (SICOMP, 2018) showed a $k$-fault tolerant reachability subgraph of size $O(2^kn)$, where $k$ is the number of edge faults. We show a simple polynomial-time algorithm to construct a subgraph $H$ of size $O(n^{3/2})$ such that, for any $f \in E$, a min-cost arborescence in $H-f$ is a 2-approximation of a min-cost arborescence in $G-f$. Thus whenever an edge fault happens, we can find a 2-approximate min-cost arborescence in $G-f$ in $O(n^{3/2})$ time.
  Our second problem is in the matroid setting. The input is a matroid $M = (E, {\cal I})$ with a function $cost: E \rightarrow \mathbb{R}$. The problem is to compute a sparse $S \subseteq E$ (called a $k$-fault tolerant preserver) such that for any $F \subseteq E$ with $|F| \le k$, the matroid $M|(S\setminus F)$ contains a min-cost basis of $M|(E\setminus F)$. We show a tight bound of $k.rank(E)$ on the size of a $k$-fault tolerant preserver.

</details>


### [64] [Min-Max Optimization Requires Exponentially Many Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13806)
*Martino Bernasconi,Matteo Castiglioni,Andrea Celli,Alexandros Hollender*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了在非凸-非凹极小极大优化中，寻找近似稳定点的算法不仅在理论上困难，且在查询复杂度上必然遭受“维度灾难”或精度指数级增长的制约，即复杂度与 $1/\varepsilon$ 或 $d$ 成指数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸-非凹极小极大优化问题在机器学习中（如 GAN 训练）广泛存在，但其求解的计算复杂性尚不明确，因此作者致力于研究此类问题在查询复杂度上的理论下限。

Method: 通过复杂性理论与优化问题的归约，分析在目标函数 $f$ 及其梯度 $\nabla f$ 的预言机（Oracle）模型下，算法找到 $\varepsilon$-近似稳定点的下界。

Result: 建立了该优化问题的查询复杂度下界，证明了任何寻找 $\varepsilon$-近似稳定点的算法都需要满足对 $1/\varepsilon$ 或维数 $d$ 的指数级查询次数。

Conclusion: 即使对于简单的非凸-非凹函数，寻找其近似稳定点在最坏情况下的查询复杂度必然是维数 $d$ 或精度 $1/\varepsilon$ 的指数级。

Abstract: We study the query complexity of min-max optimization of a nonconvex-nonconcave function $f$ over $[0,1]^d \times [0,1]^d$. We show that, given oracle access to $f$ and to its gradient $\nabla f$, any algorithm that finds an $\varepsilon$-approximate stationary point must make a number of queries that is exponential in $1/\varepsilon$ or $d$.

</details>

### [65] [Learning When to Act: Communication-Efficient Reinforcement Learning via Run-Time Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12561)
*Adam Haroon,Erick J. Rodríguez-Seda,Cody Fleming,Tristan Schuler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合点态Lyapunov安全屏蔽的强化学习方法，通过联合学习控制与触发时机，有效提升了复杂控制系统在通信受限场景下的安全性和通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全强化学习主要关注“做什么”（控制输入），忽略了通信资源受限下的“何时做”（采样/动作触发决策）。针对控制系统在保证稳定性的前提下如何实现通信/计算高效（稀疏采样）的问题进行研究。

Method: 提出了一种支持点态Lyapunov安全屏蔽（Pointwise Lyapunov safety shield）的框架，通过运行时保证层（RTA）在预测违反约束时介入，利用CARE（Control-Aware Reinforcement Learning）衍生的Lyapunov函数进行奖励设计，并引入了偏好条件扩展以实现平衡稳定性与通信代价的帕累托前沿探索。

Result: 在倒立摆、小车倒立摆及四旋翼无人机等实验中，该方法显著提升了平均采样间隔（MSI），且优于传统的触发基准。该方法具有算法无关性，能够从单个模型高效恢复性能权衡曲线，并在高维系统与模型扰动下表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功提出了一种能够同时学习控制输入与通信高效触发策略的强化学习框架。该方法通过引入运行时保证安全层（RTA shield），在实现系统稀疏采样控制的同时，保证了点态的Lyapunov稳定性，优于传统的期望约束方法，并具有良好的泛化能力与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) typically asks $\textit{what}$ an agent should do. We ask $\textit{when}$ it needs to act, and show that a single policy can jointly learn control inputs and communication-efficient timing decisions under a pointwise Lyapunov safety shield. We focus on stabilization around a known equilibrium, where CARE-based LQR backups, Lyapunov certificates, and classical Lyapunov-STC are well defined, enabling clean comparison against analytical baselines. A run-time assurance (RTA) layer overrides the policy via a one-step-ahead Lyapunov prediction and a precomputed LQR backup, providing a strictly stronger guarantee than constrained MDP methods that enforce safety only in expectation. On an inverted pendulum, cart--pole, and planar quadrotor, the learned policy achieves $1.91\times$, $1.45\times$, and $3.51\times$ higher mean inter-sample interval (MSI) than a Lyapunov-triggered baseline; a fixed LQR controller at the same average rate is unstable on all three plants, showing that adaptive timing, not a lower average rate, makes sparsity safe. A CARE-derived Lyapunov reward transfers across environments without redesign, with a single weight $w_c$ controlling the stability--communication tradeoff; ablations confirm the RTA shield is essential, with its removal reducing MSI by $1.27$--$1.84\times$ and degrading state norms. A preference-conditioned extension recovers the full tradeoff frontier from one model at $\tfrac{2}{11}$ of training compute, and SAC experiments show the results are algorithm-agnostic across discrete and continuous domains. A 12-state 3D quadrotor case study extends the framework to higher-dimensional systems where classical STC is intractable, and robustness to $\pm30\%$ mass variation and disturbances shows graceful degradation, with the RTA absorbing what the learned policy cannot.

</details>


### [66] [Towards Robust Federated Multimodal Graph Learning under Modality Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12584)
*Sirui Zhang,Haonan Wang,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Shumeng Li,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦多模态图学习中的数据孤岛和模态缺失难题，论文提出了FedMPO框架，通过拓扑感知生成、专家路由过滤和可靠性加权聚合，实现了在隐私保护下的高性能图学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的集中式多模态图学习难以适应联邦场景下的数据隔离，而现有的联邦学习方法又多针对非图数据，未充分解决多模态图学习中的模态缺失、拓扑隔离以及数据更新不可靠等问题。

Method: 提出了FedMPO框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于拓扑感知的跨模态生成，利用图上下文恢复缺失特征；(2) 缺失感知的专家路由，用于局部过滤噪声信号；(3) 可靠性感知的联邦聚合机制，根据客户端更新的可靠性动态调整权重。

Result: 在3个任务、6个数据集上的实验表明，FedMPO在模态高缺失和非独立同分布（non-IID）场景下，性能分别提升了4.10%和5.65%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为FedMPO的联邦多模态图学习框架，能够有效解决异构图数据中模态缺失、拓扑隔离及更新不可靠的问题，在各种严苛测试条件下均优于现有基准。

Abstract: Recently, multimodal graph learning (MGL) has garnered significant attention for integrating diverse modality information and structured context to support various network applications. However, real-world graphs are often isolated due to data-sharing limitations across multiple parties, and their modalities are frequently incomplete. This highlights an urgent need to develop a robust federated approach. However, we find that existing methods remain insufficient. On the one hand, centralized MGL methods that handle missing modalities overlook the knowledge sharing and generalization in federated scenarios. On the other hand, while federated MGL methods have become increasingly mature, they primarily target non-graph data. Based on these technologies, we identify a two-stage pipeline wherein client-side completion reconstructs missing modalities, and server-side aggregation integrates the client-updated parameters of both the modality generator and the backbone models. Although this serves as a general solution, we identify two primary challenges in achieving greater robustness: (1) Topology-Isolated Local Completion: Client-side modality generation struggles to effectively leverage global semantics. (2) Reliability-Imbalanced Global Aggregation: Server-side multi-party collaboration is hindered by client updates with varying modality availability and recovery reliability. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{FedMPO}, which utilizes topology-aware cross-modal generation to recover missing features using comprehensive graph context, missing-aware expert routing to locally filter out noisy recovered signals, and reliability-aware aggregation to appropriately down-weight unreliable updates. Extensive experiments on 3 tasks across 6 datasets demonstrate that FedMPO outperforms baselines, achieving performance gains of up to 4.10% and 5.65% in high-missing and non-IID settings.

</details>


### [67] [OceanCBM: A Concept Bottleneck Model for Mechanistic Interpretability in Ocean Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12639)
*Sanah Suri,Kieran Ringel,Maike Sonnewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 OceanCBM，这是一个融合地球物理概念的瓶颈模型，旨在解决海洋现象预测中的可解释性难题，通过在时空预测中引入物理约束，实现了高精度预测与物理机制洞察的双重目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习海洋预测模型虽然性能优越，但往往缺乏可解释性（黑盒），难以揭示极端海洋现象背后的物理驱动机制，且缺乏对物理真值的保证。需要一种既能保持高性能预测又能提供物理机制洞察的方法。

Method: 引入了一种针对时空预测的“概念瓶颈模型”（OceanCBM）。该模型采用混合监督学习策略：将预测目标（如混合层热含量）与来自地球物理流体动力学的专家预设概念相结合，并增加了一个额外的“自由概念”（free concept）来捕获未被预设概念涵盖的物理残差。这种架构引入了软物理约束，防止模型过度约束。

Result: 实验表明，OceanCBM 在预测混合层热含量方面表现出色，达到了与传统模型相当的精度。同时，该模型通过混合监督机制生成了高度一致的机制性表征，克服了传统方法（仅预测或仅处方）所导致的潜在结构变异性问题。

Conclusion: OceanCBM 成功在保持高性能预测的同时，实现了可解释且物理一致的表征。该模型量化了可解释性与预测性能之间的权衡，证明了通过混合监督学习概念瓶颈模型（CBM）是研究复杂海洋动力学的有效途径。

Abstract: Extreme ocean phenomena are challenging not only to predict but to diagnose, as accurate forecasts alone do not reveal the underlying physical drivers. While recent machine learning approaches achieve strong predictive skill, they remain largely opaque and provide limited guarantees of fidelity to ground-truth physics. We introduce OceanCBM, the first concept bottleneck model (CBM) for spatiotemporal prediction and mechanistic interrogation of ocean dynamics. OceanCBM uses mixed supervision to predict mixed layer heat content, a key precursor of marine heatwaves, while routing information through an intermediate layer of prescribed concepts derived from geophysical fluid dynamics and a 'free' concept. This design imposes soft physical structure without over-constraining the model, and the free concept both regularizes concept predictions and captures residual physical processes. Across ensemble initializations, we show that mixed supervision yields consistent mechanistic representations, whereas prediction-only and prescription-only baselines learn highly variable latent structures despite similar predictive performance. OceanCBM achieves interpretable, physically grounded representations without sacrificing skill, explicitly characterizing the interpretability-performance trade-off.

</details>


### [68] [Learning to Decide with AI Assistance under Human-Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12646)
*Nina Corvelo Benz,Eleni Straitouri,Manuel Gomez-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI置信度与人类置信度对齐对辅助决策学习效率的影响，证明了对齐可以有效降低决策者在反复交互中学习最优策略的复杂度和后悔值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI提供置信度有助于决策，但决策者常难以判断何时信任预测；目前关于AI置信度与人类置信度对齐程度如何影响“长期学习决策最优策略”这一过程的研究尚不充分。

Method: 将AI辅助的人机协作决策建模为带全反馈的双臂在线上下文学习问题（Two-armed online contextual learning problem）；利用置信度集合的基数推导后悔值（Regret）下界；引入Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz不等式的推广来优化后悔线上界。

Result: 1. 建立了后悔值的理论下界 Ω(sqrt(|H| * |B| * T))；2. 在完全对齐条件下，证明了最优后悔线上界为 O(sqrt(|H| * T log T))；3. 在特定条件下（当 |H| = O(log T) 且 |B| 可数时），进一步优化至 O(sqrt(T log T))；4. 实验验证了理论在真实人类决策场景中的稳健性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了AI模型与决策者之间的置信度对齐（Alignment）能够显著降低通过反复交互学习最优决策的难度，即对齐度越高，决策者的学习成本（后悔值）越低。该理论在非完全对齐的实际场景中依然稳健。

Abstract: It is widely agreed that when AI models assist decision-makers in high-stakes domains by predicting an outcome of interest, they should communicate the confidence of their predictions. However, empirical evidence suggests that decision-makers often struggle to determine when to trust a prediction based solely on this communicated confidence. In this context, recent theoretical and empirical work suggests a positive correlation between the utility of AI-assisted decision-making and the degree of alignment between the AI confidence and the decision-makers' confidence in their own predictions. Crucially, these findings do not yet elucidate the extent to which this alignment influences the complexity of learning to make optimal decisions through repeated interactions. In this paper, we address this question in the canonical case of binary predictions and binary decisions. We first show that this problem is equivalent to a two-armed online contextual learning problem with full feedback, and establish a lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{|H| \cdot |B| \cdot T} )$ on the expected regret any learner can attain, where $H$ and $B$ denote the sets of human and AI confidence values. We then demonstrate that, under perfect alignment between AI and human confidence, a learner can attain an expected regret of $O(\sqrt{|H| \cdot T\log T})$ and, when $\sqrt{|H|} = O(\log T)$ and $B$ is countable, a non-trivial generalization of the Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz inequality improves the regret bound to $O(\sqrt{T\log T})$. Taken together, these results reveal that alignment can reduce the complexity of learning to make decisions with AI assistance. Experiments on real data from two different human-subject studies where participants solve simple decision-making tasks assisted by AI models show that our theoretical results are robust to violations of perfect alignment.

</details>


### [69] [Runtime Monitoring of Perception-Based Autonomous Systems via Embedding Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12651)
*Parv Kapoor,Abigail Hammer,Ashish Kapoor,Karen Leung,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出嵌入时序逻辑（ETL），通过在学习到的嵌入空间而非传统逻辑空间中进行监控，实现了对复杂感知驱动行为的稳健验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的运行时监控依赖于将连续传感器数据映射为低维逻辑命题，但在感知驱动的场景中，这种显式映射既昂贵又脆弱且难以对齐复杂的语义目标。

Method: 提出嵌入时序逻辑（ETL），通过计算特征嵌入空间中观测值与目标参考值之间的距离来定义谓词；引入了一套基于一致性校准（Conformal Calibration）的监控程序，以确保谓词评估的可靠性和安全导向性。

Result: 在多个机器人操作任务中表现优异，与真实语义高度一致，能够精确监控复杂的时序感知行为。

Conclusion: ETL 成功实现了在学习到的嵌入空间中直接进行运行时监控，克服了传统方法对低维逻辑命题的依赖，为复杂感知任务下的自主系统提供了一种可靠、灵活的语义验证框架。

Abstract: Runtime monitoring of autonomous systems traditionally relies on mapping continuous sensor observations to discrete logical propositions defined over low-dimensional state variables. This abstraction breaks down in perception-driven settings, where such mappings require additional learned modules that are often computationally expensive, brittle, and semantically misaligned. In this work, we propose Embedding Temporal Logic (ETL), a temporal logic that performs monitoring directly in learned embedding spaces. ETL defines predicates through distances between observed embeddings and target embeddings derived from reference observations. This formulation allows specifications to capture high-level perceptual concepts, such as similarity to visual goals or avoidance of semantic regions, that are difficult or impossible to express using traditional predicates. By composing these predicates with temporal operators, ETL naturally expresses temporally extended and sequential perceptual behaviors. We introduce ETL monitors for evaluating specifications over bounded embedding traces, along with a conformal calibration procedure that provides reliable and safety-oriented predicate evaluation. We evaluate our approach across multiple manipulation environments to show that ETL achieves strong empirical agreement with ground-truth semantics, including accurate monitoring of temporally composed behaviors.

</details>


### [70] [Provable Quantization with Randomized Hadamard Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13810)
*Ying Feng,Piotr Indyk,Michael Kapralov,Dmitry Krachun,Boris Prokhorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes a computationally efficient (O(d log d)) dithered Hadamard quantization scheme that matches the MSE performance of theoretically optimal (but expensive) dense random rotation-based quantization.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dense random rotations for vector quantization are computationally expensive (Θ(d^2)). Although the randomized Hadamard transform reduces complexity to O(d log d), its discrete nature makes theoretical analysis difficult and often results in weaker guarantees compared to dense rotations. The authors aim to bridge this gap.

Method: The authors propose a dithered quantization method that applies a randomized Hadamard transform (HD) to the input vector, followed by the subtraction of a random scalar offset (dithering) before scalar quantization.

Result: The authors prove the approach is unbiased and achieves an MSE of (π√3/2 + o(1)) · 4^-b at b bits per coordinate. This result holds uniformly over all unit vectors and dimensions as the number of quantization levels increases, matching the theoretical guarantees of truly random rotation matrices.

Conclusion: Dithered quantization with a single randomized Hadamard transform achieves performance asymptotically equivalent to dense random rotations, while maintaining the computational efficiency (O(d log d)) of the Hadamard transform. Specifically, it provides unbiased quantization with tight MSE bounds.

Abstract: Vector quantization via random projection followed by scalar quantization is a fundamental primitive in machine learning, with applications ranging from similarity search to federated learning and KV cache compression. While dense random rotations yield clean theoretical guarantees, they require $Θ(d^2)$ time. The randomized Hadamard transform $HD$ reduces this cost to $O(d \log d)$, but its discrete structure complicates analysis and leads to weaker or purely empirical compression guarantees.
  In this work, we study a variant of this approach: dithered quantization with a single randomized Hadamard transform. Specifically, the quantizer applies $HD$ to the input vector and subtracts a random scalar offset before quantizing, injecting additional randomness at negligible cost. We prove that this approach is unbiased and provides mean squared error bounds that asymptotically match those achievable with truly random rotation matrices. In particular, we prove that a dithered version of TurboQuant achieves mean squared error $\bigl(π\sqrt{3}/2 + o(1)\bigr) \cdot 4^{-b}$ at $b$ bits per coordinate, where the $o(1)$ term vanishes uniformly over all unit vectors and all dimensions as the number of quantization levels grows.

</details>


### [71] [Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation via Peer Successes and Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12652)
*Weichen Yu,Xiaomin Li,Yizhou Zhao,Xiaoze Liu,Ruowang Zhang,Haixin Wang,Yinyi Luo,Chen Henry Wu,Gaurav Mittal,Matt Fredrikson,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOPD是一种新型策略内蒸馏框架，通过利用学生模型在同一任务中的多条成功和失败轨迹构建上下文，提供比独立蒸馏更丰富、更具指导性的监督信号，有效提升了复杂推理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型多采用稀疏验证器奖励（sparse verifier rewards）进行后训练，缺乏对推理过程细节的指导。虽然传统的策略内蒸馏（OPD）能提供token级监督，但它通常将每个rollout独立看待，未能利用同一Prompt下其他尝试所蕴含的潜在反馈信息。

Method: 提出了 Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) 框架。该方法利用学生模型针对同一提示词生成的本地轨迹组（local rollout group）来构建教师信号，具体包括：1. 正向同伴模仿（Positive peer imitation）；2. 对比式成功-失败条件化（Contrastive success-failure conditioning）。通过这种方式，模型能够同时从成功推理路径学习有效模式，并从失败路径提取负面行为特征。

Result: 在竞技编程、数学推理、科学问答及工具使用等基准测试中，MOPD的表现优于标准的单轨迹蒸馏基准。分析显示，引入成功-失败混合背景能使教师评分与最终验证奖励更对齐，证明了该方法提供了更忠实、更具实例适应性的监督信号。

Conclusion: 有效的策略内蒸馏（On-Policy Distillation）不应孤立处理轨迹，而应充分利用学生模型在同一提示词下的多轮次试错行为（Multi-rollout trial-and-error）。通过整合成功与失败的peer轨迹作为上下文，可以生成更具信息量的教师信号，从而显著提升模型的推理和决策能力。

Abstract: Large language models are often post-trained with sparse verifier rewards, which indicate whether a sampled trajectory succeeds but provide limited guidance about where reasoning succeeds or fails. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers denser token-level supervision by training on student-generated trajectories, yet existing methods typically distill each rollout independently and ignore the other attempts sampled for the same prompt. We introduce Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation (MOPD), a peer-conditioned distillation framework that uses the student's local rollout group to construct more informative teacher signals. MOPD conditions the teacher on both successful and failed peer rollouts: successes provide positive evidence for valid reasoning patterns, while failures provide structured negative evidence about plausible mistakes to avoid. We study two peer-context constructions: positive peer imitation and contrastive success-failure conditioning. Experiments on competitive programming, mathematical reasoning, scientific question answering, and tool-use benchmarks show that MOPD consistently improves over standard on-policy baselines. Further teacher-signal analysis shows that mixed success-failure contexts better align teacher scores with verifier rewards, indicating that the gains arise from more faithful, instance-adaptive supervision. These results indicate that effective on-policy distillation should exploit the student's multi-rollout trial-and-error behavior rather than treating rollouts as isolated samples.

</details>


### [72] [scShapeBench: Discovering geometry from high dimensional scRNAseq data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12662)
*Andrew J Steindl,João Felipe Rocha,Brian Tshilengi Di Bassinga,Zachary Warren,Matthew Scicluna,César Miguel Valdez Córdova,Shabarni Gupta,Leire Torices,Daniel Neumann,Timothy J. Mann,Ihuan Gunawan,Dhananjay Bhaskar,John G Lock,Christine L Chaffer,Guy Wolf,Smita Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对单细胞数据拓扑感知分析的需求，本文提出了首个形状检测基准 scShapeBench 和一种基于扩散几何的自动化工具 scReebTower，实现了对单细胞数据集结构（分簇、分支、轨迹等）的自动识别，优化了下游分析流程的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞分析中，不同的数据拓扑结构（如簇、轨迹、原型）需要不同的下游分析工具（如 Seurat、Monocle），但目前依赖人工视觉判断，缺乏自动化形状检测手段以支持智能分析。

Method: 构建了 scShapeBench（包含合成与专家标注的数据集）及对应的拓扑感知评价指标；提出了 scReebTower 方法，该方法利用扩散几何（diffusion geometry）提取 Reeb 图，从而自动识别数据集的拓扑结构（簇、单轨迹、多分支、原型）。

Result: 实验证明 scReebTower 在多种拓扑数据集上的分类准确性和鲁棒性均优于当前主流的 PAGA 和 Mapper 方法。

Conclusion: scReebTower 在形状检测任务中表现优于现有的 PAGA 和 Mapper 等基准方法，为单细胞分析自动选择下游流程提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: High-dimensional point cloud data arise across many scientific domains, especially single-cell biology. The shapes or topologies of these datasets determine the types of information that can be extracted. For example, clustered data supports cell-type identification, trajectory structures support transition analysis, and archetypal structures capture continua of cellular behaviors. Existing analysis pipelines often assume a specific shape.
