The standard Seurat pipeline combines UMAP visualization with Louvain clustering and therefore assumes clustered data, while tools such as Monocle and SPADE assume tree-like structures, and flow-based models such as MIOFlow and Conditional Flow Matching target trajectories. Choosing which pipeline to apply is therefore often left to bioinformaticians who visually inspect datasets before selecting an analysis strategy. With the rise of agentic AI scientists, automating shape detection is increasingly important for selecting downstream analysis pipelines. To address this problem, we introduce scShapeBench, a benchmark dataset for shape detection containing both synthetic and expert-annotated single-cell datasets. Synthetic datasets are sampled from ground-truth skeleton graphs with controlled variance. Real single-cell datasets are curated from diverse sources and annotated by experts into four categories: clusters, single trajectory, multi-branching, and archetypal. We additionally introduce scReebTower, a baseline method that uses diffusion geometry to extract Reeb graphs and connect visualization with pipeline selection. We provide topology-aware evaluation metrics and compare scReebTower against PAGA and Mapper on synthetic and real data. Our results indicate that scReebTower outperforms existing baselines. Overall, our contributions span benchmarks, evaluation metrics, and a baseline for automated shape detection in single-cell data.

</details>


### [73] [ODRPO: Ordinal Decompositions of Discrete Rewards for Robust Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12667)
*Nirmal Patel,Fei Wang,Inderjit Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 RLAIF 中自动评估器产生的噪声问题，本文提出了 ODRPO 方法，通过将离散权重分解为有序指标来消除噪声影响，在提升模型对齐效果的同时保持了极高的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 RLAIF 中，基于 LLM 的自动评分器存在严重的不确定性和噪声（受采样及提示词影响），这会干扰 GRPO 等标准优势估计器，进而导致训练表现下降，而传统的多数投票法计算开销过大。

Method: 提出有序分解鲁棒策略优化（ODRPO）框架，将 1-10 分的离散奖励分解为一系列序数二元指标，并独立计算和累积这些难度递增阈值的优势函数，从而降低评估噪声的影响并形成隐式学习课程。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B 和 Qwen3-4B 模型上，ODRPO 相比基线方法在 FACTS-grounding-v2 和 Alpaca-Evals 上分别实现了最高 14.8% 和 7.5% 的性能提升，且几乎没有额外的训练时计算开销。

Conclusion: ODRPO 通过将离散奖励重构为有序二元指标，有效缓解了 RLAIF 中自动评估器的随机性噪声问题，在保持低计算开销的同时显著提升了模型的对齐效果和训练稳定性。

Abstract: The alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) utilizes Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback (RLAIF) for non-verifiable domains such as long-form question answering and open-ended instruction following. These domains often rely on LLM based auto-raters to provide granular, multi-tier discrete rewards (e.g., 1-10 rubrics) that are inherently stochastic due to prompt sensitivity and sampling randomness. We empirically verify the stochasticity of auto-raters that can propagate and corrupt standard advantage estimators like GRPO and MaxRL, as a noisy reward samples can skew normalization statistics and degrade the global learning signal. Empirically, sampling more rewards and taking majority voting may reduce the noise and improve performance, but this approach is computationally expensive. To address this bottleneck, we introduce $\textbf{O}$rdinal $\textbf{D}$ecomposition for $\textbf{R}$obust $\textbf{P}$olicy $\textbf{O}$ptimization ($\textbf{ODRPO}$), a framework that structurally isolates evaluation noise by decomposing discrete rewards into a sequence of ordinal binary indicators. By independently computing and accumulating advantages across these progressively challenging success thresholds, ODRPO prevents outlier evaluations from corrupting the global update while establishing an implicit, variance-aware learning curriculum. Empirically, ODRPO achieves robust performance on Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-4B models, outperforming baselines with relative improvements of upto 14.8% on FACTS-grounding-v2 and 7.5% on Alpaca-Evals. Critically, these gains are achieved with negligible training-time overhead, as ODRPO requires no additional compute per step compared to standard estimators. Supported by theoretical analysis confirming its optimization stability, ODRPO provides a scalable and robust framework for aligning models within the noisy, discrete evaluation landscape of modern RLAIF.

</details>


### [74] [Rescaled Asynchronous SGD: Optimal Distributed Optimization under Data and System Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13434)
*Ammar Mahran,Artavazd Maranjyan,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种简单的 Rescaled ASGD 算法，通过按计算耗时重缩放学习率，解决了数据异构环境下分布式异步训练的偏差问题，且无需改变异步框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的异步随机梯度下降（ASGD）在异构数据环境下存在偏差。由于响应快的工作节点会进行更频繁的更新，导致最终模型倾向于以频率加权的局部目标函数，而非理想的全局目标函数。现有解决方案通常需要额外的同步阶段或内存开销，不够简洁。

Method: 提出 Rescaled ASGD 算法：不对现有的异步 SGD 架构进行改动，而是根据每个工作节点（worker）的计算耗时，按比例调整其学习率（stepsizes），使得在周期内每个 worker 对整体学习率的贡献均衡化。

Result: 理论证明在非凸设置及平滑性和有界异构性假设下，Rescaled ASGD 可收敛至全局目标函数的稳定点；复杂度分析显示其达到了最优下界，且稀疏性和数据异构性仅对低阶项产生影响；实验验证了其对正确目标函数的有效性及性能优势。

Conclusion: 通过根据计算耗时重新缩放步长，Rescaled ASGD 能够在保持异步计算优势的同时，消除由异构数据分布导致的偏差，收敛至正确的全局目标函数。

Abstract: Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD) is a standard way to exploit heterogeneous compute resources in distributed learning: instead of forcing fast workers to wait for slow ones, the server updates the model whenever a gradient arrives. Vanilla ASGD applies each arriving gradient with the same weight. When local data distributions are heterogeneous, this becomes problematic: faster workers contribute more updates, and we show theoretically that the method is biased toward a frequency-weighted average of the local objectives rather than the desired global objective. Existing remedies typically move away from the simple ASGD template by introducing gathering phases, buffering, or extra memory. We show that this is unnecessary. Keeping the standard ASGD mechanism, we recover the correct objective by rescaling worker-specific stepsizes in proportion to their computation times, so that each worker contributes the same aggregate learning rate over a cycle. In the non-convex setting, under smoothness and bounded heterogeneity assumptions, we prove that the resulting method, Rescaled ASGD, converges to stationary points of the correct global objective in the fixed-computation model. Its time complexity matches the known lower bound in the leading term, while the effects of staleness and data heterogeneity appear only in lower-order terms. Experiments confirm that the method converges to the correct objective and is competitive with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [75] [MinT: Managed Infrastructure for Training and Serving Millions of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13779)
*Mind Lab,:,Song Cao,Vic Cao,Andrew Chen,Kaijie Chen,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Kaixuan Fan,Hera Feng,Huan Feng,Arthur Fu,Jun Gao,Hongquan Gu,Aaron Guan,Nolan Ho,Mutian Hong,Hailee Hou,Peixuan Hua,Charles Huang,Miles Jiang,Nora Jiang,Yuyi Jiang,Qiuyu Jin,Fancy Kong,Andrew Lei,Kyrie Lei,Alexy Li,Lucian Li,Ray Li,Theo Li,Zhihui Li,Jiayi Lin,Kairus Liu,Kieran Liu,Logan Liu,Xiang Liu,Irvine Lu,Maeve Luo,Runze Lv,Pony Ma,Verity Niu,Anson Qiu,Vincent Wang,Rio Yang,Maxwell Yao,Carrie Ye,Regis Ye,Wenlin Ye,Josh Ying,Danney Zeng,Yuhan Zhan,Anya Zhang,Di Zhang,Ruijia Zhang,Sueky Zhang,Ya Zhang,Wei Zhao,Ada Zhou,Changhai Zhou,Yuhua Zhou,Xinyue Zhu,Murphy Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MindLab Toolkit (MinT) 是一套用于LoRA微调与在线推理的托管基础设施，通过保持基座模型常驻并将轻量级LoRA适配器解耦管理，实现了超大规模模型下的高效多策略迭代与百万级策略冷热调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模模型部署中，针对少数昂贵的基座模型频繁训练大量下游策略时，现有的全量检查点合并方式效率极低且资源浪费严重，亟需一种能够高效处理LoRA更新、分发、服务与回滚的管理机制。

Method: 设计了一套托管基础设施系统：1. 采用适配器热插拔机制，无需合并全量权重；2. 针对超大规模（Scale Up）架构（如MoE、MLA/DSA）进行优化；3. 通过轻量级适配器交换（Scale Down）降低通信成本；4. 利用张量并行和策略编排（Scale Out）实现百万级策略管理及冷热数据调度。

Result: 1. 高效性：适配器仅交换减少了最高18.3倍的响应时间；2. 扩展性：支持1T参数规模，可管理百万级策略目录；3. 性能优化：并发多策略GRPO训练显著缩短了挂钟时间（高达1.77倍），同时通过打包MoE LoRA张量将实时加载性能提升了8.5-8.7倍。

Conclusion: MinT通过将LoRA适配器管理与基座模型解耦，成功解决了在大规模、多策略场景下的高效训练与推理需求，为管理百万级LoRA策略目录提供了可扩展的架构，实现了在共享1T规模基座模型上的高效迭代。

Abstract: We present MindLab Toolkit (MinT), a managed infrastructure system for Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) post-training and online serving. MinT targets a setting where many trained policies are produced over a small number of expensive base-model deployments. Instead of materializing each policy as a merged full checkpoint, MinT keeps the base model resident and moves exported LoRA adapter revisions through rollout, update, export, evaluation, serving, and rollback, hiding distributed training, serving, scheduling, and data movement behind a service interface. MinT scales this path along three axes. Scale Up extends LoRA RL to frontier-scale dense and MoE architectures, including MLA and DSA attention paths, with training and serving validated beyond 1T total parameters. Scale Down moves only the exported LoRA adapter, which can be under 1% of base-model size in rank-1 settings; adapter-only handoff reduces the measured step by 18.3x on a 4B dense model and 2.85x on a 30B MoE, while concurrent multi-policy GRPO shortens wall time by 1.77x and 1.45x without raising peak memory. Scale Out separates durable policy addressability from CPU/GPU working sets: a tensor-parallel deployment supports 10^6-scale addressable catalogs (measured single-engine sweeps through 100K) and thousand-adapter active waves at cluster scale, with cold loading treated as scheduled service work and packed MoE LoRA tensors improving live engine loading by 8.5-8.7x. MinT thus manages million-scale LoRA policy catalogs while training and serving selected adapter revisions over shared 1T-class base models.

</details>


### [76] [A Unified Perspective for Learning Graph Representations Across Multi-Level Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12685)
*Mohamed Mahmoud Amar,Nairouz Mrabah,Mohamed Bouguessa,Abdoulaye Baniré Diallo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多尺度统一图对比学习框架，引入细粒度自加权机制以规避超参数调优，显著提升了图表示学习在多种下游任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图自监督学习方法多集中于单一抽象层级，且在优化过程中通常对所有样本施加统一的惩罚强度，导致模型优化灵活性有限且难以确定最佳收敛状态。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含节点、邻域、聚类及图级信息的统一对比框架，通过对正负样本相似度得分进行线性组合来整合多尺度信息；2. 提出了一种参数无关（parameter-free）的细粒度自加权机制，根据得分偏离目标值的程度动态分配权重，增强了优化灵活性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在多个真实数据集的大多数下游任务（节点分类、聚类、链路预测）中表现均优于当前的先进模型（SOTA），特别是在单层和多层场景下均展现出稳健的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一对比框架与无参数细粒度自加权机制能够有效提升图自监督学习的效果，通过多尺度信息整合与自适应权重调整，在分类、聚类及链路预测等任务中显著优于现有主流方法。

Abstract: Graph Self-Supervised Learning (GSSL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for generating high-quality representations for graph-structured data. While multi-scale graph contrastive learning has received increasing attention, many existing methods still predominantly focus on a single graph abstraction level. To address this limitation, we propose a unified contrastive framework that can target node-level, proximity-level, cluster-level, and graph-level information and integrate them through a linear combination of similarity scores on positive pairs and dissimilarity scores (i.e., similarity scores on negative pairs). Furthermore, current approaches typically assign uniform penalty strengths to all examples, which reduces optimization flexibility and leads to ambiguous convergence status. To overcome this, we introduce a novel parameter-free fine-grained self-weighting mechanism that adaptively assigns weights to individual similarity and dissimilarity scores. The proposed mechanism emphasizes the scores that deviate significantly from their target values. Our approach not only enhances optimization flexibility but also eliminates the computational overhead of hyperparameter tuning in conventional multi-task GSSL methods. Comprehensive experiments on real-world datasets show that our methods consistently outperform state-of-the-art approaches across downstream tasks, including classification, clustering, and link prediction, in both single-level and multi-level scenarios.

</details>


### [77] [IGT-OMD: Implicit Gradient Transport for Decision-Focused Learning under Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12693)
*Benjamin Amoh,Geoffrey G. Parker,Wesley Marrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对决策导向学习在延迟反馈下双层优化失效的问题，本文提出了IGT-OMD算法，通过隐式梯度传输修正梯度过时，在理论上实现了亚线性遗憾界并显著提升了决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在决策导向学习的在线设置中，反馈延迟会导致“过时放大（staleness amplification）”这一独特失效模式，导致双层优化中的遗憾（regret）超出传统单层延迟理论的范围。

Method: 提出了IGT-OMD算法，结合了针对超梯度的隐式梯度传输（Implicit Gradient Transport）与在线镜像下降（OMD），并采用了队列长度自适应步长策略。

Result: 在LQR、Warcraft最短路径和Sinkhorn最优传输任务中，相比单层基准，决策损失降低了17%至55%，且其实验结果符合对过时放大效应的理论预测。

Conclusion: 通过隐式梯度传输（IGT）修正双层优化中的梯度过时问题，IGT-OMD算法实现了对延迟的鲁棒性，在理论上将传输误差从二次降低为线性，并在实验中显著优于单层基准。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning trains predictive models end-to-end against downstream decision loss, but online settings suffer delayed feedback: outcomes may not arrive for many environment interactions. We identify \emph{staleness amplification}, a failure mode unique to bilevel optimization under delay, in which gradient staleness couples with inner-solver sensitivity to inflate regret beyond single-level delay theory. We prove that any black-box delayed optimizer incurs an irreducible regret cost from inner-solver approximation error, and that gradient staleness contributes a quadratically growing transport error without bilevel-aware correction. Our algorithm, \textbf{IGT-OMD}, applies Implicit Gradient Transport to hypergradients within Online Mirror Descent, re-evaluating stale gradients at the current parameters using stored inner solutions. This method reduces transport error from a quadratic to a linear dependence on delay and achieves the first sublinear regret bound for delayed bilevel optimization with queue-length-adaptive step sizes. Controlled experiments provide a \emph{mechanistic fingerprint}: transport benefit is exactly $0.0\%$ ($p=1.00$) at unit delay and grows monotonically to $9.5\%$ at fifty rounds ($p<0.001$), isolating the correction's effect. On Linear Quadratic Regulator, Warcraft shortest-path, and Sinkhorn optimal transport, IGT-OMD reduces decision loss by $17$--$55\%$ relative to single-level baselines, with phase transitions matching the theory.

</details>


### [78] [Do Fair Models Reason Fairly? Counterfactual Explanation Consistency for Procedural Fairness in Credit Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12701)
*Gideon Popoola,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究指出公平模型可能存在“结果相同但逻辑不同”的隐藏偏差，并提出了CEC框架，通过对齐反事实解释来确保算法决策过程的公平一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在敏感领域（如信用审批）虽实现了预测结果的公平，但忽略了不同群体在决策过程中可能存在“隐藏的程序偏差”（即相同的结果却基于不同的推理逻辑），这构成了公平性算法的一个盲点。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Explanation Consistency (CEC)框架，采用最近邻反事实生成方法、改进的集成梯度（Integrated Gradients）比较基准、个体层面的程序公平性指标以及相应的训练损失函数。

Result: 在合成数据及German Credit、Adult Income、HMDA数据集上的实验表明，现有的结果公平基准模型存在严重的隐藏程序偏差；CEC方法能显著降低该偏差，且对模型整体效用的影响较小。

Conclusion: 现有的结果公平（outcome-fair）模型往往忽略了推理过程的一致性。CEC框架能有效检测并缓解这种“隐藏的程序偏差”，在保证公平性的同时，以合理的效用成本实现了跨群体的决策一致性。

Abstract: Machine learning algorithms in socially sensitive domains (e.g., credit decisions) often focus on equalizing predictive outcomes. However, satisfying these metrics does not guarantee that models use the same reasoning for different groups. We show that existing outcome-fair models can still apply fundamentally different reasoning to individuals, a ``hidden procedural bias'' missed by standard fairness metrics and algorithms. We propose Counterfactual Explanation Consistency (CEC), a framework that detects and mitigates this bias by aligning feature attributions between individuals and their counterfactual counterparts. Key contributions include a nearest-neighbor counterfactual generation method, a modified baseline for integrated gradient comparisons, an individual-level procedural fairness metric, and a corresponding training loss. We introduce a taxonomy identifying ``Regime B'' (same outcome, different reasoning) as a critical blind spot. Experiments on synthetic data, German Credit, Adult Income, and HMDA mortgage data demonstrate that outcome-fair baselines exhibit substantial hidden bias, while CEC substantially reduces it with modest utility cost.

</details>


### [79] [Early Data Exposure Improves Robustness to Subsequent Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12705)
*Lawrence Feng,Gaurav R. Ghosal,Jacob Mitchell Springer,Ziqian Zhong,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现，通过将后训练数据提前引入预训练阶段（Early Exposure），相比单纯的后训练，能显著增强模型在后续微调中保持特定能力的鲁棒性，从而避免了依赖微调阶段的被动补救。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究后训练阶段习得的特定能力在后续微调过程中易被遗忘的问题，探讨可以通过哪些上游训练策略提高模型能力的鲁棒性。

Method: 在受控的三阶段语言模型流水线（预训练、后训练、下游微调）中，跨越135M和1B参数规模的模型进行实验；比较了数据分配策略（早期接触 vs 后训练）、重放（replay）和Dropout等技术对能力保持的影响。

Result: 1. 后训练阶段的即时性能无法预测下游微调后的能力保持情况；2. “早期接触”（将后训练数据混合至预训练阶段）能有效提升能力在后续微调中的鲁棒性；3. 并非极端的数据分配策略最优，存在平衡点；4. 重放和Dropout可作为补充手段进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 模型鲁棒性应被视为上游训练的一项核心指标，通过“早期接触”（early exposure）等预防性措施，而非仅在微调阶段进行补救，能更有效地防止能力遗忘。

Abstract: How can we train models whose post-trained capabilities survive subsequent fine-tuning? Rather than focusing on downstream interventions to mitigate forgetting of upstream capabilities, we study how upstream training choices - that is, the manner in which a capability is acquired - shape how robustly that capability is retained. We investigate this question in a controlled three-stage language-model pipeline: pretraining, post-training to acquire a target capability, and downstream fine-tuning on a new objective. Across 135M and 1B models, two post-training domains, and two downstream fine-tuning tasks, we find that immediate post-training performance does not reliably predict retention after subsequent fine-tuning: training recipes that look equivalent immediately after post-training can retain the target capability very differently after subsequent fine-tuning. In particular, early exposure - mixing post-training data into pretraining - consistently improves the frontier between retained upstream performance and downstream performance. In compute-matched experiments, where the target data must be allocated between pretraining and post-training, we find that the optimum lies at neither extreme. Together with our other empirical and theoretical findings, this supports the view that post-training drives immediate specialization while early exposure improves robustness to later forgetting. Replay and dropout, typically used to mitigate forgetting as it occurs during fine-tuning, provide complementary gains to early exposure when applied during post-training. Our findings suggest that robustness to subsequent fine-tuning should be treated as a first-class objective of upstream training, addressed preventatively through choices like early exposure rather than reactively during fine-tuning itself.

</details>


### [80] [Layer-wise Representation Dynamics: An Empirical Investigation Across Embedders and Base LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12714)
*Jingzhou Jiang,Yi Yang,Kar Yan Tam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Layer-wise Representation Dynamics (LRD) 框架，通过三种层级度量指标（Frenet, NRS, GFMI）系统分析大模型的层间表征动态，发现这些指标不仅能解释模型行为，还能有效辅助模型选择与推理加速剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析多关注模型单一层面的表征变化，缺乏对现代语言模型中隐藏状态在层间动态演变的全面度量与系统性研究。

Method: 提出了“层级表征动态（LRD）”框架，包含三种度量标准：1. Frenet（用于衡量全局子空间运动的测地线速度和曲率）；2. NRS（用于衡量局部最近邻保持度）；3. GFMI（用于衡量与最终层的一致性）。并在31个模型和30个MTEB任务上进行了验证。

Result: 实验发现：1. LRD 揭示了不同架构和任务间的隐藏差异；2. LRD 分数与下游任务性能正相关，可用于标签无关的模型选择（其中子空间位移指标最强）；3. GFMI 在推理阶段的层剪枝中优于随机策略。

Conclusion: LRD 框架揭示了隐藏状态在层间的演变规律，证明了层级结构分析对于模型解释、性能预测及推理加速（剪枝）具有重要的实际价值。

Abstract: Hidden states change substantially across the layers of modern language models, but most layer-wise analyses focus on one aspect of that change. We propose Layer-wise Representation Dynamics (LRD), a framework with three layer-wise measurement families: Frenet (Grassmann speed and curvature) for global subspace motion, Neighborhood Retention Score (NRS) for local nearest-neighbor retention, and Graph Filtration Mutual Information (GFMI) for alignment with the final layer. Applying LRD to 31 models (encoder-based and decoder-based embedders, plus base LLMs) on 30 MTEB tasks reveals architectural and task-level differences that are not apparent from final-layer representations alone. We then use LRD for two applications: label-free model selection and inference-time layer pruning. For selection, all three model-level scores correlate positively with downstream MTEB performance, with end-to-end subspace displacement (d_{0,L}) the strongest, and the same direction holds on a smaller base-LLM MMLU panel. For pruning, GFMI is the only measurement-guided rule that beats Random at the 15% and 20% budgets and has the best median change at every budget. Frenet is effective only at the lightest budget, while NRS does not transfer from model selection to pruning. These results show that layer-wise structure provides signal for both interpretation and deployment decisions.

</details>


### [81] [Scaling Laws for Mixture Pretraining Under Data Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12715)
*Anastasiia Sedova,Skyler Seto,Natalie Schluter,Pierre Ablin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型在有限目标数据下的混合训练策略，发现目标数据重复15-20次可显著提升性能，并提出了一种重复感知缩放定律来指导最优的混合配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型训练中，面对稀缺的目标领域数据，如何在混合通用数据时平衡“欠表现”与“重复过拟合”的权衡，缺乏系统的指导原则。

Method: 通过超过2,000次语言模型训练实验，覆盖多种模型规模、目标数据集大小及数据类型，分析混合训练中不同重复率对模型性能的影响，并推导出重复感知混合缩放定律。

Result: 稀缺目标语料库可以重复使用15-20次；发现最优重复次数取决于目标数据量、计算预算及模型规模；证明了通用数据在混合训练中起到了有效的正则化作用，允许目标数据进行更高倍数的重复。

Conclusion: 重复训练（repetition）是提升目标领域性能的核心驱动力，且混合训练比单源训练能容忍更高倍数的重复。研究提出的重复感知缩放定律为数据受限下的预训练提供了原则性的最优混合策略。

Abstract: As language models scale, the amount of data they require grows -- yet many target data sources, such as low-resource languages or specialized domains, are inherently limited in size. A common strategy is to mix this scarce but valuable target data with abundant generic data, which presents a fundamental trade-off: too little target data in the mixture underexposes the model to the target domain, while too much target data repeats the same examples excessively, yielding diminishing returns and eventual overfitting. We study this trade-off across more than 2,000 language-model training runs spanning multiple model and target dataset sizes, as well as several data types, including multilingual, domain-specific, and quality-filtered mixtures. Across all settings, we find that repetition is a central driver of target-domain performance, and that mixture training tolerates much higher repetition than single-source training: scarce target corpora can be reused 15-20 times, with the optimal number of repetitions depending on the target data size, compute budget, and model scale. Next, we introduce a repetition-aware mixture scaling law that accounts for the decreasing value of repeated target tokens and the regularizing role of generic data. Optimizing the scaling law provides a principled way to compute effective mixture configurations, yielding practical mixture recommendations for pretraining under data constraints.

</details>


### [82] [Before the Last Token: Diagnosing Final-Token Safety Probe Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12726)
*Shravan Doda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 传统的末端token安全检测器无法捕捉分布在prefill阶段的越狱提示词，作者提出通过分析隐藏状态的演变轨迹（PCA-HMM模型）能够更有效地识别潜在风险，同时避免错误报警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard final-token safety probes fail to detect jailbreak prompts because they rely on a single hidden state that may not capture adversarial evidence distributed across earlier token representations.

Method: 1. Used SafeSwitch-style probes trained on clean harmful/benign prompts.
2. Performed subspace analysis to compare missed jailbreaks with clean benign prompts.
3. Conducted token-level prefill analyses to track unsafe evidence over time.
4. Experimented with naive max-pooling over token positions.
5. Developed and evaluated a PCA-HMM (Principle Component Analysis-Hidden Markov Model) trajectory model to monitor hidden-state evolution across the prefill.

Result: 1. Probes show high recall on clean prompts but struggle with jailbreaks and produce false positives on safety-adjacent benign prompts.
2. Subspace analysis reveals a mismatch between jailbreak representations and probe-captured subspaces.
3. PCA-HMM trajectory models successfully recover many final-token misses without the high false-positive rate associated with naive pooling.

Conclusion: Final-token probes are insufficient for detecting jailbreaks due to the distributed nature of unsafe evidence in prefill trajectories. Trajectory-aware methods, such as the proposed PCA-HMM model, offer a more robust diagnostic alternative for safety monitoring.

Abstract: Final-token safety probes monitor a single hidden state after prompt prefill, but jailbreak prompts can contain probe-visible unsafe evidence distributed across earlier user-token representations that is missed by this readout. We study this prefill-time failure mode using SafeSwitch-style probes trained only on clean harmful and benign prompts across three instruction-tuned LLMs. The probes achieve high recall on clean harmful prompts, but miss many jailbreaks and can produce false positives on safety-adjacent benign prompts. Subspace analyses suggest that missed jailbreaks differ from clean benign prompts along directions that are poorly captured by the probe's representational subspace, and increasing probe bottleneck width does not reliably resolve this mismatch. Token-level prefill analyses reveal that probe-visible unsafe evidence often appears earlier in the sequence but is not exposed at the final-token readout, while naive max-pooling over token positions overfires on safe prompts. A simple PCA-HMM trajectory model, trained only on the same clean split, recovers many final-token misses from user-content prefill trajectories without the catastrophic false-positive behavior of naive token pooling, motivating trajectory-aware hidden-state analyses as diagnostic complements to final-token probes

</details>


### [83] [From Generalist to Specialist Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12733)
*Yujia Zheng,Fan Feng,Yuke Li,Shaoan Xie,Kevin Murphy,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在非参数化设定下，首次从理论上证明了可以仅通过无监督学习和稀疏性约束，实现跨时间步的任务结构识别及时间步内的任务相关表征分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用模型在下游应用中需要学习任务相关的专家表征，而“可识别性”（在无限数据和计算下恢复真实表征的能力）是衡量模型极限的核心指标。现有研究多依赖干预或特定参数约束，缺乏通用的非参数理论框架。

Method: 采用了纯非参数化的理论分析方法。在跨时间步层面，建立了基于序列和任务关联的识别理论；在时间步内，引入稀疏性正则化（sparsity regularization）作为唯一条件，论证了任务相关表征与非相关表征的分离能力。

Result: 证明了即使在序列缺乏强时间依赖、任务分配复杂且交错的情况下，任务结构依然是可识别的；进一步证明了仅需通过稀疏性正则化，即可在每个时间步中将任务相关潜变量从无关噪声中剔除。

Conclusion: 该研究为从通用模型向专家模型转换提供了第一个非参数化的可识别性理论基础。证明了在非参数化、无干预、无结构化约束的条件下，任务结构（跨时间步）和任务相关潜变量（在时间步内）均可被识别。

Abstract: Given a generalist model, learning a task-relevant specialist representation is fundamental for downstream applications. Identifiability, the asymptotic guarantee of recovering the ground-truth representation, is critical because it sets the ultimate limit of any model, even with infinite data and computation. We study this problem in a completely nonparametric setting, without relying on interventions, parametric forms, or structural constraints. We first prove that the structure between time steps and tasks is identifiable in a fully unsupervised manner, even when sequences lack strict temporal dependence and may exhibit disconnections, and task assignments can follow arbitrarily complex and interleaving structures. We then prove that, within each time step, the task-relevant latent representation can be disentangled from the irrelevant part under a simple sparsity regularization, without any additional information or parametric constraints. Together, these results establish a hierarchical foundation: task structure is identifiable across time steps, and task-relevant latent representations are identifiable within each step. To our knowledge, each result provides a first general nonparametric identifiability guarantee, and together they mark a step toward provably moving from generalist to specialist models.

</details>


### [84] [ConRetroBert: EMA Stabilized Dual Encoders for Template-Based Single-Step Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12736)
*Mohammad Jahid Ibna Basher,Ali Khodabandeh Yalabadi,Ivan Garibay,Ozlem Ozmen Garibay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConRetroBert通过将传统的分类任务重构为“检索+排序”问题，显著提升了基于模板的逆合成性能，证明了明确的化学转换规则在逆合成预测中的高效性与可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模板的逆合成方法常被认为不如无模板方法，其主要瓶颈在于将模板预测简单视作长尾类别分类问题，导致预测性能受限。作者旨在通过改进学习范式（检索+排序）来激发模板方法的潜力。

Method: 提出ConRetroBert双编码器框架：第一阶段采用对比学习预训练实现产物与模板的联合嵌入；第二阶段通过硬负采样和列表式损失函数进行排序优化，并引入EMA（指数移动平均）模板编码器以平衡检索库构建与模型动态更新。

Result: 在USPTO-50k数据集上，ConRetroBert达到62.4%的Top-1准确率；在使用大规模USPTO-Full预训练检查点微调后，准确率提升至75.4%，并在处理稀有模板的长尾预测任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: 将基于模板的逆合成预测转化为密集向量检索与列表式排序问题，能有效克服长尾分布带来的学习困难，证明了模板方法在性能和可解释性上具有竞争力。

Abstract: Template based single step retrosynthesis predicts reactants by selecting and applying an explicit reaction template, making each prediction traceable to a chemical transformation rule. This is useful for synthesis planning, but template based methods are often viewed as less competitive than template free models because template prediction is commonly formulated as global classification over a long tailed rule library. We argue that this weakness is not inherent to templates, but to the learning formulation. We present ConRetroBert, a dual encoder framework that reframes template based retrosynthesis as dense product template retrieval followed by candidate set listwise ranking. Stage 1 uses contrastive pretraining to learn a shared embedding space between products and reaction templates. Stage 2 refines template ranking over mined hard negative candidate sets with a multi positive listwise objective. To enable template side adaptation without destabilizing hard negative mining, ConRetroBert uses a slow moving exponential moving average template encoder for retrieval bank construction while updating the live template encoder through the ranking loss. On the local USPTO-50k benchmark, Stage 2 candidate set ranking improves top-1 reaction accuracy from 50.5% to 61.3%, while EMA stabilized template adaptation further improves it to 62.4%. Fine tuning from a leakage controlled USPTO-Full checkpoint reaches 75.4% top-1 accuracy on USPTO-50k. We also show that retrieval based template prediction is strong in the long tail of rare templates, and that many correct reactant predictions arise from alternative explicit templates rather than only the recorded positive label. Code and data are available at https://github.com/JahidBasher/ConRetroBert.

</details>


### [85] [Learning with Rare Success but Rich Feedback via Reflection-Enhanced Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12741)
*Yuwei Zhang,Sha Li,Changlong Yu,Qin Lu,Shuowei Jin,Chengyu Dong,Haoran Liu,Ilgee Hong,Xintong Li,Zhenyu Shi,Bing Yin,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RESD框架通过引入基于失败案例的回溯反射与经验总结机制，将失败经验转化为主动学习信号，有效解决了大模型在稀疏奖励环境下的学习效率瓶颈，并在交互效率上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的策略模型自蒸馏方法将环境反馈视为被动调节信号，严重依赖成功案例，导致模型在难以获得成功的环境（rare-success regimes）中难以学习。

Method: 提出了“反射增强自蒸馏”（RESD）框架：1. 对失败轨迹进行回溯性反射以诊断局部错误；2. 构建持久性的全局攻略（global playbook）以积累经验；3. 利用这些反馈生成token级别的动作性监督，即使没有成功案例也能引导模型优化。

Result: 实验表明，RESD在多个持续学习任务中显著优于标准自蒸馏基线；在仅使用每个提示一次交互的情况下，其早期阶段的改进速度显著快于使用8倍样本量的GRPO。

Conclusion: RESD通过将失败反馈转化为主动的纠偏指导，显著提升了大型语言模型在稀疏成功场景下的持续学习能力与样本交互效率，证明了反射机制在后训练阶段的有效性。

Abstract: Enabling Large Language Models (LLMs) to continuously improve from environmental interactions is a central challenge in post-training. While on-policy self-distillation offers a promising paradigm, existing methods predominantly treat environmental feedback as a passive conditioning signal. Consequently, they heavily rely on successful demonstrations and struggle to learn in rare-success regimes. To bridge this gap, we introduce Reflection-Enhanced Self-Distillation (RESD), a framework that transforms raw failure feedback into an active source of corrective supervision. Instead of passively appending feedback, RESD interprets failed trajectories by generating retrospective reflections to diagnose local errors, and curates a persistent global playbook to preserve reusable lessons across training steps. The enriched context enables the self-teacher to provide actionable token-level supervision even in the absence of successful rollouts. Empirical evaluations on multiple continual learning tasks demonstrate that RESD substantially outperforms standard self-distillation baselines. Furthermore, RESD achieves significantly faster early-stage improvement than GRPO with $8\times$ samples using only a single rollout per prompt, highlighting its superior interaction efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [Delightful Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13287)
*Ian Osband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对资源受限下的探索难题，本文提出了一种基于“惊喜度（Surprisal）”定价的DE探索算法，通过门控策略有效提升了信息获取的效率，并在多种实验环境中优于现有主流探索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模动作空间探索算法的局限性：传统贪婪算法（ε-greedy）盲目消耗预算，导致探索效率低下；而完全的探索算法在资源有限条件下无法处理巨大的动作空间，导致预期遗憾过高。需要一种更具策略性的资源分配机制。

Method: 提出“Delight-gated exploration (DE)”算法：一种基于“预期改进×惊喜度”的准入控制机制。该方法为探索行为设立“门槛报价”，仅在潜在的“惊喜（信息增益/不确定性折减）”超过成本阈值时执行探索，并结合了线性带宽和马尔可夫决策过程的策略优化。

Result: 在Bernoulli带宽、线性带宽和表格型MDP任务中，DE算法展现出比Thompson采样和ε-greedy更低的遗憾增长；且不同任务间无需重新调整超参数，表现出极强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DE通过引入“惊喜（surprisal）”作为探索的准入机制，确立了类似于Pandora的保留价值决策规则。该方法证明了在资源有限的情况下，通过定价探索行动的价值，可以显著提升探索效率，并实现超越Thompson采样和ε-greedy的算法通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Most exploration algorithms search broadly until uncertainty is resolved. When the action space is too large to resolve within budget, practitioners default to $\varepsilon$-greedy, which bounds disruption but spends its override blindly. We introduce \textit{Delight-gated exploration} (DE), a host--override rule that spends exploratory actions only when their prospective delight (expected improvement times surprisal) exceeds a gate price. This practical heuristic recovers a classical result: Pandora's reservation-value rule for costly search, with surprisal setting the effective inspection cost. Resolved arms exit the gate, fresh arms shut off above a prior-determined threshold, and selected linear-bandit overrides consume finite information budget. Across Bernoulli bandits, linear bandits, and tabular MDPs, the same hyperparameters transfer without retuning, and DE shows much weaker regret growth than Thompson Sampling and $\varepsilon$-greedy in the tested unresolved regimes. Delight improves acting for the same reason it improves learning: it prices scarce resources by the product of upside and surprisal.

</details>


### [87] [Low-Rank Adapters Initialization via Gradient Surgery for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12752)
*Joana Pasquali,Ramiro N. Barros,Arthur S. Bianchessi,Vinícius Conte Turani,João Vitor Boer Abitante,Rafaela Cappelari Ravazio,Christian Mattjie,Otávio Parraga,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LoRA 在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出了 SLICE 初始化方法。该方法通过协调当前与历史任务的梯度并进行 SVD 分解初始化适配器，有效解决了梯度冲突导致的知识覆盖问题，并在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 在持续学习中容易发生灾难性遗忘，核心原因在于连续任务梯度冲突时，标准的适配器初始化方式会将权重更新引导至覆盖先前学到特征的子空间中。

Method: 提出 SLICE（基于梯度手术的适配器初始化方法）：利用当前任务梯度和重放缓冲区的历史梯度，通过投影算子进行协调，并利用截断奇异值分解（SVD）来初始化 LoRA 适配器权重。

Result: 在 TRACE 基准测试和 Super-NI 任务序列（包括构造的对抗性序列）中，SLICE 相比 vanilla LoRA、LoRA-GA 和 LoRAM 表现更优，在平均性能、最终性能和遗忘度指标上均有提升，同时保持了良好的通用性能。

Conclusion: SLICE 是一种有效缓解 LoRA 持续学习中灾难性遗忘的方法，通过梯度手术对适配器进行初始化，在保持模型可塑性的同时显著提升了稳定性。

Abstract: LoRA is widely adopted for continual fine-tuning of Large Language Models due to its parameter efficiency, modularity across tasks, and compatibility with replay strategies. However, LoRA-based continual learning remains vulnerable to catastrophic forgetting, whose severity depends on how successive task gradients interact: when consecutive task gradients conflict, standard adapter initializations channel updates into subspaces that overwrite previously learned directions. We propose SLICE, a gradient-surgery-based initialization for LoRA adapters in continual learning. SLICE accumulates gradients from both the current task and a replay buffer of prior tasks, reconciles them through a projection operator, and decomposes the result via truncated SVD to initialize the adapter weights. We evaluate SLICE on the TRACE benchmark and sequences of Super-NI tasks, including a set of adversarial Super-NI sequences that we construct by mining task pairs with maximally opposing gradients. Compared to vanilla LoRA, LoRA-GA, and LoRAM, SLICE consistently achieves a better stability-plasticity trade-off, improving Average Performance, Final Performance and Forgetting metrics while preserving General Performance and In Context Performance across both standard and adversarial continual learning sequences.

</details>


### [88] [Support-Conditioned Flow Matching Is Kernel Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13386)
*Daniel Matsui Smola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了生成模型中的交叉注意力本质上是一种随时间动态调整带宽的Nadaraya-Watson核平滑，通过理论预测了其在特定几何与统计条件下的失效模式，并验证了现代模型通过学习有效缓解了这些局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示生成模型中常用的“通过交叉注意力机制进行条件控制”这一做法的数学本质，并探究其在复杂数据生成任务中失效的原因。

Method: 利用高斯最优输运路径（Gaussian Optimal-Transport path）分析有限支持集下的速度场；利用核密度估计理论推导该速度场与Nadaraya-Watson核平滑的等价性；通过对比经典模型与学习型模型在特定失效场景（高维崩溃、空间几何不匹配、支持样本不足）下的表现进行验证。

Result: 1. 证明了交叉注意力头等效于一个随时间调整带宽的Nadaraya-Watson核平滑器。2. 识别了三种导致该机制失效的 regimes：高维下的最近邻崩溃、核函数与数据几何结构不一致、以及非参数估计样本不足。3. 实验验证了现有方法（如IP-Adapter）在实践中确实在执行近似的NW平滑，且模型学习过程会针对上述弱点进行修正。

Conclusion: 通过理论推导与实验验证，证明了交叉注意力机制在生成模型中本质上是在执行一种动态带宽的Nadaraya-Watson核平滑。该研究揭示了注意力机制的局限性，并为优化生成模型的条件控制提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Generative models are often conditioned on a small set of examples via cross-attention. Under the Gaussian optimal-transport path, we show that the exact velocity field induced by a finite support set is a Nadaraya--Watson kernel smoother whose bandwidth decreases with flow time, from broad averaging at early steps to nearest-neighbor at late steps. A single Gaussian-kernel attention head exactly computes this field, connecting cross-attention conditioning to classical kernel theory. The theory predicts three failure regimes: nearest-neighbor collapse of the kernel at high dimension, mismatch between the isotropic kernel and the data geometry, and insufficient support for nonparametric estimation. Experiments on Gaussian mixtures, spherical shells, and DINOv2 ImageNet features confirm that learned conditioning improves in precisely these regimes, and that IP-Adapter's cross-attention implements approximate NW smoothing in practice.

</details>


### [89] [Constraint-Aware Flow Matching: Decision Aligned End-to-End Training for Constrained Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12754)
*Jacob K. Christopher,James E. Warner,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对物理约束生成模型中“训练目标与约束采样不匹配”的缺陷，提出了Constraint-Aware Flow Matching，通过将约束投影整合进训练过程，有效解决了分布偏移问题，实现了高质量且满足约束的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理约束生成模型在“训练中不考虑约束”与“采样中强行投影”之间存在严重错位（Mismatch），导致严重的分布偏移（Distributional Shift），从而在保证约束满足的同时牺牲了样本的生成质量。

Method: 提出了一种端到端的“约束感知流匹配”（Constraint-Aware Flow Matching）框架，核心是将约束投影操作显式地融入模型的训练目标（Objective）中，使模型学习到的动力学与后续的约束采样过程对齐。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的现实世界基准测试中表现优异，证明了该方法在处理复杂约束下的通用性、生成质量以及对约束的严格满足能力，显著优于现有的训练无关（Training-free）采样方法。

Conclusion: Constraint-Aware Flow Matching 通过在训练阶段显式结合约束投影，有效解决了训练与采样过程不匹配的矛盾，在高保真度和严格约束满足之间实现了平衡，为物理约束生成模型提供了一种高效的通用范式。

Abstract: Deep generative models provide state-of-the-art performance across a wide array of applications, with recent studies showing increasing applicability for science and engineering. Despite a growing corpus of literature focused on the integration of physics-based constraints into the generation process, existing approaches fail to enforce strict constraint satisfaction while maintaining sample quality. In particular, training-free constrained sampling methods, while providing per-sample feasibility guarantees, introduce a fundamental mismatch between the training objective and the constrained sampling procedure, often leading to performance degradation. Identifying this training-sampling misalignment as a central limitation of current constrained generative modeling approaches, this paper proposes Constraint-Aware Flow Matching, a novel end-to-end framework that explicitly incorporates constraint projections into the training objective. By aligning the model's learned dynamics with the constrained sampling process, the proposed method mitigates distributional shift induced by projection-based corrections, enabling high-quality constrained generation. The proposed approach is evaluated on three challenging real-world benchmarks, illustrating the generality and efficacy of the method.

</details>


### [90] [Trajectory-Level Data Augmentation for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13401)
*Tobias Schmähling,Matthias Burkhardt,Tobias Windisch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线强化学习数据稀缺与质量不高的问题，提出了一种利用轨迹结构与几何属性的数据增强方法，有效提升了次优数据下的模型学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离线强化学习在仅有少量次优（suboptimal）轨迹数据时难以进行有效训练的问题，针对主动定位（active positioning）任务的需求。

Method: 提出了一种轨迹增强技术（trajectory-based augmentation technique），利用任务结构、奖励与价值函数之间的几何关系，以及日志策略的数学特性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法支持次优的日志策略，能提高数据质量并显著提升离线强化学习算法在不同维度和部分可观测环境下的定位性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于轨迹的数据增强方法能有效提升离线强化学习在仅有少量次优轨迹数据情况下的训练效果，且具备理论支撑和广泛的实验验证。

Abstract: We propose a data augmentation method for offline reinforcement learning, motivated by active positioning problems. Particularly, our approach enables the training of off-policy models from a limited number of suboptimal trajectories. We introduce a trajectory-based augmentation technique that exploits task structure and the geometric relationship between rewards, value functions, and mathematical properties of logging policies. During data collection, our augmentation supports suboptimal logging policies, leading to higher data quality and improved offline reinforcement learning performance. We provide theoretical justification for these strategies and validate them empirically across positioning tasks of varying dimensionality and under partial observability.

</details>


### [91] [Achieving $ε^{-2}$ Sample Complexity for Single-Loop Actor-Critic under Minimal Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13639)
*Ishaq Hamza,Zaiwei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为离策略 Actor-Critic 方法建立了一个单循环、单时间尺度的收敛性分析，在仅需马尔可夫链不可约的最弱假设下，实现了 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\epsilon^{-2})$ 的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离策略 Actor-Critic 方法通常依赖嵌套循环或强假设（如均匀混合、均匀探索）才能获得 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\epsilon^{-2})$ 的样本复杂度，该文旨在通过单循环、单时间尺度实现突破这些限制。

Method: 采用耦合 Lyapunov 漂移（coupled Lyapunov drift）框架，结合交叉占优（cross-domination）属性，同时分析 Actor 的几何收敛率和 Critic 的 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/T)$ 收敛率。

Result: 首次证明了在单循环、单时间尺度下，对于广泛的策略更新类，达到 $\epsilon$-最优策略的样本复杂度为 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\epsilon^{-2})$。该分析框架处理了耦合更新方程和离策略学习中潜在的无界迭代问题。

Conclusion: 提出了一种适用于耦合迭代算法的耦合 Lyapunov 漂移分析框架。证明了在离策略 Actor-Critic 方法中，单循环、单时间尺度实现即可达到 $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\epsilon^{-2})$ 的样本复杂度，且无需强假设，仅需策略诱导出的马尔可夫链不可约。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish last-iterate convergence rates for off-policy actor--critic methods in reinforcement learning. In particular, under a single-loop, single-timescale implementation and a broad class of policy updates, including approximate policy iteration and natural policy gradient methods, we prove the first $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity guarantee for finding an $ε$-optimal policy under minimal assumptions, namely, the existence of a policy that induces an irreducible Markov chain. This stands in stark contrast to the existing literature, where an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity is achieved only through nested-loop updates and/or under strong, algorithm-dependent assumptions on the policies, such as uniform mixing and uniform exploration. Technically, to address the challenges posed by the coupled update equations arising from the single-loop implementation, as well as the potentially unbounded iterates induced by off-policy learning, our analysis is based on a coupled Lyapunov drift framework. Specifically, we establish a geometric convergence rate for the actor and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/T)$ convergence rate for the critic, and combine the two Lyapunov drift inequalities through a cross-domination property. We believe this analytical framework is of independent interest and may be applicable to other coupled iterative algorithms with unbounded

</details>


### [92] [Inference-Time Machine Unlearning via Gated Activation Redirection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12765)
*Vinícius Conte Turani,Otávio Parraga,João Vitor Boer Abitante,Kristen K. Arguello,Joana Pasquali,Ramiro N. Barros,Flavio du Pin Calmon,Christian Mattjie,Rodrigo C. Barros,Lucas S. Kupssinskü*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GUARD-IT 是一种无需训练和梯度的新型机器遗忘方法，通过在推理阶段动态引导激活值来移除特定记忆，在保持模型性能的同时，解决了传统权重编辑方法的局限性与量化兼容性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘方法（如基于梯度的权重更新）计算成本高、具有不可逆性，且在模型量化部署时表现不佳；而现有的简单的全局激活工程方法容易引发模型行为的非预期改变。因此需要一种更高效、稳健且不破坏模型性能的遗忘方案。

Method: 提出了一种称为“门控激活重定向”（GUARD-IT）的方法。该方法在推理阶段通过输入相关的激活引导（Activation Steering）来剔除敏感信息。它以残差流中的保持范数的旋转形式实施干预，无需修改模型权重，也无需计算梯度。

Result: 在 TOFU 和 MUSE 数据集上的实验表明，GUARD-IT 在三种模型规模下表现均优于 12 个基于梯度的基准方法，能够有效抑制记忆、保持模型效能，并支持连续遗忘及量化环境下的部署。

Conclusion: GUARD-IT 是一种高效、无需训练/梯度的机器遗忘方法，通过输入相关的激活引导实现了性能与安全性的平衡，克服了传统权重编辑方法的弊端，且在模型量化下表现稳健。

Abstract: Large Language Models memorize vast amounts of training data, raising concerns regarding privacy, copyright infringement, and safety. Machine unlearning seeks to remove the influence of a targeted forget set while preserving model performance, ideally approximating a model retrained from scratch without the forget set. Existing approaches aim to achieve this by updating model parameters via gradient-based methods. However, these updates are computationally expensive, lead to irreversible weight changes, and degrade when the model is quantized for deployment. A recent alternative to changing model weights is activation engineering, where activations are changed during inference to steer model behavior. Despite circumventing weight editing, naive activation steering introduces its own failure modes, as a single global steering vector applies the same intervention to every input, leading to unintended changes in model behavior. We introduce Inference-Time Unlearning via Gated Activation Redirection (GUARD-IT), a training- and gradient-free method that unlearns via input-dependent activation steering at inference time. The resulting intervention is applied as a norm-preserving rotation in the residual stream, leaving model weights untouched. Experiments on TOFU and MUSE show that GUARD-IT matches or exceeds 12 gradient-based baselines across three model scales, while being the only method to simultaneously preserve utility, suppress memorization, and avoid catastrophic collapse across all settings. GUARD-IT further supports continual unlearning without retraining, and remains effective under quantization, a scenario in which parameter-editing methods degrade.

</details>


### [93] [Sampling from Flow Language Models via Marginal-Conditioned Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13681)
*Iskander Azangulov,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对流语言模型（FLMs）提出了边际条件桥接（MCB）采样法，通过采样离散端点而非使用条件均值，实现了更精确的反向生成，不仅支持灵活的解码控制，还提升了生成质量与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流语言模型（FLM）采样方式（DDPM 风格）将边际分布坍缩为条件均值点，导致生成结果在空间上非 one-hot 且不准确，限制了模型性能和 decoding 控制能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于后验预测的采样方法（MCB），在反向生成过程中，不直接使用条件均值点，而是从 FLM 预测的边际分布中采样一个具体的 one-hot 端点，并基于此端点构建 OU 桥接；该方法无需额外训练，支持温度采样和核采样（nucleus sampling）。

Result: 在理论上证明了 MCB 具有更小的去噪误差，在实验上验证了该方法能够有效提升流语言模型的生成质量与多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 提出了边际条件桥接（MCB）采样方法，证明了其在去噪误差上优于传统的条件均值桥接，并通过实验证实了其能显著改善 FLM 在生成质量与多样性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Flow Language Models (FLMs) are a recently introduced class of language models which adapt continuous flow matching for one-hot encoded token sequences. Their denoisers have a special structure absent from generic continuous diffusion models: each block of the denoising mean is a posterior marginal distribution over the clean token at that position. Standard DDPM-style samplers collapse these marginals to a single conditional-mean endpoint and bridge toward this simplex-valued point, which is generally not a valid one-hot sequence. We argue that the natural sampler for an FLM is instead posterior-predictive. At each reverse step, we sample a clean one-hot endpoint from the factorized posterior defined by the FLM token marginals, and then sample the next continuous state from the analytic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck bridge conditioned on that endpoint. The method is training-free, uses the same model evaluations as standard sampling, and gives a principled interface for token-level decoding controls such as temperature scaling and nucleus truncation. We show that, under exact posterior marginals, the endpoint approximation error is exactly the conditional multi-information among token positions. The induced one-step bridge kernel preserves all token-wise posterior-predictive marginals and loses only the residual cross-position dependence. Finally, we prove a Girsanov path-space comparison showing that the marginal-conditioned bridge has a no-larger denoising-error term than the frozen conditional-mean bridge, with strict improvement whenever intermediate coordinate-wise bridge observations reveal additional information about the clean token. Experiments with FLMs show that the sampler improves the quality--diversity tradeoff. Code is available at: github.com/imbirik/mcb.

</details>


### [94] [A Hierarchical Language Model with Predictable Scaling Laws and Provable Benefits of Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13687)
*Jason Gaitonde,Frederic Koehler,Elchanan Mossel,Joonhyung Shin,Allan Sly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个分析自回归模型长程依赖的数学框架，证明了仅靠增加Transformer上下文窗口无法有效处理复杂的层次化语言，而引入推理机制能以指数级更少的内存成本实现精确采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在量化自回归模型在处理具有层次结构任务时，上下文长度与推理能力对模型性能的影响，并探讨单纯依靠增大上下文窗口与引入显式推理机制之间的效能差异。

Method: 引入了一族基于树形广播过程的合成语言，构建了“精确k-gram ansatz”模型来替代有限窗口长度的Transformer，并结合理论推导与经验验证，分析了Ising（软约束）和着色（硬约束）两类语言在不同上下文限制下的生成表现。

Result: 在软约束（Ising）任务中，有限上下文导致生成统计量的偏差；在硬约束（着色）任务中，有限上下文模型几乎无法生成合法序列。实验验证了这些理论预测，证明了模型推理能力对于突破长程依赖瓶颈的重要性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对于结构化语言，线性上下文长度（Ω(n)）是自回归模型生成正确序列的必要条件，而引入推理机制（如工作记忆）可以将所需上下文缩减至对数级（Θ(log n)），从而实现指数级的效率提升。

Abstract: We introduce a family of synthetic languages with hierarchical structure -- generated by a broadcast process on trees -- for which the role of context length and reasoning in autoregressive generation can be analyzed precisely. At the heart of our analytic approach is an \emph{exact $k$-gram ansatz} in place of transformers with context length $k$, a substitution we then validate empirically. Using this ansatz we derive explicit asymptotic predictions for distributional statistics of the sequences produced by a trained model, instantiated in two settings. For the \emph{Ising broadcast process} (a soft-constrained language), we prove that the variance of the generated sum scales log-linearly in the context depth and its kurtosis converges to that of a Gaussian -- both deviating from the true language for any sublinear context. For the \emph{coloring broadcast process} (a hard-constrained language) in the freezing regime, bounded-context autoregression produces sequences that, with high probability, are inconsistent with \emph{any} valid coloring of the underlying tree. Together these results imply an $Ω(n)$ lower bound on the context length required to faithfully sample length-$n$ sequences. In contrast, we prove that an autoregressive \emph{reasoning} model with only $Θ(\log n)$ working memory can sample exactly from the true language -- an exponential improvement. We confirm both the lower-bound predictions and the reasoning-based upper bound empirically with transformers trained on the synthetic language; the trained models track our asymptotic predictions quantitatively across a wide range of context sizes.

</details>


### [95] [Tight Sample Complexity Bounds for Entropic Best Policy Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13717)
*Amer Essakine,Claire Vernade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了熵风险敏感强化学习中样本复杂度的理论缺口，通过更新的浓度分析技术和停止准则，使最优策略识别的样本复杂度达到了理论最优下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中在有限水平风险敏感强化学习的最优策略识别问题上，样本复杂度的上限与下限存在 $e^{|β| H}$ 的差距，源于对指数效用函数浓度控制过于松散。

Method: 基于前向模型（forward-model based）的算法，结合专门适配熵准则的KL散度探索奖励（KL-based exploration bonuses）以及利用指数效用函数平滑性的 sharper concentration bounds 及新型停止准则。

Result: 将该问题的样本复杂度上界优化至与下界一致，即达到了 $O(e^{|β| H})$ 的最优复杂度，消除了之前 $O(e^{2|β| H})$ 的额外指数因子。

Conclusion: 通过创新的浓度界分析和全新的停止准则，本文解决了有限水平（finite-horizon）熵风险敏感强化学习中样本复杂度的理论缺口，实现了算法上界与已知下界的匹配。

Abstract: We study best-policy identification for finite-horizon risk-sensitive reinforcement learning under the entropic risk measure. Recent work established a constant gap in the exponential horizon dependence between lower and upper bounds on the number of samples required to identify an approximately optimal policy. Precisely, known lower bounds scale in $Ω(e^{|β| H})$ where $H$ is the horizon of the MDP, while the state-of-the-art upper bound achieves at best $O(e^{2|β| H})$ (arXiv:2506.00286v2) using a generative model. We show that this extra exponential factor can be traced to overly loose concentration control for exponential utilities. To close this open gap, we revisit the analysis of this problem through a forward-model based algorithm building on KL-based exploration bonuses that we adapt to the entropic criterion. The improvement we get is due to two main novel technical innovations. We leverage the smoothness properties of the exponential utility to derive sharper concentration bounds, and we propose a new stopping rule that exploits further this tightness to obtain a sample complexity that matches the lower bound.

</details>


### [96] [Emergent and Subliminal Misalignment Through the Lens of Data-Mediated Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12798)
*Baris Askin,Muhammed Ustaomeroglu,Anupam Nayak,Gauri Joshi,Guannan Qu,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM微调导致的“突发性错位”并非简单由有害数据引起，而是微调数据结构、预训练先验与训练策略交互作用导致的复杂现象，并首次探索了通过有害教师数据进行错位传递的“亚阈值学习”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在深入理解大语言模型在微调过程中产生“突发性错位”（EM）的机制，即模型在微调分布之外表现出超出预期的有害行为。

Method: 通过实验分析微调数据结构、预训练构成及任务难度对模型行为溢出的影响；引入并研究“亚阈值学习”（SL），对比离线策略（off-policy）与在线策略（on-policy）蒸馏对错位传递的作用。

Result: 研究发现，当微调与评估提示具有相似功能结构、提示空间允许生成连贯有害输出、或目标行为已被模型稳固习得时，错位更容易出现。同时，预训练的构成对后续错位有显著塑造作用，且通过有害教师生成的看似良性数据也能有效传递错位（SL）。

Conclusion: 突发性错位（EM）和亚阈值学习（SL）并非孤立有害数据的直接产物，而是由微调数据结构、预训练分布及训练方式共同交互作用的结果。这强调了在模型对齐研究中采用以数据为中心（data-centric）视角的重要性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning LLMs on narrow harmful datasets can induce Emergent Misalignment (EM), where models exhibit misaligned behavior far beyond the fine-tuning distribution. We argue that emergent misalignment can be better understood as a data-mediated transfer phenomenon: harmful fine-tuning examples do not induce uniform behavioral spillover, but interact with the structural properties of the dataset and the difficulty of the tasks relative to the model. Across our experiments, we find that misalignment appears more readily when fine-tuning and evaluation prompts share similar underlying functional structure, when prompts leave more room for coherent harmful completions, and when the target behavior has been more reliably learned by the model. The training pipeline itself also matters: pretraining composition shapes later misalignment. We further study Subliminal Learning (SL), where misalignment is transmitted by fine-tuning on seemingly benign data generated by a harmful teacher. Moving beyond the standard SFT setting, we for the first time compare this transfer under off-policy and on-policy distillation as well, allowing us to separate the roles of the teacher guidance and the training data distribution in transmitting misalignment. Together, these results argue for a data-centric view: Emergent/subliminal misalignment should not be treated as a simple consequence of isolated harmful fine-tuning examples, but as the result of interactions between fine-tuning data structure, pretraining distributions, and training channels.

</details>


### [97] [Discrete MeanFlow: One-Step Generation via Conditional Transition Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12805)
*Fairoz Nower Khan,Nabuat Zaman Nahim,Md Sajid Ahmed,Ruiquan Huang,Peizhong Ju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Discrete MeanFlow 将流匹配的思想扩展到离散空间，通过预测跨时间间隔的条件转移核，实现了离散数据的一次性生成，克服了传统扩散模型需要多次迭代采样的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 MeanFlow 仅适用于连续空间，由于离散状态空间缺乏平滑轨迹和空间导数，无法直接应用。因此需要一种适用于离散空间的“一步生成”框架，以避免生成过程中的迭代去噪或 ODE 积分等高计算开销。

Method: 提出 Discrete MeanFlow：引入连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）的条件转移核概念，定义平均离散速率（mean discrete rate），并构建 Discrete MeanFlow 恒等式。采用“构造性边界”设计对转移核进行参数化，确保输出概率有效且无需辅助损失函数。

Result: 在精确有限状态马尔可夫链实验中高精度恢复了分析基准；在因子化合成序列生成任务中表现优异，通过单次前向传递和类别抽取即可完成高质量生成，无需多步细化。

Conclusion: Discrete MeanFlow 成功扩展了连续空间 MeanFlow 的优势至离散空间，通过直接预测条件转移核，实现了无需迭代采样（一键生成）的离散数据生成，且具备数学上的严谨性和高效性。

Abstract: MeanFlow enables one-step generation in continuous spaces by learning an average velocity over a time interval rather than the instantaneous velocity field of flow matching. However, discrete state spaces do not have smooth trajectories or spatial derivatives, so the continuous formulation does not directly apply. We introduce Discrete MeanFlow, which replaces the motion of a point with the transport of probability mass over finite states. Our key object is the conditional transition kernel of a continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC), from which we define a mean discrete rate that measures the average change in transition probability over a time interval. We prove a Discrete MeanFlow identity that relates this finite-interval rate to the instantaneous CTMC generator at the endpoint, with the Kolmogorov forward equation replacing the spatial chain rule of continuous MeanFlow. Based on this identity, we parameterize the transition kernel directly using a boundary-by-construction design that guarantees valid probability outputs and exact boundary conditions without auxiliary losses. Since the learned kernel is itself a probability distribution, generation reduces to a single forward pass followed by one categorical draw meaning no iterative denoising, ODE integration, or multi-step refinement is required. We validate the framework on exact finite-state Markov chains, where the learned kernel recovers the analytical ground truth to high precision, and on factorized synthetic sequence generation tasks with varying alphabet sizes and sequence lengths.

</details>


### [98] [Neurodata Without Boredom: Benchmarking Agentic AI for Data Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12808)
*Ling-Qi Zhang,Kristin Branson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了人工智能Agent在处理异构神经科学数据中的表现，发现它们虽能处理部分子任务，但离不开人类监督；文章明确了Agent常见的错误模式，并为未来神经科学数据共享提出了改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经科学数据分散、格式异构，数据重用难度大且成本高。通用的数据格式往往在灵活性与描述性之间存在矛盾。研究旨在评估Agentic AI在解读、处理和整合不同范式神经数据方面的能力，探索其作为自动化数据管理工具的潜力。

Method: 选取8篇涵盖多种神经科学记录模态、行为范式及数据格式（如NWB、API、通用代码文件）的近期论文进行研究。为AI Agent提供原始数据、实验代码及论文原文，要求其完成从载入、理解到重构数据以用于下游解码器训练的完整任务，并评估其性能和错误类型。

Result: 通用编程Agent能够完成单个子任务，但难以在无错误的前提下实现完整的端到端自动化处理。研究详细分析了Agent的典型错误类型及其触发因素，发现Agent在缺乏真值参照（Ground-truth）的情况下，自行评估（Agents-as-judges）准确性较低。

Conclusion: 当前Agent在端到端的神经科学数据处理任务中尚无法完全实现自动化，且“Agent作为评判者”在缺乏基准参考时不可靠，因此人机协作（Human-in-the-loop）在神经科学数据解析中仍然至关重要。研究同时提出了适应Agent时代的神经科学数据共享最佳实践建议。

Abstract: Neuroscience data are highly fragmented across labs, formats, and experimental paradigms, and reuse often requires substantial manual effort. A persistent roadblock to data reuse and integration is the need to decipher bespoke and diverse data formatting choices. Common data formats have been proposed in response, but the field continues to struggle with a fundamental tension: formats flexible enough to accommodate diverse experiments are rarely descriptive enough to be self-explanatory, and sufficiently descriptive formats demand detailed documentation and curation effort that few labs can sustain. Agentic AI is a natural candidate to solve this problem: LLMs read code and text faster and with sustained attention to the low-level details humans tend to skim over. To measure how well agentic AI performs on this task, we selected eight recent papers studying large-scale mouse neural population recordings that shared both data and code, spanning diverse recording modalities, behavioral paradigms, and dataset formats (e.g., NWB, specialized APIs, and general-purpose Python or MATLAB files). We provided agents with the data, code, and paper, and prompted them to load, understand, and reformat the data for a common downstream task: training a decoder from neural activity to task or behavioral variables. General-purpose coding agents commonly used by scientists performed well on each sub-task, but rarely strung together a fully error-free end-to-end solution. We characterize the types of mistakes agents made and the dataset properties that elicited them, and propose data-sharing best practices for the agentic-AI era. We further find that agents-as-judges are unreliable at catching errors, especially without ground-truth references, so interactive, human-in-the-loop coding remains necessary.

</details>


### [99] [Correcting Influence: Unboxing LLM Outputs with Orthogonal Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12809)
*Shixing Yu,Promit Ghosal,Kyra Gan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决大语言模型在医疗等高风险领域缺乏可溯源性的问题，本文提出了一种基于潜在中介和稀疏自编码器的 token 级影响追踪框架，支持更精确、可审计的决策分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗等高风险领域需要大语言模型提供明确的决策依据（可溯源性），但现有的影响力函数方法在自回归模型中存在 token 独立性导致的误差问题，缺乏精确到 token 级别的溯源能力。

Method: 提出一种利用稀疏自编码器（Sparse Autoencoders）提取潜在特征，并通过雅可比向量积（Jacobian-vector products）和高效的逆海森近似（Inverse-Hessian approximations）计算 token 级影响力的框架。

Result: 实验表明该方法能够在医疗基准测试中识别出联合影响预测结果的稀疏且可解释的 token 集合，有效实现了模型的可审计性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于潜在中介（Latent Mediation）的方法，通过稀疏自编码器和雅可比向量积克服了现有影响函数在处理自回归模型时的局限性，能够实现更具解释性的 token 级可溯源分析，从而显著提升高风险领域对大模型的信任度。

Abstract: A critical step for reliable large language models (LLMs) use in healthcare is to attribute predictions to their training data, akin to a medical case study. This requires token-level precision: pinpointing not just which training examples influence a decision, but which tokens within them are responsible. While influence functions offer a principled framework for this, prior work is restricted to autoregressive settings and relies on an implicit assumption of token independence, rendering their identified influences unreliable. We introduce a flexible framework that infers token-level influence through a latent mediation approach for general prediction tasks. Our method attaches sparse autoencoders to any layer of a pretrained LLM to learn a basis of approximately independent latent features. Unlike prior methods where influence decomposes additively across tokens, influence computed over latent features is inherently non-decomposable. To address this, we introduce a novel method using Jacobian-vector products. Token-level influence is obtained by propagating latent attributions back to the input space via token activation patterns. We scale our approach using efficient inverse-Hessian approximations. Experiments on medical benchmarks show our approach identifies sparse, interpretable sets of tokens that jointly influence predictions. Our framework enhances trust and enables model auditing, generalizing to high-stakes domain requiring transparent and accountable decisions.

</details>


### [100] [AGOP as Explanation: From Feature Learning to Per-Sample Attribution in Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12816)
*Raj Kiran Gupta Katakam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将AGOP（平均梯度外积）引入模型可解释性领域，提出了一套高效、高精度的AGOP归因方法，在多个基准测试中超越了现有主流方法，并解决了传统方法（如GradCAM）的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AGOP在解释模型如何学习（Neural Feature Ansatz）方面已有研究，但尚不明确其是否能作为解释单一预测的后验属性归因（post-hoc attribution）工具。

Method: 提出AGOP-Weighted归因方法，通过将单样本梯度与训练集计算的AGOP矩阵（sqrt(diag(M) / max diag(M))）相乘来降噪并增强特征。同时定义了AGOP-Local和AGOP-Global（零推理成本）。使用XAI-TRIS和CLEVR-XAI两个基准测试进行验证。

Result: 在XAI-TRIS和CLEVR-XAI基准测试中，AGOP-Weighted在表现上显著优于集成梯度（IG），且AGOP-Global在零推理成本下实现了比IG更高的mIoU。此外，指出GradCAM在小分辨率下存在空间分辨率崩塌问题。

Conclusion: AGOP方法（特别是AGOP-Weighted和AGOP-Global）在属性归因方面表现优异，优于集成梯度（IG）和其他传统方法，且具有极高的计算效率。研究表明，即使在分类精度达到瓶颈后，AGOP相关矩阵的质量仍在持续提升。

Abstract: The Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) governs feature learning in neural networks: the Neural Feature Ansatz states that weight Gram matrices at each layer align with the corresponding AGOP matrices computed over the training distribution. We ask a complementary question: can this same quantity serve as a post-hoc attribution method for explaining individual predictions? We introduce AGOP-Weighted: a novel attribution method that multiplies the per-sample gradient by sqrt(diag(M) / max diag(M)), a training-distribution prior that suppresses gradient noise and amplifies consistently important pixels -- a combination not present in any prior attribution method. We formalise two companion variants -- AGOP-Local (per-sample gradient, equivalent to VanillaGrad) and AGOP-Global (diag(M) directly as a zero-cost saliency map) -- and implement an efficient training-time accumulation hook; AGOP-Global then requires zero inference cost (disk lookup) while AGOP-Weighted requires only a single gradient pass. We conduct the first rigorous comparison of AGOP attribution against Integrated Gradients (IG), SmoothGrad, GradCAM, and VanillaGrad across two benchmarks with pixel-level ground truth: (i) the synthetic XAI-TRIS benchmark (four classification scenarios, 8x8 images, CNN8by8) and (ii) the photorealistic CLEVR-XAI benchmark (ResNet-18 fine-tuned from ImageNet). AGOP-Weighted achieves 44% higher mIoU than IG on linear tasks; AGOP-Global achieves 7x higher mIoU than IG on multiplicative tasks (where IG falls below random) at zero inference cost. Both findings generalise to ResNet-18 on CLEVR-XAI (+18% and +37% respectively). We further show that GradCAM fails on small-resolution images due to spatial resolution collapse, and that diag(M) quality improves monotonically throughout training even after classification accuracy has plateaued.

</details>


### [101] [Training Large Language Models to Predict Clinical Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12817)
*Benjamin Turtel,Paul Wilczewski,Kris Skotheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用纵向临床病历通过“Foresight Learning”自动构建临床预测任务的方法，通过微调LoRA适配器，无需人工特征工程即可在多项临床预测指标上实现优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床病历蕴含患者随时间演变的丰富信息，但难以直接将其高效转化为临床预测任务的训练监督信号。

Method: 基于“Foresight Learning”框架，将MIMIC-III中的按时间排序的临床笔记转化为包含“过往病史上下文”、“关于未来事件的自然语言问题”及“由后续记录确定的标签”的训练样本；并使用LoRA适配器对基础模型进行微调。

Result: 在药物、手术、器官支持、微生物学和死亡率等领域生成了6,900个预测样本。微调后的LoRA模型性能显著优于仅使用提示词的基础模型（ECE从0.1269降至0.0398，Brier分数从0.199降至0.145），且在测试集上略优于GPT-5的点估计结果。

Conclusion: 该方法利用纵向临床记录实现了通用的临床预测监督，无需人工设计的结构化特征或特定任务的分类器，证明了通过自我监督方式从病历文本中提取预测监督信号的可行性。

Abstract: Longitudinal clinical notes contain rich evidence of how patients evolve over time, but converting this signal into training supervision for clinical prediction remains challenging. We extend Foresight Learning to clinical prediction by converting time-ordered MIMIC-III notes into examples consisting of past patient context, a natural-language question about a possible future event, and a label resolved from later documentation. This process yields 6,900 prediction examples from 702 admissions across medications, procedures, organ support, microbiology, and mortality. A small LoRA adapter trained on these examples improves over the prompted base model, reducing expected calibration error from 0.1269 to 0.0398 and Brier score from 0.199 to 0.145, while slightly outperforming GPT-5 point estimates on held-out questions. The approach enables reusable clinical prediction supervision from longitudinal notes without hand-engineered structured features or endpoint-specific classifiers.

</details>


### [102] [Hessian Matching for Machine-Learned Coarse-Grained Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12823)
*Sanya Murdeshwar,Sanjit Shashi,Kevin Bachelor,William Noid,Ashwin Lokapally,Razvan Marinescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合Hessian-向量积匹配的CG神经势训练框架，通过引入二阶曲率监督，有效提升了生物分子粗粒化仿真的动力学精度与可转移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于力匹配的粗粒化神经势函数仅捕捉了自由能面的梯度，导致动力学性质（特别是长时标的曲率信息）描述不足。

Method: 提出一种将力匹配（force matching）与随机Hessian-向量积（HVP）匹配相结合的框架，通过预计算投影全原子Hessian和利用随机探测向量构建无偏HVP估计器，在无需显式构建完整Hessian的情况下引入二阶曲率信息。

Result: 在9个未见过的快折叠蛋白基准测试中，8个蛋白在慢模态指标上优于普通力匹配方法；在最大蛋白质的最慢集体模态上，CG与参考分布之间的KL散度降低高达85%。

Conclusion: 引入二阶曲率信息（HVP匹配）是提高粗粒化（CG）分子动力学势函数准确性和可转移性的有效途径，能够显著改善生物分子仿真在长时标动力学上的表现。

Abstract: Coarse-grained (CG) molecular dynamics enables simulations of atomic systems such as biomolecules at timescales inaccessible to all-atom (AA) methods, but existing CG neural potentials trained via force matching capture only the gradient of the free-energy surface, leaving its curvature unconstrained. We introduce a framework that augments force matching with stochastic Hessian-vector product (HVP) matching, instilling second-order curvature information into CG potentials without constructing the full Hessian. We derive a decomposition of the target CG Hessian into a model-independent projected AA Hessian, precomputed once before training, and a model-dependent covariance correction computed online at negligible cost. We construct an unbiased stochastic estimator of the Hessian-matching objective by using random probe vectors. We evaluate our method by comparing against force matching on a benchmark of nine fast-folding proteins unseen during training. HVP matching outperforms plain force matching on 8 of 9 proteins on slow-mode metrics, with reductions of up to 85% in the Kullback--Leibler divergence between the CG and reference distributions along the slowest collective mode of the largest protein. Our results demonstrate that higher-order physical supervision is a practical path to more accurate and transferable CG potentials for biomolecular simulation.

</details>


### [103] [Orthrus: Memory-Efficient Parallel Token Generation via Dual-View Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12825)
*Chien Van Nguyen,Chaitra Hegde,Van Cuong Pham,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt,Thien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Orthrus 是一种统一的双架构框架，通过结合自回归 LLM 和并行扩散模型，在共享 KV 缓存的基础上实现了无损的并行加速推理，最高可提速 7.8 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归 LLM 推理存在严重的串行瓶颈，导致吞吐量低；而传统的扩散语言模型虽然能并行生成，但存在性能下降、训练成本高及收敛性缺乏保障的问题。旨在寻找一种兼顾高保真度和高并行效率的架构方案。

Method: 提出 Orthrus 框架，通过在冻结的 LLM 上增加一个轻量级、可训练的模块，构建自回归和扩散两种视角。利用共享的 KV 缓存（KV cache），自回归头负责上下文预填充，扩散头负责并行生成，并通过精确的一致性机制（consensus mechanism）确保推理的无损性。

Result: 实现了最高 7.8 倍的推理加速，同时保持了无损的生成精度，且仅需 O(1) 的内存缓存开销和极少的参数增加。

Conclusion: Orthrus 提出了一种创新的双架构框架，成功平衡了自回归 LLMs 的生成保真度与扩散模型的并行生成速度，为实现高效、无损的大模型推理提供了切实可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce Orthrus, a simple and efficient dual-architecture framework that unifies the exact generation fidelity of autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs) with the high-speed parallel token generation of diffusion models. The sequential nature of standard autoregressive decoding represents a fundamental bottleneck for high-throughput inference. While diffusion language models attempt to break this barrier via parallel generation, they suffer from significant performance degradation, high training costs, and a lack of rigorous convergence guarantees. Orthrus resolves this dichotomy natively. Designed to seamlessly integrate into existing Transformers, the framework augments a frozen LLM with a lightweight, trainable module to create a parallel diffusion view alongside the standard autoregressive view. In this unified system, both views attend to the exact same high-fidelity Key-Value (KV) cache; the autoregressive head executes context pre-filling to construct accurate KV representations, while the diffusion head executes parallel generation. By employing an exact consensus mechanism between the two views, Orthrus guarantees lossless inference, delivering up to a 7.8x speedup with only an O(1) memory cache overhead and minimal parameter additions.

</details>


### [104] [Quantifying Potential Observation Missingness in Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12831)
*Leo Benac,Abhishek Sharma,Alihan Huyuk,Finale Doshi-Velez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对逆强化学习中观测缺失导致奖励函数估计偏差的问题，本文提出了一种通过寻找最小观测扰动来重建专家最优性的方法，修正了观测不完整带来的评估误判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逆强化学习方法在处理实际行为数据时，往往忽略了“决策者可见信息”与“算法可见数据”之间的差异（缺失观测），导致算法误将理性的专家行为判定为次优行为，进而得出错误的奖励函数。

Method: 提出一种计算“使专家行为看似最优的最小观测扰动”的算法，利用逆向思维量化缺失观测信息对决策影响的程度。

Result: 通过合成导航任务、癌症治疗模拟器和ICU治疗数据集的实验，证明了该算法能够有效定量评估数据中缺失观测对决策过程的影响。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种有效的方法，通过寻找观测数据的最小扰动来验证专家决策的合理性，从而解决了逆强化学习中因观测数据不完整导致的奖励函数估计偏差问题。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL), which infers reward functions from demonstrations, is a valuable tool for modeling and understanding decision-making behavior. Many variants of IRL have been developed to capture complexities of human decision-making, such as subjective beliefs, imperfect planning, and dynamic goals. However, an often-overlooked issue in real-world behavioral datasets is that the recorded data may be missing observations that were available to the original decision-maker. In use-inspired settings such as healthcare, this can make expert actions appear suboptimal, even when they were near-optimal given the information available at the time. As a result, the rewards learned by standard IRL may be misleading. In this paper, we identify the minimal perturbations to the recorded observations needed for the expert's actions to appear optimal. We develop a practical algorithm for this problem and demonstrate its utility for quantifying the possible extent of missing observations in behavioral datasets through extensive experiments on synthetic navigation tasks, a cancer treatment simulator, and ICU treatment data.

</details>


### [105] [Discrete Stochastic Localization for Non-autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12836)
*Yunshu Wu,Jiayi Cheng,Longxuan Yu,Partha Thakuria,Rob Brekelmans,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Greg Ver Steeg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DSL通过改进连续扩散模型的表示方式，使其去噪器对信噪比不变，从而在不需蒸馏的情况下，提升了离散序列生成的质量并赋予模型更高的采样灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管连续扩散框架在非自回归生成中具有天然优势，但在离散序列生成任务中，其性能通常落后于掩码离散扩散模型（MDMs）。作者认为核心瓶颈在于现有的去噪过程过于依赖时间步索引的噪声机制。

Method: 引入“离散随机定位”（Discrete Stochastic Localization, DSL）：通过单元球（unit-sphere）词嵌入表示，使贝叶斯最优去噪器对信噪比（SNR）保持不变，允许模型支持多种SNR路径，包括掩码扩散路径。无需蒸馏或重训练，通过微调现有的MDLM检查点即可实现。

Result: 在OpenWebText数据集上，即使在不同的步数预算（T=128到T=1024）下，DSL均显著提升了分布保真度（MAUVE）。此外，该模型支持随机顺序自回归采样以及低至T=48步的连续到离散混合采样策略。

Conclusion: DSL框架通过解耦去噪过程对时间步的依赖，统一了连续扩散与掩码扩散模型，实现了在保持训练效率的同时显著提升离散序列生成的质量与灵活性（如支持随机顺序自回归采样等）。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion is a natural framework for non-autoregressive generation but has generally lagged behind masked discrete diffusion models (MDMs) on discrete sequence generation. We argue that the bottleneck is not continuity itself, but a representation in which denoising depends on timestep-indexed noise regimes. We introduce \emph{Discrete Stochastic Localization} (DSL), a continuous-state framework with unit-sphere token embeddings whose Bayes-optimal denoiser is invariant to the nominal signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) under the localization channel. One trained network then supports an entire family of per-token SNR paths, with endpoint masked-diffusion paths as a special case. Fine-tuning a pretrained MDLM checkpoint with DSL substantially improves distributional faithfulness (MAUVE) on OpenWebText across all step budgets from $T{=}128$ to $T{=}1024$, and the same checkpoint supports random-order autoregressive sampling, as well as a hybrid continuous-then-discrete sampler using as few as T=48 total steps -- without distillation or retraining.

</details>


### [106] [Multitask Multimodal Fusion with Tabular Foundation Models for Peak and Durability Prediction of Pertussis Booster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12852)
*Divya Sitani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专门用于处理多模态异质性缺失数据的新型对比学习框架，成功实现了对百日咳疫苗免疫应答中峰值强度与长期持久性的联合精准预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百日咳加强针的免疫应答在个体内存在显著差异，且其峰值（反映急性B细胞激活）与持久性（反映长期体液记忆）由不同的生物机制主导。由于数据样本少、模态异质且存在结构性缺失，现有计算模型难以同时准确捕捉这两个阶段的动态变化。

Method: 提出了一种多任务对比多模态融合架构：采用冻结的 TabPFN-v2 作为各模态编码器；设计了双标签监督对比损失函数（基于峰值或持久性标签的一致性构建正样本对）；结合了针对缺失数据校准的模态丢失策略以及缺失掩码注意力融合机制。

Result: 在 CMI-PB 数据集（n=158）上，该模型在峰值预测任务中实现了 0.797 的测试 AUROC，在持久性任务中实现了 0.755 的测试 AUROC，且两者均通过了置换检验。该模型是唯一一个在两个任务上同时显著优于随机猜测的模型。此外，分析发现细胞因子特征对峰值预测贡献更大，而基线抗体特征对持久性预测更为关键。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多任务对比多模态融合架构在预测百日咳加强针免疫应答的峰值强度和持久性方面表现出卓越的预测能力，优于传统基线模型，并成功揭示了免疫学上与这两项任务相关的特定生物模态贡献。

Abstract: Pertussis booster vaccination produces immune responses that vary widely across individuals in both peak magnitude and long-term durability. These two phases are governed by partly distinct biological compartments:peak reflects acute B-cell activation and antibody secretion, while durability reflects the establishment of long-term humoral memory. Yet most computational models target only one, missing the full boost-and-wane trajectory. Jointly predicting both is non-trivial because the two endpoints are biologically dissociated rather than redundant; samples are small, modalities are heterogeneous with structured missingness, and the two tasks rely on different measurement windows. We propose a multi-task contrastive multimodal fusion architecture combining frozen TabPFN-v2 per-modality encoders, a dual-label supervised contrastive loss that treats two subjects as a positive pair if they agree on the Task 1 label or the Task 2 label, modality dropout calibrated to empirical missingness, and missingness-masked attention fusion. Applied to a curated subset of the CMI-PB pertussis booster dataset (n = 158 subjects, four modalities, 44.9% with at least one modality missing; Spearman r = -0.58 between peak and durability, n = 96), the model achieves test AUROC 0.797 (95% CI [0.621, 0.948]) for peak response and 0.755 (95% CI [0.519, 0.945]) for durability, with both significant under joint label permutation (N = 1000; p = 0.002 and p = 0.045). Across logistic regression, XGBoost, and MLP baselines on raw features and on TabPFN embeddings, the proposed model is the only one whose 95% CIs lie above chance on both tasks simultaneously. Per-modality contribution analyses recover task-specific modality contributions consistent with the underlying immunology: peak prediction is carried by cytokine signatures, while durability is carried by baseline antibody features.

</details>


### [107] [Descriptive Collision in Sparse Autoencoder Auto-Interpretability: When One Explanation Describes Many Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12874)
*Jordan F. McCann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了SAE特征可解释性中普遍存在的“描述性碰撞”问题，即不同的语义特征被赋予相同的文本解释，导致解释失效。文章提出了区分性评分与新的修正指标，以解决现有自动评估忽略该问题从而高估可解释性的弊端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动可解释性流水线常为稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征生成自然语言解释，但研究发现存在“描述性碰撞”问题，即不同的特征会被赋予相同的文本解释，导致解释无法唯一标识特征，目前对此类问题的定量分析尚属空白。

Method: 通过重分析Marks等人（2025年）的人类标注数据集，定量研究单一解释描述对应多个不同SAE特征的频率；提出“区分性”（discrimination）概念；证明现有的检测式自动评分方法对碰撞不敏感，并引入了“碰撞调整检测”（collision-adjusted detection）和“区分性评分”（discrimination scoring）两种改进指标。

Result: 实证研究表明，82.1%的特征与其他特征共享同一标注，平均每个标注对应3.07个特征（如“复数名词”对应101个特征）；信息论分析显示，平均标注仅能消除70%的特征身份不确定性；忽略碰撞会导致可解释性报告指标被虚高夸大约三分之一。

Conclusion: 描述性碰撞（Descriptive Collision）是自动化可解释性评估中的一个独立失效模式。现有的评估方法忽略了这一现象，导致模型可解释性指标被高估。作者提出的纠正指标及区分性评分能更准确地衡量特征的可解释性。
