Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are now standard tools for decomposing language model activations into interpretable features, and automated interpretability pipelines routinely assign each feature a short natural-language explanation. Existing critiques of this practice focus on polysemanticity -- one feature with many meanings -- or on whether explanations predict activations. We identify a complementary, structurally distinct problem we call descriptive collision: many distinct SAE features admit the same explanation. Reanalyzing the largest publicly-available dataset of human-annotated SAE features (Marks et al., 2025), comprising 722 annotated features across Gemma 2 2B and Pythia 70M, we find that the mean annotation string is reused across 3.07 features; 82.1% of features share their annotation with at least one other feature; and the single most common annotation string ("plural nouns") labels 101 distinct features spanning 18 layers and four model components. Information-theoretically, the average annotation resolves only 70% of feature identity. We formalize a property called discrimination, prove that current detection-style auto-interpretability scoring is invariant to collision, and propose two complementary corrective metrics -- collision-adjusted detection and discrimination scoring -- that explicitly penalize explanations that fail to distinguish a feature from its neighbors. The collision problem is independent of, and additive with, previously identified failure modes of auto-interpretability; ignoring it inflates reported feature interpretability by a quantity equal to roughly one-third of the bits required to identify a feature.

</details>


### [108] [Certified Robustness under Heterogeneous Perturbations via Hybrid Randomized Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12876)
*Blaise Delattre,Hengyu Wu,Paul Caillon,Wei Yang Bryan Lim,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种统一的随机平滑框架，通过Neyman-Pearson引理解决了多模态模型中离散与连续输入并存时的鲁棒性认证难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机平滑认证方法仅针对单一模态（要么是连续的图像，要么是离散的文本），无法应对现代多模态模型中跨模态语义交互及针对异构输入的联合对抗攻击问题。

Method: 基于Neyman-Pearson引理的统一随机平滑框架，通过分析由离散噪声和连续噪声（因子分解）共同引起的联合似然排序，推导出了一个封闭形式的一维认证边界。

Result: 该方法在理论上严格推广了高斯平滑（针对图像）和离散平滑（针对文本）技术，并首次实现了针对图像-文本多模态安全过滤任务中联合扰动的模型无关认证。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的统一随机平滑框架，通过Neyman-Pearson引理实现了对离散（文本）和连续（图像）异构输入的联合鲁棒性认证，不仅在理论上统一了现有方法，还在多模态安全过滤场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing provides strong, model-agnostic robustness certificates, but existing guarantees are limited to single modalities, treating continuous and discrete inputs in isolation. This limitation becomes critical in multimodal models, where decisions depend on cross-modal semantics and adversaries can jointly perturb heterogeneous inputs, rendering unimodal certificates insufficient. We introduce a unified randomized smoothing framework for mixed discrete--continuous inputs based on an analytically tractable Neyman--Pearson formulation of the joint worst-case problem. By analyzing the joint likelihood ordering induced by factorized discrete and continuous noise, our approach yields a closed-form, one-dimensional certificate that strictly generalizes both Gaussian (image-only) and discrete (text-only) randomized smoothing. We validate the framework on multimodal safety filtering, providing, to our knowledge, the first model-agnostic Neyman--Pearson certificate for joint discrete-token and continuous-image perturbations in interaction-dependent text--image safety filtering.

</details>


### [109] [ASAP: Amortized Doubly-Stochastic Attention via Sliced Dual Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12879)
*Huy Tran,Max Milkert,David Hyde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASAP 是一种高效的训练后编译方法，成功将双重随机注意力的迭代推理过程转化为高效的固定算子操作，在维持精度的同时实现了显著的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双重随机注意力机制存在矛盾：Sinkhorn 训练高效但推理需迭代（性能瓶颈），而 Sliced-transport 虽然移除了在线迭代但训练极其耗费资源。ASAP 旨在结合两者的优点，降低推理成本。

Method: 提出 ASAP (Amortized Doubly-Stochastic Attention via Sliced Dual Projection)：训练阶段使用 Sinkhorn Scaling 以提高训练效率，推理阶段通过训练一个参数化映射（Sliced Dual Projection）来模拟 Sinkhorn 的双侧熵 c-变化，用固定的算子替代循环迭代。

Result: 在语言和视觉基准测试中表现出色，推理速度比 Sinkhorn Teacher 快 5.3 倍且精度保持一致；在下游替换任务中无需重新训练即可恢复大部分性能。

Conclusion: ASAP 提出了一种训练后编译的方法，通过学习参数化映射将 Sinkhorn 的迭代推理过程转化为固定操作，在保持与 Sinkhorn 相当的精度下，显著提升了推理速度（5.3倍），解决了双重随机注意力在推理时的计算瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Doubly-stochastic attention has emerged as a transport-based alternative to row-softmax attention, with recent Transformer variants using it to reduce attention sinks and rank collapse while improving performance. In this family, the standard approach is Sinkhorn scaling, which trains more efficiently but still repeats matrix scaling in every inference forward pass. Sliced-transport attention removes the online iteration, but its soft sorting approximation materializes dense tensors for each slice, requiring substantially more training resources than Sinkhorn attention. We introduce ASAP: Amortized Doubly-Stochastic Attention via Sliced Dual Projection, a train-then-compile method that trains the doubly-stochastic layer with Sinkhorn, then replaces the iterative scaling loop at inference with a fixed sliced-dual operator. It learns a lightweight parametric map from exact one-dimensional Kantorovich potentials to the Sinkhorn query-side dual, then reconstructs the attention plan with a two-sided entropic c-transform. Across language and vision benchmarks, ASAP keeps the cheaper training setup and remains highly competitive with recent baselines. In the main frozen-layer benchmark, ASAP is 5.3 faster than the trained Sinkhorn teacher while matching its accuracy; in downstream replacements, ASAP recovers most of the teacher performance without any retraining.

</details>


### [110] [VIP-COP: Context Optimization for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12904)
*Yilong Chen,Xueying Ding,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 VIP-COP 框架，通过高效评估并筛选表格数据中的核心样本与特征，克服了表格基础模型（TFMs）在处理大规模数据时的上下文长度限制，实现了对预测性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFMs）受限于上下文长度限制，在处理大规模数据时性能下降，且现有启发式方法无法有效过滤噪声或利用数据增强。需要一种能够优化上下文选择、剔除噪声并识别关键信息的方法。

Method: 提出 VIP-COP（Value of Importance for Prediction for hard Context OPtimization），基于在线 KernelSHAP 回归、迭代细化、价值引导的上下文采样及多保真度剪枝技术。该方法通过评估训练样本和特征对预测的重要性，显式选择最具影响力的“核心”数据子集。

Result: 在多项大规模高维数据集测试中，VIP-COP 在数据增强和噪声环境下的表现均优于现有的启发式和优化 baseline，且展现出良好的鲁棒性和可解释性。

Conclusion: VIP-COP 是一种高效、模型不可知且具有鲁棒性的测试时上下文优化框架，通过显式选择高价值数据和特征，显著优化了表格基础模型在处理大规模数据时的表现，设定了新的领域基准。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for in-context learning on structured data, enabling direct prediction on new tabular tasks without task-specific training. However, their effectiveness is constrained by context length limits, restricting application to medium-scale data and degrading performance when inference-time data exceed pretraining size distributions. Our work introduces VIP-COP, estimating the Value of Importance for Prediction of training examples and features for hard Context OPtimization for TFMs. Its explicit selection mechanism suppresses noise and isolates influential data, enabling the model to also benefit from data augmentation by prioritizing high-value augmented samples and features. VIP-COP is (i) fast, boosting performance often within minutes of optimization, based on an online KernelSHAP-based regression with iterative refinement, value-guided context sampling, and multi-fidelity pruning; (ii) budget-aware and any-time, improving with additional test-time compute unlike heuristics that produce fixed contexts; (iii) model-aware yet fully black-box, requiring no access to model internals, making it compatible with both proprietary and open-source TFMs; (iv) interpretable, identifying discrete ``Very Important Predictors'' (samples and features) that maximize signal-to-noise, which makes it (v) robust, isolating high-value data from noise. In contrast, soft-prompt optimization requires model gradients, produces abstract latent tokens, and lacks explicit signal discrimination. Extensive experiments show that VIP-COP consistently outperforms heuristic and optimized baselines across large-scale high-dimensional testbeds, including data augmentation and data-noise settings, establishing a new state of the art in test-time context refinement for TFMs.

</details>


### [111] [ERPPO: Entropy Regularization-based Proximal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13131)
*Changha Lee,Gyusang Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多智能体在复杂多维环境下的非平稳观测挑战，提出了 ERPPO 算法，通过引入 DSA 学习器量化感知不确定性，并利用自适应熵正则化动态调整策略更新，显著提升了在视觉干扰环境下的多智能体搜索与定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAPPO 在多维环境下因智能体观测的非平稳性导致无法提取最优策略，且在具有挑战性的目标定位任务中表现不佳。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ERPPO 的框架，包含两个核心模块：1. 分布时空模糊性 (DSA) 学习器，用于量化非平稳环境下的目标检测不确定性；2. 基于熵正则化的 PPO 改进策略，通过动态自适应权重（高模糊度采用 L1 正则化促进探索，低模糊度采用 L2 正则化保持稳定）来更新策略。

Result: 在基于 AirSim 的海上搜索测试中，ERPPO 在预测准确性上优于 MAPPO，且展示了更高的梯度优化效率及在视觉不确定条件下抑制误检的能力。

Conclusion: ERPPO 通过动态熵正则化有效缓解了高维环境下的非平稳性挑战，在海事搜索场景中显著提升了物体定位的准确性和搜索策略的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO) is a variant of the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm, specifically tailored for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). MAPPO optimizes cooperative multi-agent settings by employing a centralized critic with decentralized actors. However, in case of multi-dimensional environment, MAPPO can not extract optimal policy due to non-stationary agent observation. To overcome this problem, we introduce a novel approach, Entropy Regularization-based Proximal Policy Optimization (ERPPO). For the policy optimization, we first define the object detection ambiguity under multi-dimensional observation environment. Distributional Spatiotemporal Ambiguity (DSA) learner is trained to estimate object detection uncertainty in non-stationary constraints. Then, we enhance PPO with a novel Entropy Regularization term. This regularization dynamically adjusts the policy update by applying a stronger (L1) regularization in high-ambiguity observation to encourage significant exploratory actions and a weaker (L2) regularization in low-ambiguity observation to stabilize the proximal policy optimization. This approach is designed to enhance the probability of successful object localization in time-critical operations by reducing detection failures and optimizing search policy. Experiments on a testbed with AirSim-based maritime searching scenarios show that the proposed ERPPO improves accuracy performance. Our proposed method improves higher gradient than MAPPO. Qualitative results confirm that ERPPO effectiveness in terms of suppressing false detection in visually uncertain conditions.

</details>


### [112] [Data Difficulty and the Generalization--Extrapolation Tradeoff in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12906)
*Siyuan Liu,Tinghong Chen,Xinghan Li,Yifei Wang,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了SFT中数据难度选择与数据集规模之间的动态关系：最优难度并非固定，而是随着数据量的增加向更难的数据倾斜，研究并通过泛化差距与外推差距的权衡解释了这一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于SFT中数据选择（如困惑度、难度、长度等启发式方法）的研究结论往往不一致且具有上下文依赖性。该研究旨在系统性地探究数据难度在SFT中的作用，并理清数据量与数据难度如何共同影响模型表现。

Method: 结合实证研究与理论分析：利用受控的合成实验观察数据规模与难度对模型性能的影响；利用PAC-Bayesian泛化界进行理论建模与分析，探究泛化与外推之间的权衡。

Result: 研究发现不存在“绝对最优”的数据难度；在固定数据预算下，存在一个最优难度等级；当可用数据量增加时，模型倾向于从更难的数据中获益。这一发现为针对特定模型和数据条件下的难度导向数据选择提供了明确的指导。

Conclusion: 数据难度在SFT中的有效性并非一成不变，而是取决于数据集规模。随着数据预算的增加，最优的SFT数据难度会趋向于更难的数据。该研究揭示了分布内泛化差距（generalization gap）与外推差距（extrapolation gap）之间的相互作用是这一现象的底层机制。

Abstract: Data selection during supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can critically change the behavior of large language models (LLMs). Although existing work has studied the effect of selecting data based on heuristics such as perplexity, difficulty, or length, the reported findings are often inconsistent or context-dependent. In this work, we systematically study the role of data difficulty in fine-tuning from both empirical and theoretical perspectives, and find that there is no universally optimal difficulty level; rather, its effectiveness depends on the dataset size. We show that for a fixed data budget, there exists an optimal data difficulty for SFT, and that this optimal difficulty shifts toward harder data as the data budget increases. To explain this phenomenon, we conduct controlled synthetic experiments that reveal a simple underlying mechanism: the interplay between the (in-distribution) generalization gap and the extrapolation gap. We further support this mechanism through a theoretical analysis using PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds. Overall, our results clarify how data size and difficulty jointly affect the trade-off between generalization and extrapolation in SFT, providing guidance for difficulty-based data selection under certain model and data conditions.

</details>


### [113] [Revisiting DAgger in the Era of LLM-Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12913)
*Changhao Li,Rushi Qiang,Jiawei Huang,Chenxiao Gao,Chao Zhang,Niao He,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长视野LM智能体的训练困境，本研究引入DAgger方法，通过将教师引导与在线交互相结合，实现了密集监督与环境适应性的平衡，在软件工程智能体任务中取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视野LM智能体训练方法存在局限性：监督微调虽提供密集反馈但存在协变量偏移问题；强化学习虽能处理在线交互但反馈信号过于稀疏。两者均无法完美解决长任务中早期错误导致后续偏离的问题。

Method: 提出一种适用于多轮任务的DAgger框架：在训练过程中，根据设定的概率动态插值学生策略与教师策略来收集轨迹，并利用教师提供的监督标签对学生模型进行训练，从而实现从在线交互中获取密集监督信号。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified数据集上，该方法使4B和8B参数规模的模型性能分别提升了3.9和3.6个百分点；4B模型性能超越了已发布的8B系统，8B模型则逼近了部分32B量级系统的表现。

Conclusion: 将DAgger应用于长视野语言模型（LM）智能体是解决离线监督微调（协变量偏移）与强化学习（稀疏反馈）困境的有效方案，能显著提升智能体在复杂环境中的执行能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon LM agents learn from multi-turn interaction, where a single early mistake can alter the subsequent state distribution and derail the whole trajectory. Existing recipes fall short in complementary ways: supervised fine-tuning provides dense teacher supervision but suffers from covariate shift because it is trained on off-policy teacher trajectories; while reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards avoids this off-policy mismatch by learning from on-policy rollouts but with only sparse outcome feedback. We address this dilemma by revisiting Dataset Aggregation (DAgger) for multi-turn LM agents: the algorithm collects trajectories through a turn-level interpolation of student and teacher policies, and the student is then trained on these trajectories using supervised labels provided by the teacher. By directly interacting with environments, we expose the model to realistic states likely to be encountered during deployment, thereby effectively mitigating covariate shift. Besides, since the student is learned by mimicking the teacher's behavior, it receives rich feedback during learning. To demonstrate DAgger enjoys the benefits of both worlds, we tested the algorithm to train a software-engineering agent with 4B- and 8B-scale student models. On SWE-bench Verified, our DAgger-style training improves over the strongest post-training baseline by +3.9 points at 4B and +3.6 points at 8B. The resulting 4B agent reaches 27.3%, outperforming representative published 8B SWE-agent systems, while the 8B agent achieves 29.8%, surpassing SWE-Gym-32B and coming within 5 points of stronger 32B-scale agents. Together with consistent gains on the held-out SWE-Gym split, these results suggest the effectiveness of DAgger for modern long-horizon LM agents.

</details>


### [114] [IV-ICL: Bounding Causal Effects with Instrumental Variables via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12924)
*Vahid Balazadeh,Hamidreza Kamkari,Medha Barath,Ricardo Silva,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对工具变量偏识别难题，本文提出 IV-ICL 方法，利用包含式 KL 散度优化和上下文学习（in-context learning）技术，实现了高效、准确的因果效应区间估计，并引入了基于 RCT 的新型基准测试评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具变量（IV）偏识别方法存在两大瓶颈：一是依赖复杂的手动导出封闭形式解（如 Balke-Pearl 方程），二是传统的贝叶斯变分推理（最小化排他性 KL 散度）容易导致后验塌陷，无法准确覆盖整个识别集，且计算成本高。

Method: 提出 IV-ICL（Amortized Bayesian In-Context Learning）方法：将因果推断建模为元学习问题，通过优化包含式（inclusive）KL 散度来学习因果效应的边际后验分布，并以分位数形式导出识别界；同时构建了将 RCT 数据转换为具有真实因果效应的 IV 基准测试程序。

Result: IV-ICL 在合成和半合成数据集上表现优异，生成的置信区间比现有半参数、贝叶斯及插件式基准方法更可靠且信息量更大，推理速度提升了 20-500 倍。

Conclusion: IV-ICL 通过元学习（in-context learning）框架，利用包含式 KL 散度最小化策略，解决了因果推断中偏识别（partial identification）的局限性，实现了比传统变分推理更准确的区间估计和更快的推理速度。

Abstract: The instrumental-variables (IV) setting is standard for partial identification of causal effects when unobserved confounding makes point identification impossible. Existing approaches face methodological bottlenecks: closed-form bound estimands are required -- e.g., Balke-Pearl equations in binary IV -- and even when available, designing accurate estimators requires manual effort tailored to each estimand. While direct Bayesian inference of the causal effects, instead of the bounds, circumvents these challenges, it is often computationally intensive and suffers from high prior sensitivity or under-dispersed posteriors. As a remedy, we introduce IV-ICL, an amortized Bayesian in-context learning method that learns the marginal posterior distribution of the causal effects directly and derives bounds as its quantiles. Unlike standard variational inference that optimizes exclusive KL divergence, amortized Bayesian inference minimizes the expected inclusive KL, a mass-covering objective. We empirically observe that optimizing inclusive KL can recover the entire identified set across diverse data-generating processes, while exclusive-KL (e.g. with variational inference) of the same Bayesian formulation collapses onto a single mode and fails to cover the identified set. We evaluate IV-ICL on synthetic and semi-synthetic IV benchmarks and show it produces intervals that are more reliably valid and more informative compared to efficient semi-parametric, Bayesian, and plug-in baselines, at 20-500x lower inference time. Beyond methodology, we propose a procedure to convert randomized controlled trials into IV benchmarks with provably preserved ground-truth causal effects that enables a more realistic evaluation of partial-identification methods.

</details>


### [115] [The Efficiency Gap in Byte Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12928)
*Celine Lee,Jing Nathan Yan,Chen Liang,Jiaxin Shi,Yin Zhang,Jeremiah Liu,Pengcheng Yin,Fernando Pereira,Ed Chi,Derek Cheng,Alexander M. Rush,Ruoxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究分析了字节级建模在非自回归扩散模型中面临的扩展困境，指出MDM在处理原始字节时因缺乏局部连续性而面临更大的性能惩罚，并提出未来模型需引入新的结构化诱导偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨摆脱子词分词（Subword Tokenization）和自回归顺序建模（AR）这类传统先验后，采用字节级建模（Byte-level）和掩码扩散建模（MDM）所带来的计算代价与性能损耗。

Method: 通过受控的计算匹配扩展研究（compute-matched scaling study）和置换实验（permutation experiments），系统比较了自回归模型（AR）与掩码扩散模型（MDM）在字节级别建模下的性能差异。

Result: 字节建模的性能惩罚不均匀，MDM在扩展过程中的开销显著高于AR。原因在于MDM的目标函数破坏了原始字节中的局部连续性，导致模型难以高效解析语义。

Conclusion: 在字节级别模型中，直接去除结构化先验会导致性能和计算效率下降，且MDM比AR模型受到的“上下文脆弱性”影响更大。未来的通用模型开发需要引入替代的结构化偏差，以维持有效的规模化扩展轨迹。

Abstract: Modern language models have historically relied on two dominant design choices: subword tokenization and autoregressive (AR) ordering. These design decisions bake in priors that dictate a model's learning. Recently, two alternative paradigms have challenged this: byte-level modeling, which bypasses static statistically-derived token vocabularies, and masked diffusion modeling (MDM), which conducts parallel, non-sequential generation. Their intersection represents a fully end-to-end modality-agnostic generative prototype; however, removing these structural priors incurs a significant computational cost. In this work, we investigate this cost through a compute-matched scaling study. Our results reveal that the performance penalty of byte modeling is not uniform; across scale, the scaling overhead of byte modeling is worse for MDM than for AR. We hypothesize that this disparity stems from context fragility: while AR's stable causal history allows models to naturally rediscover subword patterns, the MDM objective destroys the local contiguity required to efficiently resolve semantics from raw bytes. Our findings from controlled permutation experiments suggest that future modality-agnostic designs must incorporate alternative structural biases to maintain viable scaling trajectories in the byte regime.

</details>


### [116] [The Expressivity Boundary of Probabilistic Circuits: A Comparison with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12940)
*Zhiyu Zhao,Xuejie Liu,Muhan Zhang,Anji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从输出层参数化缺失和固定路由结构的上下文编码瓶颈两个维度，深度剖析了概率回路（PCs）在语言建模任务中落后于Transformer的原因，并指出了通往更强表达能力的理论路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨为何概率回路（PCs）在自回归语言建模中性能不及Transformer，并识别其在表达能力方面的关键瓶颈。

Method: 在统一的自回归框架下，通过理论证明与实证分析，对比PCs与Transformer在输出层（概率空间与对数空间）和上下文编码（分层结构与分离秩）上的差异，并分析不同PC变体的表达能力。

Result: PCs在概率空间的预测方式限制了其刻画尖锐分布的能力（通过对数空间参数化可解决）；结构可分解性（Structured-decomposable）由于受限于固定的路由结构，难以适应异构依赖关系，而可分解PCs（Decomposable PCs）虽然表达能力更强，但在优化上仍面临巨大挑战。

Conclusion: 概率回路（PCs）在语言建模中的局限性主要源于其输出层的凸组合参数化以及上下文编码的结构约束。通过切换到对数概率空间并放宽结构限制（如使用可分解PC），有望缩小与Transformer的差距。

Abstract: Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) are deep generative models that support exact and efficient probabilistic inference. Yet in autoregressive language modeling, PCs still lag behind Transformer-based large language models (LLMs), suggesting an important expressivity gap. In this work, we compare PCs and LLMs under a unified autoregressive formulation. First, an output bottleneck: PCs parameterize predictions as convex combinations in probability space, which struggles to represent the sharp distributions typical of language; adopting a logit-space parameterization substantially narrows this gap. Second, a context-encoding bottleneck: we prove that structured-decomposable PCs can match Transformer separation rank on vtree-aligned partitions, but show, both theoretically and empirically, that this capacity is limited to partitions aligned with the fixed routing structure, leading to severe degradation when the data exhibits heterogeneous dependency topologies. We further prove that decomposable PCs are strictly more expressive than structured-decomposable ones, though effectively optimizing them remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [117] [From Instance Selection to Fixed-Pool Data Recipe Search for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12944)
*Haodong Wu,Jiahao Zhang,Lijie Hu,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决SFT数据选择局限于单一实例筛选的问题，该论文提出了AutoSelection框架，通过搜索由多种算子组成的“数据配方”而非单一评分，高效构建高质量训练子集，实现了比全量训练及传统筛选方法更优的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SFT数据选择通常局限于单一的实例排序（Top-k），忽略了各种筛选、混合及去重算子组合对数据分布的全局影响。研究旨在解决如何在有限计算预算下，通过自动搜索组合算子来构建更高质量的SFT训练集，而非依赖数据生成或增强。

Method: 提出了AutoSelection，一个双层求解器：1. 利用缓存的任务、数据和模型特征进行固定池物化（Materialization）；2. 采用预热探测、局部配方编辑、高斯过程辅助排序及停滞重种子（Reseeding）机制来搜索最优配方，从而在有限的全量微调评估预算下发现高质量数据子集。

Result: 在90K指令池上的实验表明，AutoSelection在三款基座模型上的推理表现显著优于全量数据训练、随机搜索、随机Top-k及单一算子选择器，并证实了配方结构在跨分布迁移和模型规格扩展中的核心作用。

Conclusion: SFT数据的选择不仅仅是简单的实例排序，通过组合式算子构建的“数据配方”对模型性能至关重要。AutoSelection提供了一种高效的自动化框架来发现最优数据配方，证明了数据配方的结构化设计优于盲目的实例筛选。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data selection is commonly formulated as instance ranking: score each example and retain a top-$k$ subset. However, effective SFT training subsets are often produced through ordered curation recipes, where filtering, mixing, and deduplication operators jointly shape the final data distribution. We formulate this problem as fixed-pool data recipe search: given a raw instruction pool and a library of grounded operators, the goal is to discover an executable recipe that constructs a high-quality selected subset under a limited budget of full SFT evaluations, without generating, rewriting, or augmenting training samples. We introduce AutoSelection, a two-layer solver that decouples fixed-pool materialization based on cached task-, data-, and model-side signals from expensive full evaluation, using warmup probes, realized subset states, local recipe edits, Gaussian-process-assisted ranking, and stagnation-triggered reseeding. Experiments on a 90K instruction pool show that AutoSelection achieves the strongest in-distribution reasoning average across three base models, outperforming full-data training, random recipe search, random top-$k$, and single-operator selectors. Additional Out-of-distribution graph-reasoning results, search-stability analyses, structural ablations, and 1.5B-to-7B transfer checks further show that recipe structure matters beyond individual selection operators. Code is available at https://github.com/w253/AutoSelection.

</details>


### [118] [Separating Shortcut Transition from Cross-Family OOD Failure in a Minimal Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12945)
*Hongmin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论建模揭示了OOD失效的复杂机制：模型对捷径的利用取决于训练信号强度，而这种利用是否导致失效取决于测试集分布与训练集分布的结构化差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将“捷径特征”作为解释OOD失效的通用原因，但训练时的相关性和测试时的失效并非一一对应。研究旨在通过理论模型解构模型学习捷径的过程，并量化不同评估环境下OOD失效的边界条件。

Method: 构建包含一个不变特征（invariant）和一个族群依赖型捷径特征（shortcut）的最小二乘逻辑回归（ridge-logistic ERM）模型，分析在不同正则化强度、噪声水平及分布波动下的分类器权重演化与预测误差。

Result: 研究发现：1. 脊正则化（ridge regularization）有助于缓解确定性设置下的捷径依赖；2. 当不变特征存在噪声时，模型会因捷径信号占优而发生“规则转换”；3. 该转换对OOD性能的影响高度依赖于保持数据（held-out family）的特性（如相关性强弱或标签符号是否翻转）。

Conclusion: 该研究明确区分了捷径吸引、捷径规则转换和跨族群OOD失效之间的关系，证明了训练中的捷径使用并不必然导致所有测试环境下的OOD失败，其后果取决于测试数据的分布特征。

Abstract: Shortcut features are often invoked to explain out-of-distribution (OOD) failure, but training correlation, learned shortcut use, and test-time failure need not coincide. We study a minimal binary model with one invariant coordinate and one family-dependent shortcut coordinate. In the deterministic regime, positive average shortcut correlation pulls logistic ERM toward positive shortcut weight, but ridge regularization keeps the classifier invariant-dominated and prevents deterministic OOD failure. When the invariant coordinate is noisy, ridge-logistic ERM switches to the shortcut rule once the training shortcut signal exceeds the invariant signal. Whether that transition causes failure depends on the held-out family: weaker shortcut correlation yields positive excess risk, and sign-flipped families yield above-chance error. Synthetic checks match these analytic regimes and show that the same training-side transition can have different held-out consequences. The model separates shortcut attraction, shortcut-rule transition, and cross-family OOD failure.

</details>


### [119] [U-HNO: A U-shaped Hybrid Neural Operator with Sparse-Point Adaptive Routing for Non-stationary PDE Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12965)
*Yingzhe Ma,Xiao Yang,Yuxin Xie,Zihan Xiong,Jinliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有神经算子难以兼顾 PDE 中“全局平滑”与“局部突变”特征的矛盾，本文提出 U-HNO 架构，通过空间自适应路由（SPAR）动态分配全局与局部计算资源，有效提升了计算精度与长时间模拟的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子存在局限性：傅里叶架构擅长全局建模但难以解析局部尖锐特征；局部架构善于刻画细节但缺乏长程关联及长时间 Rollout 稳定性。现有的固定混合方案强制在全局空间使用统一权衡，无法满足 PDE 中“平滑背景与局部突变共存”的需求。

Method: 提出了 U-HNO（U-shaped Hybrid Neural Operator）架构，核心创新为“稀疏点自适应路由”（SPAR）；SPAR 根据局部对比度动态决定各像素位置应由全局傅里叶分支还是局部多尺度高斯分支主导；采用分层式 Encoder-Bottleneck-Decoder 结构，并结合点向监督、H^1 梯度项及频带一致性正则化进行训练。

Result: 在 1D Burgers、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、KdV、2D/3D Navier-Stokes、Darcy Flow 等多项 PDEBench 任务中，U-HNO 在相对 L^2 和 H^1 指标上均达到 SOTA，尤其在含有剧烈局部突变特征的问题上提升显著。消融实验验证了所提各模块的必要性。

Conclusion: U-HNO 通过引入空间自适应路由机制，成功克服了傅里叶算子与局部算子在处理复杂 PDE 时存在的矛盾，在保持长程传播稳定性的同时显著提升了对局部剧烈变化特征的捕捉能力，在多项 PDE 基准测试中实现了 SOTA 性能。

Abstract: Solutions to many partial differential equations (PDEs) display coexisting smooth global transport and localized sharp features within a single trajectory: shock fronts, thin interfaces, and concentrated high-frequency content sit on top of slowly varying backgrounds. This poses a challenge for neural operators: Fourier-based architectures mix nonlocal interactions efficiently but tend to under-resolve localized non-smooth features, whereas spatially local architectures recover fine detail at the cost of long-range propagation and rollout stability. Existing hybrid operators paper over this tension with a fixed, spatially uniform fusion that forces the same trade-off everywhere.
  We propose U-HNO, a U-shaped hybrid neural operator whose central design is Sparse-Point Adaptive Routing (SPAR): at every spatial location, a per-pixel hard mask selects whether the global Fourier branch or the local multi-scale Gaussian branch should dominate, and the sparsity ratio is a function of the local contrast of the routing signal, so smooth and shock-aligned regions receive different mixtures of global and local computation. SPAR is embedded in a hierarchical encoder-bottleneck-decoder backbone with skip connections so that the dual branches and the gate operate at every resolution. Training combines pointwise supervision with a finite-difference H^1 gradient term and a band-wise spectral consistency regularizer.
  Across benchmarks spanning 1D Burgers, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky, KdV, 2D advection, Allen-Cahn, Navier-Stokes, Darcy flow, and 3D transonic compressible Navier-Stokes from PDEBench, U-HNO achieves state-of-the-art rollout accuracy on the majority of tasks in both relative L^2 and H^1 metrics, with the largest gains on problems dominated by sharp localized features. Ablations show that removing any single component substantially degrades rollout error.

</details>


### [120] [Controlling Logical Collapse in LLMs via Algebraic Ontology Projection over F2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12968)
*Hisashi Miyashita,Mgnite Inc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示了LLM内部存在可预测的代数逻辑结构；通过代数本体投影（AOP）技术，研究者能够在无需微调的情况下，利用少量代数键精准提取模型的逻辑本体关系，并证实了模型前向计算本质上是一个代数组织过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型内部是否编码了可形式化验证的代数结构，以及这种结构如何影响模型的逻辑推理能力。

Method: 提出了“代数本体投影”（AOP）：将LLM隐藏层状态投影到有限域 $F_2$ 中，并结合里氏替换原则（LSP）约束；引入“语义结晶”（SC）指标用于量化约束满足度并预测零样本准确率；通过系统提示词作为代数边界条件来研究层级行为。

Result: 在无需模型微调的情况下，AOP在未见概念对上实现了高达93.33%的零样本包含准确率（Gemma-2 Instruct）；观察到明显的“末层坍塌”现象（Late-layer Collapse），即模型最终层逻辑一致性下降，而系统提示词与指令微调的结合能有效抑制该现象。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的推理过程可以被重新构想为一种代数组织过程；通过代数投影，LLM内部的逻辑结构变得可形式化验证，从而为构建逻辑结构透明可控的模型提供了新路径。

Abstract: Do large language models internally encode ontological relations in a formally verifiable algebraic structure? We introduce Algebraic Ontology Projection (AOP), which projects LLM hidden states into the Galois Field F2 under Liskov Substitution Principle constraints, using only 42 relational pairs as algebraic keys. AOP achieves up to 93.33% zero-shot inclusion accuracy on unseen concept pairs (Gemma-2 Instruct with optimized prompt), with consistent 86.67% accuracy observed across multiple model families -- with no model tuning, but through prompt alone.
  This algebraic structure is strongly layer-dependent. We introduce Semantic Crystallisation (SC), a metric that quantifies F2 constraint satisfaction relative to a random baseline and predicts zero-shot accuracy without held-out data. System prompts act as algebraic boundary conditions: only their combination with instruction tuning prevents Late-layer Collapse -- a systematic degradation of logical consistency in the final layers, observed in 7 of 10 conditions. These findings reframe forward computation as an iterative process of algebraic organisation, and open a path toward LLMs whose logical structure is not merely approximated, but formally accessible.

</details>


### [121] [Revisiting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards from a Contrastive Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12969)
*Feng Zhang,Xinhong Ma,Ziqiang Dong,Xi Leng,Jianfei Zhao,Xin Sun,Yang Yang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 GRPO 在强化学习验证推理（RLVR）中的局限性，本文提出了 ConSPO 算法，通过对比学习范式和似然性优化，显著提升了 LLM 在复杂推理任务中的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出 GRPO 算法存在“似然性错位评分”（likelihood-misaligned scoring）和“对分数不敏感的信用分配”（score-insensitive credit assignment）两个结构性缺陷，导致其优化效率与效果受限。

Method: 1. 提出了 ConSPO 框架，将 GRPO 的剪切比率得分替换为长度归一化的序列对数概率（likelihood-based scoring）。
2. 构建了基于 InfoNCE 的对比学习目标，增强对正负样本相对分数的信用分配。
3. 引入了课程调度边距（curriculum-scheduled margin）机制，根据训练阶段动态调整优化难度。

Result: 在多种骨干模型、不同参数规模及训练数据集下，ConSPO 在多项挑战性数学推理基准测试中均显著优于 GRPO 等主流 RLVR 基线算法。

Conclusion: ConSPO 通过引入基于对比学习的优化目标和课程边距机制，解决了 GRPO 在评分对齐与信用分配方面的结构性缺陷，在数学推理任务中实现了比 GRPO 更优的性能。

Abstract: RLVR has become a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLMs' reasoning capabilities, and GRPO is one of its most representative algorithms. In this paper, we first show that GRPO admits an equivalent discriminative reformulation as a weighted positive-negative score difference. Under this view, GRPO increases sequence-level scores of verified positive rollouts and decreases those of negative rollouts, where the scores are averages of clipped token-level importance sampling ratios. This reformulation reveals two structural limitations of GRPO: likelihood-misaligned scoring, where clipped ratio-based surrogate scores are optimized instead of generation likelihoods, and score-insensitive credit assignment, where rollout-level credit is assigned without accounting for relative score gaps between positive and negative rollouts in the same group. To address these limitations, we propose ConSPO, a framework for Contrastive Sequence-level Policy Optimization in RLVR. ConSPO replaces GRPO's clipped ratio-based scores with length-normalized sequence log-probabilities, aligning the optimized rollout scores with the likelihoods used in autoregressive generation. It then optimizes a group-wise InfoNCE-style objective that contrasts each positive rollout against negative distractors from the same group, enabling credit assignment to depend on their relative scores. This contrastive formulation amplifies updates for poorly separated positives while concentrating suppressive updates on high-scoring negatives. Moreover, ConSPO introduces a curriculum-scheduled margin, guiding optimization from coarse positive-negative ordering in early training toward stronger separation in later stages. Extensive evaluations across diverse backbone models, parameter scales, and training datasets show that ConSPO consistently outperforms several strong RLVR baselines on challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [122] [CoRe-Gen: Robust Spectrum-to-Structure Generation under Imperfect Fingerprint Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12980)
*Tianbo Liu,Chixiang Lu,Jing Hao,Hengyu Zhang,Lifei Wang,Haibo Jiang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决 MS/MS 分子生成中预测指纹噪声导致的误差传播问题，CoRe-Gen 通过合成预训练、噪声模拟训练及结构强约束自回归解码，实现了更稳健、准确的从头分子结构还原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在串联质谱（MS/MS）分子结构从头生成中，现有的“谱图→指纹→结构”两阶段方法存在“条件失配”问题——解码器在训练时使用准确的指纹，但在推理时依赖有噪声的预测指纹，导致误差传播，尤其影响长尾子结构的生成。

Method: 1. 编码器优化：利用合成谱图进行预训练；2. 解码器训练：引入频率感知指纹损坏（frequency-aware fingerprint corruption）来模拟推理时的预测噪声；3. 解码机制：采用结构感知自回归解码，结合 SELFIES 表示、辅助结构监督和轻量级化学约束以提高生成准确性。

Result: CoRe-Gen 在 NPLIB1 基准测试上达到 19.54% 的 Top-1 和 29.92% 的 Top-10 精确匹配准确率，刷新了 SOTA，并保持了在大规模 MassSpecGym 基准测试上的竞争力。

Conclusion: CoRe-Gen 通过一系列针对性的技术改进（合成预训练、鲁棒性解码训练、结构化自回归生成等），有效缓解了串联质谱从谱图到分子重建过程中的“条件失配”问题，在 NPLIB1 等基准测试中实现了性能提升，提供了一种高效、稳健的从头生成方案。

Abstract: Molecular structure elucidation from tandem mass spectra (MS/MS) remains challenging, particularly for de novo generation beyond database coverage. A common approach decomposes the task into spectrum-to-fingerprint prediction followed by fingerprint-to-structure decoding, enabling the use of large-scale molecular corpora. However, at deployment, the decoder relies on predicted rather than oracle fingerprints, introducing structured errors that propagate into generation. This results in a fundamental condition mismatch, where models trained on clean inputs must operate under noisy, biased predictions, especially for long-tail substructures.
  We present CoRe-Gen that explicitly addresses this gap. CoRe-Gen improves the intermediate condition via synthetic-spectrum pretraining of the encoder, matches deployment-time noise through frequency-aware fingerprint corruption during decoder training, and mitigates residual errors using structure-aware autoregressive decoding with compositional SELFIES representations, auxiliary structural supervision, and lightweight chemical constraints. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that CoRe-Gen establishes a new state of the art on NPLIB1, achieving 19.54\% Top-1 and 29.92\% Top-10 exact-match accuracy, while remaining competitive on the more challenging MassSpecGym benchmark. Importantly, CoRe-Gen preserves the efficiency advantages of autoregressive decoding, providing a practical and scalable solution for robust spectrum-to-structure generation under realistic conditions.

</details>


### [123] [Decision Tree Learning on Product Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12983)
*Arshia Soltani Moakahr,Faraz Ghahremani,Kiarash Banihashem,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将贪心决策树学习的理论保证推广至任意乘积分布，给出了该算法可构建 $ε$-近似树的紧凑尺寸界限，并提出了一种实用的参数无关算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自顶向下贪心算法在实践中非常成功，但其理论分析长期有限。现有研究多局限于均匀分布，缺乏在更通用、更符合实际的乘积分布下的理论支持。

Method: 分析了自顶向下贪心启发式算法，在乘积分布假设下，证明了该算法构建的 $\epsilon$-近似树的大小与最优树的平均深度 ($Δ_{opt}$) 和最大深度 ($D_{opt}$) 的指数关系，并设计了无需预知最优树尺寸或深度的参数无关算法。

Result: 证得在任意乘积分布下，对于由尺寸 $s$、最大深度 $D_{opt}$、平均深度 $Δ_{opt}$ 的最优决策树计算的函数 $f$，$ε$-近似树的大小受限为 $\exp\bigl(Δ_{opt} D_{opt} \log(e/ε)\bigr)$；并在特殊情况下优于现有研究结果，且算法具备参数无关的实用优势。

Conclusion: 本文将自顶向下贪心决策树学习算法的理论保证扩展到了更广泛的任意乘积分布（product distributions），并提出了一个无需先验参数的算法，改进了现有理论边界。

Abstract: Decision tree learning has long been a central topic in theoretical computer science, driven by its practical importance. A fundamental and widely used method for decision tree construction is the top-down greedy heuristic, which recursively splits on the most influential variable. Despite its empirical success, theoretical analysis of this heuristic has been limited. A recent breakthrough by Blanc et al. (ITCS, 2020) provided the first rigorous theoretical guarantees for the greedy approach, but only under the uniform distribution. We extend this analysis to the more general and practically relevant setting of arbitrary product distributions. Our main result shows that for any function $f$ computable by an optimal decision tree of size $s$, maximum depth $D_{\text{opt}}$, and average depth $Δ_{\text{opt}}$, the greedy heuristic constructs an $ε$-approximating tree whose size grows at most with $\exp\bigl(Δ_{\text{opt}} D_{\text{opt}} \log(e/ε)\bigr)$. In the special case where the optimal tree is a full binary tree, this bound improves upon the bound of Blanc et al. and holds under a strictly broader class of distributions. Moreover, we present an algorithm based on the top-down greedy heuristic that is entirely parameter-free -- it requires no prior knowledge of the optimal tree's size or depth -- offering a practical advantage over Blanc et al.'s method.

</details>


### [124] [Not Just RLHF: Why Alignment Alone Won't Fix Multi-Agent Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12991)
*Adarsh Kumarappan,Ananya Mujoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM面对同行压力时的“随波逐流”现象并非因RLHF引起的谄媚，而是模型内在的计算缺陷。该问题无法简单通过提示词解决，必须在流水线架构上进行防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨LLM多智能体流水线中，在模拟同行异议下回答从正确转为错误的现象（Yield），并验证这一现象是否如通常所认为是由RLHF导致的谄媚引起的。

Method: 1. 跨4个模型家族对比基础模型（Base）与指令微调模型（Instruct）；2. 使用激活修补（Activation Patching）定位受影响的模型层；3. 分解攻击面因子（频道框架与共识强度）；4. 通过分析激活空间干扰模型表现。

Result: 1. 基础模型表现出与指令微调模型相似的脆弱性；2. 该漏洞定位在模型中层的窄窗口，主要由注意力机制主导；3. 压力会导致“正确推理特征”被抑制，而非触发新的谄媚机制；4. 单个辩论者即可显著降低正确率，且现有提示词防御手段效果有限。

Conclusion: 模型对同行异议的脆弱性并非由RLHF引起的谄媚引起，而是源于模型内部的计算机制。应通过流水线层面的结构化异议设计（而非简单的提示词防御）来缓解该问题。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent pipelines flip from correct to incorrect answers under simulated peer disagreement at rates we term yield, a vulnerability widely attributed to RLHF-induced sycophancy. We test this attribution across four model families and find it largely wrong: pretrained base models exhibit the same substitution pattern as their Instruct variants, averaging higher yield than Instruct. Using activation patching, we localize the corruption to a narrow mid-layer window where attention carries the causal weight and MLP contribution is negligible; patching above this window restores 96% of the clean-to-pressured P(correct) gap. The attack surface decomposes into two independent factors (channel framing and consensus strength) whose interaction produces a 47.5 percentage-point yield gap at majority consensus, preserved across jury sizes $N \in \{4, 5, 6\}$. Two converging activation-space interventions show that pressure suppresses clean-reasoning features rather than activating a new sycophancy circuit. A single correctly-arguing dissenter reduces yield by 54-73 percentage points across all framings tested, whereas the strongest prompt-level defense fails on attack variants outside its design surface. Mitigations should target the mechanism, structured dissent at the pipeline level, rather than prompt-level defenses.

</details>


### [125] [DP-Muon: Differentially Private Optimization via Matrix-Orthogonalized Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12994)
*Jihwan Kim,Chenglin Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将矩阵优化器 Muon 应用于差分隐私场景（DP-Muon），通过理论分析揭示了非线性映射导致的偏差，并提出了偏差校正版 DP-MuonBC，在维持相同隐私预算的情况下提升了微调任务的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器在非私有设置下表现优异，但其复杂的矩阵更新（动量+非线性正交化）与差分隐私机制（裁剪+加噪）之间的交互尚缺乏理论与实践分析。

Method: 提出了 DP-Muon，在保持 DP-SGD 的差分隐私保障前提下，将矩阵级梯度进行裁剪和加噪，并引入 Newton-Schulz 正交化。针对该方法的偏差问题，进一步提出了 DP-MuonBC（偏差校正版）。

Result: 证明了 DP-Muon 继承了 DP-SGD 的隐私保障；通过有限域稳定性分析建立了界；实验表明 DP-MuonBC 在 E2E 和 DART 等任务中优于传统私有微调方法。

Conclusion: DP-MuonBC 有效解决了 DP-Muon 中非线性 Newton-Schulz 映射带来的偏差，在不增加隐私预算的前提下显著提升了差分隐私微调的性能。

Abstract: We study differentially private (DP) training with Muon, a matrix-valued optimizer that updates hidden-layer weights using momentum followed by Newton--Schulz orthogonalization. While DP-SGD is well understood, the interaction between per-example clipping, Gaussian noise, momentum, and nonlinear orthogonalization in Muon has not been systematically analyzed. We formulate DP-Muon, a private Muon procedure that clips per-example matrix gradients, adds Gaussian noise to the clipped lot average, and then applies momentum and Newton--Schulz orthogonalization as post-processing. We prove that DP-Muon inherits the privacy guarantee certified by the corresponding same-lot subsampled Gaussian accountant, with no additional privacy cost from Muon-specific post-processing. On the optimization side, we establish finite-horizon and vanishing stationarity guarantees under per-matrix clipping, with bounds that separate optimization error, clipping residual, privacy noise, and Newton--Schulz approximation error. We further show that the DP-induced bias in Muon arises not in the linear momentum buffer itself, but after the nonlinear Newton--Schulz map, where Gaussian noise induces a matrix-valued heat-smoothing bias. This motivates DP-MuonBC, a bias-corrected variant that removes the leading output-level bias term while preserving the same privacy guarantee. Experiments on E2E and DART show that Muon-style matrix updates improve private fine-tuning, and that DP-MuonBC further improves utility without increasing the privacy budget.

</details>


### [126] [\emph{DRIFT}: A Benchmark for Task-Free Continual Graph Learning with Continuous Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12998)
*Guiquan Sun,Xikun Zhang,Jingchao Ni,Dongjin Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判了现有持续图学习方法对“离散任务”假设的依赖，提出了一个更贴近现实的无任务（task-free）评估基准DRIFT，并证实了当前方法在面对连续分布漂移时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CGL方法通常假设数据被划分为预定义的离散任务，但这种假设在真实世界中往往不成立（数据流是连续且无明确任务界限的），导致模型在处理非平稳环境时出现灾难性遗忘或性能大幅下降。

Method: 1. 提出一种无任务（task-free）的统一模型，将数据流建模为潜在任务分布的时变混合，以捕捉分布漂移；2. 构建了名为 DRIFT 的基准，涵盖了从硬任务切换到平滑分布漂移的多种转变动态；3. 在该设置下评估了代表性持续学习方法。

Result: 研究发现，现有的持续学习方法在无任务设置下性能表现大幅下降，证明了这些方法在很大程度上隐式依赖于任务边界信息，无法有效处理真实的连续图数据流。

Conclusion: 现有持续图学习（CGL）方法多依赖预定义的任务边界，在真实的无任务（task-free）且数据分布连续演变的场景下表现不佳。DRIFT基准的提出揭示了现有方法的局限性，并为未来研究更具鲁棒性的持续图学习提供了必要的研究方向。

Abstract: Continual graph learning (CGL) aims to learn from dynamically evolving graphs while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Existing CGL approaches typically adopt a task-based formulation, where the data stream is partitioned into a sequence of discrete tasks with pre-defined boundaries. However, such assumptions rarely hold in real-world environments, where data distributions evolve continuously and task identity is often unavailable. To better reflect realistic non-stationary environments, we revisit continual graph learning from a task-free perspective. We propose a unified formulation that models the data stream as a time-varying mixture of latent task distributions, enabling continuous modeling of distribution drift. Based on this formulation, we construct DRIFT, a benchmark that spans a spectrum of transition dynamics ranging from hard task switches to smooth distributional drift through a Gaussian parameterization. We evaluate representative continual learning methods under this task-free setting and observe substantial performance degradation compared to traditional task-based protocols. Our findings indicate that many existing approaches implicitly rely on task boundary information and struggle under realistic task-free graph streams. This work highlights the importance of studying continual graph learning under realistic non-stationary conditions and provides a benchmark for future research in this direction. Our code is available at https://github.com/gqBond/DRIFT.

</details>


### [127] [JEDI: Joint Embedding Diffusion World Model for Online Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13013)
*Jing Yu Lim,Rushi Shah,Zarif Ikram,Samson Yu,Haozhe Ma,Tze-Yun Leong,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JEDI 是一种新型端到端潜空间扩散世界模型，通过将扩散去噪损失与 JEPA 框架结合，实现了比像素 diffusion 更高效且性能更强的强化学习世界建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于像素的扩散世界模型计算成本高昂，而现有的潜空间扩散模型往往依赖于单独训练的预训练潜空间，缺乏端到端世界模型训练带来的协同优势。作者旨在弥合这一差距，利用扩散模型在多模态/随机预测上的优势与 JEPA 在高效表征学习上的优点。

Method: 提出了 Joint Embedding DIffusion (JEDI)。该方法将 JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）框架与扩散模型相结合，通过直接在潜空间中应用扩散去噪损失进行未来状态预测，实现端到端的模型学习，避免了依赖预训练的潜空间或昂贵的像素级重构。

Result: JEDI 在 Atari100k 基准测试中表现出色，优于依赖预训练潜空间的基线方法；与像素扩散模型相比，其 VRAM 使用率降低了 43%，采样速度提升 3 倍以上，训练速度快 2.5 倍。

Conclusion: JEDI 成功实现了端到端的潜空间扩散世界模型，证明了在无需重构或预训练潜空间的情况下，通过扩散去噪损失直接学习预测性表征是高效且有效的，并为 MBRL 提供了一种更优的扩展路径。

Abstract: Diffusion world models have recently become competitive for online model-based reinforcement learning, but current approaches expose a tension: pixel diffusion is effective but computationally expensive while the latest latent diffusion approach improves efficiency yet performs subpar. The latter also relies on separately trained latents rather than the end-to-end world-model objectives that have driven much of modern MBRL progress. In particular, JEPA-style predictive representation learning has emerged as an especially promising direction for world modeling and MBRL. Concurrently, diffusion-style objectives have gained traction across multiple domains, with iterative refinement as a promising approach for multimodal and stochastic targets. Taken together, these trends motivate Joint Embedding DIffusion (JEDI), the first online end-to-end latent diffusion world model. JEDI learns its latent space directly from the diffusion denoising loss with a JEPA framework, using denoising to learn and predict future latents rather than relying on reconstruction and pretrained models. We provide a theoretical motivation showing that conventional JEPA objectives induce a predictive information bottleneck, and that conditional diffusion denoising admits a closely related predictive-compression decomposition. Empirically, JEDI is competitive on Atari100k and outperforms the baseline with seperately trained latents where directly comparable. Relative to the pixel diffusion baseline, JEDI uses 43% less VRAM, over 3$\times$ faster world-model sampling, and 2.5$\times$ faster training. JEDI also exhibits a markedly different task-level performance profile from the pixel baseline, suggesting that end-to-end predictive latents change more than compute alone.

</details>


### [128] [Rethinking Efficient Graph Coarsening via a Non-Selfishness Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13021)
*Xu Bai,Bin Lu,Kun Zhang,Shengbo Chen,Xinbing Wang,Chenghu Zhou,Meng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统图粗化方法中成对匹配导致的计算瓶颈，本文提出了一种基于邻域集体干扰的非自私粗化算法（NOPE），通过低复杂度方案实现了大规模图的高效降维，同时保证了下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图粗化方法多采用“自私匹配”范式（基于成对相似度独立寻找最佳伙伴），导致在大规模图数据上产生巨大的计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出基于“非自私原则”（non-selfishness principle）的图粗化方法NOPE。利用邻域的集体干扰而非独立的成对相似度匹配进行节点聚合。同时，基于局部各向同性假设推导出NOPE*变体，将干扰评估复杂度从O(δ*d)降低至O(d)。

Result: NOPE*相比NOPE实现了1.8-10倍的加速，相比现有基准方法实现了1-3个数量级的性能提升；在粗化图上进行学习可保持与原图相当的性能，甚至在某些情况下优于基于LLM的图推理。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的非自私图粗化方法（NOPE及其变体NOPE*）能够在保证下游任务性能的同时，显著降低计算和内存复杂度，实现了效率与效果的平衡，并优于现有的成对匹配方法。

Abstract: Graph coarsening is a graph dimensionality reduction technique that aims to construct a smaller and more tractable graph while preserving the essential structural and semantic properties of the original graph. However, most existing methods rely on pair-wise similarity matching, where each node independently searches for its best partner based on global information. This selfishness matching paradigm incurs substantial computational and memory overhead. To address this problem, we shift to a non-selfishness principle that prioritizes the collective interference of neighborhood in coarsening, and propose an efficient method named NOPE, which achieves linear memory consumption and near-linear computational complexity in the number of nodes. Furthermore, we derive a faster variant NOPE*, which reduces O(δ\dot d) interference evaluation to O(d) based on the local isotropy assumption, and consequently alleviates the computational bottleneck for high-degree nodes. Experimental results show that NOPE* achieves 1.8-10\times speedup over NOPE and surpass almost all baselines with 1-3 orders of magnitude acceleration. Meanwhile, learning on coarsened graphs yields comparable performance to original graphs, and can even show superior performance over LLM-based graph reasoning owing to compact graph information. The code can be available at https://github.com/dazonglian/NOPE-main.

</details>


### [129] [Offline Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games with KL Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13025)
*Claire Chen,Yuheng Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Zixuan Xie,Shuze Daniel Liu,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 文章提出通过KL正则化而非显式悲观策略来解决离线零和马尔可夫博弈，并证明了其算法能实现$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$的快速收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决离线双人零和马尔可夫博弈中学习纳什均衡的问题，特别是在如何处理分布偏移（distribution shift）时，避免过度依赖显式的悲观策略。

Method: 1. 引入常规化离线序列均衡（ROSE）理论框架；2. 提出基于最小二乘价值估计和迭代自博弈更新的无模型算法——序列离线自博弈镜像下降（SOS-MD）。

Result: ROSE框架实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$的快速收敛率；SOS-MD算法的最后迭代在自博弈迭代次数$T$下，达到相同的$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$统计误差，以及$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$的优化误差。

Conclusion: 研究证明了KL正则化足以在离线零和马尔可夫博弈中稳定学习并实现收敛。提出的ROSE框架和SOS-MD算法在单边集中性假设下实现了更优的$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$收敛率，优于传统无正则化设置的$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning Nash equilibria in offline two-player zero-sum Markov games. While existing approaches often rely on explicit pessimism to address distribution shift, we show that KL regularization alone suffices to stabilize learning and guarantee convergence. We first introduce Regularized Offline Sequential Equilibrium (ROSE), a theoretical framework that achieves a fast $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$ convergence rate under \textit{unilateral concentrability}, improving over the standard $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$ rates in unregularized settings. We then propose Sequential Offline Self-play Mirror Descent (SOS-MD), a practical model-free algorithm based on least-squares value estimation and iterative self-play updates. We prove that the last iterate of SOS-MD attains the same $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$ statistical rate up to a vanishing optimization error of order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$ in the number of self-play iterations $T$.

</details>


### [130] [Understanding and Accelerating the Training of Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13026)
*Chunsan Hong,Sanghyun Lee,Chieh-Hsin Lai,Satoshi Hayakawa,Yuhta Takida,Yuki Mitsufuji,Seungryong Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对掩码扩散模型（MDM）训练缓慢的问题，本研究通过分析指出语言的局部性偏差是主要原因，并提出了一种“钟形时间采样”策略，成功将MDM的训练速度提升了约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型（MDM）在语言建模任务中显示出潜力，但相较于自回归模型（ARM）学习效率较低，限制了其在大规模模型上的扩展。论文旨在优化其训练效率并保持最终表现。

Method: 通过定量分析发现MDM训练缓慢的核心原因是语言的局部性偏差。针对该现象，提出了一种“钟形时间采样（bell-shaped time sampling）”训练策略，即在训练过程中调整掩码的时间步分布，以更有效地优化模型参数。

Result: 在One Billion Word Benchmark（LM1B）上，相比标准训练方法，采用该策略的MDM达到相同验证负对数似然（NLL）的速度快了约4倍，并在生成困惑度、零样本困惑度及多项下游任务中表现出更快的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过采用“钟形时间采样（bell-shaped time sampling）”策略，可以有效克服掩码扩散模型（MDM）在训练中因语言局部性偏差导致的低效问题，在保持性能的前提下显著提升训练速度。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive models (ARMs) for language modeling. However, MDMs are known to learn substantially more slowly than ARMs, which may become problematic when scaling MDMs to larger models. Therefore, we ask the following question: how can we accelerate standard MDM training while maintaining its final performance? To this end, we first provide a detailed analysis of why MDM training is slow. We find that the main factor is the locality bias of language: the predictive information for a token is concentrated in nearby positions. We further investigate how this bias slows learning and suggest a simple yet effective remedy: bell-shaped time sampling as a training strategy. Notably, MDMs trained with our training recipe reach the same validation negative log-likelihood (NLL) up to $\sim4\times$ faster than standard training on One Billion Word Benchmark (LM1B). We also show faster improvements in generative perplexity, zero-shot perplexity, and downstream task performance on various benchmarks.

</details>


### [131] [FeatCal: Feature Calibration for Post-Merging Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13030)
*Yanggan Gu,Shuo Cai,Zihao Wang,Wenjun Wang,Yuanyi Wang,Pengkai Wang,Sirui Huang,Su Lu,Jianmin Wu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FeatCal，一种针对模型合并后的高效权重校准技术。它通过理论分析特征漂移，使用闭式解逐层优化模型权重，在无需梯度下降的前提下，显著提升了合并模型的性能，并兼顾了高效率与小样本优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并虽然避免了集成多模型的计算开销，但通常会导致性能下降。作者旨在解决合并导致的“特征漂移”（feature drift）问题，以缩减合并模型与原始专家模型之间的表现差距。

Method: 提出了FeatCal方法：基于特征漂移理论，在每一层级利用少量校准集进行闭式权重更新，消除上游传播与局部失配导致的特征漂移，无需梯度下降或额外参数模型。

Result: 在CLIP和GLUE基准测试中，FeatCal在性能上显著优于现有的Surgery和ProbSurgery算法；表现出极高的采样效率（仅需少量样本）和计算速度（比基准快4倍）。

Conclusion: FeatCal 是一种高效的模型合并后处理算法，通过层级闭式解校准权重，有效弥补了合并模型与任务专家模型之间的性能差距，同时保持了模型合并的低成本优势。

Abstract: Model merging combines task experts into one model and avoids joint training, retraining, or deploying many expert models, but the merged model often still underperforms task experts. We study this performance gap through feature drift, the difference between features produced by the merged model and by the expert on the same input. Our theory decomposes this drift into upstream propagation and local mismatch, tracks how it propagates and combines through later layers in forward order, and links final feature drift to output drift. This view motivates FeatCal, which uses a small calibration set to calibrate the merged model weights layer by layer in forward order, reducing feature drift while staying close to merged weights and preserving the benefits of model merging. FeatCal uses an efficient closed-form solution to update model weights, with no gradient descent, iterative optimization, or extra modules. On the main CLIP and GLUE benchmarks, FeatCal beats Surgery and ProbSurgery, the closest post-merging calibration baselines: 85.5% vs. 77.0%/78.8% on CLIP-ViT-B/32 Task Arithmetic (TA) and 85.2% vs. 83.7%/82.2% on FLAN-T5-base GLUE. On CLIP-ViT-B/32, 8 examples per task reach 82.9%, and 256 examples per task take 53 seconds, about 4x faster than both baselines, showing better sample efficiency and lower calibration cost.

</details>


### [132] [Bridging Domain Gaps with Target-Aligned Generation for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13054)
*Minung Kim,Jeongmo Kim,Gwanwoo Choi,Seungyul Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对跨域离线强化学习中分布不匹配和目标域数据稀缺的问题，本文提出了TCE框架，利用双重生成模型合成与目标一致的状态转移，有效扩展了状态空间覆盖，提升了策略迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决跨域离线强化学习中环境动力学差异的问题，特别是在目标域数据极其有限时，如何有效利用源域数据并减少分布不匹配（distributional mismatch）。

Method: 提出了目标对齐覆盖扩展（Target-aligned Coverage Expansion, TCE）框架，结合双重基于分数的生成模型（dual score-based generative model），通过选择性整合源域数据或通过目标对齐生成技术来扩展状态覆盖范围，从而解决分布不匹配问题。

Result: 在多种跨域离线强化学习环境中的实验表明，TCE框架的表现持续优于当前最先进的跨域离线强化学习基线模型。

Conclusion: TCE通过双重基于分数的生成模型实现了源域到目标域的高效迁移，在处理跨域离线强化学习任务（尤其是目标域数据稀缺时）方面展现出优越性能，证明了在目标域引导下扩展状态覆盖范围的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-domain offline reinforcement learning aims to adapt a policy from a source domain to a target domain using only pre-collected datasets, where environment dynamics may differ. A key challenge is to leverage source data while reducing distributional mismatch, particularly when the target dataset is extremely limited. To address this, we propose Target-aligned Coverage Expansion (TCE), a framework that decides how source data should be used, either by directly incorporating target-near transitions or by expanding state coverage through target-aligned generation, guided by theoretical analysis. TCE builds on a dual score-based generative model to synthesize target-consistent transitions over an expanded state region. Extensive experiments across diverse cross-domain environments show that TCE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art cross-domain offline RL baselines.

</details>


### [133] [Ergodic Trajectory Design by Learned Pushforward Maps: Provable Coverage via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13063)
*Ehsan Aghazadeh,Masoud Malekzadeh,Ahmad Ghasemi,Hossein Pishro-Nik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Epushforward的框架，通过最优传输流匹配（CFM）将简单的解析轨迹映射为符合特定空间密度分布的遍历轨迹，高效解决了机器人与无人机在受限环境下的覆盖覆盖问题，实现了约束与轨迹生成的解耦，大幅降低了实时计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遍历覆盖（ergodic coverage）算法在线优化计算成本极高，且难以灵活集成各种复杂的物理约束（如无人机的避障、能耗限制等）。传统的重构算法缺乏通用性，限制了在大规模多智能体系统中的应用。

Method: 提出“Epushforward”框架：1. 使用解析形式的潜在轨迹在简单域（环形域）上实现均匀遍历；2. 利用“最优传输条件流匹配”（optimal-transport conditional flow matching, CFM）离线学习一个映射函数，将潜在轨迹的分布推向目标分布；3. 将各类物理约束（如避障、加速度限制）作为损失函数中的可微分项进行优化。

Result: 1. 证明了加速度-能量界限及 $O(1/\sqrt{K})$ 的遍历收敛速度（$K$ 为轨迹周期数）；2. 给出了基于CFM训练误差的近似误差界限；3. 实验表明该方法支持多智能体 fleet 部署且无需在线重训练。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于“推前”（pushforward）框架的遍历覆盖方法。该方法通过将均匀遍历的潜在轨迹映射到目标密度函数，实现了物理约束与遍历性需求的解耦。该方法在理论上具有收敛保证，在实践中支持多智能体协同且无需在线重计算，在大规模部署中具有显著的效率优势。

Abstract: Designing continuous trajectories whose time-averaged occupancy provably matches a prescribed spatial density (the \emph{ergodic coverage} problem) is central to UAV-assisted data collection and sensing, robotic exploration, and mobile monitoring. For flying agents in particular, this challenge is acute: trajectories must balance coverage fidelity against tight energy budgets, no-fly zones, and acceleration limits. Existing methods either re-optimize each trajectory online (with cost growing in the horizon and re-running for every target, agent, and realization) or rely on bespoke analytical constructions that must be re-derived for each new constraint. We propose a \emph{epushforward} framework that decouples ergodicity from density matching: an analytic latent trajectory provides exact uniform ergodicity on a simple annular domain, and a single map, learned offline by optimal-transport conditional flow matching, transports this latent occupancy onto the prescribed target density. The composed trajectory is then asymptotically ergodic with respect to the learned pushforward distribution, with deviation from the target controlled by the flow-matching training loss. Once trained for a given target density and constraint set, the map serves an unbounded number of trajectories and a multi-agent fleet without per-agent retraining, and many differentiable operational constraints (no-fly zones, acceleration ceilings, or fairness penalties) enter as additive soft penalties in the training loss without re-deriving the design. We prove three results (an acceleration-energy bound, an $O(1/\sqrt{K})$ ergodic convergence rate in the number of trajectory cycles $K$, and an approximation-error bound) that combine into an end-to-end coverage bound estimable from CFM training diagnostics (certified given an architectural Lipschitz bound on $v_θ$).

</details>


### [134] [Spectral Flattening Is All Muon Needs: How Orthogonalization Controls Learning Rate and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13079)
*Tien-Phat Nguyen,Truong Nguyen,Minh-Phuc Truong,Tuc Nguyen,James Bailey,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon通过Newton-Schulz迭代强制对动量进行谱平坦化，避开了传统优化中由最大奇异值导致的步长瓶颈，从而在大学习率下实现稳定且快速的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在实践中展现出能容忍更大学习率且收敛更快的特性，但其背后的理论原理尚未清晰。研究旨在揭示Muon如何通过动量修正提升优化效率。

Method: 1. 使用牛顿-舒尔茨（Newton-Schulz）迭代将动量矩阵正交化（奇异值置为1）；2. 从谱平坦化视角分析优化动力学；3. 在Kronecker因子化曲率模型下重构Muon为预条件梯度法。

Result: 1. 证明了Muon的稳定步长取决于平均奇异值而非最大奇异值；2. 显示在Kronecker曲率模型下，Muon通过改善有效收敛因子提升性能；3. 实验显示Muon在SGD发散的学习率下仍能保持稳定，并能显著缩短达到特定精度所需的Epoch数。

Conclusion: Muon通过谱平坦化（spectral flattening）机制，通过将动量矩阵奇异值归一化，解决了标准梯度下降中由最大奇异值导致的步长瓶颈，从而实现了更好的训练稳定性与收敛速度。

Abstract: Muon orthogonalizes the momentum buffer before each update, replacing its singular values with ones via Newton-Schulz iterations. This simple change lets Muon tolerate far larger learning rates and converge faster than other optimizers, but why? We show that the mechanism is spectral flattening, and develop two results around it. First, we prove that Muon's maximal stable step size scales with the average singular value of the gradient rather than the largest, which bottlenecks standard gradient descent. Second, we recast Muon as a preconditioned gradient method and show, under a Kronecker-factored curvature model, that it improves the effective convergence factor, with the improvement controlled by the spectrum of the gradient covariance. Extensive experiments validate both results: Muon remains stable at learning rates that cause SGD to diverge within the first few iterations, and reaches accuracy milestones several epochs earlier even at identical step sizes. Taken together, our results offer a principled, geometric explanation for Muon's empirical success.

</details>


### [135] [Bayesian Nonparametric Mixed-Effect ODEs with Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13088)
*Julien Martinelli,Maksim Sinelnikov,Harri Lähdesmäki,Quentin Clairon,Mélanie Prague*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异质性动力系统建模，提出了一种名为 MEGPODE 的贝叶斯非参数混合效应 ODE 模型，利用高斯过程先验和闭式回归技术，在无需反复求解 ODE 的情况下，实现了对个体和种群动力学的高精度建模与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性混合效应 ODE 模型依赖参数化向量场，易受结构错误指定影响；现有的非参数 ODE 方法通常假设单一共享动力系统，无法处理不同主体间的固有异质性。

Method: MEGPODE：将每个个体的向量场分解为共同的种群分量和个体特异性偏差，两者均使用高斯过程（GP）先验；采用状态空间 GP 轨迹先验结合虚拟配置观测（virtual collocation observations），通过卡尔曼平滑进行轨迹更新，实现向量场的闭式回归，避免了训练期间对每个个体进行重复 ODE 求解。

Result: 在多个涵盖振荡和生物医学系统的异质 ODE 基准测试中，MEGPODE 在种群动力场恢复和个体级轨迹预测方面均优于现有的强基准模型。

Conclusion: MEGPODE 是一种有效的贝叶斯非参数混合效应 ODE 模型，能够准确捕捉种群动态并进行个体轨迹预测，克服了传统参数化模型对结构先验的依赖和现有非参数方法无法处理异质性的局限。

Abstract: Dynamical modelling is central to many scientific domains, including pharmacometrics, systems biology, physiology, and epidemiology. In these settings, heterogeneity is often intrinsic: different subjects or units follow related but distinct continuous-time dynamics. Classical nonlinear mixed-effects Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) models address this by combining population-level structure with subject-specific effects, but they rely on a parametric vector field and are therefore vulnerable to structural misspecification and unmodelled mechanisms. This motivates nonparametric approaches that can retain principled uncertainty quantification, yet existing nonparametric ODE methods typically assume a single shared dynamical system rather than an explicit mixed-effect hierarchy over subject-specific dynamics. We propose MEGPODE, a Bayesian nonparametric mixed-effect ODE model in which each subject's vector field is decomposed into a shared population component and a subject-specific deviation, both endowed with Gaussian process (GP) priors. To avoid repeated ODE solves per subject during training, we combine state-space GP trajectory priors with virtual collocation observations, yielding Kalman-smoothing trajectory updates and closed-form regressions for the vector fields. Across controlled heterogeneous ODE benchmarks spanning oscillatory, biomedical systems, MEGPODE improves population-field recovery and subject-level trajectory prediction relative to strong baselines.

</details>


### [136] [LIFT: Last-Mile Fine-Tuning for Table Explicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13424)
*Divij Khaitan,Ashish Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Lift的表格提取方法，先用LLM进行初步提取，再用轻量级SLM进行针对性的错误修复，有效解决了训练数据不足和鲁棒性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对从非结构化文本提取表格的任务，现有的端到端微调模型往往在训练数据有限时表现不佳，且对输入格式的变化缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一种名为“最后一公里微调”（Lift）的两阶段流水线：首先利用预训练的大语言模型（LLM）从非结构化文本中提取初始表格，随后利用微调后的小语言模型（SLM，1B-24B参数）对提取结果进行纠错与修复。

Result: 在包含2596个表格的基准测试中，Lift在TEDS指标上匹配或超过了端到端微调方法，尤其在仅使用1000个训练样本时，性能提升了高达0.144点，并表现出对输入格式变化更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: “最后一公里微调”（Lift）是一种高效的表格提取策略，在训练数据有限或需要强鲁棒性的场景下，提供了优于端到端微调的替代方案，且无需牺牲精度。

Abstract: We propose last-mile fine-tuning, or Lift, a pipeline in which a pre-trained large language model extracts an initial table from unstructured clipboard text, and a fine-tuned small language model (1B-24B parameters SLM) repairs errors in the extracted table. On a benchmark of 2,596 tables from three datasets, Lift matches or exceeds end-to-end SLM fine-tuning on tree-edit-distance-based similarity (TEDS) metric while requiring as little as 1,000 training examples - where it outperforms end-to-end fine-tuning by up to 0.144 TEDS points. We term this approach last-mile fine-tuning and show it also more robust to input format variability. Comparisons with self-debug and end-to-end fine-tuning approaches show that last-mile fine-tuning provides an attractive option when training data is limited or when robustness to input variation is sought without compromising on accuracy.

</details>


### [137] [Margin-calibrated Classifier Guidance for Property-driven Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13101)
*Najwa Laabid,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对逆合成规划中辅助分类器引导效果不佳的问题，本文提出了Sequence Completion Ranking (SCR) 方法，通过对比学习增强引导能力，显著提升了多步化学合成路径的成功率与生成多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单步逆合成模型在进行多步规划时，依赖于预训练的自回归生成器。虽然可以通过辅助分类器进行推断引导，但现有的基于交叉熵损失训练的分类器往往无法克服模型在稀疏数据集上习得的无条件分布偏差。因此，需要一种更有效的机制来引导生成符合特定化学约束或专家偏好的路径。

Method: 提出了一种名为“序列完成排序”（Sequence Completion Ranking, SCR）的新方法。该方法利用对比论证和基于边缘（margin-based）的损失函数对分类器进行校准，使其能够在解码过程中有效区分不同的序列延续，从而实现在不重新训练生成器的情况下进行有效的路径引导。

Result: 在USPTO-190数据集上，相较于未引导的生成器（成功率为16.8%），SCR方法在执行反应类型引导时成功率提升至78.4%，在Tanimoto相似度引导下提升至95.3%，并为33个此前无法求解的目标提供了有效的合成路径。同时，该方法显著提升了生成路径的多样性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的SCR方法有效解决了传统辅助分类器无法在合成路径规划中有效引导自回归模型的问题。通过对比论证和基于边缘的损失函数，该方法不仅显著提高了受约束的多步合成成功率，还弥补了无模板方法与基于模板方法之间的多样性差距，证明了引导生成在Retrosynthesis任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Synthesis planning seeks an efficient sequence of chemical reactions that produce a target molecule. Typically, a pretrained single-step (autoregressive) retrosynthesis model is repeatedly invoked to generate such a sequence. Classifier guidance can, in principle, help steer the output of single-step model toward reactions that satisfy specific constraints or accommodate chemist's preferences during inference without having to retrain the autoregressive generator. We expose the insufficiency of auxiliary classifiers trained with cross-entropy loss to override the unconditional token-level distributions learned from typical sparse single-disconnection reaction datasets. We overcome this issue with a novel method called Sequence Completion Ranking (SCR), which employs contrastive argumentation and a margin-based loss to calibrate the classifier so that it can meaningfully discriminate between continuations during decoding. We formally establish that margin-calibrated classifiers can expand the set of property-satisfying sequences reachable under guided beam search. Empirically, on USPTO-190, given chemist-specified guidance targets, SCR substantially improves multi-step solve rates from $16.8\%$ (unguided generator) to $78.4\%$ with reaction-type guidance and $95.3\%$ with Tanimoto guidance, unlocking valid routes for 33 targets ($17.4\%$) previously unsolvable with baselines. Our method also effectively closes the long-standing diversity gap between template-free and template-based methods.

</details>


### [138] [OSDN: Improving Delta Rule with Provable Online Preconditioning in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13473)
*Chenyu Zhou,Hongpei Li,Yuerou Liu,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Online Scaled DeltaNet (OSDN)，通过引入在线更新的逐特征预处理矩阵来增强 DeltaNet 的上下文关联回忆能力，在保持高效并行计算的同时显著提升了模型的推理与学习质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力机制和状态空间模型虽然具备恒定内存特性，但在上下文关联回忆（associative recall）任务上表现欠佳。虽然 Delta Rule 引入了在线梯度下降，但其单一标量门控忽略了目标函数的特征级曲率，限制了学习效率。

Method: 提出在线缩放 DeltaNet (OSDN)，在原有 DeltaNet 标量门控的基础上，引入通过超梯度反馈在线更新的对角预处理矩阵（diagonal preconditioner），并利用代数等价性将其转化为逐特征的键（Key）缩放，同时引入自适应预处理遗忘机制 (APF) 处理非平稳上下文。

Result: 在 340M 参数规模下，JRT 上下文回忆性能较 DeltaNet 提升 32%；在 1.3B 参数规模下，回忆残差比降低 39%，且在困惑度和 LongBench 等通用任务上保持性能持平。

Conclusion: OSDN 通过引入在线预处理机制，在保持 DeltaNet 的硬件友好型块并行计算特性的同时，显著增强了模型在上下文关联回忆任务中的表现，证明了该方法在十亿参数规模下的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Linear attention and state-space models offer constant-memory alternatives to softmax attention, but often struggle with in-context associative recall. The Delta Rule mitigates this by writing each token via one step of online gradient descent. However, its step size relies on a single scalar gate that ignores the feature-wise curvature of the inner objective. We propose Online Scaled DeltaNet (OSDN), which augments the scalar gate with a diagonal preconditioner updated online via hypergradient feedback. Crucially, this right-preconditioning is algebraically equivalent to a per-feature scaling of the write-side key. This equivalence allows OSDN to strictly preserve the hardware-friendly chunkwise parallel pipeline of DeltaNet without incurring high-dimensional state overhead. Theoretically, by exploiting the exact-quadratic structure of the inner regression loss, we establish super-geometric convergence against a right-Newton comparator and prove an algorithm-aligned token-local residual contraction bound. To handle non-stationary contexts, we further introduce Adaptive Preconditioner Forgetting (APF) to dynamically refresh stale calibration. Empirically, OSDN demonstrates strong performance across scales. At the 340M-parameter scale, OSDN improves JRT-style in-context recall by 32% over DeltaNet. Scaling to 1.3B parameters, it achieves a 39% reduction in the recall residual ratio while maintaining parity on general downstream tasks (e.g., perplexity and LongBench) -- demonstrating that our online-preconditioning mechanism effectively transfers and amplifies at the billion-parameter scale.

</details>


### [139] [KAST-BAR: Knowledge-Anchored Semantically-Dynamic Topology Brain Autoregressive Modeling for Universal Neural Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13133)
*Haoning Wang,Wenchao Yang,Shuai Shen,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 KAST-BAR 模型，通过 Dual-Stream Hierarchical Attention 获取脑电复杂时空拓扑，并利用知识锚定语义分析器（KASP）将生理信号与专家语义对齐，成功解决了 EEG 基础模型中的模态鸿沟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 EEG 基础模型在复杂时空拓扑建模上存在不足，且原始生理信号与高级文本语义之间存在严重的模态鸿沟。

Method: 1. DSHA 编码器：通过双流分层注意力机制建模非欧几里得空间下的局部时序动态与全局空间背景；
2. KASP 模块：合成基于物理基础和实例级的文本 Profile；
3. STAR 模块：利用潜在专家查询（Latent Expert Queries）动态重构并优化 EEG 表达。

Result: 在 21 个数据集上进行大规模预训练，并在 6 个下游任务中均表现出优于现有基准模型（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: KAST-BAR 通过引入知识锚定和动态拓扑建模，有效地弥合了 EEG 信号与语义空间之间的鸿沟，为构建通用的脑电基础模型提供了一种新的高效范式。

Abstract: While EEG foundation models have shown significant potential in universal neural decoding across tasks, their advancement remains constrained by the inadequacy modeling of complex spatiotemporal topology, as well as the inherent modality gap between low-level physiological signals and high-level textual semantics. To address these challenges, we propose a Knowledge-Anchored Semantically-Dynamic Topology Brain Autoregressive Model (KAST-BAR), which dynamically aligns physiological representations derived from multi-level brain topology with an expert-level semantic space. Specifically, we design a Dual-Stream Hierarchical Attention (DSHA) encoder that accurately captures the brain's intrinsic non-Euclidean topology by modeling local temporal dynamics with global spatial contexts. On this basis, a Knowledge-Anchored Semantic Profiler (KASP) is proposed to synthesize physically-grounded and instance-level textual profiles, which subsequently drive a Semantic Text-Aware Refiner (STAR) to dynamically reconstruct EEG representations using Latent Expert Queries. By conducting large-scale pre-training on 21 diverse datasets to build a foundation model, KAST-BAR effectively integrates expert-level medical knowledge into EEG signal representations, consistently achieving superior performance across six downstream tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/KAST-BAR/KAST-BAR

</details>


### [140] [Effective Context in Transformers: An Analysis of Fragmentation and Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13485)
*Amirmehdi Jafari Fesharaki,Mohammadamin Rami,Aslan Tchamkerten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过马尔可夫模型证明，在有限上下文下，Transformer的表示方式至关重要：字节级表示因“碎片化”导致信息丢失，而子词分词则通过高效压缩有效地扩大了模型的有效上下文窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的输入表示方案（如字节、字符、子词）在信息上虽然可以是等价的，但在固定的有限上下文窗口限制下，它们对信息的暴露程度不同，导致模型预测能力的差异。现有研究缺乏对这种差异的理论解释。

Method: 基于马尔可夫源（Markov sources）建立数学模型，分析了“碎片化”（Fragmentation）对最优有限上下文对数损失（log-loss）的影响，证明了其预测性能的下降；同时研究了“贪心分词”（Greedy tokenization）对上下文窗口的扩展效能，并提出了用于评估分词器性能的诊断指标。

Result: 1. 证明了“碎片化”会导致有限上下文对数损失在理论上增加，解释了ByT5等字节级模型性能低于子词模型的原因。2. 证明了贪心分词可以将短的Token窗口映射为长的源上下文窗口，并提出了描述这一过程的损失保证，为评估分词器提供了量化诊断手段。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个信息论框架，解释了在有限上下文窗口约束下，表示方案（Tokens vs. Bytes/Characters）对Transformer预测性能的本质影响。证明了碎片化会导致预测损失增加（解释了字节级模型的劣势），而合理的贪心分词则能有效扩展有效上下文窗口（验证了子词分词的优势）。

Abstract: Transformers predict over a representation of a sequence. The same data can be written as bytes, characters, or subword tokens, and these representations may be lossless. Yet, under a fixed context window, they need not expose the same information to the model. This raises a basic question: how does the choice of representation change what a finite-context predictor can achieve?
  We study this question on Markov sources and uncover two complementary phenomena. First, we observe that moving to smaller representation units can hurt prediction even when the context window is enlarged to cover the relevant source history. To explain this, we introduce fragmentation: a lossless recoding that replaces each source symbol by several smaller units. We prove that fragmentation can strictly increase the optimal finite-context log-loss, showing that the gap is not merely an optimization or capacity issue, but can be intrinsic to the representation. This gives a theoretical account of the finite-context gap observed in byte- and character-level models such as ByT5 and CANINE relative to subword-tokenized models. Second, we study the opposite direction: greedy tokenization -- BPE, WordPiece, and related methods -- which groups source symbols into larger units. We show that tokenization can make a short token window behave like a longer source-context window, and we give a loss guarantee describing when this is achievable. The guarantee depends on how reliably token windows span the needed source history, together with the compression rate of the tokenizer. This also yields a simple diagnostic for real tokenizers: measuring how much source context a fixed token window reliably contains. Together, the two directions establish a finite-context information-theoretic framework for reasoning about representation choices in Transformers.

</details>


### [141] [Collaborating in Multi-Armed Bandits with Strategic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13145)
*Idan Barnea,Ofir Schlisselberg,Yishay Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多智能体贝叶斯老虎机问题，该论文提出了 CAOS 机制，通过信息共享诱导长期参与的战略性智能体实现协作，克服了“搭便车”问题并保证了良好的学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注单次决策的短寿命智能体，而忽略了在多轮决策场景下，长期参与的战略性智能体为避免探索成本而产生“搭便车”行为的问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为 CAOS 的机制，将协作维持为纳什均衡（Nash equilibrium），并在存在战略性行为的智能体之间实现信息共享。

Result: CAOS 机制成功维持了协作，在保证纳什均衡的同时，实现了较强的遗憾（regret）界，性能接近完全协作系统。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在没有货币转移的情况下，仅通过信息共享即可诱导长期参与的多智能体在多臂老虎机问题中实现协作，并达到接近完全协作系统的性能。

Abstract: We study collaborative learning in multi-agent Bayesian bandit problems, where strategic agents collectively solve the same bandit instance. While multiple agents can accelerate learning by sharing information, strategic agents might prefer to free-ride and avoid exploration. We consider a setting with persistent agents that participate in multiple time periods. This is in contrast to most previous works on incentives in multi-agent MAB, which assume short-lived agents, namely each agent has a single decision to make and optimizes their expected reward in that single decision. As in the multi-agent MAB model with incentives, our model does not have monetary transfers, and the only incentives are through information sharing.
  We propose \texttt{CAOS}, a mechanism that sustains collaboration as a Nash equilibrium while achieving strong regret guarantees. Our results demonstrate that collaborative exploration can be sustained purely through information sharing, achieving performance close to that of fully cooperative systems despite strategic behavior.

</details>


### [142] [Temper and Tilt Lead to SLOP: Reward Hacking Mitigation with Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13537)
*Ye Wang,Jing Liu,Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于“锐化对数意见池（SLOP）”的推理时对齐优化方法，通过参考模型温度调节及权重校准算法，解决了多奖励模型集成中的性能与安全性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐技术虽提供了轻量级的替代方案，但缺乏针对多奖励模型融合的优化，且存在奖励模型被“欺骗”（Reward Hacking）的风险，需要更稳健的集成与校准机制。

Method: 1. 引入参考模型温度调节（Reference-model temperature adjustment）；2. 将推理时对齐扩展至生成奖励模型集成（SLOP）；3. 提出一种用于校准SLOP权重参数的算法以缓解奖励欺骗。

Result: 实验表明，所提算法在保持对齐效果的同时，显著提升了模型抵御奖励欺骗的稳健性。

Conclusion: 通过引入参考模型温度调节和SLOP（锐化对数意见池），该方法为推理时对齐提供了更具通用性的框架；通过校准权重参数，有效缓解了奖励欺骗问题，并在保持对齐性能的同时提高了鲁棒性。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment techniques offer a lightweight alternative or complement to costly reinforcement learning, while enabling continual adaptation as alignment objectives and reward targets evolve. Existing theoretical analyses justify these methods as approximations to sampling from distributions optimally tilted toward a given reward model. We extend these techniques by introducing reference-model temperature adjustment, which leads to further generalization of inference-time alignment to ensembles of generative reward models combined as a sharpened logarithmic opinion pool (SLOP). To mitigate reward hacking, we propose an algorithm for calibrating SLOP weight parameters and experimentally demonstrate that it improves robustness while preserving alignment performance.

</details>


### [143] [Continual Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models via Program Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13162)
*Hung Le,Svetha Venkatesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对持续学习中 LoRA 容易遗忘的问题，提出了 ProCL 框架，通过引入结构化的程序内存（槽位机制）动态管理参数，在保持高效的同时实现了更好的知识稳定性和迁移能力，且无额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
