Motivation: 现有的 LoRA 方法在处理持续学习（Continual Learning）任务时，常面临灾难性遗忘问题，且忽略了对低秩空间进行精细化管理以平衡知识获取与保留的需求。

Method: 提出了一种基于神经科学“互补学习系统”的 ProCL 框架（Program memory-inspired Continual LoRA），将 LoRA 调节器组织为动态检索的结构化内存槽位，并结合基础适配器进行知识的长期积累。

Result: 在多个持续学习基准测试中，ProCL 优于现有的持续 LoRA 策略，显著降低了灾难性遗忘并提升了知识保留能力。

Conclusion: ProCL 提供了一种在不增加推理成本的前提下，有效平衡持续学习中模型可塑性与稳定性的方案，证明了通过结构化程序内存管理 LoRA 适配器能增强持续学习性能。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT), particularly Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), has become a standard approach for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) under limited compute. However, in continual settings where models are updated sequentially with small datasets, conventional LoRA updates struggle to balance rapid adaptation and knowledge retention. Existing methods typically treat the low-rank space as a homogeneous update region, lacking mechanisms to regulate how short-term updates are consolidated over time. We propose a continual LoRA framework with \textbf{Pro}gram memory, inspired by \textbf{C}omplementary \textbf{L}earning Systems in neuroscience. Our approach, dubbed \textbf{ProCL}, organizes LoRA adapters into structured program memory slots that are dynamically retrieved through input-conditioned attention. This enables rapid and localized adaptation, encouraging similar inputs to reuse shared adapter regions while reserving unused capacity for future data. The slots are then combined with the underlying adapter, which maintains a distributed representation that gradually accumulates knowledge across tasks to balance plasticity and stability. Our method operates entirely within the LoRA parameterization and incurs no additional inference cost. Experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate improved retention and reduced catastrophic forgetting over other continual LoRA strategies.

</details>


### [144] [Finding the Weakest Link: Adversarial Attack against Multi-Agent Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13170)
*Maxwell Standen,Junae Kim,Claudia Szabo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多智能体强化学习系统中的通信扰动攻击，通过基于梯度的分析方法精准定位漏洞，并提出对抗性损失函数，有效提升了对通信系统的干扰破坏能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统高度依赖通信进行信息共享与协作，这使得系统在面对通信扰动攻击时存在安全脆弱性，作者旨在定量分析并提升此类攻击的有效性。

Method: 1. 使用雅可比矩阵（Jacobian）的梯度信息识别最易受攻击的消息、智能体及时间步长；2. 提出两种专门的对抗性损失函数，在攻击成功率与攻击影响之间进行权衡以优化扰动效果；3. 在导航、捕食者-猎物（Predator-Prey）和交通路口（TrafficJunction）三个多智能体环境中进行了实验验证。

Result: 研究提出的消息选择方法在绝大多数场景下表现优于随机攻击；结合受害者选择、消息选择、时序策略及对抗损失函数的方法，在测试的30个场景中有一半场景显著提升了攻击效果。

Conclusion: 通过利用梯度信息和专门设计的对抗性损失函数，研究证明了针对多智能体通信系统的单目标扰动攻击是可行的，且在干扰系统性能方面优于随机攻击策略。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems rely on communication for information sharing and action coordination, which exposes a vulnerability to attacks. We investigate single-victim communication perturbation attacks against Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning-trained systems and propose methods that use gradient information from the Jacobian to identify which messages, agent, and timesteps are most susceptible to attack and have the greatest impact on the system. We enhance these methods with two proposed adversarial loss functions that trade-off attack success for attack impact which also create more effective perturbations. We empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods against two different multi-agent communication methods in navigation, PredatorPrey, and TrafficJunction environments. Our results show that our novel message selection method achieves a similar or greater impact than random message selection across almost all tested scenarios. Our victim selection, message selection, tempo, and loss functions improve attack effectiveness in half of the thirty scenarios we tested.

</details>


### [145] [Multi-Objective and Mixed-Reward Reinforcement Learning via Reward-Decorrelated Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13641)
*Yang Bai,Kaiyuan Liu,Ziyuan Zhuang,Jiahong Zhou,Rongxiang Weng,Xin Chen,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多任务强化学习中奖励分布不均和维度相关性导致的不稳定性，论文提出了 RDPO 算法，通过分位数归一化和马氏白化优化奖励处理，显著提升了模型在复杂任务中的指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂多任务及混合奖励的强化学习环境中，异构奖励分布和奖励维度间的强相关性会导致标量优势（scalar advantages）计算不稳定，进而影响策略优化效果。

Method: 提出 Reward-Decorrelated Policy Optimization (RDPO)，包含两个核心步骤：1. Magnitude-Aware Quantile normalization（幅度感知分位数归一化），用于稳定不同类型奖励的优势分配；2. Mahalanobis whitening（马氏白化），用于处理各奖励子空间内的相关性冗余。

Result: 在 LongCat-Flash 模型后训练阶段应用 RDPO 后，模型在指令遵循、写作质量及应对复杂提示词的鲁棒性方面均有显著提升，且在代码和推理任务上维持了与基线模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: RDPO 通过归一化和去相关处理，有效缓解了多任务和混合奖励环境下的优势估值不稳定问题，从而显著提升了模型在指令遵循、写作质量和鲁棒性方面的表现，同时保持了推理和编程的竞争力。

Abstract: Complex reinforcement learning environments frequently employ multi-task and mixed-reward formulations. In these settings, heterogeneous reward distributions and correlated reward dimensions often destabilize the construction of scalar advantages. To address these challenges, we propose Reward-Decorrelated Policy Optimization (RDPO), a reward-processing method designed to explicitly target both failure modes. RDPO first utilizes Magnitude-Aware Quantile normalization to stabilize prompt-level advantage allocation across binary, fractional, and continuous rewards. It then applies Mahalanobis whitening within each active reward subspace to mitigate correlation redundancy prior to aggregation. When applied during the post-training of LongCat-Flash, RDPO enhances instruction following, writing quality, and robustness to hard prompts while remaining broadly competitive on reasoning and coding evaluations.

</details>


### [146] [Do Heavy Tails Help Diffusion? On the Subtle Trade-off Between Initialization and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13175)
*Hamza Cherkaoui,Hélène Halconruy,Antonio Ocello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过理论和实验挑战了生成模型中引入重尾噪声的流行做法，证明了其相比传统高斯（轻尾）噪声会导致更差的采样误差界限和更难的估计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主张在扩散和流模型中引入重尾噪声，以期更好地恢复目标分布的尾部并提高生成多样性（基于“重尾数据与重尾噪声更匹配”的直觉）。该论文旨在重新审视这一范式，探讨其在理论上的合理性。

Method: 通过理论分析与实验验证相结合的方法：1. 建立了驱动重尾（HT）和轻尾（LT）噪声的两种代表性扩散模型的采样误差界限；2. 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上进行实验，验证了所推导的误差权衡。

Result: 理论分析显示HT噪声会导致统计估计问题更具挑战性，采样误差界限不如LT噪声。实验结果证实了该预测的误差权衡，证明了HT噪声在稀有区域探索方面的局限性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，使用重尾（HT）噪声会使统计估计问题变得更困难，导致采样误差界限较差。因此，该研究质疑了在生成模型中引入重尾噪声以改善稀有区域探索这一增长趋势的有效性。

Abstract: Recent works have proposed incorporating heavy-tailed (HT) noise into diffusion- and flow-based generative models, with the goals of better recovering the tails of target distributions and improving generative diversity. This motivation is intuitive: if the data are heavy-tailed, HT noise may appear better matched than light-tailed (LT) Gaussian noise. However, replacing Gaussian noise by HT noise also changes the underlying estimation problem. In this paper, we revisit this paradigm through a combined theoretical and empirical study, establishing sampling-error bounds for two representative diffusion models driven by HT and LT noise. We show that HT noise makes the statistical estimation problem harder, leading to less favorable sampling-error bounds. We support these findings with experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, empirically recovering the predicted error trade-off. Our results call into question a growing design trend in generative modeling and challenge the use of HT noise to improve rare-region exploration.

</details>


### [147] [Beyond Perplexity: A Geometric and Spectral Study of Low-Rank Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13652)
*Namrata Shivagunde,Vijeta Deshpande,Sherin Muckatira,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现低秩预训练方法无法完全仿效全秩训练的几何特性，且单一的困惑度指标无法全面衡量低秩模型的优劣。该研究通过多维度几何与光谱特征对比，揭示了各低秩方法在表征学习和损失空间上的演化差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型预训练的内存成本高昂，低秩预训练虽作为解决方案被广泛研究，但现有评估主要依赖单一指标（验证困惑度），缺乏对模型解（solution）质量的深入理解，尚不清楚低秩约束是否会从根本上改变模型学习的本质。

Method: 在三个模型规模（60M、130M、350M）下，对比了五种低秩预训练方法（GaLore, Fira, CoLA, SLTrain, ReLoRA）与全秩训练。通过16个维度的指标进行量化评估，包括：损失平面的随机/主成分方向分析、检查点间的线性插值、权重的光谱结构以及激活值的相似度分析。

Result: 低秩方法找到了与全秩训练几何结构完全不同的解。全秩训练在随机方向上处于更尖锐的盆地（basins），但在主成分方向更平滑。GaLore在激活值逼近上表现最接近全秩训练。实验证明低秩方法的训练动力学与全秩存在实质差异。

Conclusion: 低秩预训练方法与全秩训练并不等价，不同低秩方法之间也存在显著差异。验证困惑度（Perplexity）无法作为衡量模型质量的单一指标，结合几何（Loss Landscape）和谱分析（Spectral Analysis）指标能更准确地预测下游性能。

Abstract: Pre-training large language models is dominated by the memory cost of storing full-rank weights, gradients, and optimizer states. Low-rank pre-training has emerged to address this, and the space of methods has grown rapidly. A central question remains open: do low-rank methods produce models that generalize comparably to full-rank training, or does the rank constraint fundamentally alter the solutions reached? Existing comparisons rely almost entirely on validation perplexity from single-seed runs, often carried forward from prior literature. Yet perplexity is a poor proxy for solution quality; two methods can match on perplexity while converging to different loss landscape regions and internal representations. We close this gap by characterizing the solutions found by five low-rank pre-training methods, GaLore and Fira (memory-efficient optimizers), CoLA and SLTrain (architecture reparameterizations), and ReLoRA (adapter-style updates with periodic resets), against full-rank training at three model scales (60M, 130M, 350M). We evaluate each along 16 metrics across four dimensions: 1-D loss landscape along random/top-K PCA directions, 1-D interpolation between checkpoints, spectral structure of the weights and learned updates, and activation similarity to full-rank training. We show that low-rank methods are not equivalent to full-rank training, nor to one another, even when validation perplexity is close. Full-rank training settles into a sharper basin than low-rank methods along random directions, while the reverse holds for the top-1 PCA direction. Each method converges to a geometrically distinct basin. Low-rank activations diverge from full-rank in later layers as training progresses, with GaLore tracking full-rank most closely. Further, validation perplexity does not translate to downstream performance at every scale. Adding geometric and spectral metrics improves the prediction.

</details>


### [148] [Stable Attention Response for Reliable Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13181)
*Penghui Wen,Zexin Hu,Sen Zhang,Patrick Filippi,Xiaogang Zhu,Allen Benter,Thomas Bishop,Zhiyong Wang,Kun Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对降水临近预报中注意力机制的跨样本不稳定性问题，本文提出了 HARECast 框架，通过引入头部注意力响应能量的正则化机制，有效减少预测误差并刷新了主流基准测试的 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制驱动的临近预报方法虽然提升了表示能力，但忽略了注意力响应在不同样本间的跨样本不稳定性，导致预测表现不佳，且理论分析表明这种能量波动会放大预测误差下界。

Method: 提出 HARECast 框架，通过引入一种基于头部注意力响应能量（Head-wise Attention Response Energy）的分组正则化目标函数，在训练过程中约束不同样本间注意力响应能量的波动，并将其集成到包含重构分支与扩散预测器的通用预报架构中。

Result: HARECast 在 SEVIR 和 MeteoNet 基准测试中均达到了当前学术界的领先水平（SOTA），证明了抑制注意力响应能量跨样本波动对提升预报性能的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在模型中显式调节头部注意力响应能量的跨样本波动，HARECast 显著提升了降水临近预报的稳健性与精度，为解决注意力机制在动态场景下的训练不稳定性提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting remains challenging due to the highly localized, rapidly evolving, and heterogeneous nature of atmospheric dynamics. Although recent methods increasingly adopt attention-based architectures in both unimodal and multimodal settings, they mainly emphasize stronger representation learning and prediction capacity, while paying less attention to the stability of attention responses across samples. In this work, we show that cross-sample instability of attention-response energy is an important and previously underexplored source of forecasting unreliability. Empirically, inaccurate forecasts are associated with larger attention-response energy variance across heads and layers. Theoretically, we show that cross-sample variability can propagate through self-attention, and enlarge a lower bound on prediction error. Based on this insight, we propose HARECast, a Head-wise Attention Response Energy-regulated framework for precipitation nowcasting. HARECast explicitly models head-wise attention-response energy and stabilizes it through a group-wise regularization objective that reduces cross-sample fluctuations. The proposed formulation is generic and applicable to both unimodal and multimodal nowcasting architectures. We instantiate HARECast in a standard forecasting pipeline with reconstruction branches and a diffusion-based predictor, and evaluate it on commonly used benchmarks--SEVIR and MeteoNet. Experimental results demonstrate that HARECast achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [149] [N-vium: Mixture-of-Exits Transformer for Accelerated Exact Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13190)
*Aleksander Lorenc,Frédéric Berdoz,Joël Mathys,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: N-vium 引入了一种混合出口注意力机制，通过在模型深度上进行部分并行计算和 Token 自适应路由，在不损失精度的情况下，实现了 1.5B 模型推理速度 57.9% 的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前提升推理效率的方法通常是通过减少单个 Token 的计算量（FLOPs）来换取速度，但这往往会牺牲模型性能（模型质量下降）。作者旨在一种不降低模型质量的前提下，通过增加单位时间内有效计算量（FLOPs per second）来提升端到端推理速度。

Method: 提出“N-vium”混合出口（mixture-of-exits）架构：1. 在 Transformer 的不同深度附加多个预测头；2. 通过 Token 自适应路由机制将下一词预测表示为各出口的动态混合；3. 使用延迟计算（deferred computation）策略，在后续时间步批处理上层计算，以实现硬件上的部分并行化。

Result: 在 1.5B 参数规模下进行预训练，N-vium 在不降低困惑度（即性能无损）的情况下，相比同参数量和同数据量的基线 Transformer，实现了 57.9% 的挂钟推理速度提升。

Conclusion: N-vium 通过在深度上引入混合出口策略，成功提升了推理的实际挂钟效率（wall-clock speedup），在保持模型困惑度（Perplexity）不变的前提下，实现了推理速度的显著提升。

Abstract: Improving the inference efficiency of autoregressive transformers typically means reducing FLOPs per token, usually through approximations that degrade model quality. We introduce N-vium, a mixture-of-exits transformer that partially parallelizes computation across depth on standard hardware, increasing effective FLOPs per second rather than minimizing compute per token. N-vium attaches prediction heads at multiple depths and defines the next-token distribution as a learned mixture over these exits, with token-adaptive routing. This formulation strictly generalizes the standard transformer, which is recovered exactly when routing assigns zero mass to all intermediate heads. Sampling from the mixture is exact, and complete KV caches are recovered by deferring the upper-layer computation and batching it with later tokens. We pretrain N-vium at scales up to 1.5B parameters. Our largest model reaches 57.9% wall-clock speedup over a parameter- and data-matched standard transformer at no perplexity cost.

</details>


### [150] [ECG-NAT: A Self-supervised Neighborhood Attention Transformer for Multi-lead Electrocardiogram Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13194)
*Mahsa Gazeran,Sayvan Soleymanbaigi,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Amjad Seyedi,Fardin Akhlaghian Tab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ECG-NAT，一种结合掩码自编码器预训练与分层邻域注意力 Transformer 的高效 ECG 分类框架，显著提升了在稀疏标签场景下的精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ECG 分类方法难以同时解决信号变异性、噪声干扰、标注数据匮乏以及模型精度与计算效率之间的权衡问题。目前大多数方法难以平衡局部形态特征提取与全局节奏依赖捕获，且缺乏高效的多尺度特征提取能力。

Method: 提出一种名为 ECG-NAT 的两阶段自监督学习框架：1.生成式预训练：采用掩码自编码器（MAE）在未标注的多导联 ECG 数据上学习鲁棒的领域不变表征；2.判别式微调：结合监督对比损失与交叉熵损失进行微调；核心组件是分层邻域注意力 Transformer，用于高效捕捉从心跳形态到心律模式的多尺度时域特征。

Result: 在基准数据集上表现优异，仅使用 1% 的标注数据就达到了 88.1% 的分类准确率，在低资源环境下表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: ECG-NAT 提出了一种高效且鲁棒的 ECG 分类框架，通过分层注意力机制兼顾了局部形态与全局节奏特征。该方法在极低标注数据量（1%）下表现出优异性能，且兼顾计算效率，具有极高的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) arrhythmia classification remains challenging due to signal variability, noise, limited labeled data, and the difficulty in achieving both accuracy and efficiency in models. While self-supervised learning reduces label dependency, most methods target either global contextual features or local morphological patterns, but rarely implement hierarchical multi-scale feature extraction. ECG signals require architectures that simultaneously capture fine-grained beat-level morphology and broader rhythm-level dependencies with computational efficiency. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes the Electrocardiogram Neighborhood Attention Transformer (ECG-NAT), a novel self-supervised learning approach tailored for multi-lead ECG classification. Our two-stage approach begins with generative pretraining, using a masked autoencoder to reconstruct partially masked ECG signals across multiple diverse datasets, enabling the model to learn robust, domain-invariant representations from unlabeled data. This is followed by discriminative fine-tuning with a dual-loss function that combines supervised contrastive and cross-entropy losses, aligning representation learning with label prediction. The hierarchical attention mechanism efficiently captures multi-scale temporal features from localized beat morphology to broader rhythm patterns at low computational cost. ECG-NAT achieves robust performance on benchmark datasets, with 88.1\% accuracy using only 1\% labeled data, demonstrating strong efficacy in low-resource settings. The framework combines superior classification performance with computational efficiency, making it practical for real-time ECG diagnosis. The code will be made available upon acceptance at: https://github.com/Mahsagazeran/ECG-NAT.

</details>


### [151] [McCast: Memory-Guided Latent Drift Correction for Long-Horizon Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13197)
*Penghui Wen,Yu Luo,Lintao Wang,Mengwei He,Patrick Filippi,Thomas Francis Bishop,Zhiyong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统降水临近预报在长时序预测中的误差累积与漂移问题，提出 McCast 模型，通过引入漂移校正记忆库（DCBank）对潜在演变进行主动且显式的修正，从而提升了预测的长期稳定性和连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归降水临近预报方法存在严重的误差累积问题，导致长时序预测偏离物理事实；现有研究多关注单步预测精度，却忽略了气象系统的全局时间演变以及对漂移的主动修正。

Method: 提出了一种名为 McCast 的记忆引导潜在漂移校正方法。核心组件是“漂移校正记忆库（DCBank）”，包含两个阶段：1. Corrective Latent Extractor（从当前预测和参考状态提取初步校正）；2. Correction-Aware Memory Retrieval（利用时间组织的记忆对校正进行细化）。该方法通过显式且主动地校正自回归潜在演变，以克服累积误差。

Result: 在 SEVIR 和 MeteoNet 两个广泛使用的基准数据集上均获得了最优性能（SOTA），尤其是在挑战性的长时序预测任务中表现显著。

Conclusion: McCast 通过显式的漂移校正机制，在不单纯依赖提高单步预测精度的情况下，实现了更具时间连贯性和可靠的长时序降水临近预报，在 SEVIR 和 MeteoNet 基准测试中达到了 SOTA 性能。

Abstract: Existing precipitation nowcasting methods typically adopt an autoregressive formulation, where future states are predicted from previous outputs. However, such an approach accumulates errors over long rollouts, causing forecasts to drift away from physically plausible evolution trajectories. Although various studies have attempted to alleviate this problem by improving step-wise prediction accuracy, they largely neglect the global temporal evolution of meteorological systems and lack mechanisms to actively correct drift during rollouts. To address this issue, we propose McCast, a memory-guided latent drift correction method for precipitation nowcasting. Rather than treating memory as an unordered dictionary of latent states for passive conditioning, McCast leverages temporally organized memory to actively correct autoregressive latent evolution. Specifically, McCast introduces a Drift-Corrective Memory Bank (DCBank) that explicitly estimates the temporally consistent drift corrections to calibrate the divergent trajectory. DCBank performs drift correction in two stages: a Corrective Latent Extractor first predicts an initial correction from the current prediction and a reference latent state, and a Correction-Aware Memory Retrieval module then refines the initial correction using temporally organized historical memory. By explicitly correcting latent evolution, instead of improving step-wise prediction accuracy only, McCast produces more temporally coherent and reliable long-horizon forecasts. Experiments on two widely used benchmarks, SEVIR and MeteoNet, show that McCast achieves state-of-the-art performance, particularly in challenging long-horizon forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [152] [Switching Successor Measures for Hierarchical Zero-shot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13207)
*Stefan Stojanovic,Alexandre Proutiere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前向-后向（FB）表示的“切换后继度量”方法，实现了无需额外监督、固定视野或人工设计子目标的分层零样本强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分层强化学习方法通常依赖于过于严格的设计（如固定的时间抽象或预设的目标条件），导致其应用范围被局限在目标到达类任务中，难以处理更通用的奖励函数。

Method: 提出了“切换后继度量”（Switching Successor Measures），通过单一的前向-后向（FB）表示提取高层子目标选择策略和底层控制策略，并设计了算法 FB π-Switch。

Result: 在目标条件任务和通用奖励任务中，FB π-Switch 均优于非分层基线模型，并在目标条件设定下达到了现有的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 结构化后继度量（Successor Measures）为分层零样本强化学习提供了一种灵活的底层表示，能够克服传统方法在固定时间抽象或目标条件限制上的局限性，适用于更广泛的奖励函数任务。

Abstract: Hierarchical reinforcement learning can improve generalization by decomposing long-horizon decision-making into simpler subproblems. However, existing approaches often rely on restrictive design choices, such as fixed temporal abstractions or goal-conditioned objectives, which largely confine them to goal-reaching tasks and limit their applicability to general reward functions. In this paper, we introduce switching successor measures, an extension of successor measures that enables hierarchical control in zero-shot reinforcement learning without additional supervision, fixed horizons, or manually designed subgoals. We show that switching successor measures arise naturally from classical successor measures while preserving their underlying structure. Building on this result, we propose FB $π$-Switch, an algorithm that extracts both a high-level subgoal-selection policy and a low-level control policy directly from forward-backward (FB) representations, allowing hierarchical behavior to emerge from a single learned representation. Experiments on both goal-conditioned and general reward-based tasks show that FB $π$-Switch improves over non-hierarchical baselines and matches state-of-the-art hierarchical methods in goal-conditioned settings. These results demonstrate that structured successor representations provide a flexible foundation for hierarchical zero-shot reinforcement learning beyond goal-reaching tasks. Our project website is available at: https://stestokth.github.io/switching-successors/.

</details>


### [153] [Machine Learning-Driven Multimodal Spectroscopic Liquid Biopsy for Early Multicancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13218)
*Alejandro Leonardo García Navarro,Javier Cachón Ortiz,Javier González Colsa,Samuel García Díaz,Carlos Viadero Valderrama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过结合FTIR、拉曼和EEM荧光光谱，并利用低水平数据融合与XGBoost算法，提出了一种用于乳腺癌和结直肠癌识别的高性能多模态液体活检方法，实现了近乎完美的诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前癌症诊断对快速、微创、免标记及可扩展性策略的需求，利用光谱液体活检能够全面表征生物液体中生化改变的优势，探索多模态光谱与机器学习融合在多癌种检测中的潜力。

Method: 构建了一个集成了傅里叶变换红外光谱（FTIR）、拉曼光谱和激发-发射矩阵（EEM）荧光光谱的多模态液体活检框架。通过低水平数据融合（LLDF）整合不同模态的生化信息，并采用XGBoost机器学习模型进行癌症分类。研究评估了包含三种单模态、三种双模态及全多模态在内的七种实验配置。

Result: 多模态融合方案表现最佳，在乳腺癌和结直肠癌的检测中分别取得了0.997和0.994的ROC-AUC值，同时实现了高度平衡的灵敏度和特异性，优于单一模态的表现。

Conclusion: 多模态光谱数据融合（FTIR + 拉曼 + EEM荧光）结合机器学习（XGBoost）能够显著提升癌症诊断的性能，实现极高的分类准确率。此方法为开发快速、微创、免标记的癌症液体活检提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, making the development of rapid, minimally invasive, label-free and scalable diagnostic strategies a major challenge in modern oncology. In this context, spectroscopic liquid biopsy has emerged as a promising alternative, as it enables the holistic characterization of biochemical alterations in biological fluids. In this work, we propose a multimodal spectroscopic liquid biopsy framework for multicancer detection based on the combination of Fourier Transform Infrared (FTIR) spectroscopy, Raman spectroscopy, and Excitation-Emission Matrix (EEM) fluorescence spectroscopy together with Machine Learning (ML) methodologies. Serum samples from breast cancer patients, colorectal cancer patients, and healthy controls were analyzed through the three spectroscopic modalities. After modality-specific preprocessing, low-level data fusion (LLDF) was employed to integrate the complementary biochemical information encoded within the different spectroscopic measurements, and classification was performed using XGBoost models. Seven experimental configurations were evaluated, including the three unimodal approaches, all pairwise bimodal configurations, and the full multimodal approach of FTIR, Raman, and EEM fluorescence. The results show that although several individual modalities achieved high discrimination performance, the multimodal fusion provided the most balanced overall results, reaching a ROC-AUC of 0.997 for breast cancer and 0.994 for colorectal cancer, together with highly balanced sensitivity and specificity values.

</details>


### [154] [Mix, Don't Tune: Bilingual Pre-Training Outperforms Hyperparameter Search in Data-Constrained Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13225)
*Paul Jeha,Anastasiia Sedova,Louis Béthune,Skyler Seto,Jes Frellsen,Pierre Ablin,Natalie Schluter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对低资源语言预训练，本研究通过大规模实验证明：混合高资源辅助语言数据比单纯调整超参数更能显著提升模型性能，且该优势随模型规模扩大而大幅增加。建议在实践中优先采用混合策略，并通过μP迁移超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大多数语言预训练中数据匮乏导致的过拟合和泛化能力下降问题，探索如何通过超参数调整与辅助数据混合来提升模型在低资源环境下的性能。

Method: 研究人员在150M至1.43B参数的四种模型规模下，以阿拉伯语（低资源）为目标、英语为辅助语言，进行了约1000次预训练实验，对比了侵略性超参数调整（如高权重衰减）与辅助语言数据混合策略，并采用了μP技术进行超参数迁移。

Result: 实验表明：1. 数据混合在验证集损失和下游任务上均优于超参数调整，且随模型规模增大差距扩大；2. 混合策略带来的增益等同于增加2-3倍目标语言数据（验证集损失）或2-13倍（下游任务）；3. 目标语言的验证集损失指标未能完全体现数据混合带来的知识提升。

Conclusion: 在低资源语言预训练中，混入高资源辅助语言数据相比单纯的超参数调整效果更显著且更具扩展性。传统的验证集损失函数低估了数据混合带来的知识增益。建议优先采用跨语言数据混合，并结合μP（maximal update parametrization）进行超参数迁移。

Abstract: For most languages of the world, language model pre-training operates in a data-constrained regime where models must repeat their training data many times, degrading generalization. Two remedies exist: aggressive hyperparameter tuning such as high weight decay, and mixing in data from a high-resource auxiliary language to directly aid the low-resource target. While hyperparameter tuning regularizes the model by shrinking weights to restrict network capacity, auxiliary data mixing uses a tunable mixing ratio to expand the training distribution and diversify the training signal with new knowledge. Both offer a principled way to improve training in a data-constrained domain. We compare these levers systematically across four model scales from 150M to 1.43B parameters, using Arabic as the low-resource target and English as the auxiliary, over approximately 1000 pre-training runs. Three findings emerge. First, mixing yields larger improvements than hyperparameter tuning on both validation loss and downstream task accuracy, and the gap grows with model size. Second, we quantify how much mixing helps: it boosts performance by an amount equivalent to 2--3$\times$ the unique target data on validation loss and 2--13$\times$ on downstream task accuracy, with the gain scaling steeply with model size. Third, this divergence reveals that target-language validation loss systematically underestimates mixing's value. Mixing regularizes by diversifying the training signal and contributes knowledge the repeated target corpus cannot supply; validation loss captures only the first effect. Our practical recommendations are: mix in a high-resource language, prioritize the mixing ratio over hyperparameter tuning, and transfer hyperparameters from a small proxy model via $μ$P.

</details>


### [155] [Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization for LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13230)
*Xinyu Liu,Kechen Jiao,Chunyang Xiao,Runsong Zhao,Junhao Ruan,Bei Li,Jiahao Liu,Qifan Wang,Xin Chen,Jingang Wang,Tong Xiao,JingBo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对在线LLM蒸馏中RKL机制在分布偏差较大时失效的问题，论文提出TGPO算法，通过引入教师提供的密集方向性引导，显著提升了蒸馏的效果和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Reverse KL（RKL）的LLM蒸馏方法，在学生与教师分布差异显著时，因缺乏有效的负反馈（uninformative negative feedback）而导致蒸馏效率低下或性能提升受限。

Method: 提出“教师引导策略优化”（Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization, TGPO）。该方法在保持在线（on-policy）更新的同时，通过利用基于学生路径（rollout）条件的教师预测，为学生模型提供密集的、具有方向性的监督信号，而非仅仅依赖传统的RKL反馈。

Result: 在复杂推理基准测试中，TGPO显著优于标准的蒸馏基准方法，并对不同的教师模型展现出良好的鲁棒性；同时，该方法可无缝集成至现有的RLVR框架中，无需额外标注。

Conclusion: TGPO通过引入教师引导的密集方向性反馈，克服了传统Reverse KL在学生与教师分布差异较大时的性能瓶颈，为在线LLM蒸馏提供了一种高效、稳健且易于集成的新范式。

Abstract: The convergence of reinforcement learning and imitation learning has positioned Reverse KL (RKL) as a promising paradigm for on-policy LLM distillation, aiming to unify exploration with teacher supervision. However, we identify a critical limitation: when the student and teacher distributions diverge significantly, standard RKL often fails to yield meaningful improvement due to uninformative negative feedback. To address this inefficiency, we propose Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization (TGPO), an on-policy algorithm that incorporates dense directional guidance by leveraging teacher predictions conditioned on the student's rollout. Because TGPO remains on-policy, the algorithm integrates seamlessly with existing RLVR frameworks without requiring additional data annotation. Experiments on complex reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that TGPO significantly outperforms standard baselines and is robust to different teachers.

</details>


### [156] [EMO: Frustratingly Easy Progressive Training of Extendable MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13247)
*Linghao Jin,Chufan Shi,Huijuan Wang,Nuan Wen,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EMO是一种MoE渐进式训练框架，通过根据训练阶段动态增加专家数量来避免前期资源浪费，在保证性能的前提下，有效解决了传统MoE训练中内存与通信开销过大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE模型虽然降低了单Token计算量，但大规模专家池带来了严重的内存与通信开销，导致训练效率低下。现有方法在训练伊始就分配全部专家，导致资源浪费且无法充分利用早期训练阶段的容量。

Method: 提出了一种名为EMO的渐进式训练框架，将专家容量视为可扩展资源；基于稀疏性扩展定律（Scaling Law）模型，推导出分阶段的计算最优Token分配策略，在训练初期使用较少专家，随训练过程逐步增加专家池规模。

Result: 在大规模实验中，EMO在达到与固定专家模型相当性能的同时，缩短了训练时间并降低了GPU计算成本。

Conclusion: EMO通过在训练过程中动态扩展专家数量，成功缓解了MoE模型在大规模部署中的内存与通信瓶颈，在保持性能的同时显著提升了训练效率和成本效益。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer a powerful way to scale model size without increasing compute, as per-token FLOPs depend only on k active experts rather than the total pool of E experts. Yet, this asymmetry creates an MoE efficiency paradox in practice: adding more experts balloons memory and communication costs, making actual training inefficient. We argue that this bottleneck arises in part because current MoE training allocates too many experts from the beginning, even though early-stage data may not fully utilize such capacity. Motivated by this, we propose EMO, a simple progressive training framework that treats MoE capacity as expandable memory and grows the expert pool over the course of training. EMO explicitly models sparsity in scaling law to derive stage-wise compute-optimal token budgets for progressive expansion. Empirical results show that EMO matches the performance of a fixed-expert setup in large-scale experiments while improving wall-clock efficiency. It offers a surprisingly simple yet effective path to scalable MoE training, preserving the benefits of large expert pools while reducing both training time and GPU cost.

</details>


### [157] [LightSplit: Practical Privacy-Preserving Split Learning via Orthogonal Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13265)
*Mert Cihangiroglu,Alessandro Pegoraro,Phillip Rieger,Antonino Nocera,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 LightSplit 的新型分割学习方法，通过固定随机投影在切分层实现高效降维与隐私保护，既降低了通信开销，又减少了原始特征的暴露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割学习中切分层的激活值传输存在通信带宽占用高以及容易遭受重构攻击泄露隐私的双重挑战。目前的方案通常在隐私保护和通信效率之间做单一侧重，且往往引入复杂的额外机制。

Method: 在分割学习（SL）的切分层（Cut-layer）应用轻量级、固定的正交随机投影（Orthogonal Random Projection）。该投影充当信息瓶颈，限制了实例特异性信息的传输，并将原始激活值降维，同时保持端到端的梯度可导性。

Result: LightSplit 可实现高达 32 倍的维度压缩，同时维持原始准确率的 95% 以上，且在 IID 和非 IID 数据分布下均表现出稳定的训练动力学。

Conclusion: LightSplit 通过固定的正交随机投影，在不引入额外可训练参数的前提下，有效实现了通信高效与隐私保护的平衡。该方法证明了在显著降低传输开销的同时，能够保持模型的高精度训练，为边缘设备上的协作学习提供了一种轻量级方案。

Abstract: Split learning (SL) enables collaborative training by partitioning a neural network across clients and a central server, but the cut-layer interface introduces a key challenge: high-dimensional activations incur substantial communication overhead while exposing representations vulnerable to reconstruction attacks. Existing approaches typically address efficiency or privacy in isolation, relying on additional mechanisms such as sparsification, quantization, or noise injection. We propose LightSplit, which limits information exposure and reduces communication overhead by applying a lightweight fixed orthogonal random projection at the cut layer. Based on Shannon's information theory, this projection acts as an information bottleneck that restricts instance-specific information and suppresses exploitable per-sample signals. By transmitting low-dimensional projections instead of raw activations, the server operates on lifted representations without requiring architectural modifications, ensuring compatibility with existing SL architectures. By avoiding additional trainable components on the client, the method remains lightweight and suitable for edge devices while preserving end-to-end differentiability via exact gradient propagation. As the projection is non-invertible, part of the original representation is irreversibly discarded at the client, LightSplit reduces the information available for reconstruction and limits information exposure. We extensively evaluate LightSplit on state-of-the-art benchmarks in both IID and non-IID settings across varying projection dimensions and client scales. Our results show that the method retains more than 95% of the baseline accuracy at up to 32x reduction in transmitted dimensionality while maintaining stable training dynamics.

</details>


### [158] [Byzantine-Robust Distributed Sparse Learning Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13283)
*Yuxuan Wang,Lixin Zhang,Kangqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通信高效且具备拜占庭鲁棒性的高维稀疏分布式估计框架，适用于多种线性模型，并兼顾统计效率与对抗攻击能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在 Byzantine 攻击（节点可能发送恶意数据）的情况下，解决高维稀疏线性模型在分布式环境下的鲁棒估计与通信效率问题。

Method: 结合局部L1正则化鲁棒估计与服务器端的鲁棒聚合算法（适用于伪Huber回归、分位数回归及稀疏SVM）。

Result: 理论上证明了该框架具有非渐近收敛性保证，在Mild条件下可达到近优的统计速率；模拟结果表明其在高维估计、特征选择及分类任务中具有很强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种具备通信效率且对拜占庭攻击具有鲁棒性的高维稀疏线性模型分布式估计框架，在理论上达到了近优的统计收敛速度，并通过实验验证了其在多种攻击场景下的稳健性。

Abstract: We revisit Byzantine robust distributed estimation for high-dimensional sparse linear models. By combining local $\ell_1$-regularized robust estimation with robust aggregation at the server, the framework applies to pseudo-Huber regression, quantile regression, and sparse SVM. We show that the resulting estimators yield non-asymptotic guarantees and attain near-optimal statistical rates under mild conditions, while remaining communication-efficient. Simulations confirm strong robustness in estimation, support recovery and classification accuracy under various Byzantine attacks.

</details>


### [159] [PaMM: Periodic Motif Memory for Atomistic Models with an Explicit Local-Structure Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13297)
*Ryan Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文引入了 PaMM 模块，通过在原子建模中显式加入对原子对和三元组基序的查找记忆，弥补了传统模型隐式编码局域结构的不足，显著提升了周期性体系（如 OMAT 数据集）的能量和受力预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性晶体结构在不同单元格内重复出现相似的局部配位基序，但现有的等变原子模型通常仅隐式地编码这些特征，缺乏对外在结构模式的有效建模。

Method: 提出了 PaMM (Periodic Motif Memory) 机制，对 UMA eSCN-MD 边缘编码器进行增强。该方法通过将原子对 ($Z_j, Z_i, b_r$) 和三元组 ($Z_j, Z_i, Z_k, b_θ$) 特征映射至固定大小的哈希表（查找表），并将提取出的记忆特征通过“门控加权（gate-only）”或“仿射变换（affine-equipped）”方式与基础边缘表示相融合。

Result: 在 10k 和 20k 训练步长的实验中，PaMM 均优于纯基线模型，且在能量和受力预测误差（MAE）上表现更佳。消融实验证明，这种增益来源于结构化的基序组织，而非简单的模型参数增加。同时在 OMAT24 的跨数据集测试中也显示了一致的小幅提升。

Conclusion: 在当前的周期性原子建模（以 UMA-S + OMAT 为例）中，引入显式的原子对（pair）和三元组（triplet）基序记忆（motif memory）是一种有效的归纳偏置（inductive bias），可以辅助提升模型性能。

Abstract: Periodic crystals repeatedly instantiate similar local coordination motifs across translated cells and chemically related structures, but current equivariant atomistic models usually encode these patterns only implicitly in dense edge features. We introduce PaMM, a periodic motif memory that augments the UMA eSCN-MD edge encoder with explicit pair and triplet lookup features. Pair motifs are keyed by $(Z_j, Z_i, b_r)$ and triplet motifs by $(Z_j, Z_i, Z_k, b_θ)$, hashed into fixed-size tables and fused with the baseline edge representation through lightweight gate-only and affine-equipped variants. We evaluate PaMM in a matched UMA-S OMAT setting and focus on a narrow question: whether explicit motif memory helps at a fixed intermediate training budget. At the 10k-step checkpoint, both PaMM variants improve over the plain baseline; gate-only gives the best energy MAE, while the affine-equipped variant gives the best force MAE. A matched 20k follow-up keeps the same operating-point picture. Aligned controls show that the gain weakens for pair-only, triplet-only, random-bucket, and parameter-matched MLP alternatives, suggesting that the benefit is tied to structured pair/triplet organization rather than generic added capacity. A within-OMAT24 source-family evaluation also shows small but consistent gains across held-out generation families. We therefore make a focused claim: in the studied UMA-S + OMAT regime, explicit pair/ triplet motif memory is a useful inductive bias for periodic atomistic modeling. We do not claim broad cross-dataset transfer, a uniquely preferred fusion variant, or strong scientific interpretability beyond a more inspectable local-structure interface.

</details>


### [160] [Safe Bayesian Optimization for Uncertain Correlations Matrices in Linear Models of Co-Regionalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13302)
*Jannis Lübsen,Annika Eichler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将安全多任务贝叶斯优化的保障范围从限制较多的 ICM 扩展至更具灵活性的 LMC，通过一致误差界理论证明了其有效性，并通过实验验证了其在提升优化性能上的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多任务贝叶斯优化安全保障主要基于内在协区域化模型（ICM），其对任务间相关性的建模较为有限，无法满足更复杂的应用场景。

Method: 将安全多任务贝叶斯优化（Safe Multi-task BO）的保障机制扩展至线性模型协区域化（Linear Model of Coregionalization, LMC）；导出了针对 LMC 核的向量值函数高斯过程的一致误差界（Uniform Error Bounds）。

Result: 理论上导出了 LMC 核下的误差界；实验证明在安全多任务贝叶斯优化基准测试中，使用 LMC 相比 ICM 表现更佳。

Conclusion: 在线性模型核（LMC）下，多任务贝叶斯优化能够比内在协区域化模型（ICM）更灵活地建模跨任务相关性，并能实现有效的安全性能提升。

Abstract: This paper extends safety guarantees for multi-task Bayesian optimization with uncertain correlation matrices from intrinsic co-reginalization models to linear models of co-reginalization. The latter allows for more flexible modeling of the inter-task correlations by composing multiple features. We derive uniform error bounds for vector-valued functions sampled from a Gaussian process with a linear model of co-reginalization kernel. Furthermore, we show the potential improvement of performance using linear models of co-reginalization in a numerical comparison on a safe multi-task Bayesian optimization benchmark.

</details>


### [161] [MPINeuralODE: Multiple-Initial-Condition Physics-Informed Neural ODEs for Globally Consistent Dynamical System Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13305)
*Lake Yang,Antonio Malpica-Morales,Frank Ioannis Papadakis Wood,Serafim Kalliadasis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决 Neural ODE 在泛化能力和长程预测上的局限，本研究提出的 MPINeuralODE 结合了物理驱动约束与多初始条件课程学习，显著提升了模型对动力系统的恢复精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 Neural ODEs 往往只拟合训练轨迹，在处理未知初始条件和长跨度动态预测时泛化性较差。

Method: 提出 MPINeuralODE 模型，核心在于集成软物理信息残差（soft physics-informed residual）与多初始条件（MIC）多次打靶（multiple-shooting）课程学习方法。其物理项负责锚定向量场幅度，而 MIC 则扩展了训练数据的支持集（support）。

Result: 在 Lotka-Volterra 系统实验中，MPINeuralODE 的样本外误差和长跨度 MSE 均优于现有的数据驱动方法，相较基准 Neural ODE 降低了 26% 的误差，且在哈密顿漂移（Hamiltonian drift）性能上与 PINN 相当。

Conclusion: MPINeuralODE 通过结合物理约束与多初始条件训练机制，有效改善了神经常微分方程（Neural ODEs）在未知初始条件和长跨度预测下的泛化能力与稳定性。

Abstract: Neural ordinary differential equations (Neural ODEs) often fit training trajectories while generalizing poorly to unseen initial conditions and long horizons. We propose MPINeuralODE, which combines a soft physics-informed residual with a Multiple-Initial-Condition (MIC) multiple-shooting curriculum whose ingredients are structurally complementary: the physics term anchors the vector-field magnitude on the support that MIC enlarges. We evaluate along three axes: out-of-sample error, long-horizon stability, and Hamiltonian drift, which together expose whether the learned dynamics recover the underlying vector field. On Lotka-Volterra, MPINeuralODE achieves the lowest out-of-sample and long-horizon MSE among data-driven methods, with a 26% reduction over the baseline Neural ODE, while essentially matching the PINN ablation on Hamiltonian drift.

</details>


### [162] [Shortcut Mitigation via Spurious-Positive Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13340)
*Phuong Quynh Le,Jörg Schlötterer,Sari Sadiya,Gemma Roig,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需额外标注或均衡数据的深度学习模型偏差修正方法，通过识别并抑制特定神经元来消除模型对虚假特征的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的“捷径学习”（shortcut learning）缓解策略往往依赖于标注数据、均衡测试集或训练集中必须包含所有属性组合，而这些条件在实际应用中难以满足。

Method: 提出了一种针对性模型分析方法，通过识别模型依赖虚假特征的少量样本，进而定位中间层中相关的关键神经元，并通过正则化手段削弱其影响。

Result: 该方法有效地促使模型关注更具信息量的特征，而非利用虚假特征，从而在不增加额外数据负担的情况下提高了模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过识别并抑制模型中依赖虚假特征的相关神经元，无需额外的标注数据或均衡数据集即可有效缓解模型的“捷径学习”问题，提升稳健性。

Abstract: Shortcut mitigation strategies commonly rely on training data annotations, group-balanced held-out data or the presence of all groups, i.e., all combinations of (spurious) attributes and classes, in the training data. However, these requirements are rarely met in practice. We instead propose a method for targeted model analysis to identify a small set of instances in which the model relies on spurious attributes. Using that set and following ``this feature should not be used for prediction'' reasoning, we identify highly relevant neurons in an intermediate layer and regularize their impact. This ensures that models learn to depend on informative features rather than being right for the wrong reasons, thereby improving robustness without requiring additional balanced held-out data or annotations.

</details>


### [163] [Contextual Bandits for Resource-Constrained Devices using Probabilistic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13346)
*Marco Angioli,Kevin Johansson,Antonello Rosato,Amy Loutfi,Denis Kleyko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了概率 HD-CB，通过随机概率更新和数值范围约束替代累加与二值化，在显著降低内存与计算需求的同时，实现了边缘设备上的高效决策学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 HD-CB 算法采用基于累加的学习规则，随着时间推移，动作向量数值会不断增长，导致溢出。而现有的周期性二值化方法会丢弃幅度信息，导致决策质量下降，且标准线性 CB 算法难以满足边缘计算的资源约束。

Method: 引入了概率更新规则（Probabilistic Update Rule），将原有的确定性累加修改为针对向量组件进行随机子集更新；引入时间衰减更新概率机制，将组件值严格限制在 [-k, +k] 范围内，从而在无需周期性二值化的情况下防止了数值溢出。

Result: 在标准化合成 CB 基准测试中，概率 HD-CB 在相同精度下持续优于二值化 HD-CB；在使用仅 3 位（3-bit）精度时，其性能表现接近于原始高精度 HD-CB。，同时降低了预期的更新计算成本。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于概率更新规则的低精度 HD-CB 方法。该方法能够有效限制向量值范围从而防止溢出，同时避免了传统周期性二值化带来的信息损失，实现了在保持低精度（3 bit）的同时达到高性能，非常适合资源受限的端侧设备。

Abstract: Contextual bandits (CB) are online sequential decision-making problems under partial feedback that underpin many adaptive services. There is a growing demand to deploy CB agents directly on-device, under strict constraints on memory, compute, and energy. However, standard linear CB algorithms are often impractical for resource-constrained devices with their unfavorable scaling in computational and memory costs. Recently, HD-CB, a CB approach based on hyperdimensional computing principles, has been proposed to model and solve CB problems by moving into high-dimensional spaces. HD-CB offers faster convergence, favorable scalability, and improves memory efficiency compared to linear CB algorithms. However, its learning rule is accumulation-based: the values of action vectors grow over time, requiring high precision. While periodic binarization can prevent overflow in low-precision components, it may discard important information about magnitudes and degrade decision quality. This paper introduces probabilistic HD-CB, a low-precision variant that replaces deterministic accumulation with a probabilistic update rule. At each step, only a random subset of vector components is updated, with a time-decaying update probability, and component values are constrained to a predefined range [-k,+k]. This approach enables low-precision components, prevents overflow without periodic binarization, and reduces the expected update cost in proportion to the fraction of updated components. Off-policy evaluation on standardized synthetic CB benchmarks using the Open Bandit Pipeline shows that probabilistic HD-CB consistently outperforms binarized HD-CB at equal precision, while approaching the performance of HD-CB with as few as 3 bits per component.

</details>


### [164] [GeoFlowVLM: Geometry-Aware Joint Uncertainty for Frozen Vision-Language Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13352)
*Mayank Nautiyal,Li Ju,Andreas Hellander,Ekta Vats,Prashant Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoFlowVLM 是一个用于双编码器视觉-语言模型的后处理适配器，通过黎曼流匹配在超球面上学习嵌入的联合分布，从而精确量化跨模态的偶然不确定性和分布外的认识不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的双编码器视觉-语言模型（VLM）通常将数据映射为确定性点，无法有效表达偶然不确定性（跨模态歧义）和认识不确定性（分布外情况），且现有的后处理方法往往无法同时覆盖上述两点或者是忽略了嵌入空间的超球几何特性。

Method: 提出 GeoFlowVLM 方法，在 $\mathbb{S}^{d-1} \times \mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ 产品超球空间上利用单一掩码速度场进行黎曼流匹配（Riemannian flow matching），通过联合分布学习，推导出条件检索熵（评估偶然不确定性）和边际典型性得分（评估认识不确定性）。

Result: 模型在三个检索基准测试中，检索熵与 Recall@1 表现出近乎理想的单调校准；在四个零样本分类基准测试中，边际典型性得分实现了稳健的选定准确率（selective accuracy）校准。

Conclusion: GeoFlowVLM 通过引入基于黎曼流匹配的后处理适配器，有效地在双编码器多模态模型中量化了偶然不确定性和认识不确定性，解决了现有方法难以同时处理两种不确定性及忽略超球几何结构的问题，在检索与零样本分类任务中展现了优异的校准效果。

Abstract: Standard dual-encoder vision-language models that map images and text to deterministic points on a shared unit hypersphere through $\ell_2$ normalization typically expose neither \emph{aleatoric} uncertainty (cross-modal ambiguity) nor \emph{epistemic} uncertainty (lack of training-distribution support). Existing post-hoc methods either recover at most one of the two uncertainty components, or ignore the hyperspherical geometry of these models' embeddings. We propose \textbf{GeoFlowVLM} as a post-hoc adapter that learns the joint distribution of paired $\ell_2$-normalised dual-encoder VLM embeddings on the product hypersphere $\mathbb{S}^{d-1} \times \mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ via Riemannian flow matching with a single masked velocity field. A consistency result shows that, in the population limit, the trained network exposes the joint flow and both cross-modal conditional flows as valid Riemannian flow-matching velocity fields on their respective domains. We derive two quantities from this single model: a conditional retrieval entropy that quantifies aleatoric ambiguity with a decision-theoretic interpretation via a Fano-type bound, and a marginal-typicality epistemic score justified by an exact chain-rule decomposition of the joint NLL. This decomposition isolates a cross-modal pointwise-mutual-information term that is structurally discriminative rather than epistemic, and is empirically the only consistently uninformative standalone component. Empirically, the entropy tracks Recall@1 with near-ideal monotonic calibration across three retrieval benchmarks in both directions, and the marginal-typicality sum yields consistently calibrated selective accuracy across four zero-shot classification benchmarks.

</details>


### [165] [Teaching and Learning under Deductive Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13384)
*Jan Arne Telle,Brigt Håvardstun,Jose Hernandez-Orallo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对存在推理错误和随机性的学习者的PAC机器学习框架，通过复杂度分析和实验验证，解决了在“非完美”学习环境中如何构建最优小样本教学集的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习模型多假设学习者进行完美演绎推理，但人类和LLM在小样本学习中往往存在推理错误和随机性，现有框架无法顾及这些现实限制。

Method: 提出了一种修正后的PAC（Probably Approximately Correct）学习框架，将学习者的演绎错误和随机性考虑在内；通过参数化复杂度分析处理最优教学集计算问题，并使用XP算法进行求解，同时辅以实验对比LLM教学行为。

Result: 定义了考虑错误水平的PAC教学模型，给出了计算最优PAC教学集的相关问题复杂度，提供了基于教学集大小参数化的XP算法，并给出了紧凑的运行时间界。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，在学习者存在演绎推理错误的情况下，教师仍能通过构建“可能近似正确（PAC）”的教学集来实现有效的教学目标，且相关计算问题在参数化复杂度理论下是可解的，实验也验证了该框架在描述LLM教学行为方面的有效性。

Abstract: Most models of machine teaching and learning assume the learner makes no errors in its internal deductive inference. However, humans and large language models in few-shot learning regimes are two important examples of learners where this does not hold. They fail on some consistency checks, and they can fail stochastically. In this paper we introduce a teaching and learning framework that takes these deductive errors into account. We specifically study the case of machine teaching, as different characterizations of the teacher can account for both machine teaching and learning. In an overhauled Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) setting, we study theoretically that, for some estimated error level, the teacher must find a PAC teaching set that with high probability will lead the learner to guess a hypothesis that is approximately correct. We study the computational complexity of six different problems related to computing optimal PAC teaching sets. We give XP algorithms parametrized by size of teaching set, with tight runtime bounds under standard complexity assumptions like ETH. These results are complemented with a small experimental study of which teaching and learning protocols can best represent the observed behavior in some LLM teaching sessions.

</details>


### [166] [Taming the Long Tail: Rebalancing Adversarial Training via Adaptive Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13395)
*Lilin Zhang,Yimo Guo,Yue Li,Jiancheng Shi,Xianggen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长尾分布下深度学习模型易受对抗攻击的问题，本文提出了 RobustLT 框架，通过自适应调整对抗扰动，实现了对抗鲁棒性与类别平衡的协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练主要针对平衡数据集，忽略了真实场景下的长尾分布挑战。研究发现，对抗性扰动会改变训练分布，能够被用于同时对抗不平衡性与模型脆弱性。

Method: 提出名为 RobustLT 的即插即用框架，通过在对抗训练过程中自适应调整扰动大小，旨在解决长尾分布下的类不平衡问题与对抗脆弱性。

Result: 在多个长尾数据集上的实验结果表明，RobustLT 相比传统方法能显著提升模型的对抗鲁棒性并改善长尾分类表现。

Conclusion: RobustLT 能够有效调节对抗训练过程中的扰动，在长尾数据集上同时提升模型的对抗鲁棒性和类别平衡性能，验证了对抗性扰动在缓解样本不平衡问题上的协同效应。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are highly vulnerable to adversarial examples, i.e.,small perturbations that can significantly degrade model performance. While adversarial training has become the primary defense strategy, most studies focus on balanced datasets, overlooking the challenges posed by real-world long-tail data. Motivated by the fact that perturbations in adversarial examples inherently alter the training distribution, we theoretically investigate their impact. We first revisit adversarial training for long-tail data and identify two key limitations: (i) a skewed training objective caused by class imbalance, and (ii) unstable evolution of adversarial distributions. Furthermore, we show that perturbations can simultaneously address both adversarial vulnerability and class imbalance. Based on these insights, we propose RobustLT, a plug-and-play framework that adaptively adjusts perturbations during adversarial training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RobustLT consistently enhances adversarial robustness and class-balance on long-tailed datasets. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/zhang-lilin/RobustLT}{https://github.com/zhang-lilin/RobustLT}.

</details>


### [167] [The Diffusion Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13399)
*Akhil Premkumar,Sarah Lucioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过交替训练策略，成功引入扩散模型作为编码器，克服了其与解码器之间的训练冲突，在提升特征表达能力的同时保持了高效的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分自编码器（VAE）中的重参数化技巧虽能简化训练，但严重限制了编码器分布的表达能力。本文旨在利用扩散模型的强大表达能力构建编码器，但需克服扩散模型编码器与解码器在潜在变量学习上的冲突。

Method: 提出一种新型编码器，通过模仿期望最大化（EM）算法的交替训练策略（alternating training scheme）来协调编码器与解码器之间的交互，解决两者在潜在空间估计上的更新冲突。

Result: 该方法实现了编码器与解码器之间更可靠的同步，同时保留了标准扩散模型简单且高效的训练目标。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于扩散模型的编码器架构，通过交替训练方案成功解决了传统训练中的梯度冲突与同步问题，在保持扩散模型高效训练特性的同时，提升了模型对潜在空间表示的学习能力。

Abstract: We construct a new kind of encoder, leveraging the expressive power of diffusion models. In a traditional variational autoencoder, the encoder and decoder jointly negotiate a latent representation of the input. This is made possible by the reparameterization trick, which simplifies training at the cost of restricting the encoder to a simple family of distributions. Replacing this encoder with a diffusion model requires rethinking how the decoder pressure can be transmitted back to the encoder, given that they tend to update their internal estimates of the latent in opposing directions. We solve this problem with an alternating training scheme, inspired by the expectation-maximization algorithm. Our method enables more reliable synchronization between encoder and decoder, while preserving the simple and efficient training objective of standard diffusion models.

</details>


### [168] [When is Warmstarting Effective for Scaling Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13405)
*Neeratyoy Mallik,Maciej Janowski,Johannes Hog,Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Josif Grabocka,Frank Hutter,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大规模模型增长（warmstarting）的局限与准则，发现简单增长策略优于复杂算子，并确定了增长因子存在效率上限，通过拟合缩放法则为模型增长提供了实用的决策指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型增长（warmstarting）在实际大规模训练中应用有限，现有研究过度强调保持初始性能以及缺乏对增长因子（growth factor）与训练效率之间系统性关系的分析。

Method: 通过对密集MLP和大规模语言模型进行实证研究，对比不同增长因子下的训练效率，分析增长策略与超参数及缩放行为的相互作用，并拟合缩放法则（Scaling Laws）以预测增长效能。

Result: 发现保持模型初始化性能并非获得最优最终性能的必要条件；识别出了增长因子的上限，超过该上限后从头训练效率更高；量化了2倍增长的效能，并建立了指导实践的缩放法则。

Conclusion: 研究指出，模型增长（growth）并非在任何情况下都优于从头训练。2倍增长因子是实现收敛加速的最可靠选择，在大规模计算预算下优势递减。研究为工业界提供了模型增长的预测性指导准则及性能界限。

Abstract: Model growth from a given checkpoint aims to accelerate training of a larger model, offering potential resource savings. Despite recent interest, warmstarting has seen limited practical adoption in large-scale training. We attribute this to two underexplored factors: (1) an overemphasis on preserving the smaller model's performance at initialization, which constrains operator design for new architectures, and (2) insufficient analysis of how growth interacts with hyperparameters and scaling behavior, compounded by inconsistent growth factors across the literature.
  We show that preserving the base model's initial post-growth performance is not necessary for strong final performance, and that simple, architecture-agnostic growth strategies can outperform more complex warmstarting operators. Crucially, we empirically identify an upper bound on the growth factor $g$ beyond which training from scratch is more efficient. We observe this across multiple ablation setups. Notably, this limit is also present, but unreported, in prior published results. Across our experiments on dense MLPs and dense language models, we find that a $2\times$ growth factor is the most reliable in yielding convergence speedups, with gains most pronounced under 20 tokens/parameter budgets and diminishing as budget increases. We fit scaling laws over these observations to provide predictive guidance for practitioners deciding when and how much to grow. Together, our analysis provides practical guidelines and empirical limits for model growth.

</details>


### [169] [Strategic PAC Learnability via Geometric Definability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13426)
*Yuval Filmus,Shay Moran,Elizaveta Nesterova,Nir Rosenfeld,Alexander Shlimovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出战略分类在一般情况下可能破坏可学习性（将有限VC维变为无限），并提出通过几何可定义性约束假设类和成本函数的方法，成功保障了战略环境下的学习可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究认为战略分类（即个体为了获取正面决策而改变特征）中的 induced hypothesis class 的复杂性在某些情况下可控，但在一般情况下，作者希望探讨战略行为是否会将简单的学习问题转化为不可学习的问题，并寻找保证可学习性的通用结构条件。

Method: 研究证明了战略诱导的假设类在一般情况下可能导致VC维无穷大；随后提出了一种“几何可定义性”假设，利用一阶公式描述假设空间和成本结构，结合模型论工具分析其样本复杂度。

Result: 1. 证明了对于一般的VC维为1的假设类，战略行为可能导致诱导出的假设类具有无穷大的VC维；2. 在几何可定义性假设下，证明了战略学习的样本复杂度取决于定义公式的复杂性，从而确保了可学习性。

Conclusion: 作者引入了基于一阶逻辑（在实数域 R_exp 上）的几何可定义性假设。在该假设下，证明了战略学习的复杂性（VC维）得到了有效控制，即只要底层假设类和成本结构具有良好的几何结构，战略分类问题依然是可学习的。

Abstract: Strategic classification studies learning settings in which individuals can modify their features, at a cost, in order to influence the classifier's decision. A central question is how the sample complexity of the induced (strategic) hypothesis class depends on the complexities of the underlying hypothesis class and the cost structure governing feasible manipulations. Prior work has shown that in several natural settings, such as linear classifiers with norm costs, the induced complexity can be controlled. We begin by showing that such guarantees fail in general - even in simple cases: there exist hypothesis classes of VC dimension $1$ on the real line such that, even under the simplest interval neighborhoods, the induced class has infinite VC dimension. Thus, strategic behavior can turn an easy learning problem into a non-learnable one. To overcome this, we introduce structure via a geometric definability assumption: both the hypothesis class and the cost-induced neighborhood relation can be defined by first-order formulas over $\mathbb{R}_{\mathtt{exp}}$. Intuitively, this means that hypotheses and costs can be described using arithmetic operations, exponentiation, logarithms, and comparisons. This captures a broad range of natural classes and cost functions, including $\ell_p$ distances, Wasserstein distance, and information-theoretic divergences. Under this assumption, we prove that learnability is preserved, with sample complexity controlled by the complexity of the defining formulas.

</details>


### [170] [Q-Flow: Stable and Expressive Reinforcement Learning with Flow-Based Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13435)
*JaeHyeok Doo,Byeongguk Jeon,Seonghyeon Ye,Kimin Lee,Minjoon Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-Flow 提出一种新颖的价值传播机制，在无需对数值求解器反向传播的情况下，解决了基于流的强化学习策略在表达能力与训练稳定性之间的矛盾，实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的强化学习策略在利用其高表达能力进行价值最大化时存在困难，因直接梯度优化需要对数值求解器进行反向传播，导致训练不稳定；而限制表达能力又会损害模型性能。

Method: 提出 Q-Flow 框架，利用流动力学的确定性特性，将终端轨迹的价值显式地传播到沿策略流的中间潜状态，从而避免了对数值求解器进行反向传播。

Result: 在 OGBench 离线强化学习基准测试中，Q-Flow 表现优于现有最优基准模型，平均提升了 10.6 个百分点，且在在线适应任务中表现出良好的稳定性。

Conclusion: Q-Flow 成功缓解了基于流的策略在强化学习中表现力和优化稳定性之间的权衡难题，通过直接在策略诱导的流上进行梯度传播，显著提升了离线学习的表现，并支持在线适应。

Abstract: There is growing interest in utilizing flow-based models as decision-making policies in reinforcement learning due to their high expressive capacity. However, effectively leveraging this expressivity for value maximization remains challenging, as naive gradient-based optimization requires backpropagating through numerical solvers and often leads to instability. Existing approaches typically address this issue by restricting the expressive capacity of flow-based policies, resulting in a trade-off between optimization stability and representational flexibility. To resolve this, we introduce Q-Flow, a framework that leverages the deterministic nature of flow dynamics to explicitly propagate terminal trajectory value to intermediate latent states along the policy-induced flow. This formulation enables stable policy optimization using intermediate value gradients without unrolling the numerical solver, effectively bridging the gap between stability and expressivity. We evaluate Q-Flow in the offline learning setting on the challenging OGBench suite, where it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by an average of 10.6 percentage points, while also enabling stable online adaptation within the same framework.

</details>


### [171] [Discovery of Hidden Miscalibration Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13484)
*Katarzyna Kobalczyk,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种发现LLM隐式局部误校准的诊断框架，通过学习输入空间的校准感知表示，有效地识别并修正了传统全局校准方法难以处理的特定输入区域的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的校准评估方法通常仅关注全局置信度，忽略了输入空间中存在的局部结构；模型可能在某些输入上过度自信，而在其他输入上欠自信，导致全局诊断掩盖了局部校准失败的问题。

Method: 提出了一种无需预定义数据切片的发现隐式误校准区域的诊断框架；通过学习输入空间的“校准感知（calibration-aware）”表示，并在学习到的几何空间内利用核平滑（kernel smoothing）来估计符号化的局部误校准（signed local miscalibration）。

Result: 在4个LLM基准测试和12个LLM模型上验证了输入依赖性校准异质性的普遍性；实验证明所挖掘的“误校准场”可用于局部置信度修正，在等渗回归和温度缩放等全局方法失效的区域能有效降低校准误差。

Conclusion: 该研究通过揭示LLM中普遍存在的输入依赖性校准异质性，并证明了基于“误校准场”的局部修正方法比全局校准方法（如温度缩放）更有效，从而为解决模型潜在的局部校准失败提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Calibration is commonly evaluated by comparing model confidence with its empirical correctness, implicitly treating reliability as a function of the confidence score alone. However, this view can hide substantial structure: models may be systematically overconfident on some kinds of inputs and underconfident on others, causing global reliability diagnostics to obscure localised calibration failures. To address this, we formulate the problem of discovering hidden miscalibration regimes without assuming access to predefined data slices. We define the corresponding miscalibration field and propose a diagnostic framework for estimating it. Our approach learns a calibration-aware representation of the input space and estimates signed local miscalibration by kernel smoothing in the learned geometry. Across four real-world LLM benchmarks and twelve LLMs, we find that input-dependent calibration heterogeneity is prevalent. We further show that the discovered fields are actionable: they support local confidence correction and reduce calibration error in systematically miscalibrated regions where confidence-based methods such as isotonic regression and temperature scaling are less effective.

</details>


### [172] [A Unified Three-Stage Machine Learning Framework for Diabetes Detection, Subtype Discrimination, and Cognitive-Metabolic Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13464)
*Vishal Pandey,Ruzina Haque Laskar,Rishav Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究开发并验证了一个三阶段机器学习框架，不仅实现了高精度的糖尿病预测，还通过无监督聚类发掘了糖尿病患者的潜在亚型，并量化了血糖控制与认知能力之间的显著相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前糖尿病研究多局限于二分类问题，且对亚型分类及血糖-认知相互作用的研究不足，旨在提供一个可重复、可解释的三阶段框架以推进糖尿病的精准医疗。

Method: 构建了一个包含三个阶段的机器学习框架：1. 监督学习（五种分类器+堆叠集成）进行糖尿病预测，并通过SHAP进行特征重要性解释；2. 基于K-Means的无监督聚类实现糖尿病患者的亚型划分；3. 基于多重假设检验校正的统计分析探索血糖与认知功能的相关性。

Result: 1. 预测阶段：SVM-RBF和逻辑回归实现最优ROC-AUC（0.825），随机森林准确率最高（0.762），且发现葡萄糖、BMI和年龄是关键预测特征；2. 聚类阶段：成功识别出具有临床意义的糖尿病亚型；3. 统计阶段：证实血糖控制与认知功能之间存在显著正相关。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了三阶段机器学习框架在糖尿病检测、亚型聚类及血糖-认知关联分析中的有效性，强调了使用可解释性模型（如SHAP）和统计严谨性对于临床辅助决策和糖尿病分型研究的重要性。

Abstract: Diabetes mellitus affects over 537 million adults worldwide and remains a major challenge in preventive healthcare. Existing machine-learning studies primarily formulate diabetes prediction as a binary classification problem, while subtype-oriented analysis and glycaemic-cognitive associations remain comparatively underexplored. We present a reproducible three-stage machine learning framework for diabetes detection, subtype-oriented clustering, and metabolic-cognitive association analysis. In Stage 1, five supervised classifiers together with a stacking ensemble are benchmarked on the NCSU Diabetes Dataset using stratified five-fold cross-validation and evaluation metrics including ROC-AUC, balanced accuracy, recall, and F1-score. SVM-RBF and Logistic Regression achieve the highest ROC-AUC ($0.825 \pm 0.026$), while Random Forest achieves the highest accuracy ($0.762 \pm 0.030$). SHAP explainability identifies Glucose, BMI, and Age as the dominant predictive biomarkers. In Stage 2, silhouette-validated K-Means clustering ($k=2$, silhouette $\approx 0.116$) is applied to confirmed diabetic cases using Glucose, Insulin, and Age, recovering clinically plausible subtype-oriented partitions without requiring ground-truth subtype labels. In Stage 3, statistical analysis of the Ohio Longitudinal Cognitive Dataset ($n=373$) reveals a significant positive association between glycaemic control and cognitive function ($ρ_s = 0.208$, $p = 5.29 \times 10^{-5}$), which survives Holm correction. The findings support the utility of statistically grounded and interpretable ML pipelines for reproducible diabetes analytics and subtype-aware exploratory analysis.

</details>


### [173] [Decoupled and Divergence-Conditioned Prompt for Multi-domain Dynamic Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13540)
*Haonan Yuan,Qingyun Sun,Junhua Shi,Xingcheng Fu,Jianxin Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个多领域动态图基础模型 DyGFM，通过解耦架构和提示学习解决了跨域动态图建模中的负迁移难题，实现了高效且通用的图学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的“预训练-微调”范式在多领域动态图建模中常导致严重的负迁移问题，且目前尚无通用的多领域动态图基础模型（GFM），难以处理不同领域间不一致的语义与时序模式。

Method: 1. 双分支预训练策略：实现语义与时序的解耦；2. 跨域路由机制：利用发散感知专家选择以减轻负迁移；3. 发散条件提示生成器：注入轻量级可学习图提示，实现高效微调。

Result: 在连续动态图基准测试中，DyGFM 在节点分类和链路预测任务上均超越了 12 个现有最优基线，展现出显著的效果和效率优势。

Conclusion: DyGFM 通过解耦语义与时序、引入跨域路由及发散条件提示机制，有效解决了多领域动态图预训练中的负迁移问题，为通用动态图基础模型提供了新框架，在多项基准测试中优于当前最优模型。

Abstract: Dynamic graphs are ubiquitous in real-world systems, and building generalizable dynamic Graph Foundation Models has become a frontier in graph learning. However, dynamic graphs from different domains pose fundamental challenges to unified modeling, as their semantic and temporal patterns are inherently inconsistent, making the multi-domain pre-training difficult. Consequently, the widely used "pretrain-then-finetune" paradigm often suffers from severe negative knowledge transfer. To the best of our knowledge, there exists no multi-domain dynamic GFM. In this work, we propose DyGFM, a Dynamic Graph Foundation Model over multiple domains based on decoupled and divergence-conditioned prompting. To disentangle transferable semantics from the domain-specific dynamics, we introduce a dual-branch pre-training strategy with semantic-temporal decoupling. To alleviate negative transfer during domain adaptation, we further develop a cross-domain routing mechanism with divergence-aware expert selection. To enable efficient downstream fine-tuning, we design a divergence-conditioned prompt generator that injects lightweight, learnable graph prompts tailored to semantic and temporal traits. Extensive experiments on continuous dynamic graph benchmarks demonstrate that DyGFM consistently outperforms 12 state-of-the-art baselines on both node classification and link prediction tasks, achieving superior effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [174] [Path-independent Flow Matching for Multi-parameter Generative Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13487)
*Francisco Téllez,AmirHossein Zamani,Philippe Martin,Shuang Ni,Guy Wolf,Eugene Belilovsky,Sina Sanjari,Yanlei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出路径无关流匹配 (PiFM)，通过多参数框架确保分布变换的路径独立性，并提供一种高效的无模拟训练方法，在 Wasserstein 重心近似与多变场景生成任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配主要针对单参数，无法满足多参数变换场景下的“路径无关”要求（即变换仅取决于起始分布和目标分布，与具体路径无关）。

Method: 提出路径无关流匹配（PiFM），在多维参数域上引入结构性约束以强制执行路径无关性；推导出便于训练的无模拟（simulation-free）目标函数，用于回归多参数条件概率路径。

Result: PiFM 实现了分布间的路径无关变换，能准确近似 Wasserstein 重心，并在生成出分布样本及路径插值任务中表现优于传统方法。

Conclusion: PiFM 成功将流匹配扩展至多参数领域，在保证路径无关性的同时实现了分布间的稳定插值，并为 Wasserstein 重心计算提供了一种高效的模拟无关（simulation-free）训练方案，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Flow Matching is a powerful framework for learning transport maps between probability distributions. Yet its standard single-parameter formulation is not designed to capture multi-parameter variations where the resulting transport should be path-independent. Path independence is crucial because it ensures that transformations depend only on the initial and target distributions, not on the specific path. In this work, we introduce Path-independent Flow Matching (PiFM), a method for learning vector fields whose induced flows yield path-independent transport between distributions. We show that PiFM generalizes Flow Matching to higher-dimensional parameter domains while enforcing structural conditions that ensure consistency of composed transformations. In addition, we show that, under suitable assumptions, PiFM approximates the Wasserstein barycenter, linking the framework to a notion of distributional interpolation. To enable practical training, we propose a tractable, simulation-free objective that regresses onto multi-parameter conditional probability paths. We showcase empirically that PiFM outperforms other approaches on both synthetic and real world data in interpolating path-independent trajectories and generating desired out of distribution samples.

</details>


### [175] [Self-Supervised On-Policy Reinforcement Learning via Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13554)
*Asim Osman,Sasha Abramowitz,Mark Bergh,Ulrich Armel Mbou Sob,Ruan John de Kock,Omayma Mahjoub,Oussama Hidaoui,Noah De Nicola,Arnol Manuel Fokam,Felix Chalumeau,Daniel Rajaonarivonivelomanantsoa,Siddarth Singh,Refiloe Shabe,Juan Claude Formanek,Simon Verster Du Toit,Arnu Pretorius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在线对比强化学习算法CPPO，消除了对人工奖励函数和经验回放缓冲区的依赖，在多种单/多智能体环境下展现了比传统CRL和PPO更优或相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对比强化学习（CRL）算法大多局限于离线策略（off-policy）训练和连续动作空间，与现代高效的在线策略（on-policy）训练流程（如PPO）脱节，尚无法充分应用到离散动作、单智能体及多智能体等广泛领域。

Method: 提出了一种全新的在线对比强化学习算法CPPO（Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation）。该方法直接从对比Q值中导出策略优势，并利用标准的PPO目标函数进行优化，彻底摆脱了对回放缓冲区（replay buffer）和人工定义奖励函数的依赖。

Result: 在18项包含连续与离散、单智能体与多智能体协作的任务中，CPPO在14项任务中显著优于既有CRL基线；与使用人工稠密奖励函数的标准PPO相比，在12项任务中表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: CPPO成功将对比强化学习（CRL）引入了策略梯度框架，证明了在无需人工设计奖励函数的情况下，通过对比学习获得的Q值可以有效指导策略训练，且在多种环境配置下表现优于现有CRL方法，甚至能媲美使用稠密奖励函数的基准PPO。

Abstract: Contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL) learns goal-conditioned Q-values through a contrastive objective over state-action and goal representations, removing the need for hand-crafted reward functions. Despite impressive success in achieving viable self-supervised learning in RL, all existing CRL algorithms rely on off-policy optimisation and are mostly constrained to continuous action spaces, with little research invested in discrete environments. This leaves CRL disconnected from widely used and effective, modern on-policy training pipelines adopted across both single-agent and multi-agent RL in continuous and discrete environments. To establish a first connection, we introduce Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation (CPPO). CPPO is an on-policy contrastive RL algorithm that derives policy advantages directly from contrastive Q-values and optimises them via the standard PPO objective, without requiring a reward function or a replay buffer. We evaluate CPPO across continuous and discrete, single-agent and cooperative multi-agent tasks. Whilst the existence of an on-policy approach is inherently useful, we observe that \textbf{CPPO not only significantly outperforms the previous CRL baselines in 14 out of 18 tasks, but also matches or exceeds PPO's performance, which uses hand-crafted dense rewards, in 12 out of the 18 tasks tested.}

</details>


### [176] [Dynamical Predictive Modelling of Cardiovascular Disease Progression Post-Myocardial Infarction via ECG-Trained Artificial Intelligence Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13568)
*Riccardo Cavarra,Lupo Lovatelli,Shaheim Ogbomo-Harmitt,Shahid Aziz,Adelaide De Vecchi,Andrew King,Oleg Aslanidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对医学领域ECG数据标注稀缺的问题，本研究提出了一种结合对比学习与多任务监督学习的预训练模型，大幅提升了心肌梗死术后的预后预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心肌梗死严重影响生命安全，但基于心电图（ECG）的传统深度学习模型在医学领域受限于标注数据稀缺，性能不佳。需要探索更有效的预训练策略来处理未标记的心电图数据。

Method: 提出了一种预训练AI模型：利用自监督对比学习整合患者特定的时序信息，并结合监督多任务学习头进行微调，专门用于心肌梗死后的预后预测。

Result: 在心肌梗死术后预后预测任务中，该模型与从零训练的模型相比，AUC显著提高（0.794 vs 0.608）。

Conclusion: 提出的结合对比学习与多任务监督学习的预训练模型，在极少标记数据的医学场景下，能显著提升心肌梗死（MI）预后预测的性能，优于从零训练的模型。

Abstract: Myocardial infarction (MI) is a leading cause of death, and its adverse outcomes are urgent to predict. Yet ECG-based prognostic models underperform because deep learning requires large, labelled datasets, which are scarce in medicine. Foundation models can learn from unlabelled ECGs via selfsupervision, but medically relevant training strategies remain underexplored. We propose a pretrained artificial intelligence model that combines patient-specific temporal information using contrastive learning with supervised multitask heads, then fine-tunes on post-MI outcome prediction. The proposed model outperformed a model trained from scratch (0.794 vs 0.608 AUC) showing that clinically structured ECG modelling improves classification in limited data regimes.

</details>


### [177] [Spatiotemporal downscaling and nowcasting of urban land surface temperatures with deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13566)
*Solomiia Kurchaba,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合静止卫星与极轨卫星数据，利用U-Net实现LST数据降尺度，再通过ConvLSTM模型实现了高时空分辨率（1km, 15min）的热环境短临预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的卫星地表温度（LST）产品无法兼顾高空间分辨率与高时间分辨率，存在显著的“时空权衡”问题，限制了其在城市气候和生态研究中的应用。

Method: 1. 使用U-Net架构将SEVIRI/MSG（空间分辨率较低）映射到Terra/Aqua MODIS（空间分辨率较高）数据，实现LST的时空降尺度；2. 使用ConvLSTM架构基于降尺度后的LST序列进行15至75分钟的短临预报（Nowcasting）。

Result: 1. 降尺度模型在测试集上实现了 RMSE = 1.92°C 和 MBE = 0.01°C；2. 短临预报模型在15-75分钟的预测范围内 RMSE 仅为 0.57-1.15°C，显著优于持久性预测和气候滚动中位数基准模型。

Conclusion: 该研究成功提出了一种结合静止卫星和极轨卫星数据产生高时空分辨率LST的方法，并验证了基于ConvLSTM的短临预报模型的有效性，实现了在1km/15min尺度下的高精度LST监测与预测。

Abstract: Land Surface Temperature (LST) is a key variable for various applications, such as urban climate and ecology studies. Yet, existing satellite-derived LST products provide either high spatial or high temporal resolution, resulting in a fundamental trade-off between the two. To address this trade-off, we combine observations from a geostationary and a polar orbiting satellite and provide LST fields at high spatial and high temporal resolution (1 km at 15-min intervals). We demonstrate their application for intraday forecasting of LSTs. To estimate LST fields at high spatiotemporal resolution, a U-Net model is trained to map LST fields from SEVIRI/MSG (3 km and 15 min resolution) to LST fields from Terra/Aqua MODIS (1 km, 4 overpasses per day) that are collocated in space and time. The presented model has been trained on LSTs across large European cities with a population exceeding 1 million inhabitants, and achieves an RMSE = $1.92$°C and near-zero bias MBE = $0.01$°C on the hold-out test set. As a second step, we present an LST nowcasting model based on ConvLSTM architecture, trained across downscaled LST fields with forecast lead times of 15 to 75 minutes. The nowcasting model outperforms a persistence and a Climatological Rolling Median benchmarks, with RMSEs of $0.57$ to $1.15$°C for the considered lead times and biases ranging from $-0.1$ to $0.14$°C. An additional validation conducted against independent MODIS overpasses confirms robust performance. Our LST forecast model at high spatiotemporal resolution is directly applicable to operational satellite-based LST monitoring.

</details>


### [178] [Robust and Explainable Bicuspid Aortic Valve Diagnosis Using Stacked Ensembles on Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13730)
*Christos Chrysanthos Nikolaidis,Vasileios Sachpekidis,Nikolas Moustakidis,Theofilos Moustakidis,Pavlos S. Efraimidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种AI集成模型，仅利用常规胸骨旁长轴（PLAX）超声电影序列，即可准确区分二叶式（BAV）与三叶式主动脉瓣（TAV），并通过Grad-CAM和SHAP实现了模型诊断过程的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经胸超声心动图（TTE）是诊断二叶式主动脉瓣（BAV）的一线手段，但其诊断结果极度依赖操作人员的专业水平和图像质量，因此需要AI辅助以提高诊断的稳定性和客观性。

Method: 开发了一种基于多主干视频集成学习（Multi-backbone video ensemble）的模型。模型采用了抗泄漏的分层外交叉验证（leakage-aware, stratified outer cross-validation）协议，并集成了Grad-CAM（帧级定位）和SHAP（贡献度量化）以提供模型的可解释性。

Result: 在90例患者研究中，校准后的集成模型取得了0.907的F1-score和0.877的召回率，证明了其在区分BAV与正常三叶式主动脉瓣（TAV）方面具有较高的性能。

Conclusion: 基于PLAX视频切片的AI模型能够准确识别二叶式主动脉瓣（BAV），具备临床辅助决策支持潜力，特别是在非专科或资源匮乏地区有助于实现早期诊断。

Abstract: Transthoracic echocardiography (TTE) is the first-line imaging modality for diagnosing bicuspid aortic valve (BAV), yet diagnostic performance varies with operator expertise and image quality. We developed an explainable AI model that distinguishes BAV from tricuspid aortic valves (TAV) using routinely acquired parasternal long-axis (PLAX) cine loops. A multi-backbone video ensemble was trained and evaluated using a leakage-aware, stratified outer cross-validation protocol on $N{=}90$ patient studies (48 BAV, 42 TAV). Across fixed outer splits and 10 random seeds, the calibrated stacked ensemble achieved an outer-CV F1-score of $0.907$ and recall of $0.877$. Frame-level Grad-CAM localized salient evidence to the aortic root and leaflet plane, while globally aggregated SHAP values quantified each video backbone's contribution to the stacked prediction, enabling transparent, case-level auditability. These findings indicate that PLAX-based video ensembles can support reliable BAV/TAV classification from routine echocardiographic cine loops and may facilitate earlier detection in non-specialist or resource-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [179] [High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13768)
*Or Ordentlich,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用协方差矩阵优化LLM权重量化的问题，证明了基于“注水算法”的速率分配策略能够使量化达到近最优水平，并对比分析了WaterSIC方案与GPTQ算法的性能优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）的权重量化过程中，利用权重的协方差矩阵信息（而非仅进行校准无关的量化）能进一步降低量化误差，接近理论失真极限。

Method: 将权重矩阵量化建模为加权均方误差（WMSE）源编码问题，并利用信息论中的“注水算法”（Waterfilling）来优化量化速率分配；分析了WaterSIC方案（标量INT量化器）在基变换下的鲁棒性及高码率性能。

Result: WaterSIC方法不仅实现了基变换无关性，且在高码率下极度接近理论失真极限（差距仅为0.25 bit/entry）；实验表明，GPTQ在应用随机旋转后，其性能与WaterSIC非常接近，误差仅在0.1 bit以内。

Conclusion: 在LLM权重量化中，利用协方差信息进行速率分配（水填充）是实现近最优量化的关键。WaterSIC方法在理论上已达到接近信息论失真极限的性能，而实践中常用的GPTQ（结合随机旋转）在性能上也表现出近乎最优的特性。

Abstract: This is the second part of the work investigating quantized matrix multiplication (MatMul). In part I we considered the case of calibration-free quantization, whereas here we discuss the setting where covariance matrix $Σ_X$ of the columns of the second factor is available. This setting arises in the ubiquitous task of weight-only post-training quantization of LLMs.
  Weight-only quantization is related to the problem of weighted mean squared error (WMSE) source coding, whose classical (reverse) waterfilling solution dictates how one should distribute rate between coordinates of the vector. We show how waterfilling can be used to improve practical LLM quantization algorithms (GPTQ), which at present allocate rate equally. A recent scheme (known as ``WaterSIC'') that only uses scalar INT quantizers is analyzed and its high-rate performance is shown to be (a) basis free (i.e., characterized by the determinant of $Σ_X$ and, thus, unlike existing schemes, is immune to applying random rotations); and (b) within a multiplicative factor of $\frac{2πe}{12}$ (or 0.25 bit/entry) of the information-theoretic distortion limit. GPTQ's performance, in turn, is affected by the choice of basis, but for a random rotation and actual $Σ_X$ from Llama-3-8B we find it to be within 0.1 bit (depending on the layer type) of WaterSIC, suggesting that GPTQ with random rotation is also near optimal, at least in the high-rate regime.

</details>


### [180] [ENSEMBITS: an alphabet of protein conformational ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13789)
*Kaiwen Shi,Carlos Oliver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ensembits 是首个对蛋白质构象系综进行标记的工具，通过蒸馏训练实现了从单体结构推断动态信息的潜能，填补了蛋白质语言模型对动力学建模的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的蛋白质结构标记器（PSTs）局限于静态局部几何，无法捕捉蛋白质的构象动力学、关联运动及多种构象状态。

Method: 提出了 Ensembits，一种基于残差向量量化变分自编码器（Residual VQ-VAE）的架构。核心技术创新包括：使用帧蒸馏（frame distillation）目标在分子动力学数据集上进行训练，实现对变长系综的置换不变编码，并具备从单体结构预测动态标记的能力。

Result: Ensembits 在 RMSF 预测任务中优于所有同类方法；在 per-residue 运动幅度的 ANOVA 测试中表现最强；在 EC、GO 分类、结合位点/亲和力预测及零样本突变效应预测任务中，能在训练数据量显著更少的情况下达到或超越现有静态标记器水平。

Conclusion: Ensembits 为蛋白质语言模型增加了动力学维度，是首个针对蛋白质构象系综的标记器。它证明了通过蒸馏目标可以从静态结构推断动态信息，为蛋白质设计从“静态”向“构象系综”的范式转移提供了核心工具。

Abstract: Protein structure tokenizers (PSTs) are workhorses in protein language modeling, function prediction, and evolutionary analysis. However, existing PSTs only capture local geometry of static structures, and miss the correlated motions and alternative conformational states revealed by protein ensembles. Here we introduce Ensembits, the first tokenizer of protein conformational ensembles. Ensembits address challenges inherent to tokenizing dynamics: deriving informative geometric descriptors across conformations, permutation-invariance encoding of variable-size ensembles, and conquering sparsity in dynamics data. Trained with a Residual VQ-VAE using a frame distillation objective on a large molecular dynamics corpus, Ensembits outperforms all related methods on RMSF prediction, and is the strongest standalone structural tokenizer on an token-conditioned ANOVA test on per-residue motion amplitude.
