Ensembits further matches or exceeds static tokenizers on EC, GO, binding site/affinity prediction, and zero-shot mutation-effect prediction despite using far less pretraining data. Notably, the distillation objective enables Ensembits to predict dynamics token from one single predicted structure, which alleviates dynamics data sparsity. As the field moves from static structure prediction toward ensemble generation, Ensembits offer the discrete vocabulary needed to bring dynamics into protein language modeling and design.

</details>


### [181] [Improving Reproducibility in Evaluation through Multi-Level Annotator Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13801)
*Deepak Pandita,Flip Korn,Chris Welty,Christopher M. Homan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对AI评估中的可重复性危机，研究通过多层自举方法建模标注员差异，提出了优化评估中样本量与标注密度权衡的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估领域存在可重复性危机，主要源于标注员的主观偏差和标准评估中较小的样本量及缺乏持久性标识符，导致模型评估结果不可靠。

Method: 利用带有大规模评分和持久性标注员标识符的数据集，提出了一种多层自举（multi-level bootstrapping）方法，用于模拟标注员行为并分析统计显著性所需的N与K的权衡。

Result: 该研究揭示了通过增加标注员一致性建模和合理分配标注资源，可以有效提升模型评估的可重复性和统计显著性。

Conclusion: 通过多层自举方法量化了N（样本量）和K（每个样本的标注量）之间的权衡，并强调了在评估中纳入持久性标注员标识符对于减少方差和提高结果可重复性的必要性。

Abstract: As generative AI models such as large language models (LLMs) become more pervasive, ensuring the safety, robustness, and overall trustworthiness of these systems is paramount. However, AI is currently facing a reproducibility crisis driven by unreliable evaluations and unrepeatable experimental results. While human raters are often used to assess models for utility and safety, they introduce divergent biases and subjective opinions into their annotations. Overcoming this variance is exceptionally challenging because very little data exists to study how experimental repeatability actually improves as the annotator pool grows. Standard evaluation practices typically rely on a small number of annotations per item (often 3 to 5) and lack the persistent rater identifiers necessary to model individual variance across items. In this work, we introduce a multi-level bootstrapping approach to realistically model annotator behavior. Leveraging datasets with a large number of ratings and persistent rater identifiers, we analyze the tradeoffs between the number of items ($N$) and the number of responses per item ($K$) required to achieve statistical significance.

</details>


### [182] [Scale-Sensitive Shattering: Learnability and Evaluability at Optimal Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13684)
*Shashaank Aiyer,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Han Shao,Tom Waknine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过引入新的覆盖数分析技术，证明了实值函数类在泛化与学习理论中的尺度灵敏等价性，解决了学习理论中关于精确增长率与学习界限的多个长期开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Anthony和Bartlett关于实值函数类学习理论中精确尺度界定的开放性问题，纠正关于乘法2因子间隙的猜测，并改进现有的上界。

Method: 引入对经验l∞覆盖数的直接界限分析，取代了传统的经过填充数（packing numbers）的间接推导；利用该方法建立了关于胖破碎维度（fat-shattering dimension）的精确度量熵界。

Result: 证明了对于实值类，特定尺度下的均匀收敛、不可知学习与胖破碎维的有限性在理论上是等价的；改善了度量熵上界（在γ/2尺度获得O(log^2 n)，在2γ尺度获得O(log n)）；建立了IPM可估计性的精确二分法（3-弱可评价性）。

Conclusion: 该研究统一了实值函数类学习理论中不同尺度之间的关系，闭合了PAC学习基本定理在实值情形下的尺度敏感版本，并解决了度量熵界和积分概率度量（IPM）可估计性方面的多个长期未决问题。

Abstract: We study the optimal scale at which real-valued function classes exhibit uniform convergence and learnability. Our main result establishes a scale-sensitive generalization of the fundamental theorem of PAC learning: for every bounded real-valued class and every $γ>0$, uniform convergence at scale $γ$, agnostic learnability at scale $γ/2$, and finiteness of the fat-shattering dimension at every scale $γ'>γ$ are equivalent. This resolves a question by Anthony and Bartlett (Cambridge Univ. Press 1999) on the precise scales governing learnability, refuting a conjecture attributed there to Phil Long that a multiplicative 2-factor gap is unavoidable, and improves the upper bounds of Bartlett and Long (JCSS 1998), which incur such a loss.
  The key technical ingredient is a direct bound on empirical $\ell_\infty$ covering numbers, avoiding the standard detour through packing numbers. As a consequence, we obtain sharp asymptotic metric-entropy bounds in terms of the fat-shattering scale $γ$: an $O(\log^2 n)$ bound holds already at scale $γ/2$, while an $O(\log n)$ bound holds at scale $2γ$. We further show that the $O(\log^2 n)$ bound is sometimes tight. These results resolve open questions by Alon et al. (JACM 1997) and Rudelson and Vershynin (Ann. of Math. 2006).
  As an application, we establish a sharp dichotomy for bounded integral probability metrics: every such IPM is either estimable or cannot be weakly evaluated within any multiplicative factor $c<3$, while $3$-weak evaluability always holds, resolving an open question from Aiyer et al. (ICML 2026). We also highlight several open questions on quantitative sample complexity and evaluability.

</details>


### [183] [Polyhedral Instability Governs Regret in Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13692)
*Yuetai Li,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Kaiyuan Zheng,Luyao Niu,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Linda Bushnell,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过引入“多面体不稳定性”指标（区域切换次数），推导出衡量组合优化决策遗憾的新理论界限，表明较低的结构切换频率可显著降低在线学习的遗憾，为高维组合优化问题提供了更紧的性能刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决组合动作在线决策问题中，现有凸松弛方法往往因维度灾难导致遗憾界过高的问题，寻找一种能够反映问题结构动力学特性（即区域稳定性）的更精确的遗憾分析框架。

Method: 提出基于多面体结构分析的复合遗憾界理论，通过计算有效区域切换次数（RS_T）来界定遗憾上界；针对子模-凹函数博弈，采用Lovász凸化技术并将复杂度归约为排列切换次数（SC_T）。

Result: 理论上证明了遗憾界为 Θ(sqrt((1+RS_T) * T * log V_max))；在子模-凹博弈中，遗憾界表现为 Θ(sqrt((1+SC_T) * T * log n))；实验结果表明，在最短路径和影响力最大化等实际问题中，系统表现出低不稳定性（低切换率），证明了该边界在实践中具有指导意义。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了组合动作在线决策问题中遗憾（Regret）与“多面体不稳定性”（即活动区域切换次数）之间的核心联系，证明了在全信息反馈下，遗憾界由区域切换次数及顶点数量决定，填补了专家模型与维度依赖优化模型之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: Many online decision problems over combinatorial actions are addressed via convex relaxations, leading to online convex optimization with piecewise linear objectives and induced polyhedral structure. We show that regret in such problems is governed by \emph{polyhedral instability}: the number of changes of the active region. Under full information feedback and fixed partition assumptions, if $\mathrm{RS}_T$ denotes the number of region switches and $V_{\max}$ the maximum number of vertices per region, we prove $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{RS}_T)\,T\,\log V_{\max}})$ interpolating between experts-like and dimension-dependent OCO rates. For online submodular--concave games under Lovász convexification, this reduces to the permutation-switch count $\mathrm{SC}_T$, yielding the matching rate $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{SC}_T)\,T\,\log n})$. Experiments on synthetic and real combinatorial problems (shortest path, influence maximization) validate the predicted scaling and indicate that low-instability regimes can arise in practice without explicit enumeration of actions.

</details>


### [184] [MILM: Large Language Models for Multimodal Irregular Time Series with Informative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13711)
*Hsing-Huan Chung,Shijun Li,Yoav Wald,Xing Han,Suchi Saria,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MILM通过将不规则时间序列转化为XML格式，利用LLM进行两阶段微调，有效捕捉了时间采样模式和观测数值中的预测信号，在医疗诊断任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）等多模态不规则时间序列包含重要的时间与通道模式，LLM具有强大的跨领域通用处理能力，但如何有效结合采样模式与观测数值是当前面临的挑战。

Method: 提出MILM模型，将MITS数据表征为XML格式的时间序列三元组，并采用两阶段微调策略：第一阶段仅基于采样模式进行预训练，第二阶段整合采样模式与观测数值进行联合训练。

Result: MILM在多个EHR数据集上取得了最优（MILM-2S）和次优（MILM-Direct）的分类性能。实验证明采样模式携带关键预测信号，且两阶段训练策略在值缺失场景下表现显著优于单阶段训练。

Conclusion: 利用LLM处理多模态不规则时间序列（MITS）是有效的。通过两阶段训练（先模式后数值）能使模型更好地捕获采样模式中蕴含的预测信号，特别是在观测值缺失的现实场景中表现出显著的鲁棒性和优势。

Abstract: Multimodal irregular time series (MITS) consist of asynchronous and irregularly sampled observations from heterogeneous numerical and textual channels. In healthcare, for example, patients' electronic health records (EHR) include irregular lab measurements and clinical notes. The irregular timing and channel patterns of observations carry predictive signal alongside the numerical values and textual content. LLMs are natural candidates for processing such heterogeneous data, given their extensive pretrained knowledge spanning textual and numerical domains. We introduce MILM (Multimodal Irregular time series Language Model), which represents MITS as time-ordered triplets in Extensible Markup Language (XML) format and fine-tunes an LLM through a two-stage strategy for MITS classification. The first stage trains on value-redacted MITS to predict from sampling patterns alone, and the second stage trains on full MITS to jointly model sampling patterns and observed values. Our two-stage model (MILM-2S) and its single-stage counterpart (MILM-Direct) achieve the best and second-best average performance on multiple EHR datasets. Further value redaction evaluations confirm that sampling patterns carry predictive signal and that MILM-2S learns to exploit them. In the value pending evaluation we introduce, where some values are unavailable at prediction time, MILM-2S outperforms MILM-Direct by a larger margin compared to standard evaluation. For MILM-2S, preserving the time and channel of value-pending observations as additional sampling information further improves in-hospital mortality prediction.

</details>


### [185] [Distinguishing performance gains from learning when using generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13731)
*Lixiang Yan,Samuel Greiff,Jason M. Lodge,Dragan Gašević*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 尽管生成式AI能提升学习产出，但由于缺乏对深度认知参与的培养，往往反而阻碍了真正的高质量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前教育中盲目整合生成式AI现象，作者指出过度依赖AI辅助可能导致“学习表层化”，忽略了深度学习所需的认知与元认知参与。

Method: 本文是对生成式AI在教育应用现状及其对学习过程影响的理论综述与批判性分析。

Result: 研究结果表明，当前的生成式AI应用范式虽然能够提升即时绩效（performance），但却削弱了学生进行深度思考（deep processing）和元认知监控（metacognitive monitoring）的主动性。

Conclusion: 生成式AI若未经过精心设计以促进认知参与，很难实现高质量学习目标的达成。该领域需要从单纯的工具集成转向旨在促进深度认知和元认知处理的教学策略开发。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly being integrated into education, where it can boost learners' performance. However, these uses do not promote the deep cognitive and metacognitive processing that are required for high-quality learning.

</details>


### [186] [Learning POMDP World Models from Observations with Language-Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13740)
*Valentin Six,Frederik Panse,Mathis Fajeau,Lancelot Da Costa,Mridul Sharma,Alfonso Amayuelas,Tim Z. Xiao,David Hyland,Philipp Hennig,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pinductor 是一种利用 LLM 先验学习 POMDP 世界模型的新框架，通过迭代优化极大地减少了模型训练所需的环境交互次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中，仅从观测-动作序列学习世界模型通常需要大量的环境交互，成本高昂；旨在探索利用语言模型先验来减少对环境交互的需求。

Method: 提出 Pinductor 框架：利用大语言模型（LLM）通过少量观测-动作轨迹提出候选 POMDP 模型，并基于信念优化（belief-based）似然得分进行迭代改进。

Result: Pinductor 在不依赖隐状态特权信息的情况下，达到了与已知隐状态的 LLM 方法相当的性能和样本效率，并显著优于传统的表格型 POMDP 基准；同时表现出良好的扩展性与稳健性。

Conclusion: 语言模型先验能够显著提升部分可观测环境下的世界模型学习效率，为构建通用智能体提供了新路径，且模型性能随LLM能力的增强而扩展。

Abstract: Whether navigating a building, operating a robot, or playing a game, an agent that acts effectively in an environment must first learn an internal model of how that environment works. Partially-observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) provide a flexible modeling class for such internal world models, but learning them from observation-action trajectories alone is challenging and typically requires extensive environment interaction. We ask whether language-model priors can reduce costly interaction by leveraging prior knowledge, and introduce \emph{Pinductor} (POMDP-inductor): an LLM proposes candidate POMDP models from a few observation-action trajectories and iteratively refines them to optimize a belief-based likelihood score. Despite using strictly less information, \emph{Pinductor} matches the performance and sample efficiency of LLM-based POMDP learning methods that assume privileged access to the hidden state, while significantly surpassing the sample efficiency of tabular POMDP baselines. Further results show that performance scales with LLM capability and degrades gracefully as semantic information about the environment is withheld. Together, these results position language-model priors as a practical tool for sample-efficient world-model learning under partial observability, and a step toward generalist agents in real-world environments. Code is available at https://github.com/atomresearch/pinductor.

</details>


### [187] [GHGbench: A Unified Multi-Entity, Multi-Task Benchmark for Carbon Emission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13743)
*Yifan Duan,Siyuan Zheng,Lihuan Li,Chao Xue,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GHGbench是一个专门针对公司和建筑级温室气体预测的统一开源基准平台，研究发现跨区域迁移是当前预测任务的瓶颈，并证实了多模态数据与表格基础模型在提升预测鲁棒性方面的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前实体级碳排放预测在数据获取、规模、粒度及评估标准方面存在的碎片化问题，旨在提供一个统一、规范化且具有挑战性的基准测试平台。

Method: 构建了包含公司和建筑两个轨道的开源数据集与基准测试（GHGbench），定义了同分布与跨区域/城市迁移任务，并对比了梯度提升树、表格基础模型、MLP、FT-Transformer、多模态融合及LLM辅助等多种基线模型，采用多种子配对引导（multi-seed paired-bootstrap）进行严格评估。

Result: 1. 建筑排放预测的挑战性高于公司排放；2. 模型在分布外（OOD）迁移的性能差距远大于模型内部的性能差距；3. 表格基础模型首次在多城市建筑排放任务中显著优于调优后的树模型；4. 遥感嵌入能有效弥补表格数据泛化能力的不足。

Conclusion: GHGbench揭示了现有模型在跨区域迁移中的系统性失灵（如灾难性迁移失败），强调了多模态遥感嵌入对提升泛化性能的重要性，并证明了表格基础模型在建筑排放预测任务中具备超越传统调优树模型的显著潜力。

Abstract: Open datasets and benchmarks for entity-level carbon-emission prediction remain fragmented across access, scale, granularity, and evaluation. We introduce GHGbench, an open dataset and benchmark for company- and building-level greenhouse-gas prediction. The company track contains 32,000+ company-year records from 12,000+ firms with Scope 1+2 and Scope 3 disclosures and financial/sectoral signals; the building track harmonises 491,591 building-year records from 13 open sources into a single schema across 26 metropolitan areas (10 U.S., 15 Australian, 1 Singaporean), with climate covariates and multimodal remote-sensing embeddings. GHGbench defines canonical splits with in-distribution and cross-region/city transfer as primary tasks and temporal hold-out plus short-horizon forecasting as supplementary appendix evidence; headline baselines span gradient-boosted trees, a tabular foundation model, MLP, FT-Transformer, and multimodal fusion, with an LLM panel as auxiliary, all evaluated under multi-seed paired-bootstrap tests. Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) building emissions are structurally harder than company emissions; (ii) the in-distribution to out-of-distribution gap dwarfs any within-model gap across both the company track and the building track, and a tabular foundation model is, to our knowledge, the first baseline to open a paired-bootstrap-significant gap over tuned trees on a multi-city building-emissions task; (iii) multimodal remote-sensing embeddings help precisely where tabular generalisation breaks. GHGbench also exposes catastrophic city transfer and the sector-factor lookup ceiling as systematic failure modes. Code and reconstruction recipes are available at GHGbench.

</details>


### [188] [Min Generalized Sliced Gromov Wasserstein: A Scalable Path to Gromov Wasserstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13753)
*Ashkan Shahbazi,Xinran Liu,Ping He,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入广义切片技术提出了一种高效的 min-GSGW 框架，通过学习非线性投影实现低成本的 Gromov--Wasserstein 距离求解，在保持刚体运动不变性的同时实现了卓越的几何匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) 求解器计算复杂度高，难以处理大规模几何匹配任务；传统的切片方法表达能力有限，无法完全捕捉几何结构信息。

Method: 提出 min-GSGW 框架，核心是学习成对的非线性切片器（generalized slicers），将输入测度投影到一维空间进行处理，并通过优化投影空间中的 monotone coupling 来导出原始空间中的传输计划，同时开发了无需每实例优化的摊销（amortized）变体。

Result: 在动物网格匹配、马匹网格插值及 ShapeNet 部件迁移等任务中，min-GSGW 显著降低了计算成本，并生成了高质量的几何对应关系。

Conclusion: min-GSGW 利用广义切片技术有效解决了 GW 问题，在保证刚体运动不变性的前提下，实现了比现有 GW 求解器更高效、准确的几何匹配与形状分析。

Abstract: We propose min Generalized Sliced Gromov--Wasserstein (min-GSGW), a sliced formulation for the Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) problem using expressive generalized slicers. The key idea is to learn coupled nonlinear slicers that assign compatible push-forward values to both input measures, so that monotone coupling in the projected domain lifts to a transport plan evaluated against the GW objective in the original spaces. The resulting plan induces a GW objective value, and min-GSGW minimizes this cost directly in the original spaces. We further show that min-GSGW is rigid-motion invariant, a crucial property for geometric matching and shape analysis tasks. Our contributions are threefold: 1) we introduce generalized slicers into the sliced GW framework, 2) we construct a slicing-based efficient GW transport plan; and 3) we develop an amortized variant that replaces per-instance optimization with a learned slicer for unseen input pairs. We perform experiments on animal mesh matching, horse mesh interpolation, and ShapeNet part transfer. Results show that min-GSGW produces meaningful geometric correspondences and GW objective values at substantially lower computational cost than existing GW solvers.

</details>


### [189] [Fast and effective algorithms for fair clustering at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13759)
*Claudio Mantuano,Manuel Kammermann,Philipp Baumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对聚类成本与公平性之间的冲突，本文提出了一个灵活且具扩展性的公平聚类通用框架，并提供了三种针对不同应用场景（质量导向、均衡导向、性能导向）的启发式算法，有效解决了现有方法在大规模场景下的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类算法在涉及个人（如客户、员工等）的公平敏感领域应用日益广泛，但现有的公平聚类方法在成本与公平性的权衡控制上能力有限，且在处理大规模数据集时往往存在扩展性差或解质量低的问题。

Method: 提出了一种包含公平聚类控制的通用框架，并基于此开发了三种启发式算法：第一种侧重解的质量与约束灵活性；第二种在保持高质量的同时提升扩展性；第三种专注于极高扩展性，能够在秒级处理百万级数据。

Result: 实验表明，所提三种启发式算法在基准数据集上的性能优于现有主流方法，并成功实现了对成本-公平性权衡的精确调节，同时具备高效处理百万级数据的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的公平聚类通用框架及三种启发式算法在控制成本与公平性权衡方面表现优异，并在处理从中小规模到数百万级数据规模的聚类问题时，在质量和效率上均超越了现有方法。

Abstract: Clustering is an unsupervised machine learning task that consists of identifying groups of similar objects. It has numerous applications and is increasingly used in fairness-sensitive domains where objects represent individuals, such as customers, employees, or students. We address a fair clustering problem in which objects belong to protected groups. The problem consists of partitioning the objects into a predefined number of clusters while attaining a user-defined target level of fairness, meaning that each protected group is sufficiently represented in each cluster. The objective is to minimize the clustering cost, defined as the sum of squared Euclidean distances between the objects and the centers of their clusters. Since clustering cost and fairness are generally in conflict, managing the trade-off between them is essential in practical applications. Existing methods provide limited control over this trade-off and either fail to scale to large datasets or, when they scale, produce low-quality solutions. We propose a general framework for fair clustering that provides precise control over the cost-fairness trade-off and introduce three heuristics based on it. The first heuristic focuses on solution quality and the flexibility to incorporate additional constraints, the second improves scalability while retaining high solution quality, and the third is designed for maximum scalability, producing solutions for instances with millions of objects in seconds. The proposed heuristics outperform existing approaches in comprehensive numerical experiments on benchmark datasets. The source code of our heuristics and instructions for reproducing the experiments are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [190] [Toward AI-Driven Digital Twins for Metropolitan Floods: A Conditional Latent Dynamics Network Surrogate of the Shallow Water Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13761)
*Phillip Si,Yuan Qiu,Omar Sallam,Jeremy Feinstein,Ziang He,Eugene Yan,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决水动力模拟计算缓慢的问题，本文提出了一种基于神经ODE和坐标解码的深度学习模型CLDNet，实现了比传统求解器快115倍的城市级洪水快速预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的二维浅水方程（SWE）求解器在城市尺度上的计算开销巨大（例如96小时预测需耗时约55分钟），无法满足洪水数字孪生对快速集合预报和数据同化的需求。

Method: 提出 Conditional Latent Dynamics Network (CLDNet)：一种基于潜在神经ODE（Latent Neural ODE）的降维建模方法，结合了基于坐标的解码器（Coordinate-based decoder）。利用降雨驱动动力学，并整合地形静态特征进行解码，从而实现任意点的深度和流量重构，摆脱了对规则网格的依赖。

Result: CLDNet 将96小时的流域预报时间从55分钟缩短至29秒（加速115倍）；在德克萨斯州基准测试中优于VAE-ConvLSTM和FNO基准模型；在Des Plaines流域案例中实现了86%的临界成功指数。

Conclusion: CLDNet 实现了水动力模拟的重大加速（约115倍），同时保持了较高的预测精度，能够处理复杂且不规则的流域，是实现实时洪水数字孪生和集合预报的有效解决方案。

Abstract: AI-driven flood digital twins demand fast hydrodynamic surrogates for ensemble forecasting and observation assimilation. Yet even GPU-accelerated two-dimensional shallow water equation (SWE) solvers still require $\sim 55$ minutes per $96$-hour run on a $\sim 4.2$-million-active-cell metropolitan basin (the Des~Plaines River basin at $30\,\mathrm{m}$ resolution), making such workloads prohibitive at native resolution. We present the Conditional Latent Dynamics Network (CLDNet): a low-dimensional latent neural ODE driven by rainfall, paired with a coordinate-based decoder conditioned on static terrain (elevation, slope, Manning roughness) that reconstructs depth and discharge at arbitrary query points. Pointwise decoding decouples memory from grid size and handles irregular watersheds natively, enabling metropolitan-scale training on a single compute node and direct queries at exact gauge coordinates without raster snapping. We evaluate CLDNet on a synthetic $250{,}000$-cell Texas benchmark and on a new Des~Plaines case study of $114$ real-rainfall Stage~IV storms whose reference simulator we validate against United States Geological Survey (USGS) gauges at the April~2013 flood-of-record (Nash--Sutcliffe efficiency $0.57$--$0.94$ on mean-recentered water-surface elevation). CLDNet roughly halves the relative root-mean-squared error of an unconditional baseline, outperforms regular-grid VAE--ConvLSTM and FNO baselines on the Texas benchmark (both presuppose a Cartesian grid and do not apply to the irregular Des~Plaines watershed), reaches a critical success index of $\approx 86\%$ at the $0.5\,\mathrm{m}$ inundation threshold, and produces a full $96$-hour basin-wide forecast in $\sim 29$ seconds -- a $\sim 115\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [191] [Attention Once Is All You Need: Efficient Streaming Inference with Stateful Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13784)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向流式数据的有状态Transformer推理引擎，通过持久化KV缓存和空闲周期预计算，解决了传统引擎在高上下文负载下预填充开销过大的问题，实现了性能的大幅提升与低延迟查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer推理引擎是请求驱动的，每次查询需支付O(n)的预填充（prefill）成本，在处理持续增长数据的流式工作负载时，这种开销导致极高的延迟。

Method: 1. 采用基于有状态会话（Stateful Sessions）的数据驱动计算模型，维护持久化KV缓存；2. 引入Flash Queries技术，利用空闲GPU周期预评估查询；3. 实现多租户连续批处理调度器，支持单元预算准入和前缀感知分组预填充（prefix-aware grouped prefill）。

Result: 在流式市场数据基准测试中，相比vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM和llama.cpp等传统引擎，实现了最高5.9倍的性能提升，并将查询延迟与积累上下文大小解耦。

Conclusion: 通过引入有状态会话模型和Flash Queries预计算机制，在保证全二次自注意力机制精度的前提下，实现了流式工作负载下的恒定查询延迟，显著优于传统的无状态推理引擎。

Abstract: Conventional transformer inference engines are request-driven, paying an O(n) prefill cost on every query. In streaming workloads, where data arrives continuously and queries probe an ever-growing context, this cost is prohibitive. We introduce a data-driven computational model centred on stateful sessions: a persistent KV cache advanced incrementally as new data arrives, so prefill is moved off the critical path and query latency becomes O(|q|), independent of accumulated context size. Building on this, Flash Queries reclaim idle GPU cycles between data arrivals to pre-evaluate registered questions and return cached answers before the user asks, a pattern that is structurally impossible in stateless engines because they discard intermediate state between requests. A multi-tenant continuous-batching scheduler with cell-budget admission and prefix-aware grouped prefill lets dozens of stateful sessions coexist on a single GPU while preserving full quadratic self-attention. On streaming market-data benchmarks the reference implementation achieves up to 5.9x speedup over conventional inference engines (vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, llama.cpp), holding query latency constant as accumulated context grows.

</details>


### [192] [Interpretable Machine Learning for Antepartum Prediction of Pregnancy-Associated Thrombotic Microangiopathy Using Routine Longitudinal Laboratory Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13786)
*Chuanchuan Sun,Zhen Yu,Qin Fan,Qingchao Chen,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于常规实验室指标的机器学习模型，可有效区分P-TMA患者与正常妊娠，并识别出孕早期胱抑素C作为关键风险指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P-TMA起病隐匿，其临床表现（血小板减少、蛋白尿）与正常妊娠生理变化高度重叠，常规诊断方法难以实现早期预测。

Method: 回顾性研究，纳入142例P-TMA患者及158例对照，利用146个纵向实验室指标，对比五种机器学习算法，最终选定梯度提升（Gradient Boosting）模型。

Result: 梯度提升模型在独立测试集中表现优异，AUROC为0.872，AUPRC为0.883，敏感性为0.750，特异性为0.812。

Conclusion: 利用常规纵向实验室检查数据，机器学习模型能有效预测妊娠相关血栓性微血管病（P-TMA）风险，其中孕6周的胱抑素C具有早期监测价值。

Abstract: Background: Pregnancy-associated thrombotic microangiopathy (P-TMA) is rare but life-threatening. Early risk prediction before overt clinical presentation remains challenging, as the associated laboratory abnormalities are subtle, multidimensional, and frequently masked by common physiological changes such as gestational thrombocytopenia and pregnancy-related proteinuria, thus overlapping heavily with benign obstetric and renal conditions. This complexity is poorly captured by univariate or rule-based approaches; however, it is addressable by machine learning, which can extract latent, time-dependent risk signatures from longitudinal clinical tests. Methods: This retrospective study included 300 pregnancies comprising 142 P-TMA cases and 158 controls. After exclusion of identifiers and non-informative variables, 146 longitudinal laboratory predictors were retained. Participants were divided into a training cohort (80%) and a held-out test cohort (20%) using stratified sampling. Five algorithms were evaluated: logistic regression, support vector machine with radial basis function kernel, random forest, extra trees, and gradient boosting. The final model was selected by mean cross-validated AUROC, refitted on the full training cohort, and evaluated once in the held-out test cohort. Interpretability analyses examined global feature importance and distributional patterns of leading predictors. Results: Gradient boosting was prespecified by cross-validation in the training cohort. The model achieved an AUROC of 0.872 (95% CI: 0.769-0.952) and an AUPRC of 0.883 (95% CI: 0.780-0.959) in a held-out test cohort, with sensitivity of 0.750 and specificity of 0.812. Conclusions: Longitudinal clinical laboratory tests obtained during routine care contained informative and clinically plausible signals for P-TMA risk. Notably, cystatin C at week 6 showed promise as an early monitoring indicator.

</details>


### [193] [Force-Aware Neural Tangent Kernels for Scalable and Robust Active Learning of MLIPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13788)
*Eszter Varga-Umbrich,Zachary Weller-Davies,Paul Duckworth,Jules Tilly,Olivier Peltre,Shikha Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于力感知神经正切核（NTK）和线性筛选框架的MLIP主动学习方法，解决了大规模筛选中的效率、信息利用率及分布偏移鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习原子间势（MLIP）的主动学习难以同时解决算力扩展性、力场信息融合及候选池偏差分布下的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 1. 提出了一种基于分块特征空间后验方差筛选的线性扩展获取框架；2. 将神经正切核（NTK）扩展至力感知领域，提出了力NTK及联合能量-力NTK作为相似性度量。

Result: 在OC20数据集上，所提方法实现了最低的能量和力MAE/RMSE；在T1x、PMechDB和RGD基准测试中表现优异且效率远高于委员会方案；在候选池偏差实验中展现出比传统方法更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，利用预训练的MLIP结合NTK技术，可以实现可扩展、具备受力感知及分布鲁棒性的主动学习，为基础模型的微调提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Active learning for machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) must address several challenges to be practical: scaling to large candidate pools, leveraging energy-force supervision, and maintaining robustness when candidate pools are biased relative to the target distribution. In this work, we jointly address these challenges. We first introduce a linearly scaling acquisition framework based on chunked feature-space posterior-variance shortlisting. By avoiding materialisation of the candidate and train set kernels, this approach enables screening of ~200k structures within hours and applies broadly to acquisition strategies that score candidates based on molecular similarity metrics. We then extend the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) to a force-aware setting via mixed parameter-coordinate derivatives, yielding a force NTK and a joint energy-force NTK that provide natural similarity metrics for vector-field prediction. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the joint energy-force NTK on the OC20 dataset, where force-aware acquisition is crucial: it achieves the lowest energy and force MAE and RMSE across all metrics and distribution splits. Across T1x, PMechDB, and RGD benchmarks, our force NTK methods remain competitive with established baselines while being significantly more efficient than committee-based approaches. Under a controlled candidate-pool shift case study on T1x, acquisition based on pretrained MLIP embeddings and NTKs remains robust, whereas committee-based methods exhibit higher variance. Overall, these results show that a single pretrained MLIP can enable scalable, force-aware, and distribution-robust active learning for foundation-model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [194] [Uncertainty-Driven Anomaly Detection for Psychotic Relapse Using Smartwatches: Forecasting and Multi-Task Learning Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13816)
*Nikolaos Tsalkitzis,Panagiotis P. Filntisis,Petros Maragos,Niki Efthymiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Transformer架构和多源数字表型（心脏、运动、睡眠）的智能手表监测框架，通过后期融合策略提升了精神病复发预警的准确度，在基准测试中超越了现有最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决现有精神病复发早期检测方法在现实环境下的局限性，利用数字表型技术进行持续、被动的行为与生理监测，以提升预警的准确性和稳定性。

Method: 开发了两种基于智能手表的深度学习框架：一是基于心脏动力学预测的异常检测，二是融合睡眠、运动和心脏信号的多任务学习模型。两者均利用Transformer编码器，并使用MLP集成来估计不确定性，最后通过后期融合（Late-fusion）策略结合两种架构的异常得分。

Result: 在第2届e-Prevention大挑战数据集上，该融合模型表现优于竞赛获胜基准，相对提升了8%。消融实验证实了所整合多种表型数据的有效性。

Conclusion: 整合心脏、运动和睡眠等多种数字表型数据，对于在现实场景中高保真地检测精神病复发至关重要。提出的融合框架能够捕获互补的生理特征，从而提供更稳健、更准确的预测。

Abstract: Digital phenotyping enables continuous passive monitoring of behavior and physiology, offering a promising paradigm for early detection of psychotic relapse. In this work, we develop and systematically study two smartwatch-based frameworks for daily relapse detection. The first forecasts cardiac dynamics and flags deviations between predicted and observed features as indicators of abnormality. The second adopts a multi-task formulation that fuses sleep with motion and cardiac-derived signals, learning time-aware embeddings and predicting measurement timing. Both pipelines use Transformer encoders and output a daily anomaly score, derived from predictive uncertainty estimated via an ensemble of multilayer perceptrons to improve robustness to real-world wearable variability. While each framework independently demonstrates strong predictive power, we show that they capture complementary physiological signatures. Consequently, we propose a late-fusion strategy that synergistically combines the anomaly signals from both architectures into a unified decision score. We benchmark our methodology on the 2nd e-Prevention Grand Challenge dataset, where our fused model achieves a 8% relative improvement over the competition-winning baseline. Our results, supported by extensive ablation studies, suggest that the integration of diverse digital phenotypes, cardiac, motion, and sleep, is essential for the high-fidelity detection of psychotic relapse in real-world settings.

</details>


### [195] [Reducing cross-sample prediction churn in scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13826)
*Gordan Prastalo,Kevin Maik Jablonka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了科学机器学习模型在不同训练采样下的预测不一致性（churn），并提出通过 Twin-bootstrap 联合训练方法显著降低该分歧，提高模型预测的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学机器学习评估过于侧重“预测准确率”，忽视了模型的“稳定性”——即模型对不同训练数据采样的敏感度。即使多个模型准确率相似，它们在具体样本上的标签预测可能会出现严重分歧。

Method: 1. 定义了“跨样本预测波动”指标：衡量在同一数据集的不同重采样（bootstrap）下，模型在测试集预测结果上的分歧程度。
2. 提出了降低 churn 的两种方法：
   - K-bootstrap bagging：通过多次重采样集成降低波动。
   - Twin-bootstrap：一种联合训练架构，引入对称KL散度（sym-KL consistency loss）约束，强制两个模型在不同重采样下保持预测一致性。

Result: 1. 在9个化学基准测试中，模型在准确率上差异仅 1.3-4.2%，但在单个样本上的标签预测分歧高达 8.0-21.8%。
2. 常规参数侧方法（如 Deep Ensembles, MC Dropout）无法有效抑制该波动。
3. K-bootstrap bagging 可降低 40-54% 的 churn。
4. Twin-bootstrap 在同等计算量下，比 bagging (K=2) 进一步降低了 45% 的中位数波动，且不损失准确率。

Conclusion: 跨样本预测波动（Cross-sample prediction churn）是评估科学机器学习模型时应与预测准确率并列的重要指标。现有参数侧方法效果有限，本文提出的数据侧方法（尤其是twin-bootstrap）能显著降低波动，提高预测稳定性。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning reports predictive performance. It does not report whether the same prediction would survive a different draw of training data. Across $9$ chemistry benchmarks, two classifiers trained on independent bootstraps of the same training set agree on aggregate accuracy to within $1.3\text{--}4.2$ percentage points but disagree on the class label of $8.0\text{--}21.8\%$ of test molecules. We call this gap \emph{cross-sample prediction churn}. The standard parameter-side techniques (deep ensembles, MC dropout, stochastic weight averaging) do not reduce this gap; two data-side methods do. The first is $K$-bootstrap bagging, which cuts the rate $40\text{--}54\%$ on every dataset at no accuracy cost ($K{\times}$-ERM compute). The second is \emph{twin-bootstrap}, our proposal: two networks trained jointly on independent bootstraps with a sym-KL consistency loss between their predictions, which at matched $2{\times}$-ERM compute reduces churn a further median $45\%$ beyond bagging-$K{=}2$. Cross-sample prediction churn deserves a column alongside predictive performance in scientific-ML benchmark reports, because without it the parameter-side and data-side methods are indistinguishable on the metric they actually differ on.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [OxyEcomBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Foundation Models across E-Commerce Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13173)
*Yong Liu,Ximan Liu,Guoqing Yang,Bing Bai,Xiaoqiang Xu,Zhen Chen,Ke Zhang,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对电商领域大模型性能提升难的问题，本文提出了OxyEcomBench基准测试集，通过涵盖电商全生态的真实场景评估，揭示了通用大模型在电商专业知识应用上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型（LLMs）与多模态模型（MLLMs）在通用领域表现出色，但在电商场景因复杂的领域知识、长尾产品证据、异构视觉数据及多方利益相关者需求，存在显著的性能落地鸿沟，且现有评测集无法全面评估这些挑战。

Method: 构建了OxyEcomBench基准测试集，涵盖双语（中英）、多模态（单/多图）及多轮对话；包含3种核心角色（平台、商家、顾客）、6个能力维度、29个任务，共6,300个经过专家验证的真实电商案例，并应用了P0-P3四级难度等级评估体系。

Result: 对20个主流大模型和多模态模型进行测试，结果显示顶尖模型在OxyEcomBench上的表现依然中规中矩，证明了通用模型在电商特定知识注入方面的缺失导致了性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通用大模型在电商领域的表现受限于特定领域知识的匮乏，现有通用模型难以直接填补该专业领域的性能鸿沟，业界需要针对电商场景进行更深入的知识注入与优化。

Abstract: LLMs and MLLMs have become indispensable tools across a wide range of applications. E-commerce, however, poses distinctive challenges -- including intricate domain knowledge, long-tail product evidence, heterogeneous visual data, and the interplay among multiple stakeholder roles -- that diverge substantially from the general world knowledge these models are primarily trained on, often causing a notable gap between their open-domain and e-commerce performance. To systematically quantify this gap, we introduce OxyEcomBench, a unified multimodal benchmark comprising approximately 6,300 high-quality instances for real-world bilingual Chinese--English e-commerce. Although several e-commerce benchmarks have been proposed, they typically adopt a single stakeholder perspective, target a narrow set of tasks, or address isolated challenges, making it difficult to holistically assess models' understanding of the full e-commerce pipeline. OxyEcomBench addresses these limitations by jointly covering platform operators, merchants, and customers across 6 capability aspects and 29 tasks, supporting text-only and mixed-modality inputs with single-image, multi-image, single-turn, and multi-turn configurations. All data is sourced from authentic e-commerce platforms and verified by domain experts. The benchmark further adopts a difficulty-aware design with a four-level P0--P3 rubric applied to all 29 tasks whose difficulty admits stable expert consensus, and rigorously prioritizes visually salient multimodal cases in which key evidence resides in images rather than text alone. Evaluations on 20 mainstream LLMs and MLLMs show that even the leading models attain modest performance and that performance gaps narrow on OxyEcomBench, suggesting that insufficient e-commerce-specific knowledge infusion mutes the advantages of advanced general-purpose models in this domain.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [SPLIT: SymPathy for Large jobs Improves Tail latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13749)
*Zhouzi Li,Mor Harchol-Balter,Alan Scheller-Wolf*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 该研究解决了M/G/n多服务器队列在大尾作业下的响应时间尾部优化问题，发现与单服务器系统不同，多服务器系统需要对大作业提供一定的“同情”（资源倾斜）才能实现强尾优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单服务器系统中强尾优策略（如SRPT）的研究已很成熟，但对于现代计算工作负载中常见的多服务器系统（M/G/n队列），在大尾分布作业下的调度优化问题长期未得到解决。

Method: 提出并研究了针对M/G/n队列的调度策略，探讨了在已知及未知作业长度情况下的强尾优性（strong tail optimality），并引入了对大作业分配资源的机制。

Result: 首次给出了M/G/n队列在大尾分布作业下的强尾优或近似强尾优调度策略，证明了在多服务器场景下，为了达到最优尾部响应时间，必须允许大作业在一定程度上共享资源。

Conclusion: M/G/n多服务器队列中，强尾优策略必须对大作业表现出适度的“同情”（sympathy），即不能盲目地只考虑短作业，这与单服务器系统的结论存在本质差异。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic response time tail in the M/G/n multi-server queue with heavy-tailed (regularly varying) job sizes, a setting representative of modern computing workloads. For single-server systems, tail optimization is well understood: under heavy-tailed job sizes, policies such as SRPT that strictly prioritize short jobs are strongly tail optimal, and giving any priority to large jobs is harmful. For multi-server systems, the question has been almost entirely open.
  This paper gives the first strongly tail-optimal scheduling policies for the M/G/n queue with heavy-tailed job sizes. Our central finding is that the multi-server case is intrinsically different from the single-server case: giving a small amount of ``sympathy'' to large jobs is essential for strong tail optimality. We establish strong (or arbitrarily close to strong) tail optimality across the full stability region, both with and without knowledge of job sizes.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Beyond Centralization: User-Controlled Federated Recommendations in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12527)
*Manel Slokom,Alejandro Bellogin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出并实地部署了一个具备隐私保护（联邦学习）和用户控制功能的推荐系统，证明了用户在拥有控制权时不仅能维持推荐效果，且能提升参与度和满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统依赖集中式数据导致隐私泄露并限制了用户控制权；虽然联邦学习能保持本地化数据，但其在真实用户场景下的表现和用户行为交互研究尚存空白。

Method: 设计并部署了一个实时的联邦推荐系统。该系统利用联邦学习技术在本地处理数据以保护隐私，并集成了用户控制机制，允许用户在“个性化”与“多样性增强”排名目标之间进行切换。开展了一项为期53天的实地研究，共22名参与者，涵盖8807个商品目录。

Result: 1. 当拥有明确选择权时，用户表现出对个性化的偏好（CTR：65.37% vs. 62.07%）；2. 用户积极使用控制机制（满意度评分3.93/5，共发生248次设置更改）；3. 用户通过即时反馈能够有效理解其交互如何影响推荐结果。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了用户控制权、隐私保护与高效个性化推荐可以在实际系统中协同工作，为交互式隐私保护推荐系统提供了一种可行的实践方法。

Abstract: Recommendation systems typically require centralized user data, limiting user control and raising privacy concerns. Federated learning offers an alternative by keeping data on-device, but its impact on real user behavior remains largely unexplored. We present a live federated recommender system that allows users to control the recommendation objective while keeping their data local. In a 53-day deployment with 22 participants and a catalog of 8807 titles, users interacted with recommendations and switched between personalization and diversity-enhanced ranking. We find that users prefer personalization when given explicit choice (65.37\% vs.\ 62.07\% CTR), actively engage with control mechanisms (3.93/5 satisfaction; 248 settings changes), and develop an understanding of how their interactions affect recommendations through immediate feedback. Our results show that user control, privacy, and effective personalization can be combined in a working system. We demonstrate a practical approach to interactive, privacy-preserving recommendation. Code and demo materials are available at: https://github.com/SlokomManel/federated-recommendations-participants

</details>


### [199] [MLPs are Efficient Distilled Generative Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12617)
*Zitian Guo,Yupeng Hou,Clark Mingxuan Ju,Neil Shah,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 为了解决生成式推荐模型在推理时的延迟瓶颈，本文提出了 SID-MLP，通过一种轻量级的 MLP 蒸馏范式取代了冗余的 Transformer 层级解码，实现了推理速度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 Semantic ID 的生成式推荐模型，其实际部署受限于自回归解码（Beam Search）带来的高推理延迟；指出层级化的 ID 结构使得后续 Token 预测并不需要复杂的 Transformer 注意力机制。

Method: 提出了 SID-MLP 框架：将重型自回归教师模型的知识蒸馏至位置特定的 MLP 头中，通过单次全局上下文捕捉替代复杂的逐点注意力机制；并进一步提出 SID-MLP++，通过替换 Transformer 编码器实现更深度的性能优化。

Result: SID-MLP 相比于教师模型，在保持同等预测精度的前提下，实现了 8.74 倍的推理加速，且具有极强的兼容性和即插即用特性。

Conclusion: 针对生成式推荐系统，通过蒸馏将重型 Transformer 解码器替换为轻量级 MLP 头，在保持高预测精度的同时，显著降低了推理延迟，验证了 MLP 蒸馏作为结构化 Semantic ID 推荐加速方案的有效性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation models employing Semantic IDs (SIDs) exhibit strong potential, yet their practical deployment is bottlenecked by the high inference latency of beam-expanded autoregressive decoding. In this work, we identify that standard attention-heavy Transformer decoders represent a structural overkill for this task: the hierarchical nature of SIDs makes prediction difficulty drops sharply after the first token, rendering repeated attention computations highly redundant. Driven by this insight, we propose SID-MLP, a lightweight MLP-centric distillation framework that fundamentally simplifies the decoding paradigm for GR. Instead of executing complex, step-by-step attention mechanisms, our approach captures the global user context in a single operation, decoupled from sequential token prediction. We then distill the heavy autoregressive teacher into position-specific MLP heads, eliminating the dense attention overhead while preserving prefix and context dependencies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SID-MLP matches the accuracy of teacher models while accelerating inference by 8.74x. Crucially, this distillation strategy can serve as a plug-and-play accelerator for different backbones and tokenizer settings. Furthermore, we introduce SID-MLP++, extending our distillation framework to replace the Transformer encoder, unlocking further latency reductions. Ultimately, our work reveals that decoder-side MLPs distillation is an effective acceleration path for structured SID recommendation, while full encoder replacement offers an additional speed--accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [200] [EcoGEO: Trajectory-Aware Evidence Ecosystems for Web-Enabled LLM Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12887)
*Hengwei Ye,Jiasheng Mao,Zhenhan Guan,Zheng Tian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了EcoGEO范式，通过TRACE框架协调网页间的结构与内容，不仅优化单页，更通过塑造智能体的浏览轨迹来提升生成式搜索引擎的影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式引擎优化（GEO）多集中于单页面优化，忽略了现代Web智能体通过多步骤浏览、链接跳转及跨页面合成证据的复杂交互行为。需要一种能从环境水平衡量和优化智能体信息获取过程的范式。

Method: 提出名为TRACE（Trajectory-Aware Coordinated Evidence Ecosystem）的方法。该方法构建一个受控的证据生态系统，通过协调入口页面与多个异构支持页面，利用共享术语、内部链接及一致的产品属性，引导智能体的浏览轨迹。

Result: 在OPR-Bench上，TRACE方法在产品推荐任务中显著优于传统的页面级GEO基准；轨迹级指标验证了其改善了获取过程，增加了目标网页的抓取率和路径相关性。

Conclusion: 本研究提出并验证了“生态系统生成式引擎优化”（EcoGEO）范式，证明了针对智能体浏览轨迹设计环境（而非仅优化单页内容）能更有效地影响LLM智能体的搜索决策。

Abstract: Web-enabled LLM agents are changing how online information influences search outcomes. \ Existing Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) studies mainly focus on individual webpages. \ However, agentic web search is not a single-document setting: an agent may issue queries, crawl pages, follow links, reformulate searches, and synthesize evidence across multiple browsing steps. \ Influence therefore depends not only on page content, but also on how pages are organized, connected, and encountered along the agent's browsing trajectory. \ We study this shift through \textbf{Ecosystem Generative Engine Optimization} (\textbf{EcoGEO}), which treats GEO as an environment-level influence problem for web-enabled LLM agents. \ To instantiate this perspective, we propose \textbf{TRACE}, a \textbf{Trajectory-Aware Coordinated Evidence Ecosystem}. \ Given a recommendation query and a fictional target product, our method builds a controlled evidence environment that coordinates an agent-facing navigation entry page with heterogeneous support pages. \ These pages use shared terminology, internal links, and consistent product attributes to introduce, verify, and reinforce the target product. We evaluate our method on OPR-Bench, a benchmark for open-ended product recommendation. \ Experiments show that it consistently outperforms page-level GEO baselines in final target recommendation. \ Trajectory-level metrics further show increased initial target-result crawls, target-specific follow-up searches, and internal-link crawls, suggesting that the gains come from shaping the agent's evidence-acquisition process rather than merely adding more target-related content. \ Overall, our findings support an ecosystem research paradigm for GEO, where web-enabled LLM agents are studied in relation to the broader evidence environments that guide search, browsing, and answer synthesis.

</details>


### [201] [Same Image, Different Meanings: Toward Retrieval of Context-Dependent Meanings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12905)
*Ayuto Tsutsumi,Ryosuke Kohita*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文分析了图像含义随叙事上下文变化的特性，提出了L1-L4语义分级框架，并发现抽象语义的检索极度依赖叙事背景的注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨图像含义的强上下文依赖性（即同一图像在不同叙事中具有不同含义），并解决现有图像检索系统在处理此类抽象、受语境影响的语义时能力不足的问题。

Method: 构建了L1-L4语义分级框架，从上下文无关（L1）到高度依赖（L4）；通过合成叙事上下文与查询语句，在受控环境下评估了不同抽象层级的图像检索性能；测试了上下文注入位置（图像侧 vs 查询侧）对检索的影响。

Result: 实验表明，混凝土类（对象、动作）查询在无需上下文时即可检索；而越抽象的语义（氛围、意图）越依赖叙事背景；在图像端进行上下文富集（Image-side enrichment）比在查询端更有效。

Conclusion: 本文提出了L1-L4框架来量化图像语义的上下文依赖性，揭示了语义抽象度越高，对叙事上下文的依赖越强。尽管引入上下文能提升检索效果，但处理高度抽象的图像含义仍是一个重要的开放性难题。

Abstract: A scene of two people in the rain can convey hope and warmth in a reunion story or sorrow and finality in a farewell story. We investigate this context-dependent nature of image meaning and its implications for retrieval. Our key observation is that context dependency correlates with semantic abstraction: concrete elements (objects, actions) remain stable across contexts, while abstract elements (atmosphere, intent) shift with context. We operationalize this as the L1--L4 framework, organizing image semantics from context-independent (L1) to maximally context-dependent (L4). Using synthetic story contexts and queries for controlled evaluation, we examine how injecting narrative context into embeddings affects retrieval across abstraction levels. Concrete queries are retrievable without context, while abstract levels increasingly depend on narrative grounding. Where context is injected also matters, with image-side enrichment proving particularly effective. The most abstract level, however, remains challenging even with full context, highlighting context-dependent image retrieval as an important open problem. Our framework and findings lay groundwork toward retrieval systems that handle the context-dependent meanings images acquire in narrative settings.

</details>


### [202] [RAG-Enhanced Large Language Models for Dynamic Content Expiration Prediction in Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13052)
*Tingyu Chen,Wenkai Zhang,Li Gao,Lixin Su,Ge Chen,Dawei Yin,Daiting Shi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 百度搜索提出了一种基于LLM的动态内容过期预测框架，将时效性评估从静态时间阈值升级为查询细分的逻辑推理，显著优化了搜索结果的语义时效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业Web搜索中，传统静态时间过滤方法无法匹配不同信息的生命周期，导致搜索结果虽然时效新但语义已过时，无法满足用户对时效性的真实意图。

Method: 提出了一个基于LLM的查询感知动态内容过期预测框架（Query-Aware Dynamic Content Expiration Prediction Framework），通过提取文档的细粒度时间上下文，结合LLM推理查询特定的“有效性范围”（validity horizon），并配合幻觉缓解策略以确保工程可靠性。

Result: 在线A/B测试和离线评估表明，该框架显著提升了搜索内容的实时性及用户体验指标。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了利用LLM进行语义级时效性推理在工业级搜索系统中的有效性，解决了传统静态时间窗口方法在处理多样化信息寿命时的局限性。

Abstract: In commercial web search, aligning content freshness with user intent remains challenging due to the highly varied lifespans of information. Traditional industrial approaches rely on static time-window filtering, resulting in "one-size-fits-all" rankings where content may be chronologically recent but semantically expired. To address the limitation, we present a novel Large Language Models (LLMs)-based Query-Aware Dynamic Content Expiration Prediction Framework deployed in Baidu search, reformulating timeliness as a dynamic validity inference task. Our framework extracts fine-grained temporal contexts from documents and leverages LLMs to deduce a query-specific "validity horizon"-a semantic boundary defining when information becomes obsolete based on user intent. Integrated with robust hallucination mitigation strategies to ensure reliability, our approach has been evaluated through offline and online A/B testing on live production traffic. Results demonstrate significant improvements in search freshness and user experience metrics, validating the effectiveness of LLM-driven reasoning for solving semantic expiration at an industrial scale.

</details>


### [203] [A Standardized Re-evaluation of Conversational Recommender Systems on the ReDial Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13053)
*Ivica Kostric,Krisztian Balog*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究通过标准化重评估七种对话推荐系统方法，揭示了当前学术界在评估流程、模型架构贡献及指标选择上存在的系统性偏差，指出多数性能提升源于LLM能力及“重复缺陷”，而非算法创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对对话式推荐系统（CRS）基准数据集（如ReDial）在预处理和使用上的不一致性，以及由于LLM主干能力和外部数据干扰，导致不同研究间难以公平比较，且现有评估指标可能存在对虚高指标的误导，旨在通过标准化评估揭示真实的性能差异。

Method: 在标准化条件下，对三种架构系列下的七种知名CRS方法进行了重新评估（复现研究），并运用用户中心效用指标评估系统效果。

Result: 1. 发现“颗粒度差距”：细粒度排名（Recall@1）对实现细节高度敏感；2. 揭示“重复快捷方式”：近50%的准确率提升源于模型重复已提及内容，而非真正的推荐；3. LLM能力贡献：性能提升更多依赖于LLM主干，而非架构创新；4. 传统指标局限：Recall常夸大实际的对话有效性。

Conclusion: 现有的CRS评估方法存在缺陷，传统的Recall指标夸大了系统效能。研究主张采用更透明、受控的基准测试，并优先考虑评估模型的新颖性和交互效率，而非单纯追求准确率。

Abstract: Recent years have seen a surge of research into conversational recommender systems (CRS). Among existing datasets, ReDial is the most widely used benchmark, cited in hundreds of studies. However, variations in how the dataset is preprocessed and used in experiments, particularly in the definition of ground-truth items, make it difficult to compare results across studies. These comparisons are further complicated by confounding factors such as the choice of the underlying large language model (LLM) and the use of external data sources. In this work, we revisit seven prominent CRS methods across three architectural families and evaluate them under standardized conditions. Our reproducibility study reveals a ``granularity gap,'' where fine-grained ranking (Recall@1) is highly sensitive to implementation details, while our replicability analysis shows that nearly 50% of reported accuracy stems from ``repetition shortcuts'' that are absent in novelty-focused evaluation. Furthermore, we find that performance gains are often driven more by the capacity of the LLM backbone than by specific architectural innovations. Finally, by applying user-centric utility metrics, we demonstrate that traditional recall frequently overstates a system's actual conversational effectiveness. This work establishes a transparent, controlled baseline and promotes evaluation practices that prioritize novelty and interaction efficiency.

</details>


### [204] [LeanSearch v2: Global Premise Retrieval for Lean 4 Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13137)
*Guoxiong Gao,Zeming Sun,Jiedong Jiang,Yutong Wang,Jingda Xu,Peihao Wu,Bryan Dai,Bin Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LeanSearch v2 是一个用于 Lean 4 的双模式检索系统，通过迭代推理与改进的检索流水线，首次实现了在大规模数学库中对定理完整前提集合的全局高效获取，显著提升了机器辅助定理证明的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具（语义搜索或单步前提选择）无法一次性获取证明完整定理所需的全部前提，导致在 Lean 4 定理证明中难以高效检索散落的引理。

Method: 提出了一套包含两种模式的检索系统：(1) 标准模式：采用“层次非形式化”的 Mathlib 语料库配合 Embedding-Reranker 流水线进行单步检索；(2) 推理模式：基于标准模式，通过迭代式的“草拟-检索-反思”(sketch-retrieve-reflect) 循环实现全局前提检索。

Result: 标准模式在无领域微调下取得 SOTA 检索表现 (nDCG@10: 0.62)；推理模式在定理基准测试中，前提群组恢复率达 46.1%；在下游定理证明实验中，该系统使证明成功率提升至 20%，远高于基准的 4%。

Conclusion: LeanSearch v2 证明了在定理证明中，高质量的全局前提检索能够显著提升证明生成成功率，其提出的双模式检索框架优于现有的单步前提选择系统。

Abstract: Proving theorems in Lean 4 often requires identifying a scattered set of library lemmas whose joint use enables a concise proof -- a task we call global premise retrieval. Existing tools address adjacent problems: semantic search engines find individual declarations matching a query, while premise-selection systems predict useful lemmas one tactic step at a time. Neither recovers the full premise set an entire theorem requires. We present LeanSearch v2, a two-mode retrieval system for this task. Its standard mode applies a hierarchy-informalized Mathlib corpus with an embedding-reranker pipeline, achieving state-of-the-art single-query retrieval without domain-specific fine-tuning (nDCG@10 of 0.62 vs. 0.53 for the next-best system). Its reasoning mode builds on standard mode as its retrieval substrate, targeting global premise retrieval through iterative sketch-retrieve-reflect cycles. On a 69-query benchmark of research-level Mathlib theorems, reasoning mode recovers 46.1% of ground-truth premise groups within 10 retrieved candidates, outperforming strong reasoning retrieval systems (38.0%) and premise-selection baselines (9.3%) on the same benchmark. In a controlled downstream evaluation with a fixed prover loop, replacing alternative retrievers with LeanSearch v2 yields the highest proof success (20% vs. 16% for the next-best system and 4% without retrieval), confirming that retrieval quality propagates to proof generation. We have open-sourced all code, data, and benchmarks. Code and data: https://github.com/frenzymath/LeanSearch-v2 . The standard mode is publicly available with API access at https://leansearch.net/ .

</details>


### [205] [Granite Embedding Multilingual R2 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13521)
*Parul Awasthy,Aashka Trivedi,Yushu Yang,Ken Barker,Yulong Li,Bhavani Iyer,Martin Franz,Meet Doshi,Riyaz Bhat,Vignesh P,Vishwajeet Kumar,Todd Ward,Abraham Daniels,Rudra Murthy,Madison Lee,Luis Lastras,Jaydeep Sen,Radu Florian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: IBM 推出 Granite Embedding R2 系列多语言检索模型，支持 200+ 语言及 32k 上下文，具备长文档检索与代码检索能力，提供高性能轻量化（97M）与全尺寸（311M）版本，旨在优化企业级检索应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在满足企业级应用对多语言、长文本检索的需求，同时解决现有单语模型覆盖面不足、上下文窗口有限以及部署效率待提升的问题。

Method: 1. 架构：基于 ModernBERT 架构，并扩展了多语言词表；2. 训练：包含多语言（200+种）与编程代码数据，支持 32,768 token 的长上下文窗口；3. 优化技术：使用模型剪枝技术构建 97M 轻量化版本；支持 Matryoshka 表示学习以实现维度灵活调控。

Result: 1. 在多语言、跨语言搜索、代码检索及长文档检索任务中达到行业领先水平；2. 97M 紧凑模型在 100M 参数以下的多语言检索基准测试中排名第一；3. 提供 Apache 2.0 开源许可。

Conclusion: Granite Embedding R2 模型证明了在企业级、多语言、长文本检索场景下，通过现代架构（ModernBERT）和特定优化技术（剪枝、Matryoshka 表示学习）可以在维持高性能的同时兼顾灵活性与部署效率。

Abstract: We introduce the multilingual Granite Embedding R2 models, a family of encoder-based embedding models for enterprise-scale dense retrieval across 200+ languages. Extending our English-focused R2 release, these models add enhanced support for 52 languages and programming code, a 32,768-token context window (a 64x expansion over R1), and state-of-the-art overall performance across multilingual and cross-lingual text search, code retrieval, long-document search, and reasoning retrieval datasets. The release consists of two bi-encoder models based on the ModernBERT architecture with an expanded multilingual vocabulary: a 311M-parameter full-size, and a 97M-parameter compact model built via model pruning and vocabulary selection that achieves the highest retrieval score of any open multilingual embedding model under 100M parameters. The full-size also supports Matryoshka Representation Learning for flexible embedding dimensionality. Both models are trained on enterprise-appropriate data with governance oversight, and released under the Apache 2.0 license at https://huggingface.co/collections/ibm-granite, designed to support responsible use and enable unrestricted research and enterprise adoption.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [Online Conformal Prediction: Enforcing monotonicity via Online Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12668)
*Eduardo Ochoa Rivera,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种支持嵌套预测集的在线共形预测框架，能够在单一模型下为不同风险偏好同时提供准确、高效且嵌套的 uncertainty quantification。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线共形预测方法多针对单一覆盖水平，无法满足实际应用中（如气象预测、风险管理）多用户、多风险偏好下对嵌套且同时校准的预测区间需求。此外，联合估计多个覆盖水平可以利用分位数间的信息共享提高统计效率。

Method: 提出了两种基于在线优化视角的新型在线共形预测（Online Conformal Prediction）方法，利用在线优化的小遗憾（small regret）思想来控制分位数估计误差，并施加嵌套性约束，从而实现各覆盖水平间的信息共享与非交叉约束。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持各覆盖水平下稳定的覆盖率（coverage）的同时，实现了严格嵌套的预测集，且在统计效率上优于现有的在线共形预测基线模型。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两种在线共形预测方法不仅能够满足不同覆盖水平下的同时校准需求，确保预测集的嵌套性，还通过在线优化手段实现了更优的统计效率和稳定性，为处理异构风险需求提供了有效的框架。

Abstract: Conformal prediction provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification with finite-sample coverage guarantees. While recent work has extended conformal prediction to online and sequential settings, existing methods typically focus on a single coverage level and do not ensure consistency across multiple confidence levels. In many real-world applications, such as weather forecasting, macroeconomic prediction, and risk management, different users operate under heterogeneous risk tolerances and require calibrated uncertainty estimates across a range of coverage levels. In such settings, it is desirable to produce prediction sets corresponding to different coverage levels that are nested and valid simultaneously. In this paper, we propose two novel online conformal prediction methods that output \emph{nested prediction sets} across a range of coverage levels, enabling simultaneous uncertainty quantification across the entire risk spectrum. Beyond interpretability, jointly estimating multiple coverage levels is known to improve statistical efficiency in classical quantile regression by enforcing non-crossing constraints and sharing information across quantiles. Our approaches leverage an online optimization perspective with small regret that translates to quantile estimation error control while enforcing nestedness of prediction sets. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets, including applications in forecasting tasks with heterogeneous risk requirements, demonstrate that our method achieves stable coverage across all levels, strictly nested prediction sets, and improved efficiency compared to existing online conformal baselines.

</details>


### [207] [A Unified Framework for Critical Scaling of Inverse Temperature in Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12697)
*Tomohiro Hayase,Ryo Karakida*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于“间隙计数函数”的统一理论，解决了长上下文 Self-Attention 稳定性中 Logit 重缩放比例的定义争议，明确了决定注意力分布浓度及熵崩溃的临界逆温度尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 Self-Attention 中用于稳定性的长度依赖 Logit 重缩放机制缺乏理论共识，现有的分析提出了多种冲突的定标律（从 $(\log n)^{1/2}$ 到 $\log n$ 及 $(\log n)^2$ 等）。

Method: 通过定义注意力行中每个间隙的“间隙计数函数” $N_n$（即在最大值附近有多少个竞争项），分析 softmax 浓度特性，并推导出表征上尾累积的临界逆温度缩放尺度。

Result: 证明了该临界尺度是 softmax 浓度分界的关键：尺度过小会导致顶部竞争项无法区分，尺度过大会导致注意力熵崩溃。该框架成功统一了文献中已知的多种定标律。

Conclusion: 提出了基于间隙计数函数 $N_n$ 的统一理论框架，能够解释并统一先前关于长上下文 Self-Attention 稳定性的各类冲突定标律，并为注意力分数族提供了直接的诊断方法。

Abstract: Length-dependent logit rescaling is widely used to stabilize long-context self-attention, but existing analyses and methods suggest conflicting inverse-temperature laws for the context length $n$, ranging from $(\log n)^{1/2}$ to $\log n$ and $(\log n)^2$. We provide a general theory showing that the desirable scale is determined by the gap-counting function $N_n$ of each attention row. Counting how many competitors lie within each gap from the maximum, we define an upper-tail accumulation scale and prove that it gives the critical inverse-temperature scale for softmax concentration: below this scale, the top competitors remain unseparated, whereas above it, the attention entropy collapses. This framework unifies prior scaling laws as different $N_n$ and yields a direct diagnostic for attention-score families, from idealized theoretical models to more practical transformers.

</details>


### [208] [Robust Sequential Experimental Design for A/B Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12899)
*Qianglin Wen,Xiangkun Wu,Chengchun Shi,Ting Li,Niansheng Tang,Yingying Zhang,Hongtu Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在模型错误设定下依然稳健的序贯实验设计框架，解决了现有方法对模型依赖过强的问题，有效提升了 A/B 测试的鲁棒性和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实验设计方法过度依赖于假设模型的正确性，在模型设定错误时表现欠佳。为了克服这一缺陷，研究者旨在开发一种在模型误设情况下依然具有稳健性的序贯实验设计方法，以提高 A/B 测试的样本效率。

Method: 构建了一个统一的稳健序贯实验设计框架，涵盖语境强盗（Contextual Bandit）和动态设置；通过理论证明其对最坏情况均方误差的界定能力，并使用合成数据及真实科技公司数据进行验证。

Result: 理论上确立了该设计对最坏情况均方误差的约束；实证结果表明，该方法在合成数据集和真实工业数据集上均表现出卓越的效果。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的稳健序贯实验设计框架，能够在模型错误设定的情况下，有效限制实验估计量的最坏情况均方误差，从而提升 A/B 测试在实际应用中的可靠性。

Abstract: Experimental design has emerged as a powerful approach for improving the sample efficiency of A/B testing, yet existing designs rely critically on correctly specified models. We study robust sequential experimental design under model misspecification and develop a unified framework that covers both contextual bandit and dynamic settings. Theoretically, we prove that our design bounds the worst-case mean squared error of the estimated treatment effect. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach using synthetic and real-world datasets from a leading technology company.

</details>


### [209] [When Should an AI Workflow Release? Always-Valid Inference for Black-Box Generate-Verify Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12947)
*Young Hyun Cho,Will Wei Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对LLM迭代工作流，本文提出了一种无需传统假设、支持任意停止的统计工具，通过硬负参考池和电子过程，有效控制了错误输出风险并优化了停止时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM迭代工作流（生成-评估-修订）面临决策困境：由于评估分数是自适应生成的，传统的校准方法失效，导致模型容易过早输出错误结果或在无法解决的任务上死循环。

Method: 提出了一种无需传统校准假设的释放封装器：(1) 构建高分失败案例的“硬负参考池”以校准评估分数；(2) 利用电子过程（e-process）累积证据，从而在任意停止条件下提供统计有效性。

Result: 在MBPP+代码生成任务中，该方法显著减少了错误输出，同时在可行任务上保持了稳健的释放性能，优于基准停止规则。

Conclusion: 本文提出的“始终有效”释放封装器（always-valid release wrapper）能够有效缓解大模型迭代工作流中的过早释放问题，在保证对不可行任务提供有限样本错误控制的同时，确保在可行任务上仍能实现有效率的释放。

Abstract: LLM-enabled AI workflows increasingly produce outputs through iterative generate-evaluate-revise loops. Each iteration can improve the candidate, but it also creates a release decision: when to stop and output the current result? This raises a statistical challenge because deployment-time evaluator scores are adaptively generated and repeatedly monitored, yet the likelihood models or exchangeability assumptions typically used for calibration are unavailable. We propose an always-valid release wrapper for existing generator-evaluator pipelines. The wrapper builds a hard-negative reference pool of high-scoring failures, calibrates deployment-time evaluator scores against this pool, and accumulates the resulting evidence with an e-process. This separates two roles: the reference pool turns black-box scores into conservative evidence, while the e-process provides validity under optional stopping. In theory, we show that a conservative reference pool yields finite-sample control of the probability of releasing on infeasible tasks, that is, tasks for which the given workflow is not capable of producing a reliable solution. We also characterize conditions under which the same conservative rule still achieves nontrivial release on feasible tasks. In an MBPP+ coding-agent case study, the wrapper reduces premature incorrect release relative to baseline stopping rules while still releasing on tasks for which the workflow repeatedly accumulates moderate supporting evidence.

</details>


### [210] [What is Learnable in Valiant's Theory of the Learnable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13840)
*Steve Hanneke,Anay Mehrotra,Grigoris Velegkas,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究重新审视了Valiant 1984年的原始学习模型，利用自适应查询压缩理论证明了该模型下的可学习类介于PAC与无查询模型之间，并解决了半空间类在该模型下的可学习性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正对PAC学习模型起源的常见误解（Valiant 1984年论文实际定义了不同模型），旨在重新探讨Valiant原始模型下的概念可学习性及其与PAC模型、无查询模型的关系。

Method: 1. 提出了“自适应查询压缩方案”（adaptive query-compression scheme）作为可学习性的特征刻画；2. 利用该方案证明了有限域及任意域下的严格夹逼性质；3. 针对d维半空间类设计了多项式时间的样本/查询高效算法，并给出了下界证明。

Result: 1. 得出了一个充要条件：类可学习当且仅当存在多项式大小的自适应查询压缩方案；2. 证明了Valiant模型下的可学习类严格介于PAC模型和无查询模型之间；3. 首次证明d维半空间在有查询的情况下是可学习的，给出了高效算法及相应的样本/查询复杂性下界。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了Valiant 1984年原始模型具有丰富的理论内涵，证明了成员查询（membership queries）能严格扩大可学习类的范围。该模型位于PAC模型与无查询变体之间，并首次为半空间类提供了高效的学习算法。

Abstract: Valiant's 1984 paper is widely credited with introducing the PAC learning model, but it, in fact, introduced a different model: unlike PAC learning, the learner receives only positives, may issue membership queries, and must output a hypothesis with no false positives. Prior work characterized variants, including the case without queries. We revisit Valiant's original model and ask: *Which classes are learnable in it?*
  For every finite domain, including Valiant's Boolean-hypercube setting, we show that a class is learnable if and only if every realizable positive sample can be certified by a poly-size adaptive query-compression scheme. This is a new variant of sample compression where the learner certifies samples via a short interaction with the membership oracle. Our characterization shows that learnability in Valiant's model is strictly sandwiched between learnability in the PAC model and the variant of Valiant's model without membership queries. This is one of the rare cases where introducing membership queries changes the set of learnable classes, and not just the sample or computational complexity.
  Next, we study the natural extension of the model to arbitrary domains. While we do not obtain an exact characterization, our techniques readily generalize and show that the same strict sandwiching persists. Finally, we show that $d$-dimensional halfspaces, which are not learnable without queries, are learnable with queries: we give a $\mathrm{poly}(d) \tilde{O}(1/ε)$ sample and $\mathrm{poly}(d) \mathrm{polylog}(1/ε)$ query algorithm, and prove that at least $Ω(d)$ samples or queries are necessary. To our knowledge, this is the first algorithm for halfspaces in Valiant's model.
  Together, these results uncover a surprisingly rich theory behind Valiant's original notion of learnability and introduce ideas that may be of independent interest in learning theory.

</details>


### [211] [State-of-art minibatches via novel DPP kernels: discretization, wavelets, and rough objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13127)
*Hoang-Son Tran,Pranav Gupta,Rémi Bardenet,Subhroshekhar Ghosh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对DPP在机器学习子采样中的应用瓶颈，本文提出基于小波的新型DPP构建方法，并引入了一种能够将连续DPP通过低秩分解映射为离散核的转换机制，从而在保障低计算成本的同时，实现了对复杂（低正则性）目标函数的高效采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行列式点过程（DPPs）在生成子集（如小批量、核集）方面表现出潜力，但目前存在两个主要挑战：缺乏具备方差减少特性的DPP实例，以及缺乏将这些特性有效转化为离散数据集处理的机制。

Method: 1. 提出了基于小波（Wavelets）的欧氏空间DPPs，并证明了其在方差减少方面具有优于现有技术的准确性保证；2. 开发了一种通用的转换机制，将连续DPPs转换为离散核，在保留方差衰减特性的同时，实现了离散核的低秩分解以降低采样开销。

Result: 研究证明了新型DPPs具有更好的准确性保证，并大幅扩展了DPP在机器学习任务中的应用范围，使其能够处理具有任意低正则性的目标函数，同时具备自适应的速率保证。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了基于小波的新型欧氏空间行列式点过程（DPPs），并通过一种通用的转换机制将其转化为离散核，从而实现了高效的子采样。该研究不仅优化了欠采样在低正则性目标函数下的性能表现，还通过低秩分解保证了采样过程的计算高效性。

Abstract: Determinantal point processes (DPPs) have emerged as a kernelized alternative to vanilla independent sampling for generating efficient minibatches, coresets and other parsimonious representations of large-scale datasets. While theoretical foundations and promising empirical performance have been demonstrated, there are two challenges for current proposals for DPP-based coresets or minibatches. The first is the need for families of DPPs with certain key variance reduction properties, usually constructed in a continuous setting, of which there are few known examples. The second is the need for an ad-hoc construction of a discrete DPP defined on a given dataset, that inherits such variance reduction. In this work, we contribute to the programme of establishing DPPs as a subsampling toolbox for ML by advancing on these two fronts. First, we propose new DPPs on the Euclidean space based on wavelets, with provably better accuracy guarantees than the best known rates. Second, we introduce a general method to convert such continuous DPPs, which are more amenable to proving analytical statements, into discrete kernels, which are pertinent for subsampling tasks such as minibatch and coreset constructions. This conversion mechanism simultaneously preserves the desired variance decay and reveals a low-rank decomposition of the discrete kernel, which makes sampling the corresponding DPP computationally inexpensive. En route, we enlarge the class of ML tasks amenable to improvements via DPP-based minibatches and coresets to include objective functions with arbitrarily low regularity, and rate guarantees that explicitly adapt to this regularity.

</details>


### [212] [On Hallucinations in Inverse Problems: Fundamental Limits and Provable Assessment Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13146)
*David Iagaru,Nina M. Gottschling,Anders C. Hansen,Josselin Garnier*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个评估成像反问题中AI幻觉的理论框架，证明幻觉源于反问题的病态性，并提供了量化幻觉幅度和评估重建忠实度的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在成像反问题应用中存在的“幻觉”（即生成虚假但逼真的细节）问题严重影响了模型的可靠性，特别是在缺乏真实数据的情况下，缺乏评估幻觉的理论依据。

Method: 构建了一个理论框架，推导出幻觉产生的必要与充分条件，并提出基于前转模型（forward model）的可计算界限；开发了相应的算法来评估特定模型重建结果的忠实度，并估计任意重建模型在给定输入下幻觉的最小下界。

Result: 在三个成像任务中验证了该方法的普适性，证明其不仅适用于传统模型，也能用于现代生成式模型，能够有效量化分析AI幻觉。

Conclusion: AI模型在成像反问题中的“幻觉”现象不仅是模型缺陷，更是反问题病态特性的本质属性；所提出的理论框架为量化和评估这种幻觉提供了可计算、普适的科学依据。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) has transformed imaging inverse problems, from medical diagnostics to Earth observation. Yet deep neural networks can produce hallucinations, realistic-looking but incorrect details, undermining their reliability, especially when ground truth data is unavailable. We develop a theoretical framework showing that such hallucinations are not merely artifacts of particular models, but can arise from the ill-posed nature of the inverse problem itself. We derive necessary and sufficient conditions for hallucinations, together with computable bounds on their magnitude that depend only on the forward model. Building on this theory, we introduce algorithms to: (1) estimate the minimum hallucination magnitude achievable by any reconstruction model for a given input; (2) assess the faithfulness of reconstructed details by a given reconstruction model. Experiments across three imaging tasks demonstrate that our approach applies broadly, including to modern generative models, and provides a principled way to quantify and evaluate AI hallucinations.

</details>


### [213] [Generative Modeling of Approximately Periodic Time Series by a Posterior-Weighted Gaussian Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13150)
*Elias Reich,Saverio Messineo,Stefan Huber*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对工业和信息物理系统中常见的“近似周期性”时间序列，本文提出了一种基于新型高斯过程核的随机生成模型，成功解耦了周期内的结构稳定性和周期间的动态变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的GP模型在处理“近似周期性”（即重复轨迹但在持续时间、幅度和细节上存在差异）的时间序列时，无法兼顾重复结构与重复间的变异性。需要一种既能保持结构规律性，又能体现细微动态变化的生成模型。

Method: 提出了一种基于高斯过程（GP）的新型核函数，通过两阶段构建法，实现模态内结构一致性与模态间平滑变异性的解耦。

Result: 通过在模拟数据集上的实现，验证了该模型能够有效生成具有真实感的近似周期性轨迹。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种针对近似周期性时间序列的随机生成模型，证明了通过解耦模态内结构与模态间变异性，可以有效捕捉工业与信息物理系统中的复杂周期性特征。

Abstract: Discrete automated processes in industrial and cyber-physical systems often exhibit a repetitive structure in which successive repetitions follow a common trajectory while differing in duration, amplitude, and fine-scale dynamics. Such \emph{approximately periodic} behavior poses a challenge for Gaussian Processes (GP) modeling: strictly periodic models suppress inter-repetition variability, while non-periodic models fail to capture the strong structural regularities required for generation. In this work, we propose a stochastic generative model for approximately periodic time series. The model is based on a GP whose posterior is modulated by a novel kernel. Our approach decouples intra-repetition structure from inter-repetition variability through a two-stage construction which yields a generative distribution with a identical mean function across repetitions, while allowing smooth variation between repetitions. The modeling choices are supported by an implementation in which realistic synthetic trajectories are generated from toy datasets.

</details>


### [214] [Kernel-based guarantees for nonlinear parametric models in Bayesian optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13160)
*Rafael Oliveira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于核的理论框架，为自适应采样中使用的非线性参数模型提供了性能保证，填补了现代贝叶斯优化理论的缺失环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯优化理论主要局限于高斯过程、核方法或线性模型，缺乏对实际中广泛使用的非线性参数模型在自适应数据收集下的理论保证。

Method: 利用参数空间上的核方法（Kernels），在对应的模型类上诱导出再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）结构，并结合正则化凸损失函数推导模型置信界。

Result: 导出了适用于广泛正则化凸损失函数的模型置信界，并证明了其在非线性采集策略和替代模型中的收敛性保证。

Conclusion: 提供了一个分析非线性参数模型在贝叶斯优化和自适应采样中收敛性的统一框架，弥补了理论与大规模应用模型之间的差距。

Abstract: Modern Bayesian optimization and adaptive sampling methods increasingly rely on nonlinear parametric models, yet theoretical guarantees for such models under adaptive data collection remain limited. Existing analyses largely focus on Gaussian processes, kernel machines, linear models, or linearized neural approximations, leaving a gap between theory and the nonlinear models used in practice. We develop a kernel based framework for analyzing regularized nonlinear parametric models trained on adaptively collected data. Our approach uses kernels over the parameter space to induce reproducing kernel Hilbert space structures over the corresponding model class, yielding confidence bounds for models trained with broad classes of regularized convex losses. We show how these bounds can support convergence guarantees for nonlinear acquisition and surrogate models, including randomized regularized policies that select points by maximizing a trained random model. These results provide a unified route to analyzing nonlinear parametric models in Bayesian optimization and related adaptive optimization settings.

</details>


### [215] [Coupling-Informed Transport Maps for Bayesian Filtering in Nonlinear Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13174)
*Dengfei Zeng,Lijian Jiang,Shuyu Sun,Dunhui Xiao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于最大均值差异（MMD）最小化的新型无似然运输滤波方法，通过梯度流实现运输映射的解析计算，有效解决了粒子坍缩问题，并在高维非线性场景中表现出卓越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有滤波方法在处理非线性、非高斯系统时存在的后验分布近似精度低及粒子坍缩问题。

Method: 1. 基于状态与观测变量间的耦合，利用运输映射的块三角结构；2. 将滤波分析步骤重构为最小化真实联合测度与运输近似间的最大均值差异（MMD）；3. 引入基于梯度流的无训练运输滤波方法，实现运输映射的解析计算；4. 采用域局部化技术以扩展至高维问题。

Result: 收敛性分析验证了该方法的有效性；数值实验表明，在处理非线性、非高斯场景时，该方法在精度和稳定性上均优于传统滤波算法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过基于梯度流的分析性计算解决了非凸优化难题，有效避免了粒子坍缩，并具备处理高维非线性、非高斯滤波问题的能力。

Abstract: A likelihood-free transport filtering method is proposed based on the couplings between state and observation variables. By exploiting a block-triangular structure in the transport map, the analysis step of filtering is reformulated as the minimization of the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between the true joint measure and its transport-based approximation. To circumvent the non-convexity in the MMD optimization, we introduce a training-free transport filter method via gradient flows, which leads to an analytic computation for the transport map that implies the steepest descent direction of the MMD. The proposed approach accurately approximates non-Gaussian filtering posteriors and avoids particle collapse. We provide a convergence analysis for the expectation of the MMD between the approximated posterior and the truth posterior. Finally, we extend the method to high-dimensional problems through domain localization. Numerical examples demonstrate the superior performance of our approach over conventional filtering methods in nonlinear, non-Gaussian scenarios.

</details>


### [216] [LLMs as Implicit Imputers: Uncertainty Should Scale with Missing Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13188)
*Stef van Buuren*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM在不完整上下文下的生成类比为“多重插补”，实验证明响应熵（entropy）在衡量模型不确定性方面比传统的置信度（confidence）更稳健且灵敏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM面临不完整上下文的实际部署场景中，模型应表现出类似“多重插补”（Multiple Imputation, MI）的特性，即其输出的不确定性应随着缺失信息的增加而缩放。现有常用的置信度指标可能无法有效反映这种变化。

Method: 在SQuAD数据集上通过控制上下文缺失程度，对比采样一致性（置信度）和响应熵两种不确定性度量。引入名为$\rho_R(\alpha)$的黑盒诊断指标，用于量化上下文对减少模型不确定性的贡献。

Result: 研究发现：1. 采样产生的置信度指标难以反映信息缺失，即使准确率显著下降，置信度仍保持虚高；2. 响应熵随上下文缺失增加而有效增加；3. 响应熵在解释准确率波动方面的效果显著优于置信度。

Conclusion: 相较于置信度，响应熵（response entropy）是评估LLM在不完整上下文下不确定性更灵敏的黑盒指标。研究结果表明，基于MI框架评估LLM的不确定性有助于理解模型在信息缺失时的表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in settings where the available context is incomplete or degraded. We argue that an LLM generating answers under incomplete context can be viewed as an implicit imputer, and evaluated against a criterion from the multiple imputation (MI) literature: uncertainty should scale with the amount of missing information. We assess this criterion on SQuAD, using a controlled framework in which context availability is varied across five levels. We evaluate two answer-level uncertainty measures that can be estimated from repeated sampling: sampling-based confidence (empirical mode frequency) and response entropy. Confidence fails to reflect increasing missingness: it remains high even as accuracy collapses. Entropy, by contrast, increases with context removal, consistent with the MI analogy, and explains substantially more variance in accuracy than confidence across all evidence levels (quadratic $R^2$ gap up to 0.057). We further introduce a black-box diagnostic $ρ_R(α)$ that estimates the proportion of baseline uncertainty resolved by context level $α$, requiring only repeated sampling with and without context. These results suggest that entropy is a more responsive black-box uncertainty measure than confidence under incomplete context.

</details>


### [217] [The Sample Complexity of Multiple Change Point Identification under Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13252)
*Maximilian Graf,Victor Thuot*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了Bandit反馈下的分段常数函数变点检测，提出了一种两阶段自适应算法，并证明了在非渐近意义下，变点识别的样本复杂度受跳跃幅度和变点间距的共同影响，而非单纯取决于跳跃幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多臂老虎机（Bandit）反馈机制下，研究分段常数函数的多变点定位问题，旨在以最少的采样次数，在指定的精度η和置信度1-δ下识别出预设数量的变点。

Method: 提出了一种自适应算法，该算法分为两个阶段：首先检测可能存在变点的区间，随后将这些变点的位置精确细化至指定精度η。建立了一套基于该算法的非渐近上限，并给出了对应的下限证明。

Result: 建立了该算法在样本预算上的非渐近上限和下限，证明了在一般参数δ和η下，变点定位的复杂度由跳跃幅度和变点相对位置共同决定。

Conclusion: 样本复杂度（Sample Complexity）不仅取决于跳跃（jump）幅度，还受到变点之间相对位置的共同制约，修正了以往仅关注跳跃幅度的认知。

Abstract: We study multiple change point localization under bandit feedback. An unknown piecewise-constant function on a compact interval can be queried sequentially at adaptively chosen inputs, and each query returns a noisy evaluation of the function. The goal is to identify a prescribed number of discontinuities, known as change points, within a target precision $η$ and confidence level $1-δ$, while using as few samples as possible. We propose an adaptive algorithm that first detects intervals likely to contain change points and then refines their locations to precision $η$. We establish non-asymptotic upper bounds on its sample budget, together with corresponding lower bounds. Prior work shows that jump magnitudes alone determine the asymptotic sample complexity as $δ\to 0$. We reveal that this picture is incomplete beyond this regime. We demonstrate, both empirically and theoretically, that for general $δ$ and $η$, the complexity is jointly governed by the jumps and the relative positions of the change points.

</details>


### [218] [Learning Perturbations to Extrapolate Your LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13284)
*Zetai Cen,Chenfei Gu,Jin Zhu,Ting Li,Yunxiao Chen,Chengchun Shi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过学习Embedding空间的连续扰动来提升大语言模型外推性能的新框架，并证明了其统计有效性与优越的实证表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
