Motivation: 现有提升大语言模型外推性能的方法多依赖于离散的、固定设计的扰动，缺乏灵活性且受限。

Method: 提出一种基于可学习连续潜向量转换的Token前缀扰动框架，针对边缘似然难以计算的问题，利用随机梯度下降求解无偏估计方程进行优化。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，该方法在域外（Out-of-Domain）性能上显著优于现有的前沿基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过在Embedding空间中对Token前缀进行可学习的连续扰动，能够显著提升大语言模型在外推（Extrapolation）任务中的性能。作者推导了无偏估计方程，并在过参数化机制下证明了该方法的统计性质。

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models demonstrate that injecting perturbations can substantially enhance extrapolation performance. However, current approaches often rely on discrete perturbations with fixed designs, which limits their flexibility. In this work, we propose a framework where token prefixes are perturbed by a learnable transformation of a continuous latent vector within an embedding space. To overcome the challenge of an intractable marginal likelihood, we derive unbiased estimating equations for model parameters and optimize them via stochastic gradient descent. We establish the statistical properties of the resulting estimator in over-parameterized regimes. Empirical evaluations on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our proposal yields significant gains in out-of-domain settings over a range of state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [219] [On the Limits of Latent Reuse in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13448)
*Yifeng Yu,Lu Yu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析了扩散模型在分布偏移下重用潜在空间的可靠性，界定了重用策略的边界，并为何时应转向混合训练或学习共享表示提供了理论准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型通常在低维潜在空间中训练，但当模型跨数据集（即存在分布偏移）重用潜在空间时，其可靠性缺乏系统的理论分析。研究旨在探究何时可以安全地重用潜在空间，以及何时需要学习共享表示。

Method: 建立源-目标数据集分布偏移的理论模型；通过分析主成分角度失调（principal-angle misalignment）和扩散时间尺度下的环境噪声放大效应，量化潜在空间的得分误差；并针对混合源-目标训练场景进行几何分析。

Result: 推导出了衡量重用潜在空间时目标域得分误差的两个关键指标；分析了共享潜在空间的维度需求与源-目标分布相对几何结构之间的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 潜在空间重用在源和目标子空间的几何相似度高、且目标域噪声可控时是可靠的。当不满足这些条件时，混合训练或学习共享表示方案更为必要。该工作为扩散模型在不同数据集间的迁移学习提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Diffusion models are often trained in low-dimensional latent spaces, which are then reused for related but shifted datasets. In this work, we study when such latent reuse remains reliable under distribution shift. We consider a source-target setting in which both datasets are approximately low-dimensional but may lie near different subspaces. We show that freezing and reusing a source latent space induces a target-domain score error governed by two quantities: the principal-angle misalignment between the source and target subspaces, and the target ambient noise amplified by the diffusion time scale. Motivated by these limits, we further study mixed source-target training and characterize how the required shared latent dimension depends on the relative geometry of the two distributions. Our results provide theoretical guidance on when latent reuse is reliable and when learning a shared representation may be necessary.

</details>


### [220] [Reframing preprocessing selection as model-internal calibration in near-infrared spectroscopy: A large-scale benchmark of operator-adaptive PLS and Ridge models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13587)
*Gregory Beurier,Robin Reiter,Camille Noûs,Lauriane Rouan,Denis Cornet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算子自适应校准框架，通过将预处理选择整合到模型内部，克服了传统NIRS建模中繁琐的预处理选择依赖，显著提升了建模效率与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的NIRS预处理选择严重依赖耗时且不稳定的外部网格搜索（管道搜索），且在小样本校准集上容易过拟合，缺乏模型可审计性。

Method: 引入算子自适应框架：将线性预处理编码为线性算子嵌入校准模型，将非线性或样本自适应预处理（如SNV、MSC、ASLS）作为折叠内部分支处理以防泄露；为PLS开发了基于协方差恒等式的快速NIPALS/SIMPLS变体，为Ridge回归开发了算子自适应核及对偶形式。

Result: 在50多个异构NIRS数据集上进行验证，算子自适应PLS（AOM-PLS）获得显著的性能提升（RMSEP/PLS中位数比为0.960），且在大多数数据集上胜过基线方法；训练速度快，并保留了原始波长系数的可解释性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为“算子自适应校准（operator-adaptive calibration）”的框架，实现了预处理选择与模型训练的同步优化，为近红外光谱（NIRS）的应用开发提供了一种更高效、稳健且可审计的路径。

Abstract: Near-infrared spectroscopy (NIRS) is rapid and non-destructive, but reliable calibration still depends heavily on spectral preprocessing. In routine practice, preprocessing is often selected by large external pipeline searches that are costly, unstable on small calibration sets, and difficult to audit. We introduce operator-adaptive calibration, a framework that moves linear preprocessing selection inside the calibration model. Candidate treatments are encoded as linear spectral operators, while nonlinear or sample-adaptive corrections such as SNV, MSC, and ASLS are handled as fold-local branches to prevent leakage.
  We instantiate the framework for PLS and Ridge regression. For PLS, covariance identities enable fast NIPALS and SIMPLS variants while preserving original-wavelength coefficients. For Ridge, operator-adaptive kernels yield a dual formulation with recoverable original-space coefficients. The approach was evaluated on more than 50 heterogeneous NIRS datasets against conventional PLS, Ridge, CatBoost, and CNN baselines under documented search budgets.
  Compact operator-adaptive PLS with ASLS branch preprocessing achieved a median RMSEP/PLS ratio of 0.960 with 42 wins on 57 datasets, while a deployable AOM-Ridge selector improved over tuned Ridge by a median 2.22% with 35 wins on 52 datasets. The proposed models reduce dependence on large preprocessing-HPO campaigns, produce traceable operator choices, retain interpretable coefficients, and fit in seconds for compact AOM-PLS. Operator-adaptive calibration therefore offers a practical route to faster, more robust, and more auditable NIRS method development.

</details>


### [221] [Causal Learning with the Invariance Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13589)
*Francesco Montagna,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在假设因果关系在不同环境下保持不变的前提下，仅需两个辅助环境即可识别非线性结构因果模型并实现准确的反事实推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现问题通常是不适定的（ill-posed），作者旨在探索在何种条件下，通过利用不同环境下的不变性，能够从观测数据中唯一确定复杂的非线性因果机制。

Method: 利用结构因果模型（SCM）框架，引入“因果关系在多个环境下保持不变（不变性原则）”的假设，并通过数学证明推导出仅需两个辅助环境即可恢复因果图。

Result: 理论证明了两个辅助环境对于识别任意非线性因果机制及进行准确的反事实推理是充分条件；并在合成数据上验证了该理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 在拥有两个辅助环境的前提下，非线性结构因果模型（SCM）及其函数机制不仅是可识别的，而且能够实现准确的溯因推理（counterfactual inference）。

Abstract: Causal discovery, the problem of inferring the direction of causality, is generally ill-posed. We use the language of structural causal models (SCM) to show that assuming that the causal relations are acyclic and invariant across multiple environments (e.g., the way minimum wage affects employment rate is stable across different geographical regions), \textit{only} two auxiliary environments are sufficient to infer the causal graph for arbitrary nonlinear mechanisms. Moreover, we demonstrate that this implies identifiability of the SCM functional mechanisms: as a corollary, we show that \textit{two} auxiliary environments are sufficient to guarantee correct counterfactual inference. We empirically support our theoretical results on synthetic data.

</details>


### [222] [Conformal Anomaly Detection in Python: Moving Beyond Heuristic Thresholds with 'nonconform'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13642)
*Oliver Hennhöfer,Maximilian Kirsch,Christine Preisach*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Python库'nonconform'，它将共形异常检测方法标准化，使用户能够通过统一接口轻松进行异常检测的校准、p值计算及错误率控制，弥合了理论与机器学习工程实践之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异常检测系统大多仅输出非校准的分数，导致阈值设定主观且缺乏统计学解释；共形异常检测虽能转化为具有统计有效性的p值，但缺乏便捷的工具实现。

Method: 开发了Python开源库'nonconform'，该库支持多种共形化策略（如split-conformal、各种偏移感知策略），并与scikit-learn和pyod集成，提供统一的校准、p值生成及错误发现率（FDR）控制接口。

Result: 实证结果表明，基于'nonconform'实现的各类方法能够实现具有统计学原理保障的异常检测，有效解决了阈值选择的盲目性。

Conclusion: 'nonconform'工具降低了共形异常检测的门槛，使得统计上严谨的异常监测能够更便利地集成到机器学习工作流中，有助于提高实验和生产环境下的可复现性。

Abstract: Most anomaly detection systems output scores rather than calibrated decisions, leaving practitioners to choose thresholds heuristically and without clear statistical interpretation. Conformal anomaly detection addresses this limitation by converting anomaly scores into calibrated p-values that are valid under the statistical assumption of data exchangeability, with a growing literature extending this idea beyond that setting. We present 'nonconform', a Python package for applying conformal anomaly detection within existing machine-learning workflows, and use it as the basis for an implementation-grounded introduction to the field. The package integrates with 'scikit-learn', 'pyod', and custom anomaly detectors, and provides a unified interface for calibration, p-value generation, and false discovery rate control. It supports several conformalization strategies, ranging from simple split-conformal calibration to more data-efficient and shift-aware extensions. Through a progression from foundational concepts to advanced conformalization strategies, complemented by code examples, the paper connects the statistical ideas behind conformal anomaly detection to their practical use in 'nonconform'. Empirical results demonstrate that the implemented methods enable statistically principled anomaly detection. Together, the package and exposition aim to make core conformal anomaly detection workflows more accessible and reproducible in experimental and production-oriented settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [Comparing the Performance of Heterogeneous Conjugate Gradient and Cholesky Solvers on Various Hardware Using SYCL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13209)
*Tim Thüring,Alexander Strack,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SYCL的异构计算方法，通过让CPU与GPU协同求解大规模对称正定线性系统，显著提升了CG法及Cholesky分解的性能和跨平台兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代HPC系统中CPU和GPU资源由于“同构化”使用（仅依赖GPU）导致CPU算力闲置浪费，无法在大规模对称正定线性系统求解任务中发挥系统整体最大性能。

Method: 利用SYCL编程模型，开发了针对共轭梯度法（CG）和Cholesky分解的异构协同并行实现方案，通过任务分解使CPU和GPU同时参与线性系统的求解计算。

Result: 实验表明，对于大规模矩阵，异构实现相比GPU-only方案，CG方法性能提升高达32%，Cholesky分解提升高达29%；在不同厂商（NVIDIA, AMD, Intel）的硬件上，Cholesky分解均取得了至少12%的性能增益。

Conclusion: 在异构计算环境（CPU+GPU）中利用SYCL实现算子卸载与协同计算，能够有效突破单GPU计算的性能瓶颈，且具备良好的跨硬件平台可移植性。

Abstract: Many important real-world applications, such as System Identification with Gaussian Processes, involve solving linear systems with symmetric positive-definite matrices. The iterative CG method and direct solvers based on the Cholesky decomposition are two popular methods that can be applied in this case. Since often very large systems have to be solved when dealing with such real-world scenarios, GPUs are commonly used to accelerate the computations. However, homogeneous approaches that only leverage the GPU in the system do not take full advantage of the often powerful CPUs located in modern HPC systems. In this work, we present multi-vendor, heterogeneous implementations of the CG method and the Cholesky decomposition that leverage the CPU and GPU of a heterogeneous system simultaneously using SYCL. Furthermore, we compare their runtime behavior to traditional, homogeneous approaches. The results show that for large matrices, our heterogeneous implementation is up to 32 percent faster for the CG method and up to 29 percent faster for the Cholesky decomposition compared to the corresponding GPU-only implementations. In addition, for large matrices, our heterogeneous implementation of the Cholesky decomposition can achieve at least 12 percent faster runtimes across several systems with GPUs from NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel.

</details>


### [224] [Communication Efficient Byzantine Agreement with Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12935)
*Muhammad Ayaz Dzulfikar,Seth Gilbert*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在拜占庭共识中利用预测（predictions）加速的方法，通过改进算法设计，成功在保持最优轮次性能的同时，将通信复杂度从 $\Omega(n^3)$ 大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的利用预测（predictions）加速拜占庭共识的算法主要存在两个缺陷：一是处理预测错误的能力有限，二是通信开销过大（需交换 $\Omega(n^3)$ 比特，这被认为是必须的）。研究旨在降低算法的通信复杂度，同时保持其处理大量错误预测的能力和最优的轮次复杂度。

Method: 提出两种算法：1. 无验证算法，通信复杂度为 $\tilde{O}(n^{2.5})$；2. 带验证算法，通信复杂度达到最优的 $O(n^2\kappa)$。两种算法均基于对预测机制的优化，去除了对分享大量预测比特信号的依赖。

Result: 成功突破了原有的 $\Omega(n^3)$ 通信壁垒，实现了在非认证环境下 $\tilde{O}(n^{2.5})$ 和认证环境下 $O(n^2\kappa)$ 的通信复杂度，且均达到了最优的轮次复杂度。

Conclusion: 通信开销（即分享预测信息）并非实现快速拜占庭共识的必要高昂成本，通过算法优化可以在保持最优轮次复杂度的同时显著降低通信复杂度。

Abstract: In Byzantine agreement with predictions each process begins with an input value and some (unreliable) prediction bits. Recently, it has been shown that with \emph{classification predictions} -- where the predictions predict each process to be honest or faulty -- Byzantine agreement can be completed more quickly than without predictions, circumventing the traditional $Ω(f)$ round lower bound. However, existing algorithms either handle limited prediction errors or send too many messages. Moreover, they all exchange $Ω(n^3)$ bits -- enough to allow the processes to approximately agree on the classifications. In fact, it almost seemed necessary to share a significant number of prediction bits if one wanted to tolerate a high number of incorrect predictions.
  In this paper, we show that this high level of communication (and sharing of predictions) is not inherent by developing an unauthenticated algorithm with $\tilde{O}(n^{2.5})$ communication complexity. Furthermore, with authentication, we give an algorithm with optimal $O(n^2κ)$ communication complexity (where $κ$ is a security parameter). All of our results have optimal round complexity for any number of errors in the predictions.

</details>


### [225] [PipeSD: An Efficient Cloud-Edge Collaborative Pipeline Inference Framework with Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13319)
*Yunhe Han,Yunqi Gao,Bing Hu,Mahdi Boloursaz Mashhadi,Yitong Duan,Pei Xiao,Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PipeSD 是一种针对云边协作环境优化的推测解码框架，通过流水线调度重叠计算与通信、并利用双阈值机制优化验证触发，显著提升了 LLM 推理效率并降低了能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的云边协同推断框架在应用推测解码时存在两个限制：1. 令牌生成与通信串行化，导致资源利用率低；2. 云端非自回归验证触发机制僵化，易引发提前验证或代价高昂的回滚。

Method: 1. 提出基于动态规划的令牌批量流水线调度机制，实现令牌生成与通信的重叠；2. 设计双阈值非自回归验证（NAV）触发机制，并引入轻量级贝叶斯优化自调参器以提高验证灵活性。

Result: 在云边测试床上的实验表明，PipeSD 相比现有基线实现了 1.16 倍至 2.16 倍的加速，并将能耗降低了 14.3% 至 25.3%。

Conclusion: PipeSD 通过流水线调度和灵活的验证机制解决了云边协同推理的资源利用率低和验证成本高的问题，为 LLM 的高效部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding can significantly accelerate LLM inference, especially given that its cloud-edge collaborative deployment offers cloud workload offloading, offline robustness, and privacy enhancement. However, existing collaborative inference frameworks with speculative decoding are constrained by (i) sequential token generation and communication with low resource utilization, and (ii) inflexible cloud non-autoregressive verification (NAV) triggering that induces premature verification or costly rollbacks. In this paper, we propose PipeSD, an efficient cloud-edge collaborative pipeline inference framework with speculative decoding. PipeSD overlaps token generation and communication by a token-batch pipeline scheduling mechanism optimized by dynamic programming, and improves verification flexibility through a dual-threshold NAV triggering mechanism with a lightweight Bayesian optimization autotuner. We implement PipeSD using llama-cpp-python, PyTorch, and FastAPI, and evaluate it on a real-world cloud-edge testbed with two draft-target model pairs across four scenarios. Results show that PipeSD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving 1.16x-2.16x speedup and reducing energy consumption by 14.3%-25.3%.

</details>


### [226] [TurboGR: An Accelerated Training System for Large-Scale Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13433)
*Huichao Chai,Zhixin Wu,Xuemiao Li,Shiqing Fan,Hengfeng Wang,Maojun Peng,Lu Xu,Yaoyuan Wang,Yibo Jin,Wei Guo,Yongxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了针对生成式推荐系统的昇腾NPU协同训练系统，通过优化锯齿状算子、分布式通信和负采样策略，解决了NPU在处理稀疏、不规则推荐任务时的性能瓶颈，显著提升了训练效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐（GR）在通用Transformer架构下展现出良好的扩展性，但在昇腾（Ascend）NPU上存在系统级部署难题，主要源于缺乏高性能锯齿状（jagged）算子实现，以及稀疏计算操作与NPU密集计算架构的不匹配。

Method: 提出了名为“Ascend-affinity training system”的系统架构，核心技术包括：(i) 基于融合算子的锯齿状数据加速与动态负载均衡；(ii) 层次化稀疏并行、半异步训练及细粒度流水线编排；(iii) 异步卸载、锯齿感知FP16量化及批内Logits共享的负采样优化。

Result: 在KuaiRand-27K数据集上，系统支持高达2亿（0.2B）参数的训练，模型算力利用率（MFU）达到54.71%，扩展性系数达到0.97。

Conclusion: 通过针对昇腾NPU架构量身定制的三大创新技术（锯齿状算子加速、分布式通信优化、负采样优化），成功克服了生成式推荐系统在NPU上部署面临的性能瓶颈，实现了高利用率和近线性的扩展性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm that replaces fragmented, scenario-specific architectures with unified Transformer-based models, exhibiting scaling-law behavior where recommendation quality improves systematically with increased model capacity and training data. However, deploying GR at scale on Ascend NPUs faces fundamental system-level challenges. These challenges are further exacerbated on Ascend NPUs due to the absence of high-performance implementations for jagged operators and the architectural mismatch between irregular sparse primitives and NPU's dense-computation-optimized design. In this paper, we present \model, an Ascend-affinity training system for generative recommendation that systematically addresses these bottlenecks through three core innovations: (i) Ascend-affinity jagged acceleration, including fusion operators that eliminate padding redundancy and dynamic load balancing that reduces inter-device imbalance from 47\% to 2.4\%; (ii) distributed communication optimization, comprising hierarchical sparse parallelism, semi-asynchronous training with proven convergence guarantees, and fine-grained pipeline orchestration that sustains 94\% NPU utilization; and (iii) negative sampling optimization via asynchronous offloading, jaggedness-aware FP16 quantization, and intra-batch logit sharing that expand the effective negative space without additional embedding lookups. Evaluated on the KuaiRand-27K dataset, \model supports training at up to 0.2B parameters and achieves 54.71\% MFU with near-linear scalability (0.97).

</details>


### [227] [Sustainable Graph Analytics Workload Scheduling with Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning in Edge-Cloud Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13489)
*P. Ramicetty,H. Moore,S. Qi,A. Islam,M. Ghose,D. Milojicic,C. Bash,S. Pasricha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MERSEM 是一种结合进化算法和强化学习的新型框架，旨在优化边缘-云环境中的图工作负载分配，在显著降低 SLA 违规的同时，有效减少系统的运营碳足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着图分析任务在现代智能系统中的扩展，其在异构边缘-云环境下的执行导致了高能耗和碳排放挑战，现有的调度方案难以在服务质量（SLA）与环境可持续性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出了一个多目标进化强化学习框架（MERSEM），将进化搜索（用于全局解空间探索）与强化学习（用于局部自适应策略优化）相结合，针对异构边缘-云环境中的图工作负载进行联合调度优化。

Result: 实验表明，与现有最先进（SOTA）方法相比，MERSEM 最高可降低 45% 的 SLA 违规率，并减少 12% 的碳排放。

Conclusion: MERSEM 通过结合进化搜索与强化学习，证明了在边缘-云环境下有效平衡 SLA 约束与环境可持续性（减少碳排放）的可行性，为大规模图计算任务的资源调度提供了高效的可持续解决方案。

Abstract: Graph analytics powers modern intelligent systems such as smart cities, cyber-physical infrastructure, IoT security, and large-scale social networks. As these workloads scale in complexity, their execution in heterogeneous edge-cloud environments results in higher energy use and carbon emission footprint. To address this challenge, we propose MERSEM, a multi-objective evolutionary reinforcement learning framework for sustainable edge-cloud system management. MERSEM integrates evolutionary search with reinforcement learning (RL) to solve the problem of graph workload allocation and scheduling. The evolutionary component explores diverse global solutions, while the RL agent refines decisions through adaptive local optimization. The framework is designed to jointly minimize service-level agreement (SLA) violations and carbon emissions by considering dynamic carbon intensity, resource heterogeneity, and workload characteristics. Experimental results demonstrate that MERSEM outperforms the state-of-the-art with up to 45% SLA violation reductions and up to 12% carbon emission reductions.

</details>


### [228] [MARLIN: Multi-Agent Game-Theoretic Reinforcement Learning for Sustainable LLM Inference in Cloud Datacenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13496)
*H. Moore,S. Qi,D. Milojicic,C. Bash,S. Pasricha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 为降低大语言模型（LLM）推理带来的高昂环境成本，本文提出了 MARLIN 框架，通过多智能体强化学习在保证推理性能的同时，显著降低了碳排放、水耗及能源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理阶段的能耗占整个生命周期的 90%，且推理请求的激增带来了严重的碳排放和水资源消耗问题，亟需提升云数据中心 LLM 推理服务的可持续性。

Method: 提出了一种基于多智能体博弈论的强化学习框架（MARLIN），用于协同优化云数据中心环境下 LLM 推理的 TTFT、碳排放、水资源消耗和能源成本。

Result: 与现有的最先进 LLM 推理管理框架相比，MARLIN 将 TTFT 降低了至少 18%，碳排放降低了 33%，水耗降低了 43%，能源成本降低了 11%。

Conclusion: MARLIN 框架通过多智能体强化学习成功实现了性能（TTFT）与环境可持续性（碳排放、水耗、能源成本）的协同优化，证明了在 LLM 推理服务中平衡性能与环境目标的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become increasingly prevalent in cloud-based platforms, propelled by the introduction of AI-based consumer and enterprise services. LLM inference requests in particular account for up to 90% of total LLM lifecycle energy use, dwarfing training energy costs. The rising volume of LLM inference requests is increasing environmental footprints, particularly carbon emissions and water consumption. To improve sustainability for LLM inference serving in cloud datacenter environments, we propose a novel multi-agent game-theoretic reinforcement learning framework called MARLIN to co-optimize time-to-first token (TTFT), carbon emissions, water usage, and energy costs associated with LLM inference. MARLIN demonstrates a reduction of at least 18% in TTFT, 33% in carbon emissions, 43% in water usage, and 11% in energy costs compared to state-of-the-art LLM inference management frameworks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [A detailed algorithmic study on a reuse-aware, near memory, all-digital Ising machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12959)
*Siddhartha Raman Sundara Raman,Lizy K. John,Jaydeep P. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SACHI 是一种通过改造 CPU L1 缓存实现的全数字 Ising 加速器，无需 ADC/DAC，在提升性能和能效的同时，通过与 CPU 流水线的深度集成实现了高可靠性和大规模问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Ising 加速器（如量子、光学或基于 CMOS 的架构）通常局限于特定问题，且过度依赖 ADC/DAC 电路，在嵌入式存储技术中存在工艺偏差导致的可靠性挑战。

Method: 提出了一种全数字架构 SACHI，通过基于 SRAM 的近存计算（PIM）技术，将 CPU 的 L1 缓存重新利用为 Ising 加速器。该方法集成了 CPU 流水线，采用了重用感知（reuse-aware）的计算和映射策略，避免了 ADC/DAC 的使用。

Result: 与现有的 BRIM 架构相比，SACHI 在资产配置、分子动力学、图像分割和旅行商问题等任务中，性能提升了 300 倍，能效提升了 80 倍，且部分工作负载的硬件复用率高达 4000 倍。

Conclusion: SACHI 证明了利用通用 CPU 中的商品化 SRAM 结构，可以实现可靠、高效且全数字化的 Ising 加速，无需专业的 ADC/DAC 电路，且具有很高的硬件复用潜力。

Abstract: Recently, nature-inspired computing approaches have gained significant attention for solving difficult optimization problems, particularly through Ising machines for NP-complete applications. Existing Ising accelerators range from quantum and optical annealers to CMOS-based von-Neumann and in-memory architectures. However, many prior designs are specialized accelerators limited to specific problem classes, rely on ADC/DAC circuits, and suffer from reliability challenges due to process-variation-sensitive embedded memory technologies.
  This paper presents SACHI, an all-digital Ising architecture implemented by repurposing the L1 cache of a CPU using SRAM-based processing-in-memory techniques. SACHI eliminates the need for ADCs/DACs, improves reliability compared to prior approaches such as BRIM, and enables Ising acceleration with minimal hardware overhead integrated into the CPU pipeline. The paper also provides detailed architectural analysis and pseudo-code for the proposed algorithms.
  The key contributions of SACHI are: (i) tight integration of the accelerator with the CPU pipeline, (ii) reuse of existing cache hardware for acceleration, (iii) higher parallelism enabled through reuse-aware computation, and (iv) improved performance and energy efficiency for large-scale, high-precision optimization problems using novel compute and mapping strategies.
  Compared to BRIM, SACHI achieves 300x performance improvement and 80x energy reduction across applications including asset allocation, molecular dynamics, image segmentation, and traveling salesman problems. Additionally, reuse factors up to 4000x are observed for several workloads. This work demonstrates that reliable and efficient all-digital Ising acceleration can be achieved using commodity SRAM structures tightly integrated with general-purpose processors.

</details>


### [230] [PoisonCap: Efficient Hierarchical Temporal Safety for CHERI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13210)
*Yuecheng Wang,Jonathan Woodruff,Alfredo Mazzinghi,Peter Rugg,Alexandre Joannou,Samuel W. Stark,Robert N. M. Watson,Simon W. Moore*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PoisonCap 是一种为 CHERI 硬件架构设计的高效时序安全方案，通过新型“中毒”能力格式，在不增加性能负担的情况下，实现了严格的用后释放保护和内存初始化安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 CHERI 时序安全方案（如 Cornucopia Reloaded）仅能提供较弱的“用后重分配”安全，且无法强制执行初始化安全，亟需一种兼顾高性能与严格时序安全性的解决方案。

Method: 引入了一种新型的“中毒（poison）”能力格式，用于强制执行严格的用后释放和初始化安全，利用能力边界（capability bounds）实现内存中毒特权的优雅委托，并结合微架构优化实现隔离内存的高效缓存管理。

Result: PoisonCap 能够替代 Cornucopia 的阴影位图，自动清零重分配内存或可选地在读取未初始化内存时触发陷阱，且在与执行清零操作的 Cornucopia 基准对比时，未引入额外的性能开销。

Conclusion: PoisonCap 成功在不增加性能开销的前提下，显著增强了 CHERI 系统的内存安全性，提供了比现有技术更强的用后释放（use-after-free）及初始化安全性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present PoisonCap: scalable temporal safety with strict use-after-free protection and initialisation safety for CHERI systems. Efficient memory safety is an increasing priority for programming languages, operating systems, and hardware designs, and CHERI is a leading hardware/software system that provides native spatial safety and a foundation for temporal memory safety. Cornucopia Reloaded, the current state-of-the-art CHERI temporal safety solution, provides use-after-reallocation safety instead of stronger use-after-free safety, and is not able to enforce initialisation safety. We show that a new 'poison' capability format can be used to enforce strict use-after-free and initialisation safety, and also to communicate memory state to the microarchitecture for efficient cache management of quarantined memory. We enable elegant delegation of memory poisoning privilege using capability bounds to allow nested allocators to enforce safety on their consumers without disturbing upstream allocators. PoisonCap can replace the Cornucopia shadow bitmap, and also automatically zeros memory on reallocation, or optionally traps on read-before-write to enforce initialisation safety. As a result, it incurs no fundamental overhead relative to a Cornucopia baseline that zeros before reallocation, strengthening CHERI temporal safety without performance overhead.

</details>


### [231] [Reward-Weighted On-Policy Distillation with an Open Property-Equivalence Verifier for NL-to-SVA Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13501)
*Qingyun Zou,Yingze Li,Tianen Liu,Bingsheng He,Weng-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种RWOPD（奖励加权在线策略蒸馏）方法，通过引入形式化验证反馈（PEC）引导SVA生成模型学习属性等价性，有效解决了现有微调方法在复杂断言生成中的稳定性问题，并实现了高性能的轻量级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于监督微调的SVA（SystemVerilog断言）生成模型在处理有界延迟和活性属性时存在模型坍塌问题（仅依赖少数模板），且过分依赖标记级别的模仿而非断言规范的属性等价性。

Method: 提出“奖励加权在线策略蒸馏”（RWOPD）。该方法通过SymbiYosys+Z3形式化等价性检查器（PEC）对学生模型的输出进行评分，利用验证通过的轨迹进行奖励加权前向KL散度梯度更新，使模型从单纯的标记模仿转向属性等价性学习。

Result: 基于Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct蒸馏后的模型在NL2SVA-Human和NL2SVA-Machine数据集的pass@1, pass@5, pass@10指标上均超越了现有的专用SOTA模型及671B参数的通用模型。

Conclusion: RWOPD方法通过将形式化验证反馈集成到训练循环中，成功解决了SVA生成中的模型坍塌问题，实现了在小参数量下对大型通用模型及专用模型的超越，为NL2SVA任务设定了新的SOTA。

Abstract: LLM-based generation of SystemVerilog Assertions (SVA) is often reported as nearing saturation, with the strongest specialized model reaching ${\sim}76\%$ accuracy on NL2SVA-Human. We show that this aggregate hides a temporal gap: models that appear strong overall still collapse to a few implication templates on bounded-delay and liveness specifications. The core issue is that the dominant recipe, supervised fine-tuning on NL/SVA pairs, optimizes token-level mimicry rather than the \emph{property equivalence} that defines SVA correctness. We introduce \emph{Reward-Weighted On-Policy Distillation} (RWOPD), an on-policy distillation method that samples student rollouts, scores them with an open SymbiYosys+Z3 Property-Equivalence Checker (PEC), and applies a verifier-reward-weighted forward-KL gradient from a frozen 14B teacher on verifier-passable rollouts. This keeps the supervision dense at every response token while grounding both selection and loss weight in property-equivalent behavior. RWOPD distills CodeV-SVA-14B into a Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct student that sets a new state of the art on NL2SVA-Human and NL2SVA-Machine across pass@1, pass@5, and pass@10, surpassing both specialized prior SOTA models and 671B general-purpose baselines.

</details>


### [232] [Memristor Technologies for Dynamic Vision Sensors: A Critical Assessment and Research Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13699)
*Mohamad Yazan Sadoun,Edris Zaman Farsa,Sarah Sharif,Yaser Mike Banad*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该论文指出通过事件驱动视觉（DVS）与存内计算（IMC）结合可显著提升边缘AI能效，但目前研究多停留在理论推演阶段；作者通过分类学评估了其技术成熟度，并明确了全集成DVS-忆阻器系统作为下一代核心研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI部署的主要瓶颈在于数据移动能耗，而将事件驱动视觉传感器与存内模拟计算相结合有望解决此问题。然而，目前缺乏明确的框架来区分已制造的演示原型与理论推测系统。

Method: 采用证据分级综述方法，构建了三种架构分类法，并对比现有数字神经形态硬件，通过横向评估六个应用领域（机器人、自动驾驶、AR/VR、监控、医学影像、IoT）的技术成熟度（TRL 2-5级）。

Result: 研究发现，所调研的应用领域中，一半完全依赖于理论推演；现有硬件大多处于实验阶段（TRL 2-5级），距离实际商用存在显著的技术差距。

Conclusion: 当前Edge-AI领域缺乏将事件驱动视觉传感器（DVS）与存内计算（IMC）有效集成的成熟架构。端到端的DVS-忆阻器集成系统是该领域的核心挑战，且该研究为评估未来性能提供了明确的准确率与功耗基准。

Abstract: Edge-AI deployment is bottlenecked by data-movement energy; pairing event-driven vision sensors with in-memory analog compute could lift that ceiling by orders of magnitude. Both technologies are individually mature; the framework distinguishing fabricated demonstrations from projected systems is missing. Of six application domains surveyed (robotics, autonomous vehicles, AR/VR, surveillance, medical imaging, IoT), half rest entirely on projection, and existing hardware sits at Technology Readiness Levels 2-5. This evidence-graded review applies a three-paradigm architectural taxonomy and benchmarks the gap against current digital neuromorphic alternatives. It identifies an end-to-end integrated DVS-memristor system as the field's open challenge, with testable accuracy and power targets.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Action Emergence from Streaming Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12622)
*Pengfei Jing,Victor Shea-Jay Huang,Hengtong Lu,Jifeng Dai,Xie Yan,Benjin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决端到端自动驾驶中轨迹生成缺乏可控性的问题，本文提出基于“流式意图”的VLA模型SI，通过思维链推理引导流匹配生成，实现了既具备模态多样性又高度可控的动作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端自动驾驶范式存在局限：自回归轨迹解码器倾向于产生平均化输出（失去模态），而扩散/流匹配模型虽具备模态表现力但在推理引导上缺乏可控性。因此，需要实现“动作涌现（Action Emergence）”能力，即在任意长尾场景中生成物理可行、合规的动作。

Method: 提出“流式意图（Streaming Intent）”框架，包含两个核心：1. 语义流式处理（基于因果逻辑的思维链CoT，从场景理解推导意图）；2. 时间流式处理（跨片段的时间一致性）。在模型实现上，SI通过自回归解码思维链生成意图Token，进而利用该意图引导流匹配（flow-matching）动作头，仅需两步去噪即可生成轨迹。

Result: 在Waymo端到端基准测试中表现出色，验证集RFS得分为7.96，测试集为7.74。更重要的是，该模型在保持高水平规划的同时，实现了通过改变意图类别来获得差异化驾驶策略的能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入“流式意图（Streaming Intent）”机制，SI模型首次在端到端视觉语言动作（VLA）模型中实现了符合意图的可控驾驶，无需预设轨迹库或硬编码选择器。

Abstract: We formalize action emergence as a target capability for end-to-end autonomous driving: the ability to generate physically feasible, semantically appropriate, and safety-compliant actions in arbitrary, long-tail traffic scenes through scene-conditioned reasoning rather than retrieval or interpolation of learned scene-action mappings. We show that previous paradigms cannot deliver action emergence: autoregressive trajectory decoders collapse the inherently multimodal future into a single averaged output, while diffusion and flow-matching generators express multimodality but are not steerable by reasoned intent. We propose Streaming Intent as a concrete way to approach action emergence: a mechanism that makes driving intent (i) semantically streamed through a continuous chain-of-thought that causally derives the intent from scene understanding, and (ii) temporally streamed across clips so that intent commitments remain coherent along the driving horizon. We realize Streaming Intent in a VLA model we call SI (Streaming Intent). SI autoregressively decodes a four-step chain-of-thought and emits an intent token; the decoded intent then drives classifier-free guidance (CFG) on a flow-matching action head, requiring only two denoising steps to generate the final trajectory. On the Waymo End-to-End benchmark, SI achieves competitive aggregate performance, with an RFS score of 7.96 on the validation set and 7.74 on the test set. Beyond aggregate metrics, the model demonstrates -- to our knowledge for the first time in a fully end-to-end VLA -- intent-faithful controllability: for a fixed scene, varying the intent class at inference yields qualitatively distinct yet consistently high-quality plans, arising purely from data-driven learning without any pre-built trajectory bank or hand-coded post-hoc selector.

</details>


### [234] [MindVLA-U1: VLA Beats VA with Unified Streaming Architecture for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12624)
*Yuzhou Huang,Benjin Zhu,Hengtong Lu,Victor Shea-Jay Huang,Haiming Zhang,Wei Chen,Jifeng Dai,Yan Xie,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MindVLA-U1 是一种新型统一流式自动驾驶 VLA 架构，通过共享表征协同推理语言意图与连续动作轨迹，在实现超人类规划性能的同时保持了高效的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶 VLA 模型在规划质量上往往落后于视觉到动作（VA）模型。作者认为这是由于目前的 VLA 构建方式过于孤立，未能有效统一语义理解中逻辑、时序与连续控制之间的接口。

Method: 提出了一种统一的流式 VLA 架构：1. 共享 VLM 主干实现语言 token 和流匹配连续动作轨迹的同步生成；2. 采用流式设计（按帧处理）配合学习记忆通道以保持时序上下文；3. 利用混合专家模型（MoT）支持灵活的计算分配；4. 设计了通过语言意图引导动作扩散（CFG）的路径，实现语言到控制信号的转换。

Result: 在 WOD-E2E 长尾基准测试中，MindVLA-U1 首次超过人类驾驶水平（8.20 RFS vs. 8.13 GT RFS），大幅提升了规划的平均位移误差（ADE），并达到了与 VA 模型相当的高处理吞吐量（16 FPS）。

Conclusion: MindVLA-U1 证明了通过统一架构将语义推理、时序上下文和连续控制结合，VLA 模型能够超越传统 VA 模型，在实现媲美人类驾驶性能的同时保持高效的推理能力和自然语言交互接口。

Abstract: Autonomous driving has progressed from modular pipelines toward end-to-end unification, and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a natural extension of this journey beyond Vision-to-Action (VA). In practice, driving VLAs have often trailed VA on planning quality, suggesting that the difficulty is not simply model scale but the interface through which semantic reasoning, temporal context, and continuous control are combined. We argue that this gap reflects how VLA has been built -- as isolated subtask improvements that fail to compose into coherent driving capabilities -- rather than what VLA is. We present MindVLA-U1, the first unified streaming VLA architecture for autonomous driving. A unified VLM backbone produces autoregressive language tokens and flow-matching continuous action trajectories in a single forward pass over one shared representation, preserving the natural output form of each modality. A streaming design processes the driving video framewise rather than as fixed video-action chunks, while a learned memory channel carries temporal context across frames so planned trajectories evolve smoothly without redundant multi-frame VLM modeling. The unified architecture admits fast/slow execution on dense/sparse Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbones via flexible self-attention context management, and exposes a measurable language-to-action route: a language-predicted driving intent steers action diffusion through classifier-free guidance (CFG), turning language-side intent into a control signal for continuous trajectory generation. On the long-tail WOD-E2E benchmark, MindVLA-U1 surpasses experienced human drivers for the first time (8.20 RFS vs. 8.13 GT RFS) with 2 diffusion steps, achieves state-of-the-art planning ADEs over prior VA/VLA methods by large margins, and matches VA-class throughput (16 FPS vs. RAP-DINO's 18 FPS) while preserving natural-language interfaces.

</details>


### [235] [Driving Intents Amplify Planning-Oriented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12625)
*Hengtong Lu,Victor Shea-Jay Huang,Chengmin Yang,Pengfei Jing,Jifeng Dai,Yan Xie,Benjin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对端到端自动驾驶训练中的模式坍缩难题，DIAL框架通过意图条件下的CFG扩展采样分布，并结合多意图GRPO进行偏好优化，有效提升了自动驾驶策略的决策多样性与性能上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有驾驶策略在仅有单条演示数据时出现的模式坍缩问题，即策略无法生成语义上各异的合理机动，导致即便在高采样数（Best-of-N）下表现也受限于原始演示分布的局限。

Method: 提出DIAL（Driving-Intent-Amplified RL）框架，包含两个阶段：1. 引入带分类器自由引导（CFG）的流匹配动作头，依据离散意图标签扩展采样分布；2. 使用多意图GRPO（Multi-intent GRPO）在偏好RL阶段跨意图进行优化，防止细调过程中的模式重新坍缩。

Result: 在WOD-E2E数据集上，DIAL在RFS（评分）上达到9.14，首次超越人类驾驶表现（8.13）及强基线RAP（8.5）；多意图GRPO显著提升了保留测试集的RFS评分，克服了单意图基线在训练后期性能退化的问题。

Conclusion: 在基于偏好的连续动作策略训练中，单条演示数据导致的模式坍缩（Mode Collapse）是主要瓶颈。解决该问题的关键不仅在于优化算法本身，更在于在整个偏好对齐过程中通过显式的意图建模来扩展并维护动作空间的采样分布。

Abstract: Continuous-action policies trained on a single demonstrated trajectory per scene suffer from mode collapse: samples cluster around the demonstrated maneuver and the policy cannot represent semantically distinct alternatives. Under preference-based evaluation, this caps best-of-N performance -- even oracle selection cannot recover what the sampling distribution does not contain. We introduce DIAL, a two-stage Driving-Intent-Amplified reinforcement Learning framework for preference-aligned continuous-action driving policies. In the first stage, DIAL conditions the flow-matching action head on a discrete intent label with classifier-free guidance (CFG), which expands the sampling distribution along distinct maneuver modes and breaks single-demonstration mode collapse. In the second stage, DIAL carries this expanded distribution into preference RL through multi-intent GRPO, which spans all intent classes within every preference group and prevents fine-tuning from re-collapsing around the currently preferred mode. Instantiated for end-to-end driving with eight rule-derived intents and evaluated on WOD-E2E: competitive Vision-to-Action (VA) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Supervised Finetuning (SFT) baselines plateau below the human-driven demonstration at best-of-128, with the strongest prior (RAP) capping at Rater Feedback Score (RFS) 8.5 even with best-of-64; intent-CFG sampling lifts this ceiling to RFS 9.14 at best-of-128, surpassing both the prior best (RAP 8.5) and the human-driven demonstration (8.13) for the first time; and multi-intent GRPO improves held-out RFS from 7.681 to 8.211, while every single-intent baseline peaks lower and degrades by training end. These results suggest that the bottleneck of preference RL on continuous-action policies trained from demonstrations is not only how to update the policy, but to expand and preserve the sampling distribution being optimized.

</details>


### [236] [Multistep Belief Space Dynamics Learning For Risk-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12628)
*Jason Gibson,Bogdan Vlahov,Patrick Spieler,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合结构化分布动态学习与MPC的自动驾驶框架，有效克服了传统系统在复杂越野环境下的动态不确定性预测难题，实现了更加智能、风险感知且自然的驾驶控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统与人类驾驶之间存在性能差距，尤其在复杂真实环境中，系统难以既能保持风险意识又能避免过度保守，核心难点在于如何准确预测并规划动态不确定性。

Method: 提出了一种学习框架，专门用于预测可实时优化的分布动力学（Distributional Dynamics），并将其集成进模型预测控制（MPC）中。通过引入结构化设计的模型，并在大规模真实越野驾驶数据集上进行消融实验，验证了结构的有效性，最终在全尺寸车辆上进行了实车部署。

Result: 实验结果显示，通过所提的结构化框架，车辆能够根据环境自然地调节行驶速度，在多种复杂地形下表现出持续的驾驶智能，且在实车部署中验证了方案的可行性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在MPC架构中引入结构化学习预测分布动态，能够在处理真实世界越野环境的不确定性时，实现具备风险意识的智能驾驶，且不会显得过于保守。

Abstract: As autonomous vehicles move from a simplified research setting to practical use, there exists a large gap between the dynamic behavior of a human driving and an autonomous system. Risk-aware behavior needs to naturally develop in order to scale to the demands of the real world. A major issue for risk-aware planning and control has been predicting how dynamical uncertainty evolves through time and optimizing plans that account for this without being overly conservative. Here, we present a learning framework to predict distributional dynamics that can be optimized in real time for Model Predictive Control (MPC). We explore the importance of structure when learning distributional dynamics for use in MPC. A rigorous ablation study is conducted on a large dataset of real world off-road driving that shows the impact of deviations from our proposed structure. Furthermore, we deploy our learned model and planning stack on a full sized vehicle in challenging off-road conditions. Our planning architecture is able to naturally regulate the speed of the vehicle based on the environment and consistently demonstrates intelligent behavior over miles of diverse terrain.

</details>


### [237] [Belief-Space Residual Risk for Automated Driving under Localization Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12710)
*Nijinshan Karunainayagam,Nils Gehrke,Frank Diermeyer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将自车定位不确定性整合进残余风险评估的新方法，通过分布建模与协方差融合，提升了自动驾驶系统在非理想定位条件下的安全性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶残余风险评估方法多假设自车位姿为确定性因素，忽略了城市及极端天气下定位偏差带来的显著安全隐患。

Method: 提出了一种基于信念空间的残余风险扩展方法：1）将自车位姿不确定性建模为高斯分布；2）将残余风险重构为自车位姿信念分布下的期望退化风险；3）采用基于粒子（particle-based）的风险估计框架，通过自车与目标物体不确定性的协方差融合计算碰撞概率。

Result: 实现了在风险评估中对自车定位不确定性的显式建模，克服了传统方法仅关注感知误差和延迟效应的局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过概率建模成功将定位不确定性纳入残余风险评估框架，显著提升了自动驾驶系统在复杂环境下安全评估的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Residual risk metrics have recently been introduced to assess the safety implications of automated driving systems. Existing approaches typically assume a deterministic ego pose and concentrate mainly on perception errors related to surrounding objects and latency effects. In practice, however, automated vehicles operate under considerable localization uncertainty, especially in complex urban settings and in adverse weather conditions. This work extends the spatial residual risk formulation to the belief space by explicitly modeling ego pose uncertainty as a Gaussian distribution. Residual risk is reformulated as the expected degradation-induced risk over the ego pose belief distribution. Within a particle-based risk estimation framework, localization uncertainty is incorporated into the computation of collision probabilities through covariance fusion of ego and object uncertainties.

</details>


### [238] [A Five-Layer MLOps Architecture for Connected Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12719)
*Bastian Lampe,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于自动驾驶系统的集体学习MLOps五层架构，通过跨车队数据共享与多级自评估，提升系统应对极端罕见场景的安全保障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统在开放世界中运行，面临难以预见的罕见场景（边缘案例/黑天鹅事件）挑战；利用AI/ML的自主学习能力及车队间的集体数据共享，是实现系统持续安全与性能改进的关键。

Method: 基于MLOps原则及车联网技术现状，设计了一套包含五层架构的处理流程，明确了各层级职责及其交互机制。

Result: 构建了一套标准化的五层架构模型，该架构支持跨车队知识共享与集体学习，并通过多级自评估机制增强了对复杂环境的适应性和对潜在安全隐患的识别能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的五层架构为自动驾驶系统的集体学习驱动的MLOps提供了系统性的概念蓝图，能够有效支持多级自评估，从而识别并缓解长尾场景及“黑天鹅”事件，为保障系统持续的安全与性能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The continual assurance of safety and performance of automated driving systems (ADSs) poses significant challenges. ADSs operate in complex, dynamic, open-world environments allowing a wide range of scenarios, including ones that are rare or not foreseen during initial development. While the incorporation of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) technology allows ADSs to learn from data gathered during operation and thus enables them to adapt over time, these approaches come with their own challenges. A key advantage of ADSs compared to human drivers is their greater ability to gather data collectively across a fleet of vehicles, or even across multiple fleets operated by different entities, and to learn from this data collectively. Vehicles can share and combine their data to identify additional learning opportunities otherwise missed by individual vehicles. This creates new opportunities to tackle the challenges of continual assurance of safety and performance, but requires the implementation of architectures that leverage the collective learning potential. Based on established MLOps principles and existing work in the field of connected automated driving, this paper presents a five-layer architecture for collective learning-enabled MLOps processes for ADSs. The goal of this architecture is to provide a conceptual blueprint for the design and implementation of MLOps processes by fleet operators and other relevant stakeholders. The paper describes the main responsibilities of each layer, their interactions, and how multi-level self-assessments enabled by the architecture can support the detection and reduction of edge cases including black swan events.

</details>


### [239] [The Unified Autonomy Stack: Toward a Blueprint for Generalizable Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12735)
*Mihir Dharmadhikari,Nikhil Khedekar,Mihir Kulkarni,Morten Nissov,Martin Jacquet,Angelos Zacharia,Marvin Harms,Albert Gassol Puigjaner,Philipp Weiss,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开源了“Unified Autonomy Stack”，这是一套适用于多种空中与地面机器人平台的通用自主系统，涵盖多模态感知、启发式规划与多层级安全导航，在复杂和高风险环境中表现出极高的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人自主系统通常针对单一平台或受限环境开发，缺乏跨不同机器人形态（空中与地面）及复杂挑战环境（感知退化、GNSS缺失等）的通用化、高鲁棒性解决方案。

Method: 构建了包含三大核心协同模块的系统架构：1. 多模态感知（利用因子图进行LiDAR、雷达、视觉及惯性导航融合，实现定位与语义理解）；2. 多行为规划（采用自适应采样算法进行运动与信息路径规划）；3. 多层级安全导航（结合在线地图规划、深度学习外感受策略及基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的安全预警机制）。

Result: 通过在旋翼机和足式机器人上的广泛实地测试证明，该系统在复杂几何结构、高障碍物拥挤、烟雾覆盖及GNSS拒绝等极端环境下展现了卓越的自主导航、环境探索及任务执行能力。

Conclusion: Unified Autonomy Stack 提供了一套模块化、跨形态的机器人自主运行解决方案，能够显著提升机器人在复杂、受限及感知退化环境中的鲁棒性和任务执行能力，并具备良好的可扩展性与开源可用性。

Abstract: We introduce and open-source the Unified Autonomy Stack, a system-level solution that enables resilient autonomy across diverse aerial and ground robot morphologies. The architecture centers on three synergistic modules -- multi-modal perception, multi-behavior planning, and multi-layered safe navigation -- that together deliver comprehensive mission autonomy. The stack fuses data from LiDAR, radar, vision, and inertial sensing, enabling (a) robust localization and mapping through factor graph-based fusion, (b) semantic scene understanding, (c) motion and informative path planning through sampling-based techniques adaptive across spatial scales, as well as (d) multi-layered safe navigation both through planning on the online reconstructed map and deep learning-driven exteroceptive policies alongside last-resort safety filters using control barrier functions. The resulting behaviors include safe GNSS-denied navigation into unknown and perceptually-degraded regions, exploration of complex environments, object discovery, and efficient inspection planning. The stack has been field-tested and validated on both aerial (rotorcraft) and ground (legged) robots operating in a host of demanding environments, including self-similar and smoke-filled settings, with complex geometries and high obstacle clutter. These tests demonstrate resilient performance in challenging conditions. To facilitate ease of adoption, we open-source the implementation alongside supporting documentation, validation, and evaluation datasets https://github.com/ntnu-arl/unified_autonomy_stack. A video giving the overview of the paper and the field experiments is available at https://youtu.be/l8Su8OXsM-E.

</details>


### [240] [Adaptive Smooth Tchebycheff Attention for Multi-Objective Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12771)
*Alejandro Murillo-Gonzalez,Mahmoud Ali,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多目标强化学习中非凸Pareto前沿难以覆盖与优化不稳定的矛盾，本文提出了自适应平滑切比雪夫框架，通过动态调节优化平滑度，实现了非凸区域策略的稳健发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性标量化方法无法获取非凸Pareto前沿解，而传统的非线性标量化方法（如切比雪夫）在深度强化学习中存在梯度方差大和优化不稳定的问题。

Method: 提出了一个自适应平滑切比雪夫框架（Adaptive Smooth Tchebycheff），通过引入冲突驱动控制器（conflict-driven controller），根据实时的梯度干涉情况动态调节优化景观的曲率。在目标一致时向非凸标量化推进，在梯度冲突时自动切换至稳定的平滑近似。

Result: 在机器人隐蔽视觉搜索任务中，该方法成功发现了线性基准无法覆盖的非凸区域Pareto最优策略，且相比传统的静态非线性方法具有更高的训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的自适应平滑切比雪夫（Adaptive Smooth Tchebycheff）框架能有效平衡多目标强化学习中的训练稳定性与帕累托前沿的覆盖能力，为解决非凸Pareto前沿的优化难题提供了一种鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning in robotic domains requires balancing complex, non-convex trade-offs between conflicting objectives. While linear scalarization methods provide stability, they are theoretically incapable of recovering solutions within non-convex regions of the Pareto front. Conversely, static non-linear scalarizations (e.g., Tchebycheff) can theoretically access these regions but often suffer from severe gradient variance and optimization instability in deep RL. In this work, we propose an Adaptive Smooth Tchebycheff framework that resolves this tension by dynamically modulating the curvature of the optimization landscape. We introduce a novel conflict-driven controller that regulates the optimization smoothness based on real-time gradient interference. This allows the agent to anneal toward precise, non-convex scalarization when objectives align, while elastically reverting to stable, smooth approximations when destructive gradient conflicts emerge. We validate our approach on a challenging robotic stealth visual search task -- a proxy for monitoring of protected/fragile ecosystems -- where an agent must balance search, exposure/interference minimization and exploration speed. Extensive ablations confirm that our conflict-aware adaptation enables the robust discovery of Pareto-optimal policies in non-convex regions inaccessible to linear baselines and unstable for static non-linear methods.
  Website: https://alejandromllo.github.io/research/pasta/

</details>


### [241] [Emotional Expression in Low-Degrees-of-Freedom Robots: Assessing Perception with Reachy Mini](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12786)
*Amit Rogel,Elmira Yadollahi,Guy Laban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现，即使是低自由度的非拟人化机器人，也能通过有限的表达方式传递情感意义（特别是效价与唤醒度）并影响人们的社会评价，证明了其在情感交互研究中的基准价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前人类对低自由度、非拟人化机器人如何表达并被解读情感的了解较少，研究旨在确定这类受限机器人是否能有效地进行情感传达并影响人们的社会评价。

Method: 采用在线被试内实验（within-subjects study），招募100名参与者观看包含Reachy Mini呈现10种不同情绪的短视频，通过分类识别、效价（valence）与唤醒度（arousal）评级及社会感知特质评估来分析数据。

Result: 参与者对具体情绪标签的识别率一般，但在识别情感的广义维度（效价和唤醒度）上表现较好；此外，积极的情绪表达会提升机器人给人的温暖感和社交感。

Conclusion: 即使是受限的非拟人化机器人表达，也能有效传递情感并影响社会感知，证明低自由度（low-DoF）机器人具备在大众参与的交互中进行情感沟通的潜力。

Abstract: Emotion expression is central to human--robot interaction, yet little is known about how people interpret affect on robots with sparse, non-anthropomorphic expressive capabilities. This study examined how people perceive emotional expressions displayed by Reachy Mini (Pollen Robotics and Hugging Face), a low-degree-of-freedom (low-DoF) robot with a constrained and distinctly non-human expressive repertoire. In an online within-subjects study, 100 participants viewed 10 short video clips of Reachy Mini expressing different emotions and, for each clip, identified the perceived emotion, rated its valence and arousal, and evaluated the robot on social-perception traits. Exact emotion recognition was modest overall and varied considerably across expressions, with anger, sadness, and interest recognized more reliably than emotions such as love, pleasure, shame, and disgust. However, participants were generally more successful at recovering broader affective meaning than exact emotion labels, particularly along valence and arousal dimensions. Emotional expressions also shaped social evaluation, as positive expressions were perceived as warmer and more sociable than negative ones, and animacy varied less across conditions. These findings suggest that even constrained robotic expressions can communicate affective meaning and influence social impressions, positioning Reachy Mini as a useful benchmark for studying affective communication in low-DoF robots.

</details>


### [242] [Lifelong Learning in Vision-Language Models: Enhanced EWC with Cross-Modal Knowledge Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12789)
*Hamza Ahmed Durrani,Rafay Suleman Durrani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对 LVLM 的持续学习框架，通过改进的 EWC 正则化和参数高效微调技术，在极低计算代价下显著抑制了多模态模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘，并维持了跨模态对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）在进行序列化增量学习新任务时，会面临严重的灾难性遗忘问题，且在多模态环境下维持跨模态特征对齐的难度极大。

Method: 提出了一种结合增强型弹性权重联合（EWC）与参数高效微调（PEFT）的多模态持续学习框架，具体包括：多模态费舍尔信息矩阵（FIM）计算、跨模态一致性保持机制以及考虑视觉与文本编码器依赖性的自适应正则化方法。

Result: 实验表明，该框架相比传统序列训练方式，遗忘率降低了 78%，同时在仅增加 15% 计算开销的前提下，成功保持了模态间的对齐一致性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过整合增强的 EWC 与参数高效微调技术，有效解决了多模态大模型在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，并保持了跨模态对齐，为实现动态环境下的终身学习多模态 AI 提供了技术基础。

Abstract: Large language-vision models (LVLMs) such as CLIP, Flamingo, and BLIP have revolutionized AI by enabling understanding across textual and visual modalities. These models excel at tasks like image captioning, visual question answering, and cross-modal retrieval. However, they face catastrophic forgetting when learning new tasks sequentially, particularly challenging in multi-modal settings where preserving cross-modal alignments adds complexity to the learning process. This paper presents a comprehensive continual learning framework for LVLMs that combines enhanced Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) with parameter-efficient fine-tuning techniques. We integrate multi-modal Fisher Information Matrix calculation, consistency preservation across modalities, and adaptive regularization that considers dependencies across visual and textual encoders. The framework achieves a 78% reduction in forgetting rates relative to naive sequential training approaches through extensive evaluation testing. The framework also preserves alignment between modalities during sequential learning with only 15% additional computational cost. This work advances the state of the art in lifelong learning for multi-modal AI systems, with direct applications to autonomous driving, intelligent robotic assistants, and adaptive robotic systems that must continuously learn in dynamic real-world environments.

</details>


### [243] [DynoJEPP: Joint Estimation, Prediction and Planning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12897)
*Mikolaj Kliniewski,Jesse Morris,Yiduo Wang,Ian R. Manchester,Viorela Ila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynoJEPP 提出了一种基于因子图的有向信息约束架构，解决了机器人联合规划中预测/规划反馈污染状态估计的经典难题，确保了在动态环境下的安全协作导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联合估计、预测与规划的因子图方法存在信息反馈回路，导致预测和规划过程反向污染状态估计，进而引起行为异常及不安全规划。

Method: 基于因子图（factor-graph）的框架，提出了一种新型“有向因子”（directed factor）来强制信息单向流动；并扩展至 Cooperative DynoJEPP 以支持对协同对象行为的建模。

Result: 实验表明，有向因子对于安全导航至关重要；在缺少该机制的情况下，机器人会出现频繁碰撞，而 DynoJEPP 能有效维持估计精度并实现安全路径规划。

Conclusion: DynoJEPP 通过引入有向因子成功解决了联合优化中信息反馈导致的估计偏移问题，显著提升了机器人在动态环境中的导航安全性和协作能力。

Abstract: DynoJEPP is a factor-graph-based framework that jointly formulates and simultaneously optimizes estimation, prediction, and planning in dynamic environments. In conventional factor-graph-based approaches that jointly formulate estimation, prediction, and planning, information from prediction and planning feeds back into state estimation, yielding corrupted estimates, undesired behaviors, and unsafe plans. To address this, DynoJEPP introduces a novel directed factor that enforces directional information flow within the factor graph, preventing prediction and planning from corrupting state estimation. We evaluate the impact of directed factors on inter-module interactions during navigation in both static and dynamic environments. Our results demonstrate that these factors are critical for safe operation, as without them, the robot collides in the majority of experiments. Building on this, we further introduce Cooperative DynoJEPP, which enables the ego robot to incorporate cooperative object behavior into its prediction and trajectory planning.

</details>


### [244] [Distributionally Robust Safety Under Arbitrary Uncertainties: A Safety Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12974)
*Daniel M. Cherenson,Haejoon Lee,Taekyung Kim,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对具有分布模糊性的非线性系统的高效安全过滤框架，通过将高维分布鲁棒优化简化为一维切换搜索，实现在不确定环境下的实时概率安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非线性系统中，当系统的干扰分布未知或具有任意不确定（分布模糊性）时，难以确保系统的概率安全性，且传统的分布鲁棒优化在线计算复杂度过高。

Method: 1. 采用分布式鲁棒（DR）框架，利用Wasserstein模糊集处理任意分布不确定性；2. 基于备份安全过滤框架，通过切换策略保证安全；3. 将高维DR轨迹优化简化为一维切换时间搜索；4. 设计基于有限样本保证的采样认证程序，通过对比经验故障概率与Wasserstein膨胀阈值来验证安全性。

Result: 在Dubins车辆、高速赛车和战斗机三个系统上的仿真实验证明，该方法不仅能够有效保证复杂非线性系统的概率安全，且在实时计算效率上表现优异。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种基于Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化的备份安全过滤方法，通过将复杂的安全认证简化为一维切换时间搜索，实现了在不确定分布下的非线性系统实时概率安全保证，具有高计算效率和广泛的适用性。

Abstract: In this work, we study how to ensure probabilistic safety for nonlinear systems under distributional ambiguity. Our approach builds on a backup-based safety filtering framework that switches between a high-performance nominal policy and a certified backup policy to ensure safety. To handle arbitrary uncertainties from ambiguous distributions, i.e., where the distribution is not of specific structure and the true distribution is unknown, we adopt a distributionally robust (DR) formulation using Wasserstein ambiguity sets. Rather than solving a high-dimensional DR trajectory optimization problem online, we exploit the structure of backup-based safety filtering to reduce safety certification to a one-dimensional search over the switching time between nominal and backup policies. We then develop a sampling-based certification procedure with finite-sample guarantees, where empirical failure probabilities are compared against a Wasserstein-inflated threshold. We validate our method through simulations across three systems, from a Dubins vehicle to a high-speed racing car and a fighter jet, demonstrating the broad applicability and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [245] [Local Conformal Calibration of Dynamics Uncertainty from Semantic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13028)
*Luís Marques,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCULAR 是一种新型的共形预测算法，它利用视觉感知信息在跨环境场景下对系统不确定性进行校准，无需强假设即可为未知环境中的动力学预测提供严格的概率安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的共形预测方法缺乏对状态-动作空间不同区域的区分能力，且通常需要特定环境的数据，难以在未见过的测试环境下提供可靠的不确定性保证（涵盖任意保真度的模型失配、随机扰动及认知不确定性）。

Method: 提出了一种基于共形预测（Conformal Prediction）的算法 OCULAR。通过引入感知信息（Observation-aware），利用视觉相似环境的数据对线性高斯动态模型进行局部校准；通过非渐近（non-asymptotic）和无分布（distribution-free）的校准程序，区分不同输入空间区域的不确定性水平。

Result: 经数值验证，OCULAR 在双积分器系统（含随机扰动和模型失配）中，无论是同分布还是分布外场景，均能提供有效的覆盖保证，且在不需要特定环境数据的前提下，表现出比现有基线更高的体积效率。

Conclusion: OCULAR 有效解决了跨环境下的状态预测不确定性量化问题，通过利用视觉信息实现环境间的泛化，确保在未知环境中提供具有统计学保证的概率预测，且在资源效率和鲁棒性方面优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: We introduce Observation-aware Conformal Uncertainty Local-Calibration (OCULAR), a conformal prediction-based algorithm that uses perception information to provide uncertainty quantification guarantees for unseen test-time environments. While previous conformal approaches lack the ability to discriminate between state-action space regions leading to higher or lower model mismatch, and require environment-specific data, our method uses data collected from visually similar environments to provably calibrate a given linear Gaussian dynamics model of arbitrary fidelity. The prediction regions generated from OCULAR are guaranteed to contain the future system states with, at least, a user-set likelihood, despite both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty -- i.e., uncertainty arising from both stochastic disturbances and lack of data. Our guarantees are non-asymptotic and distribution-free, not requiring strong assumptions about the unknown real system dynamics. Our calibration procedure enables distinguishing between observation-velocity-action inputs leading to higher and lower next-state-uncertainty, which is helpful for probabilistically-safe planning. We numerically validate our algorithm on a double-integrator system subject to random perturbations and significant model mismatch, using both a simplified sensor and a more realistic simulated camera. Our approach appropriately quantifies uncertainty both when in-distribution and out-of-distribution, being comparatively volume-efficient to baselines requiring environment-specific data.

</details>


### [246] [MUJICA: Multi-skill Unified Joint Integration of Control Architecture for Wheeled-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13058)
*Yuqi Li,Peng Zhai,Yueqi Zhang,Xiaoyi Wei,Quancheng Qian,Zhengxu He,Qianxiang Yu,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MUJICA 是一个统一的轮腿机器人控制框架，通过单一策略集成多种运动技能并结合电机约束建模，实现了高性能、鲁棒且环境自适应的自主运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决轮腿机器人在复杂地形中难以平衡轮式行驶与腿式控制，以及在嘈杂的本体感知和受限的电机物理约束下，难以实现高性能、鲁棒且自适应的运动控制的问题。

Method: 提出多技能统一联合控制架构（MUJICA），采用单一策略集成全向移动、高平台攀爬和故障恢复等低级技能，通过指示变量区分技能，并结合精确的直流电机约束建模进行端到端联合训练；同时引入基于仅本体感知的高级技能选择器，实现环境自适应。

Result: 在仿真和 Unitree Go2-W 实机实验中验证，MUJICA 显著增强了 Sim-to-Real 的鲁棒性，能够依据环境信息自动选择最佳运动模式，并实现平滑的技能转换，在复杂环境下表现出优异的运动性能。

Conclusion: MUJICA 框架通过统一的策略架构和精确的电机约束建模，显著提升了轮腿机器人（如 Unitree Go2-W）在非结构化环境中的适应性、鲁棒性和任务成功率，实现了多技能之间的无缝切换。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots hold promise for traversing complex terrains and offer superior mobility compared to legged robots. However, wheeled-legged robots must effectively balance both wheeled driving and legged control. Furthermore, due to noisy proprioceptive sensing and real-world motor constraints, realizing robust and adaptive locomotion at peak performance of motors remains challenging. We propose the Multi-skill Unified Joint Integration of Control Architecture (MUJICA), a unified, fully proprioceptive control framework for wheeled-legged robots that integrates diverse low-level skills-including omnidirectional moving, high platform climbing, and fall recovery-within a single policy. All skills, distinguished by unique indicator variables, are trained jointly with accurate DC-motor constraint modeling. Additionally, a high-level skill selector is learned to dynamically choose the optimal skill based solely on proprioceptions, enabling adaptive responses to the surrounding environment. Therefore, MUJICA enhances sim-to-real robustness and enables seamless transitions across diverse locomotion modes, facilitating autonomous adjustment to the environment. We validate our framework in both simulation and real-world experiments on the Unitree Go2-W robot, demonstrating significant improvements in adaptability and task success in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [247] [When Absolute State Fails: Evaluating Proprioceptive Encodings for Robust Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13067)
*Maxime Alvarez,Ryo Watanabe,Paul Crook,Afshin Zeinaddini Meymand,Suvin Kurian,Pablo Ferreiro,Genki Sano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机器人本体感觉表征方法——“片段相对坐标系”，有效解决了机器人部署中因参考系不同导致的泛化难题，提升了机器人应对未知环境的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端机器人策略在从模拟向现实部署时存在分布偏移（domain gap），尤其是当面对动态参考系（moving frames of reference）时，现有模型在未知测试条件下的泛化能力不足。

Method: 通过系统性地研究不同的本体感觉（proprioceptive state）表征策略，对比了多种关节状态编码方式（如固定参考系 vs. 移动参考系）在端到端策略训练中的表现。

Result: 在真实机器人环境的广泛实验中，相比基准方法，“基于片段的相对坐标系”表征在分布内（in-distribution）和分布外（out-of-distribution）任务中均表现出更优的性能与稳健性。

Conclusion: 使用“基于片段的相对坐标系”（episode-wise relative frame）作为本体感觉的表征方式，是提升机器人任务性能与泛化鲁棒性的最优策略。该方法能够有效利用不同参考系下采集的数据，提升机器人在未知环境下的适应能力。

Abstract: As end-to-end robotic policies are progressively deployed in the real world to solve real tasks, they face a gap between the training and inference conditions. Scaling the amount and diversity of the training data has shown some success in improving zero-shot generalization, yet robots still fail when faced with new, unseen test conditions. For instance, while robots with fixed frames of reference are common, those with moving frames pose a greater challenge for deployment. To address this specific instance of the issue, we present a study of strategies for encoding the robot's proprioceptive state to improve both in- and out-of-distribution performance at test time. Through a systematic study of joint representations, we find that a simple episode-wise relative frame provides the best trade-off between task performance and robustness, outperforming the baselines in extensive real-robot experiments conducted in a realistic test environment. The results suggest a practical path to leveraging data collected by robots with varying frames of reference and deployment to unseen test configurations.

</details>


### [248] [TouchAnything: A Dataset and Framework for Bimanual Tactile Estimation from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13083)
*Jianyi Zhou,Ziteng Gao,Feiyang Hong,Zirui Liu,Guannan Zhang,Weisheng Dai,Ruichen Zhen,Chuqiao Lyu,Haotian Wu,Yinian Mao,Xushi Wang,Yuxiang Jiang,Wenbo Ding,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对以自我为中心视频数据缺乏触觉反馈的问题，本文提出了包含大规模触觉标注的EgoTouch数据集，并开发了TouchAnything视觉转触觉预测框架，旨在实现大规模的物理感知具身表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的以自我为中心（egocentric）视频数据集缺乏触觉反馈，导致模型难以学习到物理世界交互中关键的接触、力和压力信息，从而限制了具身智能模型对物理交互动态的理解。

Method: 1. 构建数据集：收集了包含208项任务、1891个片段的多视角以自我为中心数据集EgoTouch（融合RGB视频、双侧腕部视角、3D手部姿态及可穿戴传感器压力图）。\n2. 开发框架：提出TouchAnythingbaseline模型，利用以自我为中心视角作为主要输入，并灵活融合腕部视角，通过视觉预测触觉压力图。

Result: 实验表明引入腕部视角可显著提升触觉预测准确度，相比单一以自我为中心视图，Contact IoU提升约5.0%，Volumetric IoU提升约6.1%。

Conclusion: EgoTouch不仅弥补了以自我为中心的视频数据在触觉传感方面的空白，还通过TouchAnything框架证实了视觉与触觉的强关联性，为具身智能研究中学习物理 grounded（基于物理现实）的交互表征提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Egocentric human video data, which captures rich human-environment interactions and can be collected at scale, has become a key driver of embodied intelligence research. However, existing egocentric datasets typically lack tactile sensing, a critical modality that provides direct cues about contact, force, and pressure in human-object interaction. Without such signals, models struggle to learn physically grounded representations of real-world interaction dynamics. While tactile sensors provide these cues, deploying high-quality tactile hardware at scale remains expensive and cumbersome. This raises a central question: can tactile feedback be inferred directly from visual observations, enabling scalable tactile supervision for egocentric video data and supporting physically grounded embodied learning? To enable research in this direction, we introduce EgoTouch, a large-scale multi-view egocentric dataset with dense tactile supervision for bimanual hand-object interaction. EgoTouch comprises 208 manipulation tasks spanning 1,891 episodes in diverse indoor and outdoor environments, with synchronized multi-view RGB (head-mounted egocentric and dual wrist-mounted cameras), bimanual 3D hand pose, and continuous pressure maps from wearable tactile sensors. Building on EgoTouch, we introduce TouchAnything, a baseline multi-view vision-to-touch prediction framework that uses the egocentric view as the primary input and flexibly leverages available wrist-mounted views at inference time. Experiments show that incorporating wrist-mounted views generally improves tactile prediction over egocentric-only input, achieving up to 5.0% relative improvement in Contact IoU and 6.1% relative improvement in Volumetric IoU. We will publicly release the dataset, code, and benchmark.

</details>


### [249] [Object Manipulation of the Variable Topology Truss system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13086)
*Andrew Jang-Ho Bae,Myeongjin Choi,Haorui Li,Mark Yim,TaeWon Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对变拓扑桁架（VTT）机器人缺乏操作策略的问题，提出了一种混合控制框架，通过结合执行器力反馈和基于模型的任务力计算，实现了对物体操作任务中位置与力的协同控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变拓扑桁架（VTT）机器人虽被提出用于快速部署操作，但其操作策略尚未得到充分研究，缺乏有效的协同位置与力控制方法。

Method: 提出了一种无需显式解耦的位置/力混合控制框架：在执行器层采用基于传感器的力反馈控制器以克服高摩擦，在任务层利用VTT静力学模型计算成员力以实现末端执行器节点的力控制。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统在单模块和完整VTT系统上均表现出良好的力跟踪性能，并在两种代表性配置中实现了对象操作，定量评估显示该方法具有位置和力跟踪的协同稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过实验验证了所提出的混合控制框架在VTT系统上实现一致且可靠的对象操作的可行性，填补了变拓扑桁架机器人操作策略的研究空白。

Abstract: This paper presents an object manipulation strategy for the Variable Topology Truss (VTT) system, a truss robot that comprises actuated truss members connected by passive spherical joints. Although truss robots were originally proposed as rapidly deployable manipulators, manipulation strategy has not been studied thoroughly. To enable manipulation, we introduce a hybrid control framework that regulates position and force concurrently without explicit decoupling. At the actuator level, each member employs a sensor-based force feedback controller to generate the desired axial forces despite high actuator friction. At the task level, the forces applied at the end-effector nodes are produced by computing the required member forces using a static model of the VTT. We evaluate force-tracking performance through experiments on both a single member module and the full VTT system. Finally, we demonstrate object manipulation using two representative configurations and quantitatively assess combined position and force tracking performance. Experimental results confirm that the proposed approach enables consistent and reliable object manipulation with the VTT system.

</details>


### [250] [Identification of Non-Transversal Bifurcations of Linkages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13094)
*Andreas Mueller,P. C. López Custodio,J. S. Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了运动学切锥的固有信息足以区分非横截相交的运动分支，并据此改进了现有的局部运动分析算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动学局部分析方法虽然能识别有限运动和运动曲线的切线，但在处理运动分支非横截相交（一种罕见但具有挑战性的情况）时存在局限性。

Method: 基于现有的运动学切锥算法框架进行改进，通过对构造性定义过程的深入分析，推导出一种能够处理运动分支非横截相交问题的计算方法。

Result: 提出了一种改进的算法框架，成功解决了在复杂奇异点处区分重合或相切运动分支的计算难题。

Conclusion: 运动学切锥的构造性定义中已包含区分非横截相交运动分支所需的所有信息，通过扩展现有的计算框架，可以实现对这些特殊运动情形的准确识别。

Abstract: The local analysis is an established approach to the study of singularities and mobility of linkages. Key result of such analyses is a local picture of the finite motion through a configuration. This reveals the finite mobility at that point and the tangents to smooth motion curves. It does, however, not immediately allow to distinguish between motion branches that do not intersect transversally (which is a rather uncommon situation that has only recently been discussed in the literature). The mathematical framework for such a local analysis is the kinematic tangent cone. It is shown in this paper that the constructive definition of the kinematic tangent cone already involves all information necessary to separate different motion branches. A computational method is derived by amending the algorithmic framework reported in previous publications.

</details>


### [251] [SECOND-Grasp: Semantic Contact-guided Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13117)
*Han Yi Shin,Heeju Ko,Jaewon Mun,Qixing Huang,Jaehyeok Lee,Sung June Kim,Honglak Lee,Sujin Jang,Sangpil Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SECOND-Grasp 框架结合了视觉语言的语义推理和多视角几何一致性验证，实现了兼顾语义理解与物理稳定的高成功率灵巧手抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决机器人灵巧抓取中物理稳定性与语义任务指导通常被孤立对待的问题，试图通过集成两者来实现既物理可行又符合语义意图的抓取。

Method: 提出了一种名为 SECOND-Grasp 的统一框架，包含三个核心步骤：1. 利用视觉语言模型进行粗粒度接触点推理；2. 引入“语义-几何一致性细化”（SGCR）模块，通过多视角一致性和几何验证修正接触点图；3. 通过逆运动学将接触点转换为手部姿态，为策略学习提供监督信号。

Result: 在 DexGraspNet 上，该方法在已知和未知物体类别上的抓取成功率分别达到 98.2% 和 97.7%，在意图感知抓取方面分别提升了 12.8% 和 26.2%。此外，该方法在 Shadow Hand 和 Allegro Hand 等不同硬件上也展现了良好的效果。

Conclusion: SECOND-Grasp 框架成功地将语义任务理解与物理稳定接触相结合，在提升灵巧手抓取成功率和意图理解方面表现出显著优势，并证明了其在不同数据集和硬件平台上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Achieving reliable robotic manipulation, such as dexterous grasping, requires a synergy between physically stable interactions and semantic task guidance, yet these objectives are often treated as separate, disjoint goals. In this paper, we investigate how to integrate dexterous grasping techniques, i.e., physically stable grasps for object lifting and language-guided grasp generation, to achieve both physical stability and semantic understanding. To this end, we propose SECOND-Grasp (SEmantic CONtact-guided Dexterous Grasping), a unified framework that enables robotic hands to dynamically adjust grasping strategies based on semantic reasoning while ensuring physical feasibility. We begin by obtaining coarse contact proposals through vision-language reasoning to infer where contacts should occur based on object properties, followed by segmentation to localize these regions across views. To further ensure consistency across multiple viewpoints, we introduce Semantic-Geometric Consistency Refinement (SGCR), which refines initial contact predictions by enforcing semantic consistency across views and removing geometrically invalid regions, yielding reliable 3D contact maps. Then, we derive a feasible hand pose for each contact map via inverse kinematics, generating a supervision signal for policy learning. Our approach, trained on DexGraspNet, consistently outperforms baselines in lifting success rate on both seen and unseen categories, achieving 98.2% and 97.7%, respectively, while also improving intent-aware grasping by 12.8% and 26.2%. We further show promising results on additional datasets and robotic hands, including Shadow Hand and Allegro Hand.

</details>


### [252] [Towards Long-horizon Embodied Agents with Tool-Aligned Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13119)
*Zixing Lei,Changxing Liu,Yichen Xiong,Minhao Xiong,Yuanzhuo Ding,Zhipeng Zhang,Weixin Li,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了“VLAs-as-Tools”策略，将长程机器人任务拆解为高层VLM任务规划与多项专用VLA物理工具执行任务，有效突破了单一VLA模型在复杂、长程任务中的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在处理长程任务时，面临着“长期闭环规划”与“多元物理操作”的双重计算与执行负担，导致模型在复杂任务中表现受限。

Method: 1. 架构解耦：采用高层VLM负责全局规划与任务分解，底层VLA处理具体物理操作；2. 接口设计：引入VLA工具族接口，支持显式工具选择与进度反馈，实现事件驱动的闭环重规划；3. TAPT训练：提出工具对齐后训练（Tool-Aligned Post-Training），利用工具族残差适配器（Residual Adapters）高效获取专用化工具。

Result: 在LIBERO-Long数据集上成功率提升4.8个百分点，在RoboTwin数据集上提升23.1个百分点，同时指令执行忠诚度（Non-biased Rate）提升15.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将长程任务解构为高层推理（VLM）与底层专用工具（VLA）执行，有效地解决了长程任务中规划与控制的双重负担，显著提升了机器人的执行成功率与指令执行忠诚度。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are effective robot action executors, but they remain limited on long-horizon tasks due to the dual burden of extended closed-loop planning and diverse physical operations. We therefore propose VLAs-as-Tools, a strategy that distributes this burden across a high-level vision language model (VLM) agent for temporal reasoning and a family of specialized VLA tools for diverse local physical operations. The VLM handles scene analysis, global planning, and recovery, while each VLA tool executes a bounded subtask. To tightly couple agent planning with VLA tool execution in long-horizon tasks, we introduce a VLA tool-family interface that exposes explicit tool selection and in-execution progress feedback, enabling efficient event-triggered agent replanning without continuous agent polling. To obtain diverse specialized VLA tools that faithfully follow agent invocations, we further propose Tool-Aligned Post-Training (TAPT), which constructs invocation-aligned training units for instruction following and adopts tool-family residual adapters for efficient tool specialization. Experiments show that VLAs-as-Tools improves the success rate of $π_{0.5}$ by 4.8 points on LIBERO-Long and 23.1 points on RoboTwin, and further enhances invocation fidelity by 15.0 points as measured by Non-biased Rate. Code will be released.

</details>


### [253] [Multi-Depth Uniform Coverage Path Planning for Unmanned Surface Vehicle Surveying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13123)
*Maider Larrazabal,Tong Yang,Izaro Goienetxea,Jaime Valls Miro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人船测绘，提出了一种考虑海底深度的自适应路径规划算法，通过动态调整覆盖间距和传感器参数，克服了传统方法覆盖不均的缺陷，大幅提升了测绘覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的等深面覆盖路径规划算法忽略了海底深度对多波束测深仪探测范围的影响，导致实际海底覆盖率不均匀且效率低下。

Method: 利用粗略的先验深度信息对目标区域进行预处理，动态优化路径规划中的扫描行间距，并针对不同深度自适应调整多波束测深仪的探测波束孔径。

Result: 在合成地形测试中覆盖率超过99%（对比传统方法的75%），在真实港口场景中覆盖率超过92%（对比传统方法的65%），证明了算法的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种考虑海底深度变化的自动覆盖路径规划算法，通过自适应调整扫描间距和传感器参数，显著提升了测绘覆盖率和自动化程度，具有高度的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel automatic coverage path planning algorithm for bathymetry surveying with unmanned surface vehicles. The detection range of the mapping sensor employed - a multibeam echo sounder - is heavily influenced by local seafloor depths. Hence, a path designed to uniformly cover the sea surface does not guarantee uniform coverage of the seafloor. Yet this is currently the typical process for bathymetric surveys, with the simplistic boustrophedon scheme along manually selected waypoints at constant depths being the most widespread planner used. The proposed scheme incorporates coarse prior depth information to pre-process the target region and adaptively guide path generation and sensing range configuration. By explicitly accounting for depth variations, the proposed algorithm designs a coverage path with optimised spacing between survey passes that adjusts the sensing beam aperture to achieve more consistent seafloor coverage. The proposed method is shown to offer significant improvements in both synthetic and real-world scenarios. Validations in challenging synthetic terrains achieves coverage ratios beyond 99%, a marked improvement when compared with traditional boustrophedon paths revealing a maximum 75% coverage. The same trend appears in realistic simulations using real bathymetric data from a coastal harbour, with coverage reaching over 92%, and significantly surpassing boustrophedon sweeps with coverage rates below 65%. Beyond improved performance, the scheme also brings a fully automated design, suitable for autonomous marine vehicles, thus offering practical utilities for real-world applications.

</details>


### [254] [MoCCA: A Movable Circle Probability of Collision Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13125)
*Tobias Kern,Christian Birkner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MoCCA 算法，通过优化的单圆几何形状近似方案解决自动驾驶中碰撞概率计算的计算效率与保守性之间的矛盾，并引入了基于方向方差的安全余量以确保安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中碰撞概率（POC）估计对于防撞至关重要，但现有的蒙特卡洛方法计算成本高，而传统单圆几何近似虽然计算快但过于保守，多圆近似则计算复杂度过高，难以满足实时应用需求。

Method: 提出了一种名为 MoCCA 的算法，将车辆形状简化为单个圆；通过优化算法最小化车辆间的相对距离；引入了基于方向方差的校准安全距离边缘来补偿形状近似导致的碰撞概率（POC）覆盖不足问题。

Result: MoCCA 在实现与标准单圆技术相当的低计算复杂度的前提下，有效降低了系统的保守性；同时，论文通过数学推导验证了该方法误差上限与车辆间距及方向方差的相关性，证明了其在实际应用中的可靠性。

Conclusion: MoCCA 是一种高效的车辆形状近似算法，通过基于方向方差的自适应安全距离预留，在保持计算高效性的同时，显著降低了传统单圆近似的过度保守性，并提供了理论上的误差上限保证。

Abstract: In automated driving, crash mitigation is crucial to ensure passenger safety. Accurate avoidance requires precise knowledge of the object's position and orientation. However, sensor noise and occlusions often result in tracking and prediction uncertainties. To account for these uncertainties, estimating the Probability of Collision (POC) is a critical requirement. While Monte Carlo sampling is a common estimation technique, its high computational demand and stochastic nature often render it unsuitable for real-time applications. Analytical POC calculations are simplified by approximating vehicle geometries using circular bounds. While multi-circle approximations offer higher fidelity than a single circumscribed circle, they significantly increase computational complexity. This paper proposes a shape approximation algorithm, MoCCA, which utilizes a single circle for each vehicle, optimized to minimize the relative distance between them. MoCCA maintains a computational efficiency comparable to standard single-circle techniques while reducing over-conservatism. To address the potential underestimation of POC inherent in partial coverage, we establish an upper bound for the approximation error, demonstrating that it depends primarily on inter-vehicle distance and orientation variance. Furthermore, we introduce a safety distance margin that can be calibrated solely based on orientation variance.

</details>


### [255] [Dynamics Computation of Soft-Rigid Hybrid-Link System and Its Application to Motion Analysis of an Athlete Wearing Sport Prosthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13192)
*Sunghee Kim,Yuta Shimane,Taiki Ishigaki,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对佩戴柔性假肢运动员的软硬混合链接运动分析框架，通过统一建模解决了人体骨骼与柔性假肢间的交互力分析及运动、动力学计算问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的肌肉骨骼模型多基于刚体多体系统，无法处理佩戴运动型柔性假肢时，人体骨骼与柔性假肢之间的复杂交互力及假肢变形问题。

Method: 1. 采用软硬混合链接系统统一建模方法，将柔性假肢（软体）与人体骨骼（刚体）集成；2. 应用逆运动学进行运动重建；3. 利用逆动力学估计关节转矩和地面反作用力；4. 结合肌肉截肢情况和假肢形变，评估肌肉力量。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在地面反作用力估计上的误差约为12%，并成功实现了对考虑截肢与假肢交互情形下的肌肉力量估计。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于软硬混合链接系统的运动分析框架，能够有效处理人体骨骼与柔性假肢间的相互作用，为佩戴柔性假肢的运动员提供了可靠的运动学和动力学分析工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a motion analysis framework for an athlete wearing sport-specific flexible prosthesis based on the soft-rigid hybrid-link system. Such a motion analysis is a challenging problem because we need to consider the interaction force between the rigid human skeleton system and a flexible prosthesis.
